### [1] [The \textsc{ABC}-equation revisited: missed integrable cases. Part I: classification and (quasi) integrable structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20672)
*I. S. Krasil'shchik*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 该文研究 \textsc{ABC} 方程 \(Au_xu_{yt}+Bu_yu_{xt}+Cu_tu_{xy}=0\)，在已知可积情形 \(A+B+C=0\)（Veronese web 方程）之外，新发现另外十种具有可积结构的情形，方法主要基于切覆盖与余切覆盖理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: \textsc{ABC} 方程此前仅证明在 \(A+B+C=0\)（Veronese web 方程）时可积，其余参数组合的可积性尚不清楚；本文旨在系统考察更一般的参数情形，寻找此前未知的可积结构。

Method: 技术手段本质上基于切覆盖（tangent covering）与余切覆盖（cotangent covering）理论，用于判定所考察方程是否具备可积结构。

Result: 研究发现另外十个情形（ten more cases）允许可积结构，且据作者所知，这些情形此前并未被报道。

Conclusion: 除 \(A+B+C=0\) 的 Veronese web 方程外，\textsc{ABC} 方程还存在十种新的可积情形，扩展了该方程族的可积性认识。

Abstract: We consider the \textsc{ABC}-equation $Au_xu_{yt} + Bu_yu_{xt} + Cu_tu_{xy} = 0$, $A$, $B$, $C\in\mathbb{R}$, which was proved to be integrable in the case $A + B + C = 0$ (the Veronese web equation). Our study reveals ten more cases that admit integrable structures that, to the best of our knowledge, where not known before. The techniques is based essentially on the theory of the tangent and cotangent coverings.

</details>

### [2] [Finite-size geometric crossover toward fragmentation of the potential energy shell in the quantum random energy model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21631)
*Ishita Modak,Vatsana Tiwari,Soumya Bera*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作通过构建与基态相关的势能壳层（potential energy shell），研究量子随机能量模型（quantum random energy model, QREM）在Fock空间超立方体（hypercube）上的连通性，并揭示其与有限尺寸分形维度之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QREM是研究Fock空间中有限能量密度下离域-局域化转变（delocalization-localization transition）的典型模型，但其对角能量的统计独立性与局域相互作用模型（如无序XXZ链）的关联能量形成对比，需要一个新的几何视角来刻画该转变。

Method: 构建由能量方差定义的势能壳层，考察其在Fock空间超立方体上的连通性随横场（transverse field）的变化；利用QREM对角能量的统计独立性，将壳层连通性映射为超立方体上的有效独立格点渗流（independent site percolation）问题，并用单跳近似（one-hop approximation）估计交叉尺度。

Result: 随横场变化，能量选择子图出现从全连通到碎片化（fragmented）的有限尺寸几何交叉；壳层连通性与由参与熵（participation entropy）提取的本征态有限尺寸分形维度相关联。在有限能量密度下，交叉尺度对系统尺寸L呈非单调依赖：增大L先利于连通，超过能量依赖尺度L*(ε)后趋势逆转为碎片化。

Conclusion: 势能壳层的连通性可作为刻画QREM中离域-局域化转变的几何量，其有限尺寸交叉行为通过渗流映射与单跳近似得到解析估计，并阐明交叉尺度随系统尺寸的非单调性。

Abstract: The quantum random energy model (QREM) is a paradigmatic model for studying delocalization-localization transitions at finite energy density in Fock space. In the computational basis, its diagonal energies are independent Gaussian random variables, in contrast to the correlated energies of local interacting models such as the disordered XXZ chain. In this work, we construct a potential energy shell associated with a basis state, defined through its energy variance, and investigate its connectivity on the Fock-space hypercube. As the transverse field is varied, this energy-selected subgraph undergoes a finite-size geometric crossover between fully connected and fragmented regimes. Furthermore, we observe a correlation between the shell connectivity and the finite-size fractal dimension of the eigenstates, as extracted from the participation entropy. For the QREM, the statistical independence of the diagonal energies allows the shell connectivity to be analyzed as an effective independent site percolation problem on the hypercube, with its crossover scale estimated within a one-hop approximation. At finite energy density, this estimate gives a nonmonotonic dependence of the crossover scale on system size $L$: increasing $L$ initially favors connectivity, while beyond an energy-dependent scale $L^*(\varepsilon)$ the trend reverses towards fragmentation.

</details>


### [3] [Attention over noisy examples: generalization and memorization in the exponential Hopfield network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22191)
*Francesco Alemanno*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 将 Hebbian 从样例学习协议迁移至指数型 Hopfield 模型（exponential Hopfield model，其零温更新即一步 softmax 注意力），通过信噪分析给出闭式重叠并揭示两条结论：干扰在约 √N 宽的温度窗口内指数小、可学原型数按 exp(c√N) 增长；学习失效机制由自旋玻璃相转变为记忆化（memorization），后者对应随机能量模型（random energy model）的冻结转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Hebbian 从样例学习只提供每个原型的模糊样例，其可学性受负载 α=K/N 与数据集熵 ρ=(1-r²)/(Mr²) 制约；本文将这一协议转移到指数型 Hopfield 模型，以考察 softmax 注意力式更新下的学习极限与失效模式。

Method: 把零温更新视为以未标注样例为键的 softmax 注意力步骤，对输出与未见原型之间的重叠 m⁽¹⁾=erf(1/√(2ρ_eff)) 进行信噪分析（signal-to-noise analysis）；其中 ρ_eff 为注意力熵（attention entropy），由 softmax 赋予正确类别的权重及其分散的样例数构成。并将 softmax 权重映射到随机能量模型分析冻结转变。

Result: 在随 √N 增宽的温度窗口内，原型间干扰以 Nβ 指数小，负载 α 退出，可学原型数增长为 exp(c√N)；失效机制上，成对模型经自旋玻璃相（spin-glass phase）失效，指数模型则经记忆化失效——softmax 将权重集中于最接近输入的训练样例并以其代替原型。冻结转变位于 β√(N(1-r²))≃√(2ln M)，可由承载权重的样例数测量。MNIST 上该诊断符合随机能量预测，fashion-MNIST 上则检测出类内子簇，且最佳准确率位于记忆化阈值之外，此时 softmax 读出局部子原型（sub-prototype）。

Conclusion: 指数型 Hopfield 模型在 softmax 注意力更新下具备优于成对模型的容量与不同的失效机制；记忆化被识别为随机能量模型的冻结转变，可作为可测诊断指标；实验表明超越记忆化阈值后 softmax 读出局部子原型并取得更优准确率。

Abstract: In Hebbian learning from examples a Hopfield network of $N$ neurons is not supplied with the $K$ archetypes it has to retrieve but with $M$ blurred examples of each, of quality $r$, and whether it can infer them depends on the load $α=K/N$ (interference among them) and on the dataset entropy $ρ=(1-r^2)/(Mr^2)$ (how little the examples say about them). We transfer this protocol to the exponential Hopfield model, whose zero-temperature update is a step of softmax attention with the unlabelled examples as keys. A signal-to-noise analysis gives in closed form the overlap between the output and an unseen archetype, $m^{(1)}={\rm erf}(1/\sqrt{2ρ_{\rm eff}})$: the majority-rule law of the pairwise model at small load, with $ρ$ replaced by an attention entropy $ρ_{\rm eff}$ built from the weight the softmax gives to the correct class and the number of examples it spreads it over. Two consequences follow. First, in a window of inverse temperatures $β$ that widens as $\sqrt N$ the interference between archetypes is exponentially small in $Nβ$: the load drops out and the number of archetypes that can be learnt grows as $\exp(c\sqrt N)$. Second, learning fails differently: in the pairwise model through a spin-glass phase, in the exponential model through memorization: the softmax concentrates its weight on the training example closest to the input and returns it in place of the archetype. Seen from the input the softmax weights are those of the random energy model, and memorization is its freezing transition, at $β\sqrt{N(1-r^2)}\simeq\sqrt{2\ln M}$; it is measurable on a head whose keys are examples as the number of examples carrying weight. On MNIST the diagnostic follows the random-energy prediction; on fashion-MNIST it detects sub-clusters in the classes, and the best accuracy lies beyond the memorization threshold, where the softmax reads out a local sub-prototype.

</details>

### [4] [The hadronic-vacuum-polarization contribution to the muon anomalous magnetic moment in isospin-symmetric lattice QCD with twisted-mass fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22224)
*C. Alexandrou,S. Bacchio,A. De Santis,A. Evangelista,J. Finkenrath,R. Frezzotti,G. Gagliardi,M. Garofalo,N. Kalntis,B. Kostrzewa,V. Lubicz,L. Maio,F. Margari,F. Pittler,S. Romiti,F. Sanfilippo,C. Schneider,S. Simula,N. Tantalo,C. Urbach,U. Wenger*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该工作通过格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）计算同位旋对称 QCD 下领头阶强子真空极化（HVP）对缪子反常磁矩的贡献，所得结果不确定度约 1%，与其他格点组结果一致，并强化了格点 QCD 与数据驱动色散方法之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缪子反常磁矩（muon anomalous magnetic moment）是检验标准模型（Standard Model）的关键观测量，其强子真空极化贡献主导理论误差。为与 FLAG 2024 评审及 WP25 白皮书给出的同位旋对称 QCD 定义精确匹配，需重新调整模拟参数并给出可靠的格点测定。

Method: 采用 Extended Twisted Mass Collaboration（ETMC）生成的规范组态（gauge configurations），含 $N_f = 2 + 1 + 1$ 味 Wilson-clover twisted-mass 夸克，覆盖四个格点间距与最高约 7.7 fm 的空间格点尺寸。将模拟参数重新调整以匹配 FLAG 2024 与 WP25 的同位旋对称 QCD 定义，并基于 $a \simeq 0.049, 0.057, 0.068, 0.079$ fm 的数据外推至连续极限（continuum limit）。

Result: 获得 $a_\mu^{\rm HVP, FLAG}(\rm{iso}) = (720.0 \pm 6.9) \times 10^{-10}$ 与 $a_\mu^{\rm HVP, WP25}(\rm{iso}) = (714.8 \pm 7.4) \times 10^{-10}$，相对不确定度约 1%，与其它格点组相应测定吻合良好。叠加 WP25 给出的领头同位旋破缺修正 $a_\mu^{\rm HVP, WP25}({\rm IB}) = 0.1(3.4)\times 10^{-10}$ 后，完整 HVP 值为 $a_\mu^{\rm HVP}= (714.9 \pm 8.1)\times 10^{-10}$。

Conclusion: 由该 HVP 值得到的标准模型缪子反常磁矩预言与费米实验室（Fermilab）Muon $g-2$ 实验最新结果完全一致，并加强了格点 QCD 与数据驱动（pre-CMD3）色散（dispersive）测定之间的张力。

Abstract: We present a lattice-QCD calculation of the leading-order hadronic-vacuum-polarization (HVP) contribution to the anomalous magnetic moment of the muon in isospin-symmetric QCD. We employ the gauge configurations generated by the Extended Twisted Mass Collaboration (ETMC) with $N_f = 2 + 1 + 1$ flavors of Wilson-clover twisted-mass quarks at four lattice spacings and at values of the quark-mass parameters matching the definition of isospin-symmetric QCD given in the FLAG review 2024 and in the 2025 edition of the Muon $g-2$ Theory Initiative White Paper (WP25). After retuning the simulation parameters to precisely match these two definitions of isospin-symmetric QCD, and after carrying out an extrapolation to the continuum limit based on the data at lattice spacings $a \simeq 0.049, 0.057, 0.068$ and $0.079$~fm, and spatial lattice sizes up to $L \simeq 7.7$~fm, we obtain $a_μ^{\rm HVP, FLAG}(\rm{iso}) = (720.0 \pm 6.9) \times 10^{-10}$ and $a_μ^{\rm HVP, WP25}(\rm{iso}) = (714.8 \pm 7.4) \times 10^{-10}$. Our results, which have a relative uncertainty of about $1 \%$, agree well with the corresponding determinations by other lattice groups. After adding to our determination of $a_μ^{\rm HVP, WP25}(\rm{iso})$ the leading isospin-breaking corrections provided in the WP25, namely $a_μ^{\rm HVP, WP25}({\rm IB}) = 0.1(3.4)\times 10^{-10}$, we obtain for the full HVP the value $a_μ^{\rm HVP}= (714.9 \pm 8.1)\times 10^{-10}$. This result translates into a Standard Model prediction for the muon anomalous magnetic moment that is fully consistent with the latest result by the Fermilab Muon $g-2$ experiment and strengthens the tension between lattice QCD and data-driven (pre-CMD3) dispersive determinations.

</details>


### [5] [Ergodic topology sampling toward the continuum with Stochastic Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20747)
*Claudio Bonanno,Andrea Bulgarelli,Elia Cellini,Alessandro Nada,Dario Panfalone,Davide Vadacchino,Lorenzo Verzichelli*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提供数值证据，表明在 $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ 杨-米尔斯理论（Yang-Mills theory）中，基于流（flow-based）的方法可缓解拓扑冻结（topological freezing），并分析了相关计算成本的标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点规范理论（lattice gauge theory）中，精细格距下拓扑冻结（topological freezing）会导致拓扑采样效率低下。本文旨在探索基于流的方法在该问题上的可行性。

Method: 采用开边界条件（open boundary conditions）与随机归一化流（Stochastic Normalizing Flows）相结合的计算策略，并分析其成本在格距细至 0.03 fm 时的标度（scaling）行为。

Result: 在所探索的参数范围内，平方拓扑荷（squared topological charge）的积分自相关时间（integrated autocorrelation time）与拓扑磁化率（topological susceptibility）的统计不确定度均保持可控，且处于现有计算资源可承受范围内。

Conclusion: 该结果标志着朝向在最新格点规范理论计算中使用更高效流式架构进行拓扑遍历采样（ergodic sampling）迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: In this work we provide numerical evidence that topological freezing in $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ Yang-Mills theory can be mitigated with a flow-based approach, as tested at lattice spacings as fine as 0.03~fm. We do so by analyzing the scaling of the costs of a computational strategy based on the combination of open boundary conditions and Stochastic Normalizing Flows. We show that the integrated autocorrelation time of the squared topological charge and the statistical uncertainty on the topological susceptibility remain under control throughout the explored parameter range with currently available computational resources. This marks an important step toward the use of even more efficient flow-based architectures for ergodic sampling of topology in state-of-the-art lattice gauge theory calculations.

</details>

### [6] [A Coupled Fluid Naghdi Shell Windkessel Model for Pulmonary Artery Banding: Partitioned Numerical Scheme and Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20966)
*Yifan Wang,Josip Tambaca,Suncica Canic*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个用于可变形血管中血流的三维流固耦合（fluid-structure interaction, FSI）模型，针对新生儿先天性心脏病中的肺动脉环缩术（pulmonary artery banding）场景，耦合任意拉格朗日-欧拉（Arbitrary Lagrangian Eulerian, ALE）框架下的不可压缩纳维-斯托克斯方程、Naghdi壳模型与三元件Windkessel模型，并引入无迹卡尔曼滤波（unscented Kalman filter）进行参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于新生儿先天性心脏病中肺动脉环缩术的临床背景，需要刻画局部缩窄（localized constrictions）对血流动力学与血管壁力学行为的影响。模型旨在支持对狭窄效应及其对下游血管特性依赖性的研究。

Method: 采用任意拉格朗日-欧拉框架下的不可压缩纳维-斯托克斯方程描述流体，血管壁用定义于二维参考域的三维圆柱形Naghdi壳模型（保留膜、弯曲与横向剪切变形，仅需参考中曲面的一阶导数），下游循环用三元件Windkessel模型表示。推导耦合变分形式，提出带Robin型界面条件并包含壁惯性的非迭代分区（non-iterative partitioned）格式；对线性化固定域问题在适当假设下建立离散能量估计；使用无迹卡尔曼滤波从入口压力观测中估计近端/远端阻力与顺应性。

Result: 数值实验在空间与时间加密下表现出一致的压力波传播与壁面变形。标准Naghdi壳与改进的以径向为主的壳模型对比，揭示了结构变形机制对波速与位移的影响。理想化肺动脉环缩算例展示了耦合流体-壳-Windkessel模型与参数估计的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够用于研究局部缩窄的血液动力学与力学效应及其对下游血管特性的依赖性，为相关临床问题的建模分析提供工具。

Abstract: We develop a 3D fluid structure interaction model for blood flow through deformable vessels with localized constrictions, motivated by pulmonary artery banding in neonates with congenital heart disease. The model couples incompressible Navier Stokes equations in an Arbitrary Lagrangian Eulerian framework with a Naghdi shell model for the arterial wall and a three element Windkessel model for downstream circulation. The shell describes a 3D cylindrical vessel wall with variable radius and is defined on a 2D reference domain. Its low regularity formulation retains membrane, bending, and transverse shear deformation while requiring only first derivatives of the reference midsurface, accommodating piecewise smooth geometries with discontinuous surface slopes.
  We derive the coupled variational formulation and develop a non-iterative partitioned scheme with a Robin-type interface condition incorporating arterial wall inertia. For a linearized fixed domain problem, we establish a discrete energy estimate under suitable assumptions. A unscented Kalman filter estimates proximal and distal resistances and compliance from inlet-pressure observations. Numerical experiments demonstrate consistent pressure wave propagation and wall deformation under spatial and temporal refinement. Comparisons of the standard Naghdi shell and a modified, predominantly radial shell model reveal how structural deformation mechanisms affect wave speed and displacement. An idealized pulmonary artery banding example demonstrates the coupled fluid shell Windkessel model with parameter estimation. The framework enables investigation of the hemodynamic and mechanical effects of localized constrictions and their dependence on downstream vascular properties.

</details>

### [7] [Equality of the Jellium and the Uniform Electron Gas Energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20910)
*Mihály A. Csirik*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在量子框架下证明，Lieb–Narnhofer 定义的 Jellium 基态单位体积能量与 Lewin–Lieb–Seiringer 定义的均匀电子气（uniform electron gas, UEG）能量相等。此结论此前仅在经典（classical）情形下由 Lewin–Lieb–Seiringer 及 Cotar–Petrache 独立建立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jellium 与均匀电子气（UEG）是电子气（electron gas）的两类标准模型，二者定义方式不同（前者含均匀中和背景，后者为恒定密度且无背景）。已有工作仅在经典情形下确认两者能量一致，量子情形下的对应关系尚待证明，本文即针对该空缺。

Method: 方法为数学证明：在量子设定下，对 Lieb–Narnhofer 定义的 Jellium 与 Lewin–Lieb–Seiringer 定义的 UEG，论证其基态单位体积能量（ground-state energy per unit volume）相等，即 e^ħ_Jel(ρ₀) = e^ħ_UEG(ρ₀)。摘要未给出推导细节或所用具体技术。

Result: 得到量子情形的能量等式 e^ħ_Jel(ρ₀) = e^ħ_UEG(ρ₀)，将经典情形已有的相等性结果推广至量子（含 ħ 依赖）框架。

Conclusion: 在量子情形下，Jellium 与 UEG 的基态单位体积能量严格一致，说明两种模型在能量层面等价。该结果将 Lewin–Lieb–Seiringer 与 Cotar–Petrache 的经典结论扩展至量子领域（摘要未说明进一步推论或开放问题）。

Abstract: Jellium, a system of electrons in a uniform neutralizing background, and the uniform electron gas (UEG), a system of electrons with constant density and no background, are two standard models of the electron gas. We provide numerical computations to illustrate the asymptotic formula and support the conjecture that the two bands remain separated for other values of the Berry curvature parameter.

Abstract: We continue the development, started in [AHP 25 (2023), 715-749], of a structural theory of approach to equilibrium in translation-invariant quantum spin systems. Dynamical Steady States (DSS) are defined as w*-limit points of the time-averaged evolution of an initial state. We show that if a DSS is an equilibrium state of a sufficiently regular interaction, then the specific energy of this interaction is necessarily a constant of the motion. Two distinct mechanisms lead to this conclusion, one based on dynamical surface energies and the other on conservation of specific relative entropy. We conjecture that these properties hold for a natural class of interactions.

The achieved minimum equals the increase of specific entropy.

Once locality is restored, the constants of motion determine the possible asymptotic equilibrium states. In particular, if finite-range locality is restored and the energy is the only finite-range constant of the motion, then every DSS is a thermal equilibrium state at the temperature fixed by energy conservation. The finite-time dynamical input underlying this theory is established in a companion paper.

Abstract: We study dynamical conservation laws and the propagation of thermodynamic structure in translation-invariant lattice quantum spin systems. Using Lieb-Robinson bounds [CMP 28 (1972), 251-257] in arbitrary dimension, and Araki's bounds [CMP 14 (1969), 120-157] in dimension one, we prove conservation of the specific entropy, specific energy, and specific relative entropy under suitable assumptions on the interactions. Abstract: I investigate the average and variance of species abundance distributions for fixed-point solutions to generalized Lotka-Volterra ecosystems. Two different analytical methods are constructed. First, I build up on the perturbative Random Matrix Theory approach developed in my earlier work [Phys. Rev. E. 113, L062202 (2026)], and formalize this method diagrammatically. The abundance average is given by a sum of planar diagrams with non-crossing vertex pairings which can be encoded as Dyck paths. This allows me to express the combinatorial factor multiplying perturbative terms at each order as Catalan and Narayana numbers, following which the abundance average is given by the latter's generating functions. In this way, the infinite perturbation expansion is exactly resummed in a closed-form formula. I have not been able to find a similar resummation for the abundance variance, however diagrammatic Feynman rules are expressed, following which the perturbative series can be calculated to any desirable order. Second, I do the same calculations using the cavity method, which gives closed-form formulas for both the abundance average and variance. For the average, the cavity formula gives the exact same result as the perturbative approach when the average interspecies interaction vanishes. When this is not the case, the two results are functionally different, however they are hardly distinguishable in their parametric range of validity. Comparison with numerical data indicate that the cavity result is more accurate over a larger parameter range.

</details>

*Arnaud Chéritat*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 该论文对以 $\mathbf{R1}$（称为 undertile，底层瓦片）为单调瓦片（monotile）的周期性铺砌（periodic tilings）给出完全分类，方法基于收缩（deflation）与断裂线（fracture lines）研究，并可用于重新证明相关非周期单调瓦片的非周期性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathbf{R1}$ 是若干非周期单调瓦片（aperiodic monotiles，如 Taylor-Socolar 单调瓦片）的共同底层结构，理解其铺砌结构有助于刻画和验证这些单调瓦片的非周期性，因此有必要对基于该 undertile 的铺砌进行系统分类。

Method: 采用收缩（deflation）方法，研究可执行收缩的区域之间所出现的断裂线（fracture lines），据此对断裂模式（fracture patterns）进行完全分类；该分类同时适用于基于 undertile 的非周期铺砌（non-periodic tilings）。

Result: 给出了基于 undertile 的周期性铺砌的完整分类，并给出对断裂模式的完全分类，该分类也适用于非周期铺砌情形。

Conclusion: 所得分类可用于重新证明某些现有基于 undertile 的单调瓦片（monotiles）的非周期性，从而为相关非周期单调瓦片的研究提供统一框架。

Abstract: We classify all periodic tilings with a monotile known as $\mathbf{R1}$ and called here the undertile, which underlies several aperiodic monotiles such as the Taylor-Socolar one. The classification is done using deflation, through a study of fracture lines between zones where deflation can be performed. A full classification of fracture patterns is given, that also applies to non-periodic tilings with the undertile. This can be used to reprove non-periodicity of some existing monotiles based on the undertile.

</details>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [18] [An exact computer-assisted lower bound for Fisher-information convexity along the two-dimensional heat flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20653)
*Fayez Abu-Ajamieh,Xiaoyan Huo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文研究二维热流下 Fisher 信息（Fisher information）微分不等式 I(t)I''(t)≥c[I'(t)]² 中的精确常数 c，构造了可机器验证的平方和（sum-of-squares）证书，证明 c≥231691/250000≈0.926764，并结合独立的傅里叶/高斯包络构造给出 0.926764≤c_*<0.94176665012062。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维情形下，无限制的对数凸性（log-convexity）取值 c=1 不成立，而已知的平方根凸性定理仅给出通用值 c=1/2。因此需要确定更精细的通用下界与精确常数 c_* 的范围。

Method: 采用显式二维指标记号表述证明；下界来自含严格正分母的精确有理多项式恒等式、加权全导数以及十一个正有理 Gram 矩阵；并辅以独立的精确傅里叶/高斯包络构造给出上界。

Result: 得到通用下界 c≥231691/250000≈0.926764，且全部 491 个记录的主子式（leading principal minors）严格为正、多项式恒等式残差为零；结合上界构造得到 0.926764≤c_*<0.94176665012062。

Conclusion: 在不施加对称性、对数凹性、有限方差或有限熵假设的条件下验证了更强的通用下界，但精确常数的取值仍未确定。

Abstract: We study the sharp constant in the differential inequality \[
  I(t)I''(t)\geq c\,[I'(t)]^2 \] for Fisher information along the probability-normalized heat flow on $\mathbb R^2$. The unrestricted log-convexity value $c=1$ is false in two dimensions, whereas the known square-root convexity theorem gives the universal value $c=1/2$. We construct an exact, machine-verifiable sum-of-squares certificate proving the stronger universal bound \[
  c\geq \frac{231691}{250000}=0.926764. \] Together with an independent exact Fourier/Gaussian-envelope construction, this gives \[ 0.926764\leq c_*<0.94176665012062, \] where $c_*$ is the sharp constant for positive-time Gaussian convolutions of arbitrary probability measures on $\mathbb R^2$. We formulate the proof in explicit two-dimensional index notation. The lower bound follows from an exact rational polynomial identity involving a strictly positive denominator, a weighted total derivative, and eleven positive rational Gram matrices. All $491$ recorded leading principal minors are strictly positive and the complete polynomial identity has zero residual. No symmetry, log-concavity, finite-variance, or finite-entropy assumption is imposed on the initial measure. The sharp value remains open.

</details>


### [19] [Information-Theoretic Limits for Rectangular Spiked Matrix Models with Rotationally Invariant Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16797)
*Qun Chen,Rudy Skerk,Jean Barbier,Francesco Camilli,Junjie Ma,Haochuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文研究具有加性旋转不变噪声（rotationally invariant noise）的矩形秩一尖峰矩阵模型（rank-one spiked matrix models）的推断信息论极限，利用复本方法（replica method）导出互信息变分公式与预测最小均方误差（MMSE）的鞍点方程，并建立其与正交近似消息传递（OAMP）状态演化不动点的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有秩一尖峰矩阵模型分析多假定噪声条目相互独立，难以刻画具有相关结构的噪声。该文旨在突破独立噪声假设，考察更为一般的加性旋转不变噪声下的推断极限，并厘清贝叶斯最优推断与算法性能之间的关系。

Method: 采用统计物理中的复本方法（replica method），推导信号与数据之间互信息的变分公式，以及预测秩一尖峰估计最小均方误差的鞍点方程组；随后在贝叶斯最优（Bayes-optimal）设定下，将其与近期提出的、使用贝叶斯最优去噪器（denoiser）的正交近似消息传递算法的状态演化（state evolution）不动点方程进行对照，并对若干旋转不变噪声系综与信号先验做数值求解及有限尺寸算法仿真。

Result: 在贝叶斯最优设定下，复本鞍点方程与使用贝叶斯最优去噪器的正交近似消息传递算法的状态演化不动点方程一致，从而为复本预测提供算法解释，并支持在相关不动点唯一时该算法具有贝叶斯最优性。数值结果表明：单一信息性不动点情形下该算法被预测达到最小均方误差，而存在多个不动点时出现统计—计算间隙（statistical-to-computational gap）；有限尺寸仿真与渐近预测相符。

Conclusion: 该工作将秩一尖峰矩阵模型的信息论极限分析推广至旋转不变噪声情形，并通过复本方法与正交近似消息传递状态演化的等价性，为算法最优性提供了理论依据；同时揭示了唯一不动点与多不动点两类机制，后者对应统计—计算间隙的存在。

Abstract: We study the information-theoretic limits of inference in rectangular rank-one spiked matrix models with additive rotationally invariant noise, thereby going beyond the standard assumption of independent noise entries. Using the replica method from statistical physics, we derive a variational formula for the mutual information between the signal and the data, together with a system of saddle point equations predicting the minimum mean squared error in estimating the rank-one spike. We further show that, in the Bayes-optimal setting, these saddle point equations coincide with the fixed point equations of the state evolution of a recently proposed orthogonal approximate message passing algorithm with Bayes-optimal denoisers. This equivalence provides an algorithmic interpretation of the replica prediction and supports the Bayes-optimality of orthogonal approximate message passing whenever the relevant fixed point is unique. Numerical evaluations of the saddle point equations for several rotationally invariant noise ensembles and signal priors, together with finite-size simulations of the algorithm, confirm the asymptotic predictions. They illustrate both regimes with a unique informative fixed point, where orthogonal approximate message passing is predicted to attain the minimum mean squared error, and regimes with multiple fixed points, where a statistical-to-computational gap exists.

</details>

### [20] [Mixed modified-gravity--modified-inertia MOND theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21699)
*Mordehai Milgrom*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文讨论一类同时修改牛顿动力学（Newtonian dynamics）中动能项与引力项的非相对论 MOND（modified Newtonian dynamics）理论族，以标度维数（scaling dimension）参数 η 统一刻画从纯修改引力到纯修改惯性的连续过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更基本的 MOND 理论可能同时使引力与惯性偏离牛顿版本；这类理论若遵循 MOND 基本信条，会与仅修改引力或仅修改惯性的现有理论共享主要预言，但在结构与次级预言上不同。作者由此希望在较一般的层面刻画同时修改两者的理论族。

Method: 构建一族非相对论 MOND 理论，其作用量（action）同时修改动能作用量与引力作用量；每个理论族由单一参数 0≤η≤1 张成，η 为作用量中各项深 MOND 极限（deep-MOND limit）的标度维数。纯修改引力（pure modified gravity，η=0）与纯修改惯性（pure modified inertia，η=1）为各族端点情形。修改项本身未严格指定，仅以较一般的形式处理。作者将其称为“族”（families），因为这些理论并非基于单一统一思想。

Result: 两个修改项各自单独均不能导出 MOND 现象学（MOND phenomenology），只有二者联合作用才能实现。二者结合可给出部分主要 MOND 预言：转动曲线的渐近平坦性（asymptotic flatness of rotation curves）、质量—渐近速度关系（mass-asymptotic-speed relation），以及深 MOND 的位力质量—速度弥散关系（deep-MOND virial M-σ relation）。此外，动能运动方程与引力场方程中会出现不同的插值函数（interpolating functions），各自适用于不同情形，且均不具有标准的深 MOND 极限。

Conclusion: 此类理论虽属人为构造（缺乏单一统辖两个分量修改的统一思想），但有助于揭示同时修改引力与惯性的 MOND 理论的若干特有个性，并提示可导致 MOND 现象学的理论范围相当宽广。

Abstract: It is plausible that in a more fundamental theory of MOND both gravity and inertia will differ from their Newtonian versions. Such theories, if they embody the basic tenets of MOND, will share its primary predictions with existing theories, which modify either gravity or inertia. But they are expected to differ in their structure and in their secondary predictions. Here, I discuss families of nonrelativistic MOND theories that modify both the kinetic and the gravitational actions of Newtonian dynamics. Each family is spanned by one parameter $0\leη\le 1$, which is the scaling dimension of the deep-MOND limit of all terms in the action. Pure modified gravity ($η=0$) and pure modified inertia ($η=1$) are the end cases in each family. I speak of families, since the modified action terms themselves are not strictly specified, but are treated in rather general terms. These theories are artificial in that they are not based on a single unifying idea that dictates the modification of the two components. But they are still useful in pinpointing some idiosyncracies of such theories. For example, neither of the two modified terms, by itself, leads to MOND phenomenology; only their combined effect does. I show how the two combine to make some of the primary MOND predictions, such as asymptotic flatness of rotation curves, the mass-asymptotic-speed relation, and the deep-MOND, virial, mass-velocity-dispersion ($M-σ$) relation. Another idiosyncracy of such theories is the emergence of different interpolating functions in the kinetic equations of motion and in the gravitational field equations, each relevant under different circumstances, and neither of which have the standard deep-MOND limit. Such examples alert us to the wide range of theories that can lead to MOND phenomenology.

</details>

### [21] [Hyperon--nucleon interactions at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20756)
*Hong-Fei Shen*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII 利用粲偶素（charmonium）衰变产生的超子（hyperon）与探测器材料作为固定靶，将 $e^+e^-$ 对撞机转化为超子—核子散射装置，并获得若干代表性散射与湮灭测量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超子—核子相互作用（hyperon--nucleon interactions）是理解重子—重子作用力奇异扇区（strange sector）的关键，对超核（hypernuclei）、致密重子物质（dense baryonic matter）与非微扰 QCD（non-perturbative QCD）均有意义。但超子寿命短、常规高强度超子束难以产生，导致直接实验信息稀缺。

Method: 在 BESIII 中，改用粲偶素衰变产生的超子与反超子作为入射粒子，探测器材料充当有效固定靶，从而在 $e^+e^-$ 对撞机上开展超子—核子研究。

Result: 代表性结果包括：利用 $Ξ^0$—核散射首次研究 $Ξ^{0}n\toΞ^-p$；测量弹性 $Λp\toΛp$ 与 $\barΛp\to\barΛp$ 散射；以及近期观测到 $\barΛp$ 湮灭为多介子末态。

Conclusion: 这些测量表明 BESIII 可作为有效的超子—核子散射设施，并为研究不足的重子—重子相互作用奇异扇区提供新的实验约束。

Abstract: Hyperon--nucleon interactions provide essential information on the strange sector of baryon--baryon forces, with implications for hypernuclei, dense baryonic matter, and non-perturbative QCD. Direct experimental information, however, remains scarce because hyperons are short lived and intense conventional hyperon beams are difficult to produce. At BESIII, hyperons and antihyperons produced in charmonium decays can instead be used as incident particles, while the detector material serves as an effective fixed target. This approach has enabled a new program of hyperon--nucleon studies at an $e^+e^-$ collider. Representative results include the first study of $Ξ^{0}n\toΞ^-p$ using $Ξ^0$--nucleus scattering, measurements of elastic $Λp\toΛp$ and $\barΛp\to\barΛp$ scattering, and recent observations of $\barΛp$ annihilation into multi-meson final states. These measurements demonstrate that BESIII can operate as an effective hyperon--nucleon scattering facility and provide new experimental constraints on the poorly explored strange sector of baryon--baryon interactions.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次观测到辐射粲衰变（radiative charm decay）$D^0 \to \gamma\omega$，显著度为 $6.0\sigma$；测得分支比 $\mathcal{B}(D^0\to\gamma\omega)=(2.12\pm0.40_{\rm stat}\pm0.25_{\rm syst})\times10^{-5}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在首次观测 $D^0 \to \gamma\omega$ 衰变过程，并为 $D \to \gamma V$ 跃迁的非微扰 QCD（non-perturbative QCD）计算提供基准。

Method: 基于 BESIII 探测器在质心能量（center-of-mass energy）3.773 GeV 处采集的 20.3 $\rm{fb}^{-1}$ $e^+e^-$ 对撞数据，采用基于 Particle Transformer 架构的深度学习策略进行分析。

Result: 以 $6.0\sigma$ 的显著度（significance）首次观测到 $D^0 \to \gamma\omega$，其绝对分支比（absolute branching fraction）为 $(2.12\pm0.40_{\rm stat}\pm0.25_{\rm syst})\times10^{-5}$。

Conclusion: 测量结果确认了长程强子动力学（long-distance hadronic dynamics）在辐射粲衰变中的主导地位，并为检验 $D \to \gamma V$ 跃迁的非微扰 QCD 计算提供了有价值的基准。

Abstract: We report the first observation of the radiative charm decay $D^0 \to γω$ with a significance of $6.0σ$. The analysis uses 20.3\,$\rm{fb}^{-1}$ of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected by the BESIII detector at a center-of-mass energy of 3.773\,GeV, and employs a deep learning strategy based on the Particle Transformer architecture. The absolute branching fraction of $\mathcal{B}(D^0\toγω)=(2.12\pm 0.40_{\rm stat}\pm 0.25_{\rm syst})\times 10^{-5}$ confirms the dominance of long-distance hadronic dynamics in radiative charm decays and provides a valuable benchmark for testing non-perturbative QCD calculations of $D \to γV$ transitions.

</details>

### [23] [Platelet-driven contraction enlarges accessible space in layered fibrin networks without uniformly accelerating particle escape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20762)
*Juan P. Staforelli-Vivanco,Paolo Teppa Gutierrez*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将血小板-纤维蛋白力学与示踪颗粒的布朗输运耦合，发现收缩在不均匀加速逃逸的前提下扩大了颗粒可及空间。收缩（contraction）与输运动力学（transport kinetics）由此被区分开来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血小板牵引（platelet traction）会收缩纤维蛋白网络（fibrin networks），但收缩究竟促进还是阻碍悬浮颗粒逃逸尚不明确。研究意在厘清收缩对颗粒输运的净效应及其机制来源。

Method: 采用过阻尼朗之万模型（overdamped Langevin model）描述血小板-纤维蛋白力学，并耦合有限尺寸示踪颗粒在冻结网络状态中的布朗输运（Brownian transport）。在四个丝状伪足牵引基线（0–8.8 nN）下演化九个力学重复样本，堆叠为50层、5 μm厚域，用0.5 μm示踪颗粒在0–15 s探测（29,952条轨迹，100 s观测）。

Result: 模拟收缩处于滞后（post-lag）阶段，速率与实验在同一数量级内，对应收缩相起始。相对零基线对照，所有活动基线状态均表现出更大的投影收缩和更大的示踪颗粒可及体积（+9.3%至+27.6%；每条件精确符号检验 p=0.0039）。15 s时，100 s平均存活率由27.3%降至14.3%，限制平均逃逸时间（restricted mean exit time）由56.5 s降至44.0 s；九个重复样本在8.8 nN下均缩短停留时间。较低基线时重复样本间响应存在差异，且几乎所有被分类为有起始的滞留颗粒均与出口相连。

Conclusion: 收缩扩大了可及空间，但并未一致地加速逃逸，从而将几何容纳（geometric accommodation）与输运动力学（transport kinetics）分离开来。

Abstract: Platelet traction contracts fibrin networks, yet whether contraction promotes or hinders the escape of suspended particles is unclear. We coupled an overdamped Langevin model of platelet--fibrin mechanics to Brownian transport of finite-size tracers through frozen network states. Nine mechanical replicas were evolved at four filopodial traction baselines (0--8.8\,nN), stacked into 5-$μ$m-thick domains of 50 layers and probed at 0--15\,s with 0.5-$μ$m tracers (29,952 trajectories, 100\,s observation). Benchmarked against experimental clot-contraction kinetics, the simulated contraction is post-lag, within one order of magnitude of experimental rates and corresponds to the onset of the contraction phase. Relative to the matched zero-baseline control, every active-baseline state showed greater projected contraction and a larger tracer-accessible volume ($+9.3\%$ to $+27.6\%$; exact sign test $p=0.0039$ per condition). At 15\,s, mean survival at 100\,s fell from 27.3\% to 14.3\% and the restricted mean exit time from 56.5 to 44.0\,s between the lowest and highest baseline, and all nine replicas shortened residence at 8.8\,nN. At lower baselines, responses differed among replicas, and nearly all retained tracers with a classified start were connected to an exit. Contraction therefore enlarges accessible space without uniformly accelerating escape, separating geometric accommodation from transport kinetics.

</details>

### [24] [Radiation Conditions and Uniqueness Theorems for Open Wave-guide Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20891)
*Charles L. Epstein,Rafe Mazzeo,Solomon Quinn,Andras Vasy*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文针对波导网络（wave-guide networks）模型建立辐射条件（radiation conditions）并证明多类唯一性定理（uniqueness theorem），涵盖声学与电磁波导网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波导网络模型的适定性（well-posedness）依赖辐射条件与唯一性定理；针对由时谐麦克斯韦方程组（time harmonic Maxwell equations）描述的电磁波导网络，既有理论工具尚不充分，需要推广已有 Mourre 估计（Mourre estimates）。

Method: 提出辐射条件，并为声学波导网络及电磁波导网络证明多种唯一性定理；在分析麦克斯韦波导网络时，建立对文献 [29, 32] 中 Mourre 估计的推广形式。

Result: 建立了适用于波导网络的辐射条件，并证明了声学与电磁波导网络（后者由时谐麦克斯韦方程组描述）的多类唯一性定理；同时给出 Mourre 估计的推广。

Conclusion: 波导网络的唯一性可由所提辐射条件保证，且电磁情形下的分析有赖于 Mourre 估计的推广，从而将相关理论拓展至麦克斯韦波导网络。

Abstract: We state radiation conditions and prove a variety of uniqueness theorem for models of wave-guide networks, including acoustic wave-guide networks, and electromagnetic wave-guide networks described by the time harmonic Maxwell equations. To analyze Maxwell wave-guide networks we need to establish generalizations of the Mourre estimates from [29, 32].

</details>


### [25] [Formation of Trapped Surfaces for the Einstein-Euler System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21124)
*Xinliang An,Sifan Yu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在3+1维非球对称情形下证明了爱因斯坦–欧拉系统（Einstein–Euler system）中俘获面（trapped surface）的形成，并给出分别对应剪切（shear）与流体贡献的两个充分判据，其中流体驱动判据不要求剪切通量存在正下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俘获面形成是彭罗斯奇点定理（Penrose singularity theorem）的核心前提，以往结果多限于球对称或特定物质模型。本文旨在将相关结论推广至非球对称的引力–流体耦合系统，并厘清剪切与流体注入在聚焦过程中的相对作用。

Method: 在短脉冲（short-pulse）与快速流体注入情形下取光滑特征初值（characteristic initial data），初值不含俘获面。分析结合三个要素：其零通量反映不等速度分量的强制性流体能量流（coercive fluid energy current）；适配物质项的曲率重整化（curvature renormalizations）；以及控制所有时空方向导数的加权几何估计（weighted geometric estimates）。并借助设计时空区域内的粒子数守恒（particle-number conservation）控制聚焦过程。

Result: 得到两个俘获面形成的充分判据，分别由剪切与流体贡献给出；特别地，流体驱动判据无需对剪切通量施加正下界。同时获得所需的球面积分通量（sphere-integrated fluxes）估计，并表明仅靠流体注入即可导致俘获面形成。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在非球对称的引力–流体系统中，流体注入本身足以驱动俘获面形成，而无需剪切通量的正下界支撑；所发展的多速传播（引力波、声波与流体输运）处理框架可为此类问题提供分析工具。

Abstract: We prove the formation of trapped surfaces for the Einstein--Euler system in 3+1 dimensions beyond spherical symmetry. We consider smooth characteristic initial data in a short-pulse regime with rapid fluid injection. Starting from initial data containing no trapped surfaces, we obtain two sufficient criteria for trapped surface formation, corresponding to the shear and fluid contributions, respectively. In particular, for the fluid-driven criterion, we impose no positive lower bound on the shear flux. To handle the multiple propagation speeds associated with gravitational waves, sound waves, and fluid transport, our analysis combines three ingredients: a coercive fluid energy current whose null fluxes reflect the unequal velocity components, curvature renormalizations adapted to the matter terms, and weighted geometric estimates controlling derivatives in all spacetime directions. To ensure that fluid injection alone can lead to trapped surface formation, we use particle-number conservation in a designed spacetime region to control the focusing process and obtain the desired sphere-integrated fluxes.

</details>


### [26] [Strict unimodality of the exact time-domain diffuse-light response under a Robin boundary condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21431)
*Junyong Eom,Jinwan Park*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文严格证明了半空间 Robin 边界条件下单个荧光点目标所产生的时域扩散光响应为广义伽马卷积密度，其归一化曲线单峰钟形，且对任意正参数与任意荧光寿命均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时域漫射光与荧光成像将时间点扩散函数（temporal point-spread function）的峰值位置用作稳健的标量数据，但该类峰值唯一性的严格数学依据此前缺乏普遍性证明。

Method: 以 Robin 边界条件下均匀半空间中单个荧光点目标的精确响应为对象，该响应为含互补误差函数边界修正的两个扩散格林函数因子的时间卷积；借助 Robin 因子的显式 Stieltjes 表示、双曲完全单调性、广义伽马卷积类的封闭性及钟形理论，并结合由扇形解析性与柯西估计得到的小时间可微展开及前两阶导数的变号结构进行严格论证。

Result: 对任意正的扩散系数、吸收系数、深度、源—目标与探测—目标距离以及任意非负 Robin 参数，归一化精确响应均为广义伽马卷积密度，其零延拓为钟形；证明排除切向临界点并给出唯一峰值，峰前严格递增、峰后严格递减；与指数荧光寿命密度卷积后结论对任意正寿命仍成立。

Conclusion: 论证为精确的，无需大距离、源—探测器平衡、对称性、受限时间或小参数等假设；论文附录另给出精确半空间模型的推导与微分后的端点渐近。

Abstract: Time-domain boundary measurements of diffuse light are temporal point-spread functions whose peak position is used as a robust scalar datum in diffuse optical and fluorescence imaging. We consider the exact response generated by a single fluorescent point target in a homogeneous half-space with a Robin boundary condition. The response is a temporal convolution of two diffusion Green-function factors containing a complementary-error-function boundary correction. For every positive diffusion, absorption, depth, and source--target and detector--target distance parameter, and for every nonnegative Robin parameter, we prove that the normalized exact response is a generalized gamma convolution density. Its extension by zero to the negative half-line is bell-shaped. A differentiable small-time expansion, established by sectorial analyticity and Cauchy estimates, together with the sign-change structure of the first two derivatives, excludes tangential critical points and yields a unique peak with strict increase before it and strict decrease after it. The argument is exact and requires no large-distance, source--detector balance, symmetry, restricted-time, or small-parameter assumption. The same conclusion holds after convolution with an exponential fluorescence-lifetime density for every positive lifetime. The key ingredients are an explicit Stieltjes representation of the Robin factor, hyperbolic complete monotonicity, closure of the generalized gamma convolution class, and bell-shape theory. A derivation of the exact half-space model and the differentiated endpoint asymptotics are included in two appendices.

</details>

### [27] [Degree Growth of Rational Maps with Laurent Property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21627)
*Melih Emin Can*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究具有Laurent性质（Laurent property）的递推所生成有理映射的代数熵（algebraic entropy），核心方法是把次数增长问题约化为热带递推（tropical recurrence）分析，并对Gale–Robinson族给出显式增长公式与猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数熵由Bellon与Viallet引入，用于刻画有理自映射迭代次数的指数增长，是判断映射可积性的重要指标。对Laurent递推（尤其Gale–Robinson递推）缺乏从递推关系直接判定次数增长的方法，因此需要发展更直接的判定工具。

Method: 提出一种直接方法，从递推关系本身确定次数增长，并将问题归约为相关的max-linear（即热带/tropical）递推研究。先对一大类齐次二次Laurent递推导出迭代次数的闭式公式，再将其应用于两项Gale–Robinson族，并对三项情形用热带增长率序列刻画代数熵。

Result: 得到齐次二次Laurent递推迭代次数的闭式公式；对两项Gale–Robinson族给出带周期修正项的显式二次增长公式；对三项族，以热带增长率序列表达代数熵，并基于显式计算提出“二次增长加周期修正”的一般猜想；证明了参数为p=1、q=2、r≥3的无穷族情形。

Conclusion: 次数增长可由递推对应的热带递推有效刻画，Gale–Robinson族的代数熵呈现二次增长并伴随周期性修正；三项族的一般性结论以猜想形式给出并在特定参数无穷族中得到证明。

Abstract: Algebraic entropy measures the exponential growth of the degrees of iterates of rational self-maps. It is introduced by Bellon and Viallet in \cite{BellonandViallet}. We study degree growth for rational maps arising from recurrences with the Laurent property, with particular emphasis on Gale--Robinson recurrences. We develop a direct method for determining the degree growth from the recurrence relation itself, reducing the problem to the study of associated max-linear, or tropical recurrences.
  We first obtain a closed formula for the degrees of iterates for a broad class of homogeneous quadratic Laurent recurrences. We then apply this result to the two-term Gale--Robinson family and derive an explicit quadratic growth formula with a periodic correction term. For three-term Gale--Robinson recurrences, we express the algebraic entropy in terms of the associated tropical growth rate sequence. Based on explicit computations, we formulate a conjecture for the general three-term family predicting quadratic growth with a periodic correction. We prove the conjecture for the infinite family with parameters $p=1$, $q=2$, and $r\geq3$.

</details>


### [28] [Nonpersistence of full-dimensional tori with polynomial decay in nonlinear Schrödinger equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22072)
*Zhicheng Tong,Yong Li*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该论文证明周期五次非线性薛定谔方程（quintic nonlinear Schrödinger equation）中一类多项式衰减作用量（polynomially decaying actions）的全维不变环面（full-dimensional invariant tori）具有局部非持久性（local nonpersistence），推进了 Kuksin 提出的 KAM 理论公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 所研究问题源于 Kuksin 指出的一维哈密顿偏微分方程（Hamiltonian PDEs）KAM 理论中的主要公开问题，即多项式衰减情形；Bourgain 亦就短程哈密顿系统讨论过相关问题。

Method: 构造满足 Bourgain 型丢番图条件（Bourgain-type Diophantine condition）的有界实傅里叶乘子（Fourier multiplier）；证明结合同时丢番图估计与加权同调估计、一个精确共相位变换（common-phase transformation），以及在四个相邻傅里叶坐标上的一步正规形（normal-form）操作。

Result: 对该乘子，在 3<M<4κ+1/2、任意 A>0 且 μA²>0 充分小时，以 I_j=A⟨j⟩^{-M} 为中心、半径 3/10 的闭相对作用量管（relative action tube）内不含 H¹(T) 中带完整流（complete flow）的全维不变环面，频率可任意。

Conclusion: 一致相位旋转（uniform phase rotation）迫使作用量发生偏移，重叠参数区间覆盖所有充分小的正扰动，从而得到非持久性结论；该机制有望推广至其他哈密顿偏微分方程。

Abstract: We establish local nonpersistence of full-dimensional invariant tori with polynomially decaying actions for the periodic quintic nonlinear Schrödinger equation \[ iu_t-u_{xx}+T_vu+μ|u|^4u=0,\qquad x\in\mathbb T:=\mathbb R/(2π\mathbb Z). \] For every $κ\geq1$, we construct a fixed bounded real Fourier multiplier $T_ve^{ijx}=v_je^{ijx}$ whose frequencies $λ_j=j^2+v_j$ satisfy the Bourgain-type Diophantine condition \[ \operatorname{dist}\left(\sum_j\ell_jλ_j,\mathbb Z\right) \geqγ_κ\prod_j(1+\ell_j^2\langle j\rangle^{2κ})^{-1} \] for some $γ_κ>0$ and every nonzero finitely supported integer sequence $\ell$, where $\langle j\rangle=\max\{1,|j|\}$. For this multiplier, every $3<M<4κ+\frac12$, every $A>0$, and all sufficiently small $μA^2>0$, the closed relative action tube of radius $3/10$ around $I_j=A\langle j\rangle^{-M}$ contains no full-dimensional invariant torus in $H^1(\mathbb T)$ carrying a complete flow, with arbitrary frequencies allowed. This advances the study of the polynomial-decay problem identified by Kuksin as a main open problem in KAM theory for one-dimensional Hamiltonian PDEs [Amer. Math. Soc. Transl. Ser. 2, 212 (2004), 237--258, Problem 7.1]. Bourgain discussed a related question for short-range Hamiltonian systems [Russian Math. Surveys 59 (2004), 231--246, Section 3]. The proof combines simultaneous Diophantine and weighted homological estimates, an exact common-phase transformation, and one normal-form step on four adjacent Fourier coordinates. Uniform phase rotation forces an action excursion, while overlapping parameter intervals cover all sufficiently small positive perturbations. The mechanism suggests applications to other Hamiltonian PDEs.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可解析处理的 Einstein-scalar 临界坍缩（critical collapse）模型中，坍缩物质的量子自能随 Lyapunov 指数 $D-2$ 增长，并与控制黑洞形成的经典不稳定性竞争，从而移动裸奇点（naked singularity）阈值并产生有限质量间隙。然而当维度 $D\\to\\infty$ 时，半经典可控性（semiclassical control）似乎受到威胁，故需考察该量子增长模式在大维度下的行为。

Result: 普适 Lyapunov 指数在 CSS 与 DSS 两种自相似形式下均存活。CSS 中经典趋向裸奇点的过程被俘获面（trapped surfaces）的出现所打断，大 $D$ 下得到量子墙半径 $r_h\\propto(D-2)^{-2}$，该半径在有限 $D$ 时可高于 Planck 尺度，但随 $D$ 增大维持该层级愈发困难。DSS 中同样的增长受回响调制并可保留先前回响的记忆；最简单的反常诱导作用量（anomaly-induced action）表现出累积记忆，但在球心处奇异。

Conclusion: 所构造的协变单圈作用量在 DSS 区域内能动张量保持光滑且不携带记忆，提示真实四维 DSS Choptuik 时空（Choptuik spacetime）的光滑真空极化（vacuum polarization）可能同样由其精确的回响对称性（echoing symmetry）所支配。

Abstract: In analytically tractable models of Einstein-scalar critical collapse, it has been shown that the quantum self-energy of the collapsing matter in the dominant $s$-wave sector grows with Lyapunov exponent $D-2$, eventually competing with the classical instability that controls black hole formation. This competition shifts the naked singularity threshold and produces a finite mass gap within semiclassical control, yet that control may appear threatened as the spacetime dimension $D\to\infty$. We study this quantum growing mode in both continuously (CSS) and discretely (DSS) self-similar critical spacetimes at large $D$ and establish that the universal Lyapunov exponent survives both forms of self-similarity. In CSS, the classical approach to a naked singularity is interrupted by the appearance of trapped surfaces. At large $D$, the horizon tracing reduces to a Bessel problem, yielding a quantum wall whose radius obeys $r_h\propto(D-2)^{-2}$. This radius can remain above the Planck scale at finite $D$, although maintaining this hierarchy becomes increasingly demanding as $D$ grows. In DSS, the same growth is modulated by echoing and can retain memory of previous echoes. The simplest anomaly-induced action exhibits accumulating memory but is singular at the spherical center. We construct a covariant one-loop action whose stress tensor remains smooth throughout the DSS domain and organize it into an echo-periodic structure without memory. This suggests that the smooth vacuum polarization of the genuine four-dimensional DSS Choptuik spacetime may similarly be governed by its exact echoing symmetry.

Abstract: Near the threshold between scattering and capture by a Schwarzschild black hole, a particle spends a long time close to an unstable circular orbit, which in the ultrarelativistic limit approaches the light ring. We then estimate the coefficients of the subleading pole contributions by combining numerical calculations at low multipoles with the exact asymptotics at large multipoles. Interestingly, our results also inform the analytical structure of the energy and angular momentum losses of ultrarelativistic scattering orbits near the light ring. The hair is felt across the whole divergent section, whereas the quantum correction registers only near the throat and grows as the inverse fourth power of the mass, which confines it to primordial black holes.   <summary>Details</summary>

The shot-noise-limited sensitivity floor is $S^{1/2}_n=1.6\times10^{-20}\,\mathrm{Hz}^{-1/2}$. For example, we illustrate the type of high-velocity tail that would make the thermal Higgsino viable despite stringent constraints from solar neutrinos: Reducing the flux of the tail relative to simulation results improves the Higgsino fit, while extending the tail to higher velocities at fluxes below current simulation resolution evades the solar neutrino bound. Even if xenon experiments confirm the signal event, complementary experiments are still required to test whether the underlying model is inelastic dark matter and to break degeneracies in the measurement of dark matter parameters such as mass and mass splitting. Heavy-element paleodetectors could cover most of the parameter space relevant to the LZ event. Their use of different elements could determine the dark matter mass and splitting, while their unique sensitivity to the history of the dark matter distribution could test the signal's LMC origin. Luminous detection in large neutrino detectors with heavy-element supplements could directly test the inelastic nature of the signal and measure the mass splitting, while its sensitivity to the radiative decay rate could provide strong evidence for the Higgsino interpretation.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 非可逆缺陷为反常味对称性提供了自洽的红外完备化，其显式破缺可由紫外瞬子效应实现；该机制统一解释了中微子汤川耦合的指数压低与质量层级，并兼容夸克与带电轻子的汤川结构，同时由额外离散规范对称性保证狄拉克中微子性质。

Abstract: We investigate non-invertible completions of anomalous flavor symmetries and their application to Dirac neutrino masses. We formulate symmetry defects dressed by topological quantum field theories for flavor symmetries including finite non-Abelian flavor transformations with rational anomaly characters and modular flavor symmetries with the rational part of modular anomaly cocycles, for gauge sectors with the required exact higher-form symmetry. As a concrete example, we construct a Froggatt-Nielsen (FN) model in which an order-four subgroup $\mathbb Z_4^{\rm FN}\subset U(1)_{\rm FN}$ is completed into a non-invertible defect through its mixed anomaly with an intermediate hidden $U(1)_H$ gauge sector in the infrared monopole-decoupling limit. The non-invertible FN symmetry can be explicitly broken at the energy scale where monopoles become dynamical. This breaking from the infrared viewpoint can be realized in an ultraviolet hidden $SU(2)_H$ theory as instanton effects, where a constrained instanton with topological charge one generates a fermion mixing term that violates FN charge by one unit. When independent FN-breaking terms are subleading, integrating out the heavy fermions gives $(Y_ν)_{ij}\proptoζ_Hε^{\ell_i+n_j}$ in a rotated basis for the right-handed neutrinos, to leading order in the instanton-induced fermion mixing. The nonperturbative factor provides the exponential suppression of the neutrino Yukawa couplings, while the FN charges determine the powers of $ε$. For the chosen FN charges and generic order-one coefficients, $m_2/m_3\simε$, favoring normal ordering. An independent $\mathbb Z_8^D$ gauge symmetry forbids Majorana masses. The same FN charge assignment reproduces the leading quark and charged-lepton Yukawa hierarchies.

We find sizeable cross sections for resonant $hh$ production, and also for the background-suppressed process with three Higgs bosons ($hhh$) from associated production, $pp \to hφ_i \to hhh$. By recasting ATLAS resonance searches, we set bounds on the effective new-physics scales $Λ_1$ and $Λ_2$ at the TeV scale. We also show that the High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) will probe this parameter space further, through combined measurements of the $hh$, $t\bar t$ and multi-Higgs channels.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖显式网络动力学建模，该研究试图绕过这一路径，转而以整体脉冲历史（spike histories）的系综（ensemble）描述神经元系统，以更自然地刻画宏观活动模式及其转变。

Method: 构建完整脉冲历史的系综，为每个时空构型分配有效能量泛函以量化其与内在突触交互（synaptic interactions）的一致性，得到类玻尔兹曼分布，其中有效温度（effective temperature）刻画外部输入的随机驱动；并用基于群体脉冲率密度的序参量（order parameters）进行诊断，辅以直接在脉冲序列上定义的 Metropolis-Hastings 蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟。

Result: 宏观态由粗粒化可观测量（如群体放电率密度）描述，源于偏好交互一致脉冲模式的能量贡献与偏好时空无序的熵贡献之间的竞争；非振荡区包含广泛分布近似均匀态与时间局域坍缩态，振荡区则由重复活动峰表征，这些区制可由熵、时间局域化度量与自相关指标等序参量区分。

Conclusion: 该框架在不建模显式网络动力学的前提下，成功刻画振荡与非振荡活动的转变；蒙特卡洛模拟重现了预测的宏观区制，并对自由能景观（free-energy landscape）提供了基于采样的直接探测。

Abstract: We introduce a statistical-mechanical framework for spiking neuronal systems in which entire spike trains, defined as spatiotemporal configurations of discrete events, are treated as microstates of the system. Instead of modeling explicit network dynamics, we construct an ensemble over complete spike histories and assign to each configuration an effective energy functional that quantifies its consistency with intrinsic synaptic interactions.
  This formulation leads to a Boltzmann-like distribution over spike configurations, where an effective temperature captures the influence of external inputs acting as a stochastic drive. Macroscopic states are described in terms of coarse-grained observables, such as population firing-rate densities, and arise from a competition between energetic contributions favoring interaction-consistent spike patterns and entropic contributions favoring spatio-temporal disorder.
  Within this framework, we primarily characterize the transition between oscillatory and non-oscillatory activity. The non-oscillatory regime contains both broadly distributed, approximately uniform states and temporally localized collapsed states, whereas the oscillatory regime is characterized by repeated activity peaks. These regimes are diagnosed using order parameters derived from the population spike-rate density, including its entropy, temporal localization measures, and autocorrelation-based indicators.
  To complement the analytical treatment, we perform Monte Carlo simulations that sample the ensemble of admissible spike configurations using a Metropolis-Hastings scheme defined directly on spike trains. The simulations reveal the emergence of the predicted macroscopic regimes and provide a direct sampling-based probe of the free-energy landscape.

</details>

### [58] [Discontinuous-Property Mechanical Metamaterials: A Framework for Discontinuous Lattice Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21130)
*Lihong Ao,Aymeric Ramiere*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该工作提出不连续属性力学超材料（discontinuous-property mechanical metamaterials, DPMMs），作为建立不连续晶格系统几何-力学框架的原型，通过几何正则化扩展能带理论并验证其非线性波动特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不连续性作为非线性（nonlinearity）的极限情形，在宏观连续系统中普遍存在；本文旨在以DPMM为原型，为不连续晶格系统建立几何-力学（geometric-mechanics）框架。

Method: 通过几何正则化（geometric regularization）将能带理论（band theory）扩展至具有互补约束（complementary constraints）的系统，并分析模态不连续性（modal discontinuities）；结合静态、模态与瞬态响应分析，并开展概念验证实验（proof-of-concept experiment）。

Result: 验证了不连续刚度（discontinuous stiffness）与泊松比（Poisson's ratio），并观察到强烈的非线性波混频（nonlinear wave mixing）与模式转换（mode conversion）；概念验证实验证明了DPMMs实际实现的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作以直观的DPMM概念刻画不连续晶格系统，并提供了利用力学超材料中尖锐转变（sharp transitions）实现非线性波调控（nonlinear wave manipulation）的实用框架。

Abstract: Discontinuity, as the extreme limit of nonlinearity, is common in macroscopic continuum systems. This work introduces discontinuous-property mechanical metamaterials (DPMMs) as a prototype for establishing a geometric-mechanics framework for discontinuous lattice systems. We extend band theory to systems with complementary constraints through geometric regularization and analyze modal discontinuities. By analyzing its static, modal, and transient responses, we validated discontinuous stiffness and Poisson's ratio, alongside intense nonlinear wave mixing and mode conversion. A proof-of-concept experiment demonstrates the practical feasibility of implementing DPMMs. This work characterizes discontinuous lattice systems through the intuitive DPMM concept and provides a practical framework to harness sharp transitions in mechanical metamaterials for nonlinear wave manipulation.

</details>

### [59] [Gallium Anomaly and Solar Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22219)
*Steven R. Elliott,R. G. Hamish Robertson*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 该工作将太阳视为可经由标准模型（standard model）中微子-电子弹性散射（neutrino-electron elastic scattering）标定的中微子源，用以检验镓异常（Gallium Anomaly）。结果显示测量值与太阳源强度总和一致，不呈现异常，表明问题并非源自⁷¹Ga 电荷流截面或镓实验的提取与计数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镓异常指⁷¹Ga 实测中微子俘获率与由⁷¹Ge 电子俘获衰变（electron capture decay）理论预期率之间的分歧，此前实验使用强人工源（artificial sources）进行测量。作者希望引入独立的中微子源标定途径，以判断异常来源。

Method: 将太阳作为中微子源，借助基本的、标准模型框架下的中微子-电子弹性散射反应，在地面探测器（terrestrial detectors）中进行标定，并与镓实验的测量结果比对。

Result: 结果不呈现异常：SAGE 与 Gallex/GNO 测得值为 66.1±3.1 SNU，与太阳源总测量源强度 67.5^{+1.4}_{-1.1} SNU 吻合良好。

Conclusion: 可得出结论：⁷¹Ga 电荷流截面（charged-current cross section）以及镓实验的提取与计数效率（extraction and counting efficiencies）均不能解释镓异常。

Abstract: The Gallium Anomaly is the disagreement between the measured capture rate of neutrinos by $^{71}$Ga and the rate theoretically expected from the electron capture decay of $^{71}$Ge. The experimental measurements were performed with intense artificial sources. In the present work we consider the sun as a neutrino source that can be calibrated via the fundamental, standard-model neutrino-electron elastic scattering reaction in terrestrial detectors. The result is found to be not anomalous, with good agreement between the measured value of $66.1\pm3.1$ SNU from SAGE and Gallex/GNO, and the total measured source strengths of solar sources, $67.5^{+1.4}_{-1.1}$ SNU. It may be concluded that neither the $^{71}$Ga charged-current cross section nor gallium experiment extraction and counting efficiencies account for the Gallium Anomaly.

</details>

### [60] [Integral and higher-genus quantum McKay correspondence in type D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22359)
*Andrea Brini,Jingxiang Ma*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文针对曲面奇点 $\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{BD}_{4n}$ 的最小消解（minimal resolution），建立了 Iritani 形式的 $\mathbb{C}^\times$-等变全等变换猜想（Crepant Transformation Conjecture, CTC）积分形式，并借助 Givental 量子化（quantisation）形式证明全亏格全后代 CTC，进而导出相应 Calabi-Yau 三轨形（3-orbifold）的 Bryan-Cadman-Young 零次轨道 Gromov-Witten/Donaldson-Thomas 对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于验证并推广全等变换猜想（CTC）在二面体群（binary dihedral group）商奇点这一具体情形下的成立性，并将其结果应用于轨道 Gromov-Witten 与 Donaldson-Thomas 理论之间的对应关系。摘要未进一步说明更广泛的背景动机。

Method: 方法上，首先建立 Iritani 积分形式的 $\mathbb{C}^\times$-等变 CTC；随后运用 Givental 量子化形式（Givental's quantisation formalism）证明该情形下全亏格、全后代（all-genus full-descendent）的 CTC。

Result: 在 $\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{BD}_{4n}$ 最小消解的情形下，成功建立了 Iritani 积分形式及全亏格全后代形式的 CTC。作为应用，推出了 Calabi-Yau 三轨形 $[\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{BD}_{4n}] \times \mathbb{C}$ 上 Bryan-Cadman-Young 零次形式的轨道 GW/DT 对应。

Conclusion: 该工作为二面体群商奇点提供了 CTC 的完整证明，并给出了轨道 Gromov-Witten/Donaldson-Thomas 对应在零次情形的具体推论，表明 CTC 可作为连接不同枚举不变量理论的工具。

Abstract: We establish Iritani's integral form of the $\mathbb{C}^\times$-equivariant Crepant Transformation Conjecture (CTC) for the minimal resolution of the surface singularity $\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{BD}_{4n}$, where $\mathbb{BD}_{4n}$ is the binary dihedral group of order $4n$. We then use Givental's quantisation formalism to prove the all-genus full-descendent CTC in that case. As an application, we deduce the Bryan-Cadman-Young degree-zero form of the orbifold Gromov-Witten/Donaldson-Thomas correspondence for the Calabi-Yau 3-orbifold $[\mathbb{C}^2/\mathbb{BD}_{4n}] \times \mathbb{C}$.

</details>

### [61] [Strain-enhanced superconductivity in RuO$_2$ thin films: Joint experimental and theoretical studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21163)
*Moeen ud din,Tsun-Sung Kuo,Yun-Chen Liao,Akhilesh Kr. Singh,Uddipta Kar,Wei-Li Lee,Guang-Yu Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过薄膜制备、输运测量与从头计算（ab initio calculation）相结合，研究TiO₂衬底上RuO₂薄膜的超导电性；发现[001]压应变通过软化声子、增强电子-声子耦合显著提升T_c。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究RuO₂外延薄膜的超导性质及其调控机制，明确晶格失配应变（lattice mismatch strain）对声子媒介超导电性（phonon-mediated superconductivity）的作用。

Method: 实验上在TiO₂(110)衬底上生长多个RuO₂(110)薄膜并进行输运测量；理论上采用各向同性Migdal-Eliashberg理论的McMillan形式，对RuO₂(110)与(100)外延薄膜的电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling, EPC）与T_c进行计算。

Result: 20 nm与30 nm的RuO₂(110)薄膜观测到约2.0 K的超导临界温度（critical temperature, T_c）；计算显示各薄膜声子色散动力学稳定，且[001]压应变ε_[001]使Ru d_z²主导的平带趋近费米能级（Fermi level, E_F），并显著软化布里渊区R点附近的面向外声学声子模，从而增强EPC强度λ并提高T_c，软化和T_c提升随ε_[001]增大近似线性；厚度≥20 nm薄膜的计算T_c与实验吻合。

Conclusion: 晶格失配应变是调控RuO₂及其他薄膜中声子媒介超导电性的有效途径。

Abstract: We investigate the superconducting properties of RuO$_2$ films grown on TiO$_2$ substrates by combining film fabrication and transport measurement with \textit{ab initio} calculation. Experimentally, we grow several RuO$_2$ (110) thin films on TiO$_2$ (110) substrates and observe superconductivity with critical temperature ($T_c$) of $\sim$2.0 K in 20-nm and 30-nm RuO$_2$ (110) thin films. Theoretically, we study the superconductivity in both RuO$_2$ (110) and RuO$_2$ (100) epitaxial films within McMillan formalism of isotropic Migdal-Eliashberg theory. Among the salient results, the calculated phonon dispersion relations of all the considered RuO$_2$ films show that the epitaxial films are dynamically stable. Importantly, our \textit{ab initio} calculations reveal that independent of the film orientation, the [001] compressive strain $ε_{[001]}$ pushes the flat Ru $d_{z^2}$-dominant bands towards the Fermi level ($E_F$), and significantly soften the out-of-plane acoustic phonon mode near the R point in the Brillouin zone, which substantially enhances the electron-phonon coupling (EPC) strength $λ$ and hence the calculated $T_c$ in the RuO$_2$ films. Remarkably, the amount of the phonon softening and the enhanced $T_c$ increase almost linearly with the increasing $ε_{[001]}$. The calculated $T_c$ for the films with thickness of $\geq$ 20 nm agree well with the experimental $T_c$. These findings demonstrate that among other things, lattice mismatch strain provides an effective route for tuning phonon-mediated superconductivity in RuO$_2$ and other thin films.

</details>


### [62] [Unique Charge-density-wave Order and Fluctuation-induced Phonon Softening in the Optimally-doped Trilayer Cuprate Bi$_2$Sr$_2$Ca$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{10+δ}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21305)
*S. Nakata,K. Yamamoto,J. Okamoto,D. Shiga,R. Takahashi,H. Y. Huang,H. Kumigashira,H. Wadati,A. Fujimori,S. Ishida,H. Eisaki,D. J. Huang,J. Miyawaki,H. Suzuki*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究利用 Cu L₃ 与 O K 边共振非弹性 X 射线散射（RIXS），在最佳掺杂三层铜氧化物 Bi2223 中观测到单一面内波矢的电荷密度波（CDW）序，证明其跨三层形成相干序，并揭示 CDW 涨落与声子支耦合驱动了声子软化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷密度波（charge-density-wave, CDW）序普遍存在于铜氧化物超导体中并与超导竞争，但其在三层化合物（含超导最优 CuO₂ 层数）中的行为尚不明确。

Method: 对最佳掺杂的三层铜氧化物 Bi₂Sr₂Ca₂Cu₃O₁₀₊δ（Bi2223）开展 Cu L₃ 边与 O K 边共振非弹性 X 射线散射（resonant inelastic x-ray scattering, RIXS）测量。

Result: 观测到单一面内波矢 q_CDW = (0.25, 0) 的 CDW 序，表明跨三层形成相干 CDW 序；分辨出来自静态序的 CDW 涨落，其分布在 Cu L₃ 与 O K 边之间不同；声子软化出现在 CDW 涨落与声子支相互作用处。

Conclusion: 三层铜氧化物中可形成跨越三层的相干 CDW 序，且 RIXS 声子反常（phonon anomaly）由 CDW 涨落与声子支之间的耦合驱动。

Abstract: Charge-density-wave (CDW) order is a ubiquitous feature of cuprate superconductors that competes with superconductivity. However, its behavior remains poorly understood in trilayer compounds, which contain the optimal number of CuO$_2$ layers for superconductivity. Here, we report Cu $L_3$- and O $K$-edge resonant inelastic x-ray scattering (RIXS) measurements of optimally-doped Bi$_{2}$Sr$_{2}$Ca$_{2}$Cu$_{3}$O$_{10+δ}$ (Bi2223), the trilayer cuprate with the highest superconducting transition temperature ($T_c$) among Bi-based cuprates. We observe CDW order with a single in-plane wavevector of ${\bm q}_{\text{CDW}} = (0.25, 0)$, demonstrating that a coherent CDW order is formed across the trilayers. We also resolve CDW fluctuations emanating from the static order, whose profiles differ between the Cu $L_3$ and O $K$ edges. The phonon softening occurs where these CDW fluctuations interact with the phonon branch, indicating that the RIXS phonon anomaly is driven by the coupling between CDW fluctuations and the phonon branch.

</details>


### [63] [Pairing-channel reconstruction by Ising spin-orbit coupling in a multiorbital triangular-lattice model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21369)
*Lichuan Wang,Yuhang Xiao,Ran Wang,Xinliang Huang,Ning Hao*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文揭示Ising自旋轨道耦合（Ising spin-orbit coupling, Ising SOC）在关联多轨道体系中可重构自旋涨落介导的配对通道层级，从而在非中心对称三角晶格模型中同时产生Ising三重态与拓扑单重态主导的超导相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有认识中，Ising SOC主要被视为锁定电子自旋、保护超导免受面内顺磁拆对（paramagnetic pair breaking）的机制。作者希望检验在关联多轨道（multiorbital）体系中，Ising SOC是否具有定性不同的作用，即通过重塑低能费米面、轨道-自旋相干因子与动量分辨散射相空间来重构配对通道层级。

Method: 采用含Rashba SOC、Ising SOC与在位SOC（onsite SOC）的非中心对称双轨道三角晶格模型，并在多轨道随机相近似（random-phase-approximation, RPA）框架内求解配对问题，比较单轨道极限与引入轨道自由度后的配对通道竞争。

Result: 单轨道极限下，仅在较强Ising SOC时出现以S_z=0分量为主的E表示配对态；轨道自由度使其在更小的名义Ising SOC强度下即可稳定，并产生E与A_1通道的广泛竞争。E态可保持相同的S_z=0三重态特征，而A_1态为完全能隙、变号的s±超导体，并在时间反演不变的弱配对区实现非零Z_2指数、Wilson环谱流与边界模的拓扑非平庸相；该拓扑性并非由单一轨道配对分量承载，而是由主要带内与带间配对分量协同涌现。

Conclusion: 结果确立了Ising SOC与轨道杂化（orbital hybridization）重构相互作用驱动超导的微观路径，说明在关联非中心对称体系中可产生Ising三重态与拓扑单重态主导的相，拓展了对Ising SOC功能的理解。

Abstract: Ising spin-orbit coupling (SOC) is widely known for locking electronic spins and protecting superconductivity against in-plane paramagnetic pair breaking. Here we show that, in a correlated multiorbital system, Ising SOC can play a qualitatively different role: it reconstructs the hierarchy of spin-fluctuation-mediated pairing channels by reshaping the low-energy Fermi surfaces, orbital-spin coherence factors, and momentum-resolved scattering phase space. We study a noncentrosymmetric two-orbital triangular-lattice model with Rashba SOC, Ising SOC, and onsite SOC. We solve the pairing problem within a multiorbital random-phase-approximation framework. In the single-orbital limit, an $E$-representation state with a dominant $S_z=0$ component emerges only at relatively strong Ising SOC. Orbital degrees of freedom facilitate the stabilization of this pairing state at a smaller nominal Ising SOC strength and generate a broad competition between $E$ and $A_1$ pairing channels. The $E$ state can maintain the same $S_z=0$ triplet character, whereas the $A_1$ state becomes a fully gapped sign-changing $\splusminus$ superconductor. The latter also realizes a topologically nontrivial phase in the time-reversal-invariant weak-pairing regime, with a nonzero $\mathbb{Z}_2$ index, Wilson-loop spectral flow, and boundary modes. Its topology is not carried by a single orbital pairing component, but emerges cooperatively from the dominant intra- and inter-orbital pairing components. These results establish a microscopic route by which Ising SOC and orbital hybridization can reconstruct interaction-driven superconductivity, producing both Ising-triplet and topological singlet-dominated phases in correlated non-centrosymmetric systems.

</details>


### [64] [$d$-wave superconductivity in quintuple-layer bulk superconductor La$_6$Ni$_5$O$_{12}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22212)
*Zhan Wang,Yuxin Wang,Hui-Ke Jin,Kun Jiang,Jiangping Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 基于五层Hubbard模型与变分蒙特卡洛计算，La₆Ni₅O₁₂体超导的配对对称性倾向于自旋单重态d_{x²−y²}波，扩展s波受抑；多种实验可观测量的理论结果一致指向干净节点超导体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: La₆Ni₅O₁₂中体超导（bulk superconductivity）的发现，为探究还原型Ruddlesden–Popper（RP）镍酸盐家族的配对对称性（pairing symmetry）提供了新契机，但该体系的配对形式尚待确定。

Method: 采用有效五层Hubbard模型（five-layer Hubbard model）与变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo, VMC）计算，判定能量上占优的配对态，并在优化后的超导态基础上计算多项实验可及的可观测量，同时将同一框架推广至其他还原型RP镍酸盐。

Result: 自旋单重态（spin-singlet）d_{x²−y²}波态在能量上占优，扩展s波（extended-s-wave）配对被强烈抑制。隧穿态密度呈V形低能谱，超流密度（superfluid density）随温度线性耗减，Knight位移（Knight shift）在T_c以下下降，自旋–晶格弛豫率遵循1/T₁T∝T²，共同构成干净节点超导体的自洽特征。推广计算显示无限层LaNiO₂具有稳健的d波倾向，三层La₄Ni₃O₈在标称填充下接近d波超导与非超导态的边界。

Conclusion: 体相La₆Ni₅O₁₂可作为判定镍酸盐超导体配对对称性的有前景平台，并暗示还原型RP镍酸盐家族存在共同的d_{x²−y²}波配对倾向。

Abstract: The recent realization of bulk superconductivity in the quintuple-layer nickelate La$_6$Ni$_5$O$_{12}$ opens new opportunities to probe the pairing symmetry of the reduced Ruddlesden--Popper (RP) nickelate family. Using an effective five-layer Hubbard model and variational Monte Carlo calculations, we find that a spin-singlet $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave state is energetically favored, while extended-$s$-wave pairing is strongly suppressed. Based on the optimized superconducting state, we further calculate several experimentally accessible observables. The tunneling density of states exhibits a characteristic V-shaped low-energy spectrum, the superfluid density shows a linear low-temperature depletion, the Knight shift decreases below $T_c$, and the spin-lattice relaxation rate follows $1/T_1T\propto T^2$, together providing consistent signatures of a clean nodal superconductor. Extending the same framework to related reduced RP nickelates, we find a robust $d$-wave tendency in infinite-layer LaNiO$_{2}$, while trilayer La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{8}$ at its nominal filling lies close to the boundary between $d$-wave superconductivity and a non-superconducting state. These results establish bulk La$_6$Ni$_5$O$_{12}$ as a promising platform for determining the pairing symmetry of nickelate superconductors and suggest a common $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave pairing tendency across the reduced RP nickelate family.

</details>


### [65] [Band-Resolved Superconducting Gaps via the Thermal Hall Effect in CeRu2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22361)
*Darian Hall,Luke Pritchard Cairns,Nico Rowland,Yuanqi Lyu,Kohtaro Yamakawa,Josue Rodriguez,Kirin Chanteloup,Didier Perrodin,Joel E. Moore,James G. Analytis*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究利用热霍尔输运（thermal Hall transport，$\\kappa_{xy}$），并结合比热与正常态磁输运，解决了补偿型重费米子超导体（compensated heavy-fermion superconductor）CeRu$_2$ 中配对能隙无法分配到具体费米面的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多带超导体（multiband superconductors）的常规体探测手段无法将配对能隙（pairing gap）归属到特定的电子或空穴费米面，形成能隙-能带对应关系的模糊性。

Method: 以比热和正常态磁输运（normal-state magnetotransport）作为约束，测量并分析 CeRu$_2$ 的热霍尔输运 $\\kappa_{xy}$ 与 $\\kappa_{xx}$；固定能隙大小（$\\Delta_e/k_B\\approx 13$K，$\\Delta_h/k_B\\approx 8$K）与能带迁移率（band mobilities），从而完全确定其电子自由度。

Result: 在给定能隙与迁移率条件下，$\\kappa_{xx}$ 与 $\\kappa_{xy}$ 中的电子贡献被完全确定；$\\kappa_{xy}$ 在低场（low-field）出现符号反转（sign reversal）。

Conclusion: 低场下 $\\kappa_{xy}$ 的符号反转表明较大的能隙位于重的 Ce $4f$ 电子片（electron sheets），较小的能隙位于空穴片（hole sheets），从而消除了 CeRu$_2$ 中的能隙归属模糊性。

Abstract: Standard bulk probes of multiband superconductors cannot assign pairing gaps to specific electron or hole Fermi surfaces. Here we show that thermal Hall transport ($κ_{xy}$), constrained by specific heat and normal-state magnetotransport, resolves this ambiguity in the compensated heavy-fermion superconductor CeRu$_2$. Fixing the gap magnitudes ($Δ_e/k_B\approx 13$K, $Δ_h/k_B\approx 8$K) and band mobilities completely determines the electronic degrees of freedom in $κ_{xx}$ and $κ_{xy}$. A low-field sign reversal in $κ_{xy}$ places the larger gap on the heavy Ce $4f$ electron sheets and the smaller on the hole sheets.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAMPE 的主要科学目标包括暗物质（dark matter）间接探测、宇宙线物理（cosmic-ray physics）与高能伽马射线天文学（high energy gamma-ray astronomy），因此需要一台具备大几何因子与厚成像量能器的空间探测器以实现相关观测。

Method: 依托大几何因子（large geometrical factor）与厚成像量能器（thick imaging calorimeter），DAMPE 可有效探测高达 10 TeV 的宇宙线电子（含正电子）与伽马射线，以及高达 PeV 的原子核。

Result: DAMPE 于 2015 年 12 月 17 日发射进入太阳同步轨道（Sun-synchronous orbit），已在轨平稳运行十余年，并获得了全电子能谱、质子及其他原子核能谱以及高能伽马射线谱线搜寻等科学成果。

Conclusion: 本文对 DAMPE 的仪器、在轨状态进行综述，并重点介绍其科学成果，表明该任务在暗物质间接探测、宇宙线物理与高能伽马射线天文学方面持续产出观测结果。

Abstract: The Dark Matter Particle Explorer (DAMPE, also known as ``Wukong'') is the first scientific space mission within the framework of the Strategic Pioneer Program on Space Science of the Chinese Academy of Sciences. The main scientific objectives of DAMPE include indirect detection of dark matter, cosmic-ray physics, and high energy gamma-ray astronomy. Thanks to its large geometrical factor and thick imaging calorimeter, DAMPE could effectively detect cosmic-ray electrons (including positrons) and gamma-rays up to 10~TeV, and nuclei up to PeV. Since launched on 17 December 2015 into a Sun-synchronous orbit, DAMPE has been smoothly operating on orbit for more than ten years. Here we present an overview of instrument, on-orbit status, and highlight scientific results of DAMPE, including the measurements of the all-electron energy spectrum and the spectra of protons and other nuclei, and high energy gamma-ray line search.

</details>


### [67] [Identification of ultra-high-energy neutrinos in vertical air showers with surface detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21495)
*Jaime Alvarez-Muñiz,Ruben Conceição,Pedro J. Costa,David Dias,Mário Pimenta*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨利用天顶角 θ≤60° 的垂直空气簇射（vertical air showers）探测超高能中微子（ultra-high-energy neutrinos）的可行性。通过模拟表明，对电磁与缪子成分敏感的可观测量可区分中微子与强子引发的簇射，并据此推导有效质量与有效面积。该方法虽在弥散通量分析中不具竞争力，但可为瞬变源的多信使观测提供互补的垂直探测通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前中微子搜寻主要集中于倾斜簇射（inclined showers），垂直簇射区域（θ≤60°）在很大程度上尚未被探索。作者希望评估地面探测器阵列（surface detector arrays）在该区域识别超高能中微子的潜力，以扩展观测视场。

Method: 利用中微子相互作用产生的高能轻子在近地面增强的电磁成分（electromagnetic component），采用详细的空气簇射与探测器模拟。以对电磁与缪子（muonic）含量敏感的可观测量进行判别，既使用理想化簇射量，也使用基于水切伦科夫（water-Cherenkov）与闪烁体（scintillator）探测器的真实探测器级信号。

Result: 模拟显示这些可观测量能够有效区分中微子引发与强子引发的簇射，并据此由中微子识别效率推导有效质量（effective mass）与主要推导有效面积（effective area），其背景抑制要求（background-rejection）匹配定向多信使搜寻。

Conclusion: 在弥散通量分析所需的严格背景抑制条件下，该方法不及倾斜簇射搜寻具竞争力；但其确立了垂直探测通道的可行性，扩展了地面阵列的视场，并为瞬变源（transient sources）的多信使观测提供互补工具。

Abstract: We investigate the potential of surface detector arrays to identify ultra-high-energy neutrinos using vertical air showers with zenith angle $θ\leq 60^\circ$, a region largely unexplored in current searches. The method exploits the enhanced electromagnetic component at ground level arising from energetic leptons produced in neutrino interactions. Using detailed air-shower and detector simulations, we show that observables sensitive to the electromagnetic and muonic content provide genuine discrimination between neutrino-induced and hadron-induced showers. This is demonstrated both with idealized shower quantities and with realistic detector-level signals based on water-Cherenkov and scintillator detectors. The corresponding neutrino identification efficiency is used to derive the effective mass and, primarily, the effective area of the array under background-rejection requirements appropriate to targeted multi-messenger searches. Although not competitive with inclined-shower searches under the stringent background rejection factors required for diffuse-flux analyses, this approach establishes the feasibility of a vertical detection channel, extending the field of view of surface arrays and providing a complementary tool for multi-messenger observations of transient sources.

</details>


### [68] [Extracting the Central Sound Speed of Neutron Stars through Intrinsic Stellar Structure Scaling with Reduced Equation-of-State Model Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21806)
*Jian-Hao Shi,Bao-Jun Cai,Yu-Gang Ma*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该工作通过含广义ω介子自相互作用的非线性Walecka模型构建六类状态方程（equation of state, EOS）族，并利用IPAD-TOV框架推导出不依赖EOS的中心声速平方估计量Θ，从而在现有观测无法区分EOS的情况下约束中子星核心性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推断中子星（neutron star, NS）核心性质高度依赖所假设的状态方程（EOS），即使各EOS均满足相同的核物理与天体物理约束，其高密度行为与核心性质仍可能显著不同，因此需要降低模型依赖性的方法。

Method: 采用含广义ω介子自相互作用的非线性Walecka模型构建六个EOS族，每族含10³个样本并受相同约束；随后使用无量纲Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff方程的内禀与微扰分析（IPAD-TOV），推导不依赖EOS的中心声速平方估计量Θ(X,Ψ)，其中X=P_c/ε_c为中心EOS参数，Ψ刻画恒星质量对中心能量密度的响应。

Result: 对典型1.4 M_⊙中子星，Θ与中心声速平方s_c²在各EOS族中呈线性相关（R²≈0.94–0.99）；最小化各族关系离散度得到共同最优值：全部六族为s_c²≈0.523±0.044、X≈0.173^{+0.020}_{-0.018}，排除无ω自相互作用族后为s_c²≈0.496±0.092、X≈0.167^{+0.030}_{-0.029}；对应半径（95%置信区间）分别为11.73^{+1.08}_{-0.92} km与11.92^{+1.76}_{-1.28} km。此外，高阶ω相互作用可在纯核子物质中产生内部声速峰。

Conclusion: 结果表明，共同的恒星结构关系可在降低EOS模型依赖性的前提下约束中子星核心性质，即便现有观测无法区分底层EOS描述；拟合全部EOS样本的统一标度关系支持上述两类提取结果。

Abstract: Inferring neutron star (NS) core properties remains sensitive to the assumed equation of state (EOS). Using a nonlinear Walecka model with generalized $ω$-meson self-interactions, we construct six EOS families, each containing $10^3$ samples subject to identical nuclear and astrophysical constraints. Despite satisfying these constraints, the families predict markedly different high-density behavior and core properties; higher-order $ω$ interactions can also generate interior sound-speed peaks within purely nucleonic matter. To reduce this model dependence, we use the intrinsic and perturbative analysis of the dimensionless Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff equations (IPAD-TOV) to derive an EOS-independent estimator $Θ(\rm{X},Ψ)$ of the central squared sound speed $s_{\rm c}^2$, where $\rm{X}=P_{\rm c}/\varepsilon_{\rm c}$ is the central EOS-parameter and $Ψ$ characterizes the stellar-mass response to central energy density. For canonical $1.4\,M_\odot$ NSs, $Θ$ correlates linearly with $s_{\rm c}^2$ across the families ($R^2\approx0.94$-$0.99$). The family-dependent relations converge toward a common optimum: minimizing their dispersion yields $s_{\rm c}^2\approx 0.523\pm0.044$ and $\rm{X}\approx 0.173^{+0.020}_{-0.018}$ for all six families, or $s_{\rm c}^2\approx 0.496\pm0.092$ and $\rm{X}\approx 0.167^{+0.030}_{-0.029}$ when the family without $ω$ self-interaction is excluded. The corresponding radii are $11.73^{+1.08}_{-0.92}$ and $11.92^{+1.76}_{-1.28}\,\rm km$, respectively, with $95\%$ confidence intervals. A unified scaling fitted to all EOS samples supports both extractions. These results show how common stellar-structure relations can constrain NS core properties with reduced EOS-model dependence, even when existing observations cannot distinguish among the underlying EOS descriptions.

</details>
