### [69] [Thermodynamically Consistent Machine Learning for Fracture Prediction in Heterogeneous Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20614)
*Muhamad Alim,Xiaolong He,Mei-Na Tsao,Chi-Hua Yu,J. S. Chen*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出热力学一致潜动力学辨识（tLaSDI）方法，用于异质材料断裂模式与演化预测。模型在低维潜空间学习热力学状态变量，通过参数化GENERIC形式化信息神经网络（pGFINNs）以硬约束方式嵌入能量守恒与熵生成等原理，并结合几何条件自编码器。结果显示其可比纯数据驱动的AE-LSTM更准确地捕捉断裂模式与演化，并在未见样本上保持热力学约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 断裂行为理解对评估结构完整性与寿命至关重要，而基于物理的断裂模拟耗时极高，促使发展基于机器学习的代理模型。然而现有断裂预测机器学习模型多为黑箱，且需大规模训练数据才能可靠泛化，因此需要兼具物理一致性与数据效率的替代方案。

Method: 提出面向参数化系统的热力学一致潜动力学辨识（tLaSDI），在低维潜空间中以参数化GENERIC形式化信息神经网络（pGFINNs）学习热力学状态变量，强制满足总能量守恒与熵生成等热力学原理。采用硬约束方法将物理嵌入网络架构，并引入几何条件自编码器（Geometry-Conditioned Autoencoder）学习几何构型以实现几何引导学习。

Result: 能量与熵演化历史表明，模型在未见测试样本上仍保持热力学约束。结果进一步显示，tLaSDI-pGFINNs 相较纯数据驱动的自编码器长短期记忆网络（AE-LSTM）能更准确地捕捉断裂模式及其后续断裂演化。

Conclusion: tLaSDI-pGFINNs 通过将热力学原理以硬约束方式嵌入潜动力学学习，在参数化异质材料断裂预测中兼顾物理一致性与预测精度，优于纯数据驱动的 AE-LSTM，为断裂演化代理建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Understanding fracture behavior is critical for assessing the structural integrity and longevity. Highly time-consuming physics-based fracture simulations motivate the development of machine-learning-based fracture evolution prediction with surrogate models. However, most existing machine-learning models for fracture prediction operate as black boxes and require large training datasets to generalize reliably. In this work, Thermodynamically Consistent Latent Dynamics Identification (tLaSDI) for parametric systems is introduced to predict fracture patterns and evolution in heterogeneous materials. The model learns the thermodynamic state variable in a lower-dimensional latent space using parametric GENERIC Formalism-Informed Neural Networks (pGFINNs) that enforce key thermodynamic principles, such as total energy conservation and entropy generation. This model embeds physics into the neural network architecture using a hard-constraint method that inherently complies with the underlying thermodynamic principles. The proposed tLaSDI-pGFINNs is further enhanced by a Geometry-Conditioned Autoencoder in learning the geometry configuration for effective geometry-guided learning. The energy and entropy evolution histories demonstrate that the model preserves the thermodynamic constraints on unseen testing samples. The results further show that tLaSDI-pGFINNs can more accurately capture both fracture patterns and the subsequent fracture evolution than the purely data-driven Autoencoder Long Short-Term Memory (AE-LSTM) model.

</details>


### [70] [Second-Order Homogenization of Particulate Elastomeric Composites for Structural-Scale Damage Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20624)
*Krishna Murthy Pabbu,Tarkes Dora Pallicity,Nelson Muthu*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一套计算框架，通过二阶均匀化获取颗粒弹性体复合材料的等效本构响应，并结合三参数各向同性损伤模型与混合位移-压力有限元格式，实现损伤传播分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒弹性体复合材料损伤预测困难，源于材料强非均质性、基体近乎不可压缩行为，以及基体-夹杂相互作用引发的局部应力集中。

Method: 先以二阶均匀化方法（second-order homogenization method）确定非均质材料的等效本构响应，据此标定等效超弹性材料参数用于结构仿真；引入三参数各向同性损伤模型（three-parameter isotropic damage model）描述损伤软化；采用混合位移-压力有限元格式（mixed displacement-pressure finite element formulation）缓解体积锁死。

Result: 通过不同夹杂体积分数复合材料的全尺寸仿真与均匀化响应验证框架；均匀化模型能在2%误差内重现全局力-位移响应及损伤演化特征，同时显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 所提框架可有效且高效地预测颗粒弹性体复合结构的损伤传播，在保证精度的同时大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Damage prediction in particulate elastomeric composites is challenging due to the strong material heterogeneity, nearly incompressible behavior of the matrix, and the presence of localized stress concentrations arising from matrix-inclusion interactions. In the present work, a computational framework is developed for damage propagation analysis of particulate elastomeric composite structures. The effective constitutive response of the heterogeneous material is first determined using the second-order homogenization method. The resulting homogenized response is subsequently used to identify equivalent hyperelastic material parameters for structural simulations. A three-parameter isotropic damage model is incorporated to capture damage-induced softening. A mixed displacement-pressure finite element formulation, is used to alleviate volumetric locking associated with nearly incompressible property of elastomers. The proposed framework is validated through full-scale simulations and homogenized response involving composites with varying inclusion volume fractions. Numerical investigations demonstrate that the homogenized models is able to reproduce the global force-displacement response within 2% error and damage evolution characteristics of the heterogeneous composites while significantly reducing the computational cost.

</details>

### [71] [Half-space last passage percolation with inhomogeneous first row](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21413)
*Patrik L. Ferrari,Sabrina Gernholt*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究具有确定性首行的半空间指数型最后通行渗流（exponential last passage percolation），该模型对应粒子从原点进入并受移动壁面约束的半空间 TASEP（totally asymmetric simple exclusion process）。作者依据边界强度与壁面选择，确定了重标度最后通行时间的可能极限分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半空间构型下最后通行渗流与 TASEP 的极限行为受边界条件与移动壁面影响，其分布分类尚需系统刻画。本文旨在厘清边界强度与壁面选择如何共同决定重标度最后通行时间的极限分布。

Method: 研究对象为半空间中具确定性第一行的指数型最后通行渗流模型。作为关键输入，作者建立了点对点半空间最后通行时间的若干结果，包括上尾界（upper tail bounds）、弱收敛（weak convergence），以及特定起点与终点下测地线（geodesic）触及对角线的概率界。

Result: 在边界强度与壁面选择的不同组合下，确定了重标度最后通行时间所有可能的极限分布。同时给出了点对点半空间最后通行时间的上尾界、弱收敛性，以及测地线触及对角线概率的界。

Conclusion: 半空间最后通行渗流的极限分布由边界强度与壁面选择共同决定，呈现可能的分布分类。所建立的点对点结果为进一步分析半空间模型提供了关键工具。

Abstract: We consider exponential last passage percolation in half-space with a deterministic first row. This model corresponds to a version of TASEP in half-space in which particles enter at the origin and are restricted by a moving wall. We determine the possible limiting distributions of rescaled last passage times depending on the boundary strength and the choice of the wall. As key inputs to our analysis, we establish several results for point-to-point half-space last passage times, including upper tail bounds, weak convergence, and bounds on the probabilities that geodesics touch the diagonal for certain start and end points.

</details>


### [72] [Segal's semigroup, Virasoro structure, and Ward identities in Compactified Imaginary Liouville Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22170)
*Yang Xiao,Yuxiao Xie*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究紧凑化虚刘维尔理论（Compactified Imaginary Liouville Theory, CILT）所给出的 Segal 半群（Segal's semigroup）的量子化，将半群元素与共形环域对应，恢复 Virasoro 生成元并推导 Ward 恒等式，为 (B)CILT 的共形自举（conformal bootstrap）提供工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CILT 是一种概率构造的共形场论（Conformal Field Theory, CFT），预期与极小模型、库仑气体积分及圈模型存在联系。相较实刘维尔 CFT，此处主要困难在于正性（positivity）缺失，需要新的处理手段来发展其共形结构理论。

Method: 将 CILT 给出的 Segal 半群量子化，每个元素对应由收缩双全纯映射定义的共形环域；建立量子化半群与环域 Segal 振幅的联系，借助虚高斯乘性混沌（imaginary Gaussian Multiplicative Chaos）估计与非自伴算子（non-self-adjoint operators）处理正性缺失，并利用 Segal 粘合公理与共形协变性对振幅求导。

Result: 恢复出 CILT 的 Virasoro 生成元 L_n、L̃_n（n≥0）作为该半群的生成元，并导出以全部 Virasoro 算子 L_n、L̃_n（n∈Z）表述的 Ward 恒等式；同时概述了边界 CILT（Boundary CILT, BCILT）关于半环域 Segal 半群的平行理论。

Conclusion: 结果为 (B)CILT 的共形自举纲领、边界 BPZ 方程、边界结构常数以及共形块（conformal blocks）的整体构造提供了基础性工具。

Abstract: Compactified Imaginary Liouville Theory (CILT for short) is a probabilistically constructed Conformal Field Theory (CFT), expected to have connections to minimal models, Coulomb gas integrals, and loop models. In this paper, we study the quantization of Segal's semigroup given by CILT. Each element $\mathbf T_f$ in the quantized semigroup corresponds to a conformal annulus: the unit disk $\mathbb D$ with $f(\mathbb D^\circ)$ removed, where $f:\mathbb D\to f(\mathbb D)\subset \mathbb D^\circ$ is a contracting biholomorphism. This semigroup encodes the local conformal structure of the CFT, in particular the Virasoro symmetry with central charge $<1$ for CILT. We first relate the quantized Segal's semigroup to Segal's amplitudes of annuli and recover the Virasoro generators $\mathbf L_n,\widetilde{\mathbf L}_n$ ($n\geq0$) of CILT as generators of this semigroup. Compared to the real Liouville CFT, the main difficulty here is the absence of positivity, which we address through estimates for the imaginary Gaussian Multiplicative Chaos and non-self-adjoint operators. Using Segal's gluing axiom as in the real case, we differentiate the amplitude and use conformal covariance to derive the Ward identities in terms of all Virasoro operators $\mathbf L_n,\widetilde{\mathbf L}_n$ ($n\in\mathbb Z$). We also outline the parallel theory for Boundary CILT (BCILT), concerning Segal's semigroup of semiannuli. These results provide essential tools for the conformal bootstrap program of (B)CILT, boundary BPZ equations, boundary structure constants, and the global construction of conformal blocks.

</details>

*Gefei Cai*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 核心技术手段是提出关于有限无向图上加权生成树的二次祖先不等式（quadratic ancestor inequality），并以此为基础推导两个应用结果。

Result: 对任意有限 n 顶点传递图（transitive graph），其均匀生成树（uniform spanning tree, UST）中两个独立均匀顶点间的期望距离至少为 √n−1；此外，三维环路擦除随机游走（loop-erased random walk, LERW）的增长指数（growth exponent）至少为 3/2。

Conclusion: 二次祖先不等式可有效处理加权生成树问题；其应用解决了 Benjamini–Kozma（Comm. Math. Phys. 259 (2005), 257–286）提出的猜想，并给出三维 LERW 增长指数的下界。

Abstract: We introduce a quadratic ancestor inequality for weighted spanning trees on finite undirected graphs and give two applications. First, for every finite $n$-vertex transitive graph, the expected distance in its uniform spanning tree (UST) between two independent uniform vertices is at least $\sqrt{n}-1$, resolving a conjecture in [Benjamini--Kozma, Comm. Math. Phys. 259 (2005), 257--286]. Second, we prove that the growth exponent of three-dimensional loop-erased random walk (LERW) is at least 3/2.

Result: 结果表明，Hitchin $L^2$ 度量以指数速率逼近半平坦度量；度量比较与谱周期计算显示每个模空间均为 $E_6$ 型完备 ALG 引力瞬子（complete ALG gravitational instanton of type $E_6$）。渐近估计在权重腔（weight chamber）的紧子区间上保持一致。

Conclusion: 结论为：对三孔球面上给定抛物权重族的稳定强抛物 $\mathrm{SL}(3,\C)$-Higgs 丛，其模空间具有 $E_6$ 型完备 ALG 引力瞬子的几何结构，且相关渐近估计在权重腔紧子区间上具有一致性。

Abstract: We study the large-scale geometry of the moduli spaces of stable strongly parabolic $\mathrm{SL}(3,\C)$-Higgs bundles on the three-punctured sphere for a family of parabolic weights. We glue the local Toda model metric to a decoupled metric obtained from the spectral line bundle, and perturb the resulting approximate metrics to harmonic metrics adapted to the parabolic structure. We also prove that Hitchin's $L^2$ metric approaches the semi-flat metric exponentially. The metric comparison and the spectral period calculation show that each moduli space is a complete ALG gravitational instanton of type $E_6$. The asymptotic estimates are uniform on compact subintervals of the weight chamber.

</details>


### [75] [The derived category of a generalized complex torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21960)
*Patrick Antweiler,Vicente Cortés,Julian Holstein,Markus Röser,Aldo Witte*

TL;DR: 该文为环面上任意平移不变的广义复结构（generalized complex structure）J 构造导出范畴 D(J)，统一推广了复环面的相干层导出范畴与辛环面的仿射/收敛 Fukaya 范畴，并证明其与形变路径无关且被 O(2n,2n;Z) 作用保持。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复环面的相干层导出范畴与辛环面的 Fukaya 范畴此前分属不同几何框架，缺乏统一描述。作者以广义复几何为共同语言，试图构造同时涵盖二者的导出范畴，并检验其在镜像对称（mirror symmetry）与 T-对偶（T-duality）下的行为。

Method: 以复环面 Dolbeault 代数（Dolbeault algebra）的非交换弯曲形变（non-commutative curved deformation）来定义范畴 D(J)；借助非交换傅里叶–向井对偶（non-commutative Fourier-Mukai duality）证明该范畴不依赖于所选形变路径；并考察 O(2n,2n;Z) 作用的不变性。

Result: D(J) 不依赖于形变路径的选择；O(2n,2n;Z) 对广义复结构空间的作用保持 D(J) 不变，特别地 T-对偶在 D(J) 上作用平凡。对辛环面的仿射拉格朗日（affine Lagrangian）构造了导出范畴中的具体对象，其态射空间由 Floer 上同调（Floer cohomology）给出。

Conclusion: 该构造与同调镜像对称猜想（homological mirror symmetry conjecture，含其非交换版本）相容，为复环面与辛环面的导出范畴提供了统一的广义复几何框架。

Abstract: For any translation invariant generalized complex structure J on a torus we construct the derived category D(J) generalizing the derived category of coherent sheaves on a complex torus and the affine/convergent Fukaya category of a symplectic torus. The category is defined in terms of a non-commutative curved deformation of the Dolbeault algebra of a complex torus. We show that this category is independent of the chosen deformation path, which we prove using non-commutative Fourier-Mukai duality. We also prove that the action of O(2n,2n; Z) on the space of generalized complex structures leaves the category D(J) invariant. In particular, T-duality acts trivially on D(J). We construct concrete objects associated to affine Lagrangians in the derived category associated to a symplectic torus and show that their spaces of morphisms are given by Floer cohomology. We show that our construction is consistent with the homological mirror symmetry conjecture (including its non-commutative version).

</details>


### [76] [Stratification of opers with apparent singularities and isomonodromic bubbling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22326)
*Duong Dinh,Troy Figiel,Joerg Teschner*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究亏格 $g \geq 2$ 紧黎曼曲面上 rank-2 平坦联络 de Rham 模空间的 Simpson 分层（Simpson's stratification），通过分支射影结构（branched projective structures）或带表观奇点的 oper 给出分层的表观奇点计数刻画，并构造显式全纯族实现两表观奇点碰撞消失、跨越到更小分层的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Simpson 分层刻画了 de Rham 模空间中平坦联络的闭包关系，但其在具体族中的实现方式、以及分层闭包关系的几何机制尚缺乏显式构造。本文试图通过表观奇点的碰撞过程，给出跨越分层边界的可计算模型。

Method: 将 rank-2 平坦 $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{C})$-联络表示为分支射影结构或带表观奇点（apparent singularities）的 oper，用所需表观奇点的最小数目确定所在分层；构造两个简单表观奇点碰撞并消失的全纯族；在 Higgs 丛侧用谱曲线（spectral curve）上的 Baker--Akhiezer 除子描述对应族。

Result: 所构造的全纯族在一般点附近实现了 Simpson 分层的闭包关系；对应的 Higgs 丛族中，谱曲线上两点收敛为被谱对合（spectral involution）交换的一对点；碰撞之前两表观奇点由界定一个气泡（bubble）的孪生路径相连。

Conclusion: 这些族可由 Calsamiglia、Deroin 与 Francaviglia 讨论的等单值气泡手术（isomonodromic bubbling surgery）产生，从而将 Simpson 分层闭包关系与气泡手术机制联系起来。

Abstract: On a compact Riemann surface of genus $g \geq 2$, we study Simpson's stratification of the de Rham moduli space of rank-2 flat connections. Upon representing flat $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{C})$-connections by branched projective structures, or equivalently by opers with apparent singularities, the stratum containing a flat connection is determined by the minimal number of apparent singularities needed to represent it. We construct explicit holomorphic families in which two simple apparent singularities collide and disappear, so that the family crosses into a smaller stratum; this realizes Simpson's closure relation between strata near generic points. The analogous families of Higgs bundles are described by Baker--Akhiezer divisors on the spectral curve in which two points converge to a pair of points exchanged by the spectral involution. We show that, before the collision, the two apparent singularities are joined by twin paths bounding a bubble, so that these families arise from the isomonodromic bubbling surgery discussed by Calsamiglia, Deroin and Francaviglia.

</details>

### [77] [Boundary-induced geometrical frustration in a square Rydberg-atom lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20723)
*Marco Rigobello,Luka Pavešić,Daniel Jaschke,Pietro Silvi,Marco Di Liberto,Simone Montangero,Simone Notarnicola*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本研究考察方形晶格里德堡原子阵列（Rydberg-atom arrays）中由晶格尺寸奇偶性（lattice-size parity）诱导的几何缺陷（geometric defects），在条纹密度波相（striated density-wave phase）中对奇偶尺寸晶格的域选择与缺陷构型进行了刻画，并在 Aquila 量子处理器上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清边界条件与晶格尺寸如何决定可编程量子物质（programmable quantum matter）的全局有序（global ordering），尤其是几何缺陷在密度波相中的作用。

Method: 采用方形晶格里德堡原子阵列模型，通过 Aquila 原子量子处理器（atomic quantum processor）实施态制备协议（state-preparation protocol），并以平衡态与实时动力学张量网络模拟（tensor-network simulations）作为基准对照。

Result: 奇数尺寸晶格选择单一条纹域（single striated domain）；偶数尺寸晶格在四角产生不相容域（incompatible domains），形成具有 π/2 旋转不变性（π/2-rotation invariant）且几何缺陷自晶格中心发出的态。在更大失谐（detuning）下出现 π 旋转不变（π-rotation-invariant）态，由对角两角成核的两个域构成，并被一维对角缺陷分开。

Conclusion: 结果表明边界与晶格尺寸在决定可编程量子物质全局有序中起关键作用，奇偶性可调控几何缺陷的类型与对称性。

Abstract: We study geometric defects in square-lattice Rydberg-atom arrays induced by lattice-size parity in the striated density-wave phase. While odd-sized lattices select a single striated domain, even-sized lattices seed incompatible domains at the four corners, resulting in a π/2-rotation invariant state with a geometrical defect emanating from the lattice center. At larger detunings, we instead identify a π-rotation-invariant state with two domains nucleated by opposite corners and separated by a one-dimensional diagonal defect. We probe the state-preparation protocol on the Aquila atomic quantum processor and benchmark it with equilibrium and real-time dynamics tensor-network simulations. Our results assess the role of boundaries and lattice size in determining the global ordering in programmable quantum matter.

</details>


### [78] [Casimir Sign Reversal from Finite-Range Bogoliubov Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20897)
*Fabrizio Canfora,Pablo Pais,Luigi Rosa*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究三维玻色流体在有限程修正下的零温卡西米尔效应（Casimir effect），在声学通用项 $L^{-3}$ 之外推导出非通用 $L^{-5}$ 修正，并借助泊松重求和（Poisson resummation）计算确认卡西米尔能可发生符号反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bogoliubov 谱的有限程修正会引入超越通用声学贡献的额外长程项，其可能与声子项竞争并改变卡西米尔力的性质；同时强偶极原子气体提供了纳米尺度相互作用长度的现实动机。

Method: 在最小各向同性有限程模型（minimal isotropic finite-range model）中解析推导该交叉行为，并将渐近结果与完整色散关系对比；采用与正则化无关的泊松重求和（Poisson-resummed）计算进行分析。

Result: 长程展开中存在非通用 $L^{-5}$ 修正，可与声子项竞争；泊松重求和计算确认卡西米尔能符号反转可能发生，但仅平衡 $L^{-3}$ 与 $L^{-5}$ 两项所推断的低耦合零点在定量上不可控。

Conclusion: 稳健的符号变化发生在接近旋子软化（roton softening）的稳定区域，此时卡西米尔能获得振荡的有限动量贡献；所得非线性力律可通过驱动纳米机械谐振器（nanomechanical resonator）的频率响应进行探测。

Abstract: We study the zero-temperature Casimir effect of a three-dimensional Bose fluid with finite-range corrections to its Bogoliubov spectrum. Beyond the universal acoustic contribution scaling as $L^{-3}$, the long-distance expansion contains a nonuniversal $L^{-5}$ correction that can compete with the phononic term. We derive this crossover analytically within a minimal isotropic finite-range model and test the asymptotic result against the full dispersion. A regulator-independent Poisson-resummed calculation confirms that sign reversals of the Casimir energy are possible, while showing that the apparent low-coupling zero inferred by balancing only the $L^{-3}$ and $L^{-5}$ terms is not quantitatively controlled. Robust sign changes instead occur in the stable regime close to roton softening, where the Casimir energy acquires an oscillatory finite-momentum contribution. Strongly dipolar atomic gases provide a natural motivation for microscopic interaction lengths in the nanometric regime. We further discuss how the resulting nonlinear force law could be probed through the frequency response of a driven nanomechanical resonator.

</details>


### [79] [Localized states of the non-Hermitian Schr{ö}dinger equation with a complex $1/r^{2}$ potential: from Efimov states to bipolarons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21155)
*Kyoka Tsunoda,Kazuki Yamamoto,Shimpei Endo*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 研究复 $1/r^2$ 势的非厄米薛定谔方程，解析求解束缚态与连续态，揭示 Efimov 束缚态随虚部增大的复平面旋转、激发态逐次消失，以及双极化子（bipolaron）短程局域化与例外点（exceptional point）伴随现象的离散对称性起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该复 $1/r^2$ 势模型充当厄米少体物理（Efimov 束缚态）与非厄米多体物理（介质诱导的重极化子相互作用）之间的桥梁，旨在统一理解两类物理在复势框架下的行为。

Method: 解析求解该非厄米薛定谔方程，同时得到局域态与连续态，并与数值结果及 WKB 近似进行对比；此外采用有限差分法（finite differences）求解以考察离散化效应。

Result: 随势的虚部增大，Efimov 束缚态能量获得虚部并在复平面逆时针旋转；逼近纯虚势时激发态顺序消失，Efimov 基态平滑连接至短程双极化子态。有限差分显示每个双极化子态必伴随一个在网格尺度上振荡、连续极限下不存在的局域态，二者在例外点（exceptional point）合并，一旦引入有限实部该合并即解除。

Conclusion: 纯虚势下双极化子仅由介质诱导的虚相互作用局域化，无需实吸引势；伴随态与例外点行为源于离散化薛定谔方程对任意纯虚势成立的对称性。

Abstract: We study the non-Hermitian Schrödinger equation with a complex $1/r^{2}$ potential, which bridges Hermitian few-body and non-Hermitian many-body physics: a real attractive potential describes Efimov bound states, whereas a purely imaginary one describes the medium-induced interaction between two heavy polarons at large separations. We solve this equation analytically, obtaining both the localized and continuum states, and compare the solutions with numerical and WKB results. As the imaginary part of the potential is increased, the energies of the Efimov bound states acquire imaginary parts and rotate counterclockwise in the complex plane. On approaching the purely imaginary potential, the excited states disappear sequentially, while the Efimov ground state is smoothly connected to a bipolaron state localized at short distance. Such a bipolaron is localized solely by the medium-induced imaginary interaction, without any attractive real potential. Solving the equation by finite differences, we find that each bipolaron state is necessarily accompanied by another localized state that oscillates on the scale of the mesh size and hence has no counterpart in the continuum limit. Each such pair coalesces at an exceptional point as the strength of the purely imaginary potential is varied, whereas the coalescence is lifted once a finite real part is introduced. We show that this behavior originates from a symmetry of the discretized Schrödinger equation that holds for any purely imaginary potential.

</details>

### [80] [Statistical Characterization of Erratic and Chaotic Processes through Their Extrema I. Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21620)
*Jacob Sonnenschein*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 提出一个通用统计框架，通过极值点统计来刻画不规则函数（erratic functions），以识别混沌过程（chaotic processes）的统计特征，并将其映射到热力学描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为混沌过程寻找可量化的统计签名（statistical signatures），通过极值点位置、间距及其比值等统计量，将不规则函数与已知混沌行为（如随机矩阵理论）建立联系。

Method: 分析极值点位置的概率分布函数（probability distribution functions, PDFs）、位置间距（尤其最近邻间距）及相邻间距比值；用与混沌行为相关的已知 PDF 进行拟合；在多变量情形下于 D 维空间构建极值点云并考虑极值曲线；进一步通过计算香农熵（Shannon entropy）或微分熵（differential entropy）并将 PDF 等同于 Gibbs 分布，建立"热力学"描述，导出"能量函数"及部分情形下的"温度"。

Result: 分析并考察了三类基准间距 PDF：伯努利移位映射（Bernoulli shift map）、泊松分布（Poisson）与高斯 beta 系综（Gaussian beta ensemble），以及若干其他分布。区分了边缘间距分布（marginal spacing distributions）与连续间距联合分布（joint distributions of consecutive spacings），后者可揭示单一间距 PDF 无法显示的关联与统计组织。

Conclusion: 该向热力学的映射可应用于多种混沌过程，包括非物理过程；联合分布能提供更强签名，用于区分结构化类混沌行为与不相关的不规则性。

Abstract: We propose a general statistical framework for characterizing erratic functions through the statistics of their extrema, with the aim of identifying statistical signatures associated with chaotic processes. It is based on analyzing the extrema points of erratic functions associated with these processes. One is instructed to determine the probability distribution functions ( PDF)s of the locations of the extrema points, the spacings between the locations, in particular the nearest neighbors, and the ratios between adjacent spacings. We then fit these PDFs with PDFs known to relate to chaotic behavior like of random matrix theory.
  The extrema considered establish a cloud of points in general in $D$ dimensions for erratic function of several variables. For certain systems one finds also extrema curves. We further determine the ``thermodynamic" description of the erratic functions by computing the Shannon or differential entropy and by equating the PDFs to the Gibbs one. With this map we determine the ``energy function" and for certain cases also the ``temperature".
  We analyze three benchmark spacing PDFs: the Bernoulli shift map, the Poisson and the Gaussian beta ensemble and several others.
  We want to emphasize that the map to thermodynamics can be applied to variety of chaotic processes including non-physics ones.
  We distinguish marginal spacing distributions from joint distributions of consecutive spacings. The latter probe correlations and statistical organization that are invisible in one-spacing PDFs and may provide stronger signatures for distinguishing structured chaotic-like behavior from uncorrelated irregularity.

</details>

### [81] [Infinite magic and the emergence of the continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20790)
*Leo Shaposhnik,Lorenzo Leone,Lennart Bittel,Dimitris Saraidaris,Alexander Jahn,Jens Eisert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作建立了超有限冯·诺依曼代数（hyperfinite von Neumann algebras）的类型分类与量子信息论资源"非稳定子性"（nonstabilizerness，又称 magic）之间的定量关系，论证了无限 magic 是格点系统涌现相对论量子场论的必要结构要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：一个格点系统需要量子态的哪些特征，才能涌现出相对论量子场论（relativistic quantum field theory）。作者试图将代数类型分类与量子信息资源联系起来，以解释 continuum 与 type III 因子出现的条件。

Method: 通过证明两个互补结果展开：其一，证明格点上任何稳定子（stabilizer）系统都定义出规范的 GNS 希尔伯特空间（GNS Hilbert space），其局部冯·诺依曼代数在中心分解中不含 type III 因子；其二，针对 Araki、Woods 与 Powers 的超有限因子，建立定量等价关系——极限因子为 type III 当且仅当局部优化的 magic 最小相对熵（min-relative entropy）发散。

Result: 对具有短程有序（short-range order）的稳定子系统，证得局部代数同构于 type I 或 type II 超有限因子的张量积；因此 magic 的缺失伴随局部代数中 type III 因子的缺失，从而禁止量子场论的出现。另一方面，type III 因子的出现要求 magic 不仅存在，而且需为无限量，其发散是生成 type III 因子连续谱的必要条件。

Conclusion: 结果为"无限 magic 是格点系统涌现相对论连续统的必要结构要素"这一主张提供了严格框架，表明魔术资源（magic）与代数类型分类之间存在深层联系。

Abstract: Which features of a quantum state are needed for a relativistic quantum field theory to emerge from a lattice system? We address this question by establishing a quantitative relationship between the type classification of hyperfinite von Neumann algebras and the quantum-information-theoretic resource of nonstabilizerness, also called magic. Our results establish two complementary parts of this relationship. First, we prove that any stabilizer system on a lattice defines a canonical GNS Hilbert space in which local von Neumann algebras do not contain type III factors in their central decomposition. As an explicit example, we prove that for stabilizer systems with short-range order, the local algebras are isomorphic to tensor products of hyperfinite factors of type I or type II. This demonstrates that the absence of magic is accompanied by the absence of type III factors in the local algebras, therefore prohibiting the appearance of a quantum field theory. Second, for the hyperfinite factors of Araki, Woods and Powers, we prove a quantitative equivalence: The limiting factor is of type III if and only if the locally optimized min-relative entropy of magic diverges. This divergence is necessary to generate the continuous spectrum associated to a type III factor. Thus, in this setting, the emergence of a type III factor requires not merely the presence of magic, but an infinite amount of it. These results provide a rigorous setting supporting the proposal that infinite magic is a necessary structural ingredient for the emergence of a relativistic continuum from lattice systems.

</details>


### [82] [Singular wave functions and quantum collapse in inverse-power potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21044)
*Hidetsugu Sakaguchi,Boris Malomed*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究反幂势（inverse-power potential）U(r)=-U₀/(2rⁿ) 下薛定谔方程（Schroedinger equation）中的量子坍缩（quantum collapse, QC）现象，给出 r→0 时奇异定态波函数的渐近形式与部分精确解，并通过截断势的正则化版本恢复基态（ground state, GS）与离散束缚态谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子坍缩发生时基态缺失，但有限能量处仍存在可归一化的束缚态，这些态可能具有物理意义。因此需要构造并刻画 r→0 处奇异的定态波函数，以理解坍缩情形下仍可用的解结构。

Method: 在薛定谔方程框架内对反幂势 U(r)=-U₀/(2rⁿ) 进行解析求解：推导 r→0 时定态波函数的渐近形式（对应连续谱），并在适当条件下给出波函数的若干精确解。此外，将该势在距中心有限距离处截断，作为正则化（regularized）处理方式进行分析。

Result: 坍缩情形下，定态波函数在 r→0 处呈现无限多次空间振荡；解析结果给出 r→0 的渐近形式，其构成连续谱（continuous spectrum），并给出若干精确解。正则化势下的波函数仅具有有限次空间振荡，缺失的基态重新出现，束缚态谱变为离散谱（discrete spectrum）。

Conclusion: 量子坍缩并不排除有限能量处可归一化束缚态的物理相关性；在 r→0 处奇异的定态波函数可用渐近形式与特定条件下的精确解描述。对势作有限距离截断的正则化可消除无限振荡，恢复基态并给出离散束缚态谱。

Abstract: Quantum collapse (QC) occurs under the action of the inverse-power potential U(r)=-U_{0}/(2r^{n}) in the framework of the Schroedinger equation. The corresponding stationary wave functions feature an infinite number of spatial oscillations at $r\rightarrow 0$. The ground state (GS) is missing in the presence of the QC, but the available bound states at finite energies may be physically relevant, as they are normalizable. This work aims to produce singular stationary wave functions. The analytical findings represent an asymptotic form of the stationary wave functions at $r\rightarrow 0$, which fill a continuous spectrum, and some exact solutions for the wave functions, obtained under appropriate conditions. As a regularized version, we also consider the potential truncated at a finite difference from the center. The regularized wave functions feature a finite number of spatial oscillations, the missing GS reappears, and the spectrum of the bound states is discrete.

</details>


### [83] [Operator-Space Entanglement Entropy in Random Fermionic Gaussian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21723)
*Pablo Bayona-Pena,Elena Gramellini,Lorenzo Piroli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究考察初始乘积算符在随机高斯动力学（random gaussian dynamics，由 Brownian SYK$_2$ 模型描述）下演化时的冯·诺依曼算符空间纠缠熵（von Neumann operator-space entanglement entropy, OSEE）。作者借助畴壁熔化（domain-wall melting）映射，在热力学极限下推导出含时二分 OSEE 的精确表达式，并给出晚时出现的相应高斯 Page 曲线（gaussian Page curve）的解析刻画。结果建立在全连接量子简单排斥过程（all-to-all quantum simple exclusion process）纠缠动力学近期工作之上，并讨论了 OSEE 与海森堡绘景（Heisenberg picture）中非高斯性度量（non-gaussianity measures）关联的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算符空间纠缠熵（OSEE）是刻画算符纠缠的重要量，但其在随机高斯动力学下的精确含时行为尚缺乏解析刻画。该工作旨在利用可解模型与随机矩阵理论（RMT）工具，填补这一空白，并为晚时 Page 曲线提供解析描述。

Method: 以 Brownian SYK$_2$ 模型建模初始乘积算符的随机高斯演化，利用到畴壁熔化（domain-wall melting）问题的映射，并结合随机矩阵理论工具，包括库仑气体方法（Coulomb-gas approach）与自由 Jacobi 过程（free Jacobi process）的映射。

Result: 在热力学极限下获得含时二分 OSEE 的精确表达式，并对晚时出现的相应高斯 Page 曲线给出解析刻画。该结果依托于全连接量子简单排斥过程纠缠动力学的最新进展。

Conclusion: 基于所得结果，论文讨论了将 OSEE 与海森堡绘景（Heisenberg picture）中非高斯性度量（non-gaussianity measures）建立关联的可能性，为算符纠缠与高斯性刻画之间的联系提供了探索方向。

Abstract: We study the von Neumann operator-space entanglement entropy (OSEE) of initial product operators evolved under random gaussian dynamics, as modeled by a Brownian SYK$_2$ model. By exploiting a mapping to a domain-wall melting problem, we derive an exact expression for the time-dependent bipartite OSEE in the thermodynamic limit, and obtain an analytic characterization of the corresponding gaussian Page curve emerging at late times. Our results build on recent work on the entanglement dynamics in the all-to-all quantum simple exclusion process, leveraging tools from RMT, including the Coulomb-gas approach and a mapping to the free Jacobi process. Finally, based on our results, we discuss the possibility of relating the OSEE with non-gaussianity measures in the Heisenberg picture.

</details>


### [84] [Operational Limits of Gravity-Mediated Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22097)
*Atta ur Rahman,Muhammad Noman,Ying-Jian Zhu,Jia-Xin Peng,Saeed Haddadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在双分支引力猫态（gravitational cat-state）模型下引入高斯波包有限宽度与空间关联退相干（dephasing），考察引力介导纠缠（gravity-mediated entanglement）是否具备可操作价值。结果显示，Bell非局域性与隐形传态可用性仅存在于有限参数窗口，且窗口随波包展宽或退相干增强而收缩；残余静电力可相干抵消引力非局域相位并显著压制信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力介导纠缠实验旨在检验空间叠加态中的两个大质量物体能否通过相互引力作用产生纠缠。理想模型常将物体视为点状分支，但真实波包具有有限宽度，并会遭受退相干与残余非引力作用，因此需评估这些现实效应的影响。

Method: 构建含高斯波包（Gaussian wave packets）的双分支引力猫态模型，对牛顿相互作用在有限波包宽度上做平均，并以唯象方式引入空间关联退相干。随后通过Bell不等式违背、隐形传态保真度（teleportation fidelity）、相干信息（coherent information）、Fisher信息与态可区分性（state distinguishability）等指标评估生成态的可操作性。

Result: 存在有限参数窗口，使同一量子态同时具备Bell非局域性（Bell-nonlocal）与隐形传态可用性，但这些窗口会随波包变宽或退相干增强而收缩。此外，残余静电力分支相互作用可相干抵消引力非局域相位，并强烈压制可操作信号。

Conclusion: 研究给出了有限波包宽度与静电干扰的容限边界（tolerance boundaries），这些边界应在未来引力介导纠缠实验的设计与判据中予以考虑。

Abstract: Tests of gravity-mediated entanglement aim to determine whether two massive objects in spatial superposition can become entangled through their mutual gravitational interaction. In an ideal model, the two objects are often treated as pointlike branches, but real wave packets have a finite width and are also exposed to dephasing and residual non-gravitational forces. We study these effects in a two-branch gravitational cat-state model with Gaussian wave packets. The Newtonian interaction is averaged over the finite wave-packet width, and spatially correlated dephasing is included phenomenologically. We then ask a practical question: when does the generated state remain useful rather than merely entangled? To answer this, we examine Bell violation, teleportation fidelity, coherent information, Fisher information, and state distinguishability. We find finite parameter windows where the same state is Bell-nonlocal and teleportation-useful, but these windows shrink as the wave packets become broader or the dephasing becomes stronger. We also show that a residual electrostatic branch interaction can coherently cancel the gravitational nonlocal phase and strongly suppress the operational signal. The results identify finite-width and electrostatic tolerance boundaries that should be considered in future tests of gravity-mediated entanglement.

</details>


### [85] [Classical Capacity of Single-Mode Bosonic Gaussian Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22308)
*Gerardo Adesso,Ludovico Lami,Vittorio Giovannetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作证明：对所有单模玻色高斯信道（single-mode bosonic Gaussian channel）及任意输入能量，能量约束下的 Holevo 信息具有可加性且等于其高斯对应量，因而独立制备的高斯态（含高斯随机位移）即可达到经典容量；并进一步刻画了超容量通信的误差指数与速率—误差权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声光信道的经典容量（classical capacity）决定信息可靠传输的最大速率。对于光纤与自由空间链路中普遍存在的玻色高斯信道，需判定信道多次使用间的纠缠（entanglement）或非高斯信号（non-Gaussian signals）能否超越高斯编码，即能量约束 Holevo 信息是否可加并等于其高斯对应量。

Method: 证明将纠缠不等式（entanglement inequality）与输出熵（output entropy）的紧致可加下界相联系，据此分析各输入能量下的单模玻色高斯信道；并分别考察严格总光子数截断与仅约束平均光子数两种情形下的译码性能。

Result: 上述基本问题均获肯定回答：独立制备、具高斯分布随机位移的高斯态可达容量，包括压缩（squeezing）与位移编码相竞争的低能区。严格总光子数截断下，正确译码概率随信道使用次数指数衰减，并给出衰减指数的显式界；仅约束平均光子数时，能量重标度（energy-rescaling）公式给出完整渐近速率—误差权衡。

Conclusion: 能量约束 Holevo 信息对单模玻色高斯信道可加且等于高斯值，高斯编码已达最优；平均光子数 N、允许平均译码误差 ε 下的容量等于平均光子数 N/(1−ε) 时的零误差容量。该结果确定了若干最基本光通信模型的经典通信极限。

Abstract: The classical capacity of a noisy optical channel is the maximum rate at which information can be transmitted reliably, given a constraint on the mean input energy. For the ubiquitous bosonic Gaussian channels, which include standard models of loss and noise in fibre and free-space optical links, determining this rate requires addressing whether entanglement between channel uses or non-Gaussian signals can outperform Gaussian encodings. More precisely, the question is whether the energy-constrained Holevo information is additive across arbitrarily many channel uses and coincides with its Gaussian counterpart. We answer these fundamental questions in the affirmative for every single-mode bosonic Gaussian channel at every input energy. Namely, we prove that independently prepared Gaussian states with Gaussian-distributed random displacements achieve the capacity, including in the low-energy regime where squeezing the signals competes with encoding information in their displacement. Our proof connects an entanglement inequality to a sharp, additive lower bound on output entropy. We then characterise communication above capacity: under a strict total photon-number cutoff, the probability of correct decoding decreases exponentially with the number of channel uses, and we derive explicit bounds on the decay exponent. When only the mean photon number is constrained, a simple energy-rescaling formula determines exactly the entire asymptotic rate--error trade-off: the capacity at mean photon number $N$ and allowed average decoding error $ε$ equals the vanishing-error capacity at mean photon number $N/(1-ε)$. Together, these results identify the ultimate limits of classical communication for some of the most fundamental models of optical communication.

</details>


### [86] [Quantum Work Statistics from Entanglement-Assisted Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20528)
*Akira Sone,Akram Touil,Emery Doucet,Sebastian Deffner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文论证在纠缠辅助不变性（envariance）这一纠缠基本对称性下，单次测量方案比标准两次测量方案更自洽；单次测量所得功统计量（work statistics）可由envariance自然导出，而两次测量方案的对应识别仅对Haar测度零集成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功统计量（work statistics）的定义长期存在分歧，尤以单次测量与两次测量方案之争，且涉及测量的能量代价（energetic cost of measurement）所带来的模糊性。本文试图以纠缠的基本对称性——纠缠辅助不变性（envariance）——作为判据，为方案选择提供非测量公设的物理依据。

Method: 采用基于纠缠辅助不变性（envariance）的理论论证方式，对任意驱动幺正演化（driving unitary）考察功统计量能否无需显式测量公设（measurement postulate）而从envariance直接导出，并比较单次测量方案与两次测量方案在该对称性下的适用性；论证中对两次测量方案涉及幺正集合的Haar测度（Haar measure）性质进行分析。

Result: 单次测量方案下的功统计量对任意驱动幺正都能作为envariance的直接推论自然出现，且不依赖显式测量公设；两次测量方案要达到类似识别，仅能对Haar测度为零的幺正集合实现。据此，与测量的能量代价相关的功统计量歧义得以消除。

Conclusion: 以envariance为基础的表述确立了单次测量方案在功统计量定义上的优先性与自洽性，并消解了由测量能量代价引发的歧义，为量子热力学中功统计量的规范定义提供了对称性层面的判据。

Abstract: We demonstrate that the one-time measurement scheme is more consistent than the standard two-time measurement scheme with respect to the fundamental symmetry of entanglement, namely entanglement-assisted invariance (envariance). In particular, the work statistics obtained from the one-time measurement scheme arise naturally as a direct consequence of envariance for any driving unitary, without involving an explicit measurement postulate, whereas the analogous identification for the two-time measurement scheme is possible only for a Haar-measure-zero set of unitaries. This envariance-based formulation eliminates the ambiguity in work statistics related to the energetic cost of measurement.

</details>


### [87] [The noise cost of reservoir locality in broadband quantum amplifier chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20654)
*Chengxi Li,Hao Zhu,Tie-Fu Zhang,Lin Zhuang,Wanzi Sun,Wuming Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究有限带宽（finite-band）定向量子放大器（directional quantum amplifier）中由库噪声（reservoir noise）限制的灵敏度问题，通过构造邻近格点损耗通道并与固定库方案对比，给出噪声下界与设计约束，面向低温微波阵列（cryogenic microwave array）的库设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定向量子放大器可实现弱信号读取并保护信号源，但库噪声限制了灵敏度；有限带宽抑制需在频率依赖响应与固定局域耦合、有限速率之间取得折衷。研究动机在于建立噪声下界基准并据此指导具体链结构的库设计。

Method: 构造邻近格点损耗通道（adjacent-site loss channels），在局域兼容性（local compatibility）条件下达到已知单频最小值，作为无上限基准。针对均匀链（uniform chain）与二聚化链（dimerized chain），在匹配漂移、端口、高增益带宽与完整增益/损耗速率上限条件下比较固定库。采用边界响应界（boundary-response bounds）与有限辅助（finite-auxiliary）计算进行分析。

Result: 在代表性上限 R=8、半宽 Ω=0.05 下，均匀链中最近邻算术带宽平均噪声比无限制最优值高出超过 1.01265 个量子（quanta），对所有有限 N≥64 成立。边界响应界给出必要速率及需要作用范围二（range two）的目标。在 N=64 时，匹配总耦合预算下有限辅助计算仍保持噪声优势；响应界量化了噪声-分辨率容差。

Conclusion: 理论结果为所提出的 100 kHz 设计带宽低温微波阵列的库设计与噪声定标（noise calibration）提供指导，并给出带宽内固定库方案的噪声下界及所需耦合范围条件。

Abstract: Directional quantum amplifiers enable weak-signal readout while protecting the source, but reservoir noise limits sensitivity. Finite-band suppression must reconcile frequency-dependent responses with fixed local couplings and finite rates. We construct adjacent-site loss channels attaining the known single-frequency minimum under local compatibility conditions, as an uncapped benchmark. For specified uniform and dimerized chains, we compare fixed reservoirs at matched drift, ports, high-gain band and complete gain/loss rate caps. At the representative cap $R=8$ and halfwidth $Ω=0.05$, nearest-neighbor arithmetic band average noise exceeds the unrestricted optimum by more than $1.01265$ quanta in the uniform chain for every finite $N\ge64$. Boundary-response bounds give necessary rates and targets requiring range two. At $N=64$, finite-auxiliary calculations retain a noise advantage under matched total coupling budgets; response bounds quantify noise-resolution tolerances. These theoretical results guide reservoir design and noise calibration for a proposed cryogenic microwave array with a $100$ kHz design band.

</details>


### [88] [Surviving readout errors and incomplete postselection in monitored quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20712)
*Murtaza Ali Mir,Teemu Ojanen,Ali G. Moghaddam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究读出误差（readout errors）对监测量子电路（monitored quantum circuits）动力学的影响，指出误差使后选择（postselection）不完全。在 U(1) 对称电路中，电荷锐化转变（charge-sharpening transition）在有限误差率下依然存在，但锐相（sharp phase）的涨落变为有限稳态平台。通过将涨落分解为量子与经典贡献，可用作最小化的误差缓解框架并从实验数据定位该转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监测量子电路中的后选择通常假设测量记录真实无误，但实际实验中的读出误差会使条件化基于不完美的记录而非真实记录。作者希望探究此类误差如何影响监测动力学，尤其是电荷锐化转变是否在噪声环境下仍然存续。

Method: 聚焦 U(1) 对称电路（U(1)-symmetric circuits），引入有限读出误差率并分析电荷涨落随测量率的变化。将涨落分解为量子贡献与经典贡献，据此考察测量诱导纯化（measurement-induced purification）与噪声诱导混合（noise-induced mixing）之间的竞争，并对比中途测量（mid-circuit measurements）与电路末端测量（end-of-circuit measurements）受误差影响的差异。

Result: 电荷锐化转变在有限误差率下存活，锐相原本消失的涨落被有限稳态平台取代。电荷涨落作为测量率的函数在理想转变点附近呈现浅极小值，标志测量诱导纯化与噪声诱导混合的竞争。涨落分解显示该极小值对应不完全后选择仍保留足够轨迹记忆、从而削弱噪声诱导混合的位置。

Conclusion: 电荷锐化在读出误差下以定量且实验可及的方式存续。中途测量比标准末端测量更难缓解读出误差，因为误差直接破坏后选择本身；涨落的量子-经典分解提供了最小化的缓解框架，并可利用实验数据直接定位该转变。

Abstract: We study how readout errors affect dynamics in monitored quantum circuits. Such errors make postselection effectively incomplete, conditioning trajectories on imperfect rather than true records. Focusing on U(1)-symmetric circuits, we find that the charge-sharpening transition survives at a finite error rate, with the sharp phase's vanishing fluctuations replaced by a finite steady-state plateau. The charge fluctuations show a shallow minimum near the ideal transition point as a function of measurement rate, signaling the competition between measurement-induced purification and noise-induced mixing. By decomposing the fluctuations into quantum and classical contributions, we show the minimum marks where incomplete postselection still retains enough trajectory memory to attenuate noise-induced mixing, leaving a detectable signature of the transition even in a noisy setting. Mid-circuit measurements are far harder to mitigate against readout errors than standard end-of-circuit ones, because the errors corrupt the postselection itself; the decomposition therefore offers a minimal mitigation framework and a route to locate the transition directly from experimental data. Our results show that charge sharpening survives readout errors in a quantitative and experimentally accessible way.

</details>


### [89] [Standard and Bipolar Rectification in Tilted Periodically-Driven Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20765)
*Joaquín Peñuela-Parra,Juan José Mendoza-Arenas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在倾斜势（tilted potential）与局域时间周期驱动（time-periodic drive）共同作用的费米子（fermionic）体系中，建立了粒子流与能量流的整流（rectification）机制，并揭示相互作用可使双极整流区间扩大及整流系数增强数個数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关联量子体系（correlated quantum systems）中实现输运（transport）控制对量子电路技术发展至关重要；本文旨在为耦合外部热浴的费米子系统提供一种可行的粒子与能量整流机制。

Method: 体系为耦合外部热浴的费米子系统，处于受时间周期驱动局域调制的倾斜势中；通过 Floquet 准能谱（Floquet quasienergy spectrum）分析驱动诱导电流的共振增强，并以整流系数 R 量化沿倾斜上行（正偏压）与下行（反偏压）输运的不对称性。

Result: 驱动可在无化学势或热偏压时产生粒子流与能量流；在 Floquet 准能谱出现避免交叉（avoided crossings）的共振频率处电流增强。引入偏压后，驱动诱导贡献可与偏压诱导贡献叠加、竞争甚至抵消。除标准整流外，还发现双极整流（bipolar rectification）区间——电流仅沿倾斜上行方向流动，不受偏压极性影响；两类区间边界处实现完全整流。存在粒子间相互作用时，支持双极整流的参数区域扩大，R 可增强多个数量级。

Conclusion: 本文提出的粒子与能量输运整流机制可在凝聚态（condensed matter）与冷原子（cold atom）体系中实验实现。

Abstract: Developing mechanisms to control transport in correlated quantum systems is essential for advancing quantum circuit technologies. In this work, we establish a rectification mechanism for both particle and energy flow in a fermionic system coupled to external baths, in the presence of a tilted potential modulated locally by a time-periodic drive. The drive generates particle and energy currents even in the absence of chemical or thermal bias between the baths. These drive-induced currents are enhanced at resonant frequencies at which avoided crossings occur in the Floquet quasienergy spectrum, reflecting the role of the Floquet states as intermediate channels for transport. Once a bias between the baths is introduced, the drive-induced current contribution can reinforce, compete with, or even cancel the bias-induced contribution. These effects enable rectification in the non-interacting regime, which we quantify via the rectification coefficient $\mathcal{R}$. This coefficient measures the asymmetry between transport up the tilt (forward bias) and down the tilt (reverse bias). In addition to standard rectification, where transport is reduced under reverse bias, we identify a regime of bipolar rectification, in which the current flows only up the tilt regardless of the bias polarity. At the boundary between standard and bipolar regimes, the system achieves perfect rectification, corresponding to complete suppression of the reverse-bias current. In the presence of interactions between particles, the parameter region that supports bipolar rectification is enlarged and $\mathcal{R}$ can be enhanced by many orders of magnitude. Our results establish a rectification mechanism for particle and energy transport that can be experimentally realized in condensed matter and cold atom systems.

</details>


### [90] [monoprop: Scaling Pauli and Majorana propagation via Anticommutation Indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21000)
*Aaron Miller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: monoprop 是一个面向费米子与量子比特基的开源大规模并行算符传播（operator-propagation）框架，通过引入反交换索引（anticommutation index）将单门操作代价从 O(KN) 降至 O(K/N)，并在 8192 核上追踪 9.5×10^10 个算符项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码将 K 个被追踪算符项（泡利串（Pauli strings）或马约拉纳算符（Majorana operators）乘积）表示为 2N 位字符串，每个门需以 O(KN) 代价逐一检验反交换性，成为大规模算符传播的效率瓶颈。

Method: 引入反交换索引以快速查找与门反交换的项；利用局部门仅影响其作用位点相关项、且截断后仅约 1/N 的项触及任一比特或模的性质，理论证明单门时间随 O(K/N) 标度。此外提出内存高效的代理图（surrogate graphs），记录一次传播并在新角度重放，用于能量重估与参数梯度；并给出寻找能量最小化门及自适应量子化学线路最优角度的有效方法。

Result: 数值实验验证了 O(K/N) 的标度行为，表明在固定项数下增加位点数可提升速度；monoprop 在 8192 核上近乎理想扩展（scales almost ideally），可追踪 9.5×10^10 个算符项；代理图支持快速能量重估与梯度计算。

Conclusion: monoprop 通过反交换索引与并行化显著降低算符传播的单门开销，并借助代理图与最优门/角度搜索方法，为自适应量子化学线路模拟提供可扩展的高效工具。

Abstract: We introduce monoprop, an open-source, massively parallel operator-propagation framework for fermion and qubit bases. For $N$ qubits or modes, existing codes represent each of the $K$ tracked operator terms (Pauli strings or products of Majorana operators) as a $2N$-bit string, testing each for anticommutation with the gate at $O(KN)$ cost per gate. Here, we introduce the anticommutation index which rapidly looks up the terms anticommuting with a gate. A local gate changes only the terms that touch its sites, and when terms are truncated to act on few sites, only a fraction $\sim1/N$ touch any given qubit or mode, and we use this to prove the time per gate scales as $O(K/N)$. This counterintuitive result tells us that for a fixed number of terms, increasing the number of sites can make things faster. We demonstrate this numerically, and show monoprop scales almost ideally to 8192 cores, tracking $9.5\times10^{10}$ terms. Beyond propagation, we present memory-efficient surrogate graphs, which record one propagation and replay it at new angles, for fast energy re-estimation and parameter gradients, and an efficient method for finding energy-minimising gates and optimal angles for simulating adaptive quantum-chemistry circuits.

</details>


### [91] [Casimir forces on suspended thin dielectric membranes covered with graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21437)
*Tze Ue Ngai,Zuocheng Zhang,Q. Zhang,Youssef Jeyar,Jingwei Li,Zhengtang Luo,Mauro Antezza,H. B. Chan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论与实验研究覆盖石墨烯（graphene）的薄悬浮介电层上的卡西米尔力（Casimir force），提出以薄介电膜替代块体衬底可部分恢复石墨烯的热依赖性，从而在小间距下获得显著的温度相关力变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯的卡西米尔力热效应在远小于普通金属与介电材料的间距处出现，此时力足够强可被实验测量；但支撑石墨烯的衬底会显著削弱这种增强效应，因此需寻找消除衬底影响的方案。

Method: 理论方面，将一个相互作用物体设为固体金板，另一物体为覆盖石墨烯的悬浮介电膜（15 nm 氧化硅或 13 nm 氮化硅），计算温度从 10 K 升至 294 K 时的卡西米尔压强变化；实验方面，在室温下对悬浮氮化硅膜上的石墨烯进行测量，以连接悬臂的金镀球作为力传感器。

Result: 在约 500 nm 间距处，理论预测氧化硅与氮化硅体系在升温过程中的卡西米尔压强变化分别可达 54% 与 40%；实验测得约化力梯度（force gradient）与理论预测一致，但受氮化硅光学性质不确定度影响，未能分辨热依赖性。

Conclusion: 用覆盖石墨烯的薄介电膜替代块体衬底可显著降低卡西米尔力，有助于中性纳米机械元件（neutral nanomechanical elements）在小间距下的稳定运行。

Abstract: We investigate the Casimir force on thin suspended dielectric layers covered with graphene, both theoretically and experimentally. Thermal effects of the Casimir force on graphene occur at separations significantly smaller compared to ordinary metals and dielectrics. At such small separations, the Casimir force is sufficiently strong to be measured experimentally. However, the presence of substrates to support the graphene significantly reduces the enhancement. We show theoretically that replacing the solid substrate beneath graphene with a thin dielectric membrane partially restores this strong thermal dependence. One of the interacting objects is a solid gold plate. The other object is a suspended dielectric membrane made of either 15 nm of silicon oxide or 13 nm silicon nitride, covered with graphene. At a separation of $\sim$500 nm, theoretical predictions yield changes in the Casimir pressure of up to 54 % and 40 % for silicon oxide and silicon nitride, respectively, when the temperature is increased from 10 K to 294 K. Measurements were performed for graphene on a suspended silicon nitride membrane at room temperature, using a gold-coated sphere attached to a cantilever as the force sensor. The measured force gradient is consistent with the theoretical prediction, although the thermal dependence could not be resolved, in part because of uncertainties in the optical properties of silicon nitride. Replacing a bulk substrate with a thin dielectric membrane covered with graphene provides a substantial reduction of the Casimir force, which may facilitate the stable operation of neutral nanomechanical elements at small separations.

</details>


### [92] [Quantum instanton approach to metastable collective spin systems: Numerical implementation and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21684)
*Maciej Chudak,Massimiliano Esposito,Krzysztof Ptaszynski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了基于精确准概率动力学（quasiprobability dynamics）的量子瞬子（quantum instanton）框架的数值实现方法，并将其应用于两个微观动机明确的集体自旋（collective spin）系统，成功预测了亚稳态间的切换率、一级耗散相变（first-order dissipative phase transition）点及慢弛豫率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动-耗散集体自旋系统（driven-dissipative collective spin systems）可存在多个长寿亚稳态，其间罕见切换主导慢弛豫与一级耗散相变。伴随论文已提出量子瞬子框架并仅在具简化对称性的最小模型中示意，亟需可推广的数值实现与在真实物理模型中的验证。

Method: 将量子瞬子框架进行数值实现，结合配置法（collocation）与打靶法（shooting）求解精确准概率动力学；应用于Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick模型（纯集体耗散）与协作共振荧光（cooperative resonance fluorescence，含集体与局域耗散）两个模型；并考察不同准概率表示对数值收敛的影响，与基于高斯截断的半经典Wigner方法进行比较。

Result: 通过确定亚稳吸引子间切换率的领头指数标度，预测了稳态磁化、一级耗散相变点与慢弛豫率的指数标度。预测与量子主方程（quantum master equation）计算一致，并在第二个模型无退相干情形下与精确解析结果吻合。半经典Wigner方法在不同模型与参数区间的精度存在差异。

Conclusion: 该数值框架可适用于同时含集体与局域耗散的实验相关集体自旋系统，支持了框架的可推广性与实用价值，同时表明准概率表示的选择显著影响数值收敛性。

Abstract: Driven-dissipative collective spin systems can host several long-lived metastable states; rare switching between them governs slow relaxation and first-order dissipative phase transitions associated with changes in the dominant stationary state. In the companion Letter [arXiv:2604.15091], we introduced a quantum instanton framework based on exact quasiprobability dynamics to describe this switching and illustrated it with a minimal model possessing a simplifying symmetry. Here we develop its numerical implementation, combining collocation and shooting methods, and apply it to two microscopically motivated systems: the Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick model with purely collective dissipation and the cooperative resonance fluorescence with collective and local dissipation. By determining the leading exponential scaling of switching rates between metastable attractors, we predict the stationary magnetization, the first-order dissipative phase-transition point, and the exponential scaling of slow relaxation rates. These predictions agree with quantum master-equation calculations and an exact analytical result for the second model without dephasing. We also examine how the choice of quasiprobability representation affects numerical convergence and find that the alternative semiclassical Wigner approach, based on Gaussian truncation, has varying accuracy across models and parameter regimes. These results substantiate the framework's applicability to experimentally relevant collective spin systems with both collective and local dissipation.

</details>


### [93] [Gyrators for superconducting circuit design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22145)
*Ciro Salcedo,Sasha Cocquyt,Andrew Osborne,Andrew A. Houck*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种磁通-电荷对称的哈密顿（Hamiltonian）形式化框架，仅依赖基尔霍夫定律（Kirchhoff's laws）、能量守恒与守恒关系形式，对含回转器（gyrator）、电感与电容的非线性电路（LCG circuits）给出可辛约化（symplectic reduction）的描述，并引入非线性非互易电路的电路对偶（circuit duality）与保持哈密顿谱的点变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未显式说明研究背景与痛点，仅从"仅假设可解性"这一限定表述看，动机在于以最小假设建立普适的电路哈密顿描述，并将Tellegen关于回转器的经典结论推广至非线性互易元件（如约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）与量子相位滑移（quantum phase slip））。

Method: 在仅假设基尔霍夫定律可解、能量守恒以及本构关系（constitutive relations）形式的前提下，构建磁通-电荷对称的哈密顿形式化；引入非线性非互易电路上的电路对偶概念，以及一族可插入、移除并在回转器两侧移动互易元件且保持哈密顿谱不变的点变换（point transformations）。

Result: 该形式化对非奇异、适定（well-posed）且含任意非线性LCG电路始终可进行辛约化；借助点变换将Tellegen的经典结果——回转器使互易元件表现为其对偶——推广到非线性互易元件；并给出非互易电路可变换为动力学等价互易电路的条件。

Conclusion: 磁通-电荷对称的哈密顿框架为非线性LCG电路提供了统一的辛约化与对偶处理方式，其点变换族在保持谱的前提下连接了互易与非互易电路，并给出非互易电路可等效为互易电路的条件。

Abstract: Assuming only the solubility of Kirchhoff's laws, the conservation of energy, and the form of the constitutive relations, we present a flux-charge symmetric Hamiltonian formalism that always admits a symplectic reduction for nonsingular, well-posed circuits involving gyrators, inductors, and capacitors (LCG circuits) with arbitrary non-linearity. The formalism introduces a notion of circuit duality on non-linear non-reciprocal circuits and a family of point transformations that inserts, removes, and moves reciprocal elements across gyrators while preserving the Hamiltonian spectrum. With these transformations, we extend Tellegen's classic result that gyrators make reciprocal elements behave as their dual to non-linear reciprocal elements such as the Josephson junction and quantum phase slip. Furthermore, we present conditions under which non-reciprocal circuits can be transformed to dynamically equivalent reciprocal circuits.

</details>

### [94] [Anti-successors and anti-replicators, Manin products and Koszul duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21365)
*Chengming Bai,Li Guo,Yunhe Sheng,You Wang*

Main category: math.RA

TL;DR: 该文建立二元操作元（binary operads）的反分裂（anti-splitting）与反复制（anti-replication）操作元理论：定义反双后继与反三后继，使原操作可“分裂为负的二元或三元运算之和”，并在二次情形下等同于 Manin 黑积（Manin black product）；对偶地给出反复制子并以此计算 Manin 白积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有若干具体的“反”结构（如 anti-dendriform、anti-pre-Lie、anti-pre-Poisson）散见于文献，但缺乏统一的操作元框架。作者希望在操作元层面系统刻画“分裂为负和”这一现象，并厘清其与 Manin 积等经典构造的关系。

Method: 对二元操作元（binary operad）\opd 定义反双后继 \ABSu(\opd) 与反三后继 \ATSu(\opd)，构造典范操作元态射 \opd→\ABSu(\opd)、\opd→\ATSu(\opd)；对二元二次操作元（binary quadratic operad）识别其为与 anti-pre-Lie、anti-post-Lie 操作元的 Manin 黑积。对偶地引入反复制子 \ADup 与反三复制子 \ATrip，并证明与 Koszul 对偶的交换关系。

Result: 得到同构 \ABSu(\opd)≅anti-preLie • \opd、\ATSu(\opd)≅anti-postLie • \opd（• 为 Manin 黑积）；对偶关系为 \ADup(\opd^!)≅\ABSu(\opd)^! 与 \ATrip(\opd^!)≅\ATSu(\opd)^!。由此给出用反复制子计算 Manin 白积（Manin white product）的新途径，并统一恢复 anti-dendriform、anti-pre-Lie、anti-pre-Poisson 等已知反分裂结构。

Conclusion: 反分裂与反复制可由操作元语言统一描述，并与 Manin 黑积、白积及 Koszul 对偶相容；该框架在恢复既有反分裂的同时产生新例子与对偶对。

Abstract: We develop an operadic theory of anti-splitting and anti-replication for binary operads. For a binary operad $\opd$, we define its anti-bisuccessor $\ABSu(\opd)$ and anti-trisuccessor $\ATSu(\opd)$, with the property that an operation of $\opd$ splits into a negative sum of two or three operations, yielding canonical operad morphisms $\opd \to\ABSu(\opd)$ and $\opd\to\ATSu(\opd)$. For binary quadratic operads, the process gives Manin black products, \[ \ABSu(\opd) \cong \antiprelie \bullet \opd,
  \qquad
  \ATSu(\opd)\cong \antipostlie\bullet\opd. \] Dually, we introduce the anti-duplicator \(\ADup(\mathcal P)\) and anti-triplicator \(\ATrip(\mathcal P)\) and prove \[
  \ADup(\opd^!)\cong \ABSu(\opd)^!,
  \qquad
  \ATrip(\opd^!)\cong \ATSu(\opd)^!. \] Consequently, the anti-duplicator gives a new way to calculate Manin white products. %, in particular, $\ADup(\opd)\cong \antiperm\circ\opd$. The framework recovers known anti-splittings, including anti-dendriform, anti-pre-Lie, and anti-pre-Poisson structures, in a uniform operadic form and produces further examples and dual pairs.

</details>
