### [95] [All-temperature superfluidity in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20717)
*Bilal Hawashin,Soumyadeep Chaudhuri,Igor F. Herbut,Michael M. Scherer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在短程相互作用局域理论中，二维非零温下连续对称性不能自发破缺，但U(1)模型可存在BKT相。本文利用大N展开研究(2+1)维量子场论，发现复数标量与临界O(N)模型耦合时，在biconical不动点处BKT相及超流性可延续至任意高温。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BKT相通常因高温涡旋增殖而被破坏，本文旨在探究BKT相与超流性是否能够突破这一高温限制，延续至任意高温。

Method: 采用大N展开（large-N expansion），研究(2+1)维量子场论，其中复数标量耦合至临界O(N)模型。在biconical不动点（区间耦合为负）处分析热有效势，并对有能隙涨落积分以获得大尺度下紧致相位模式的有效理论。

Result: 在biconical不动点处热有效势存在具有非零U(1)振幅的极小值圆环；积分掉有能隙涨落后得到紧致相位模式的有效理论。因不动点处温度是唯一标度，相位刚度随温度线性增长，比例系数随N线性标度并由不动点完全确定，从而使涡旋算符在N足够大时变为无关；涡旋逸度随N指数减小。

Conclusion: BKT相与超流性可在任意高温下持续存在，其关键在于不动点处相位刚度随温度线性增长，使涡旋算符无关且涡旋逸度随N指数压低。

Abstract: In local theories with short-range interactions, continuous symmetries cannot be spontaneously broken in two spatial dimensions at nonzero temperature. $\mathrm{U}(1)$-symmetric models instead admit a Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) phase with quasi-long-range order and nonzero phase stiffness, which is normally destroyed at high temperatures by vortex proliferation. Here, we show that a BKT phase, and with it superfluidity, can persist to arbitrarily high temperatures. Using the large-$N$ expansion, we study a $(2+1)$-dimensional quantum field theory in which a complex scalar is coupled to the critical $\mathrm{O}(N)$ model. At the biconical fixed point, where the inter-sector coupling is negative, the thermal effective potential has a circle of minima with nonzero $\mathrm{U}(1)$ amplitude. Integrating out the gapped fluctuations yields an effective theory for the compact phase mode at distances large compared to $1/T$. Since temperature is the only scale at the fixed point, the phase stiffness grows linearly with temperature, with a proportionality factor that scales linearly with $N$ and is fixed entirely by the fixed point. This renders vortex operators irrelevant for sufficiently large $N$. We further show that the vortex fugacity is exponentially small in $N$.

</details>


### [96] [Quantum oscillations in a solvable model of a Kondo insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20734)
*Abhisek Samanta,Mohit Randeria,Sumilan Banerjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究近藤绝缘体（Kondo insulator）中相互作用对低能态密度（SdH）与磁化强度（dHvA）量子振荡（QOs）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究强相互作用如何影响近藤绝缘体的量子振荡行为，特别是相互作用强度远超杂化能隙（hybridization-gap）绝缘体非相互作用带隙时的情形。

Method: 引入周期性安德森模型（periodic Anderson model）的推广形式，在每个格点加入Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）相互作用，该模型在大N极限（N为重f电子味数）下可精确求解，并采用解析与数值方法分析。

Result: 解析与数值结果均表明，即使相互作用强度远大于非相互作用带隙，绝缘态在谱、热力学与输运层面于低温下仍保持稳定。推导出相互作用绝缘体的Lifshitz-Kosevich（LK）公式推广形式，SdH与dHvA各有不同。SdH振荡频率F₀±δF可被相互作用弱重整化，而dHvA频率F₀不被重整化，与非相互作用未杂化能带相同；dHvA振幅基本不受相互作用影响，SdH振幅则因能隙重整化而显著增强。

Conclusion: 该模型揭示了相互作用绝缘体中量子振荡的差异化行为：dHvA振荡对相互作用不敏感，而SdH振荡频率被弱重整化且振幅显著增强，为区分两类量子振荡的相互作用效应提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study the effects of interactions on the low-energy density of states (SdH) and magnetization (dHvA) quantum oscillations (QOs) in a Kondo insulator. Toward this end we introduce a generalization of the periodic Anderson model with Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) interactions at each site that is exactly solvable in the large-$N$ limit, where $N$ is the number of flavors of the heavy $f$ electrons. We show analytically and numerically that the insulating state remains stable in terms of spectrum, thermodynamics, and transport at low temperatures even for interaction strengths much larger than non-interacting band gap of the hybridization-gap insulator. We analytically derive generalizations of Lifshitz-Kosevich (LK) formulae, distinct for the SdH and dHvA QOs, for the interacting insulator. We find that the SdH oscillation frequencies, $F_0\pm δF$, corresponding to the conduction and valence band edges, can be weakly renormalized by interaction relative to their non-interacting values. In contrast, dHvA QO frequency $F_0$ is not renormalized by interactions, and remains the same as that for the non-interacting unhybridized bands. The QO amplitudes show a remarkable difference: the dHvA amplitude is essentially unaffected by interactions, while the SdH amplitude is strongly enhanced due to gap renormalization.

</details>


### [97] [Modified anisotropy and distinct phase diagrams in the nearly isotropic $S=1$ triangular-lattice antiferromagnet $\mathrm{Rb_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21154)
*Xuejuan Gui,Xu-Guang Zhou,Chen Zhang,Zhongcen Sun,Yuan Sun,Chenglin Shang,Akira Matsuo,Yasuhiro H. Matsuda,Koichi Kindo,Henrik M. Rønnow,Weiqiang Yu,Rong Yu,Peng Cheng,Juanjuan Liu,Jieming Sheng,Jinchen Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究报告了近各向同性 $S=1$ 三角晶格反铁磁体 $\mathrm{Rb_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$ 磁各向异性与相图的调控，发现其易轴各向异性与同构类似物 $\mathrm{K_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$ 的易面各向异性相反，并观察到磁场诱导的相变分裂与磁化平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角晶格反铁磁体是探索奇异量子相的重要平台，其中磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）起关键作用。作者通过对比同构类似物来揭示近各向同性体系中各向异性对场诱导磁相行为的敏感性。

Method: 对近各向同性 $S=1$ 三角晶格反铁磁体 $\mathrm{Rb_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$ 进行磁化率与磁化测量，并与同构类似物 $\mathrm{K_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$ 进行对比，构建两者的磁相图（magnetic phase diagram）。

Result: $\mathrm{Rb_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$ 的磁化率显示易轴各向异性（easy-axis anisotropy，$g_c > g_{ab}$），与 $\mathrm{K_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$ 的易面各向异性（easy-plane anisotropy）相反；零场下的单一 Néel 转变在约 10 T 处分裂为两个转变，并在 21 T 至 37 T 之间于面外（$H\parallel c$）与面内（$H\parallel ab$）方向均出现磁化平台（magnetization plateau）。

Conclusion: $\mathrm{Rb_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$ 与 $\mathrm{K_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$ 的磁相图存在显著差异，表明近各向同性三角晶格反铁磁体中磁场诱导的磁相行为对各向异性高度敏感。

Abstract: The triangular-lattice antiferromagnet serves as a benchmark platform for realizing exotic quantum phases, in which magnetic anisotropy plays an essential role. In this work, we report modified magnetic anisotropy and phase diagrams in the nearly isotropic $S=1$ triangular-lattice antiferromagnet $\mathrm{Rb_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$, compared with those of its isostructural analog $\mathrm{K_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$. The magnetic susceptibility of $\mathrm{Rb_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$ exhibits an easy-axis anisotropy with $g_c > g_{ab}$, opposite to the easy-plane anisotropy observed in $\mathrm{K_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$. The single Néel transition observed at zero field splits into two transitions at around 10~T, and a magnetization plateau is observed between 21~T and 37~T for both out-of-plane ($H\parallel c$) and in-plane ($H\parallel ab$) field directions in $\mathrm{Rb_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$. We construct magnetic phase diagrams that are distinct between $\mathrm{Rb_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$ and $\mathrm{K_2Ni(SeO_3)_2}$. These findings highlight the sensitivity of field-induced magnetic phase behavior to anisotropy in nearly isotropic triangular-lattice antiferromagnets.

</details>


### [98] [Plasmon Collapse in a Directional Strange Metal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21209)
*Jiade Li,Peiyi He,Deng Hu,Jiakai Wang,Jifeng Shao,Wan-Shun Du,Xuetao Zhu,Jiandong Guo,Zhiwei Wang,Peng Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究以范德华化合物（van der Waals compound）MoOCl₂ 为单一材料平台，通过高动量分辨非弹性电子散射（inelastic electron scattering, INS 类技术）与方向分辨输运（direction-resolved transport）测量，揭示电子相干性与电荷动力学在同平面内两个正交晶轴上的显著二分性：一轴为相干费米液体（Fermi liquid）行为并支持长寿命等离激元（plasmon），另一轴为奇异金属（strange metal）响应并呈现过阻尼激发，展宽为连续谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异金属（strange metals）广泛存在于强关联量子物质（strongly correlated quantum matter）中，并与高温超导（high-temperature superconductivity）和量子临界性（quantum criticality）密切相关，但其微观电荷动力学尚未解决。相干集体模式（collective modes）能否在非相干电子流体（incoherent electronic fluid）中存活，抑或退化为过阻尼连续谱（overdamped continua），仍是激烈争论的问题。

Method: 采用高动量分辨（high-momentum-resolution）非弹性电子散射，并结合方向分辨输运测量，对已知具有异常“优良”等离激元（plasmon）的“坏”金属（"bad" metal）MoOCl₂ 进行研究；并开展厚度依赖（thickness-dependent）测量以考察库仑屏蔽（Coulomb screening）变化的影响。

Result: 输运显示，沿一个面内晶轴呈相干费米液体（Fermi liquid）行为，而沿正交轴呈非相干奇异金属（strange metal）响应，且该二分性直接延伸至电荷响应：费米液体方向支持明确、长寿命的等离激元，奇异金属方向则表现为过阻尼激发，随动量增加逐渐丧失集体特征。超过临界动量（q_c ≈ 0.008 Å⁻¹）后，模式反转为负色散（negative dispersion），并最终坍缩为宽而近乎无特征的连续谱，具有稳健的高能边界（E_c ≈ 1.75 eV）。厚度依赖测量表明，尽管库仑屏蔽被显著改变，q_c 与 E_c 几乎不变，指示其为非相干电荷密度涨落（incoherent charge density fluctuations）的内禀尺度。

Conclusion: 结果确立了一个独特的单一材料平台，其中相干与非相干电子态被分割于面内正交通道；并表明非相干电子流体在准粒子（quasiparticle）范式之外仍保留内禀结构化的电荷动力学。

Abstract: Strange metals are widespread in strongly correlated quantum matter and closely intertwined with high-temperature superconductivity and quantum criticality, yet their microscopic charge dynamics remain fundamentally unresolved. Whether well-defined collective modes survive in an incoherent electronic fluid or dissolve into overdamped continua remains intensely debated. Here, employing high-momentum-resolution inelastic electron scattering complemented by direction-resolved transport, we show that the van der Waals compound $\mathrm{MoOCl}_2$, a "bad" metal known to host unusually "good" plasmons, exhibits a striking dichotomy in electronic coherence and charge dynamics. Transport reveals coherent Fermi liquid behavior along one in-plane crystallographic axis and an incoherent strange metal response along the orthogonal axis. This dichotomy extends directly to the charge response. The Fermi liquid direction supports a well-defined, long-lived plasmon, whereas the strange metal direction exhibits an overdamped excitation that progressively loses its collective character with increasing momentum. Beyond a critical momentum ($q_c$ $\approx$ 0.008 $\mathring{\mathrm{A}}^{-1}$), the mode undergoes a reversal into negative dispersion and ultimately collapses into a broad, nearly featureless continuum with a robust high-energy boundary ($E_c$ $\approx$ 1.75 eV). Remarkably, thickness-dependent measurements show that both $q_c$ and $E_c$ remain nearly unchanged despite substantial modifications of Coulomb screening, revealing them as intrinsic scales of incoherent charge density fluctuations. These results establish a unique single-material platform in which coherent and incoherent electronic states are partitioned into in-plane orthogonal channels, and demonstrate that incoherent electronic fluids retain intrinsically structured charge dynamics beyond the quasiparticle paradigm.

</details>


### [99] [Thermodynamic-limit bootstrap: Solving fractional quantum Hall ground states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21317)
*Qiang Gao,Taige Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种量子多体自举（quantum many-body bootstrap）方法，可直接在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit, TDL）下求解分数量子霍尔（fractional quantum Hall）基态，给出严格的能量下界并识别基态结构与拓扑序（topological order）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联基态通常只能先在有限体系（finite systems）中计算再外推至热力学极限（TDL），外推过程可能引入不确定性。作者希望开发一种能直接在 TDL 中求解基态的方法，从而同时严格确定能量与基态本身。

Method: 提出一种量子多体自举（quantum many-body bootstrap），直接求解 TDL 下的分数量子霍尔（fractional quantum Hall）基态，对基态能量给出严格且定量紧致的下界（lower bounds），并通过局域诊断（local diagnostics）识别基态结构与拓扑序（topological order）。

Result: 对 Laughlin 与 Pfaffian 父哈密顿量（parent Hamiltonians），方法几乎逐能级复现精确的配对关联函数（pair-correlation functions）与低能轨道纠缠谱（orbital entanglement spectrum, OES）。对库仑相互作用（Coulomb interactions），其严格 TDL 能量下界与精确对角化（exact diagonalization）及密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）外推结果高度吻合，局域诊断复现了 Laughlin 与 Moore–Read 的 OES 计数及复合费米液体（composite Fermi liquid）的 Friedel 振荡。在新体系中，自举方法在 ν=1/4 与 ν=12/5 处分别识别出复合费米液体与 RR̄₃ 拓扑序。

Conclusion: 该 TDL 自举（TDL bootstrap）可同等适用于阿贝尔与非阿贝尔（Abelian and non-Abelian）、有能隙与无能隙（gapped and gapless）量子霍尔态，提供一种直接在热力学极限中同时确定能量与量子态的通用基态求解器（ground-state solver）。

Abstract: Strongly interacting ground states are usually computed in finite systems and then extrapolated to the thermodynamic limit (TDL). Here we develop a quantum many-body bootstrap that solves fractional quantum Hall ground states directly in the TDL. The method gives rigorous and quantitatively tight lower bounds on the ground-state energy and identifies the ground-state structure and topological order. For Laughlin and Pfaffian parent Hamiltonians, it reproduces exact pair-correlation functions and low-lying orbital entanglement spectrum (OES) nearly level by level. For Coulomb interactions, the rigorous TDL energy bounds closely match thermodynamic estimates from exact diagonalization and density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) extrapolations, and the local diagnostics recover established Laughlin and Moore--Read OES counting and Friedel oscillations of the composite Fermi liquid. Beyond these benchmarks, the bootstrap resolves the thermodynamic ground states at $ν=1/4$ and $ν=12/5$, identifying a composite Fermi liquid and $\overline{\mathrm{RR}}_3$ topological order, respectively. The same framework applies equally well to Abelian and non-Abelian, gapped and gapless quantum Hall states. The TDL bootstrap therefore provides a general ground-state solver that determines both the energy and the state directly in the TDL.

</details>


### [100] [Non-equilibrium Muon Dynamics Imitating Fractional Spin Excitations in a Quantum Spin Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21523)
*A. Hromov,A. Zorko,M. Gomilšek,P. J. Baker,A. Prokofiev,M. Pregelj*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在自旋-1/2链化合物 AgCuVO₄ 中，μ子（muon）极化的高斯衰减对磁场异常不敏感，通常被解读为分数自旋激发的证据；但密度泛函理论（DFT）计算表明，该现象可归因于μ子在两个近邻位点间的量子跳跃与离域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维与阻挫磁体中的分数自旋激发（fractional spin excitations）常伴随零散自旋涨落（sporadic spin fluctuations），其μ子谱学证据（如对磁场不敏感的高斯衰减）被广泛采用，但此类信号是否必然源于本征自旋涨落尚存疑问。

Method: 采用μ子谱学（muon spectroscopy）测量 AgCuVO₄——一种具有竞争性链内相互作用的自旋-1/2链化合物——在其振幅调制（amplitude-modulated）磁基态下的自旋动力学；并结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算μ子停止位点及其四极能级交叉共振（quadrupolar level-crossing resonance）谱。

Result: μ子极化呈现高斯衰减，且在施加磁场下仍显著保持稳定。DFT 计算结果支持一个双态模型（two-state model）：μ子在两个近邻位点间随机跳跃，并在微秒时间尺度上通过量子离域化（quantum delocalization）在两站点间达到平衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为磁场下稳定的μ子极化提供了微观解释，并指出此类行为未必源自本征自旋涨落，也可能由非平衡μ子动力学（non-equilibrium muon dynamics）产生。这提示对分数自旋激发类μ子信号的解读需谨慎。

Abstract: Fractional spin excitations in low-dimensional and frustrated magnets are commonly associated with sporadic spin fluctuations, as observed in quantum spin liquids, spin ices, and other complex magnetic states. In this study, we investigate the spin dynamics in the amplitude-modulated magnetic ground state of AgCuVO$_4$, a spin-1/2 chain compound with competing intrachain interactions, by muon spectroscopy. Our data reveal a Gaussian decay of muon polarization that remains remarkably resilient against applied magnetic fields---a behavior often interpreted as evidence of emergent sporadic spin dynamics. However, density functional theory calculations of muon stopping sites and their quadrupolar level-crossing resonance spectra suggest an alternative mechanism: a two-state model where the muon randomly hops between two nearby sites, eventually equilibrating across both on a microsecond timescale via quantum delocalization. These findings provide a microscopic explanation for the field-resilient muon polarization and indicate that such behavior may not always stem from intrinsic spin fluctuations, but can also arise from non-equilibrium muon dynamics.

</details>


### [101] [Flat-band ferrimagnetism in the edge-centered honeycomb antiferromagnet: From harmonic flat magnon to broad continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21580)
*Sicheng He,Zhixuan Zhao,Hanxiang Di,Shuaihua Zhang,Yanling Wu,Jun Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文以边心蜂窝晶格（edge-centered honeycomb lattice）上的自旋-1/2海森堡反铁磁体（Heisenberg antiferromagnet）为对象，检验单粒子平带（flat band）是否在多体量子磁体中作为受保护元激发存留。结果表明，线性自旋波理论（linear spin-wave theory）中的平磁振子（flat magnon）在量子涨落下转变为宽度较大的连续谱（continuum），其质心约1.55J且沿高对称路径近乎无色散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带的显著意义不仅在于无色散，更在于其将单粒子动能压缩至极小尺度，使相互作用效应成为低能物理的主导能量尺度，由此支撑了平带铁磁（flat-band ferromagnetism）、平带超导（flat-band superconductivity）与局域磁振子（localized magnons）等概念。然而，平带属于单粒子或线性自旋波语言，其能否在真实多体量子磁体中作为受保护的尖锐激发（protected sharp excitations）存留，抑或仅是非相互作用图像遗留的谱权重骨架，仍属未决的基本问题。

Method: 研究对象为边心蜂窝晶格上的自旋-1/2海森堡反铁磁体。该晶格具2:3亚晶格失配（sublattice imbalance），使亚晶格间耦合矩阵为长方形，其零空间（null space）产生单粒子层面的平带；同一失配经Lieb–Mattis定理稳定了铁磁基态（ferrimagnetic ground state）。方法上结合线性自旋波理论、无符号问题的随机级数展开量子蒙特卡洛（sign-problem-free stochastic series expansion quantum Monte Carlo）与随机解析延拓（stochastic analytic continuation）。

Result: 研究发现，量子涨落将该谐波平模（harmonic flat mode）转变为宽连续谱，其质心约为1.55J，且沿高对称路径近乎无色散。这与kagome等几何阻挫磁体中的平带情形形成对比。

Conclusion: 亚晶格失配提供了一条无阻挫路径，将平带与铁磁性统一起来；同时表明平带几何更多是组织多体谱权重，而非产生受保护的准粒子（protected quasiparticle）。

Abstract: One of the most striking aspects of a flat band is not merely its lack of dispersion, but that it reduces the single-particle kinetic energy to a very small scale, allowing interaction effects to become the dominant energy scale in the low-energy physics. This picture has underpinned concepts such as flat-band ferromagnetism, flat-band superconductivity, and localized magnons. A more fundamental question, however, remains. Flat bands belong to the language of single-particle or linear-spin-wave physics; whether they survive as protected sharp excitations in a genuine many-body quantum magnet, or merely provide a skeleton of spectral weight left by the noninteracting picture, remains unclear. We study the spin-1/2 Heisenberg antiferromagnet on the edge-centered honeycomb lattice to examine this question. In contrast to flat bands in geometrically frustrated magnets such as kagome, this lattice has a 2:3 sublattice imbalance that renders the intersublattice coupling matrix rectangular, whose null space gives rise to a flat band at the single-particle level. The same imbalance stabilizes a ferrimagnetic ground state through the Lieb--Mattis theorem and leads to an edge-site-localized flat magnon in linear spin-wave theory. Combining linear spin-wave theory, sign-problem-free stochastic series expansion quantum Monte Carlo, and stochastic analytic continuation, we find that quantum fluctuations transform this harmonic flat mode into a broad continuum whose centroid is about 1.55J and nearly dispersionless along the high-symmetry path. Sublattice imbalance thus offers an unfrustrated route that unifies flat bands and ferrimagnetism, and suggests that flat-band geometry organizes many-body spectral weight rather than giving rise to a protected quasiparticle.

</details>


### [102] [Probing Deconfined Quantum Criticality Point on a Digital Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21761)
*Mrinal Kanti Giri,Pinaki Sengupta*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用含辅助比特投影的量子虚时间演化（QITE）结合张量网络压缩，在超导量子处理器上分辨了一维阻挫 spin-1/2 链中 zFM 与 VBS 之间的退禁闭量子临界点（DQCP），其序参量交叉点的有限尺寸外推接近大规模数值给出的热力学极限值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQCP 是超越 Landau-Ginzburg-Wilson 范式的、具有不相关破缺对称性有序相之间的连续相变；一维 zFM 与 VBS 之间的此类转变已在阻挫 spin-1/2 链中被数值确立，但能否在存在有限尺寸效应与器件噪声的量子处理器上分辨该转变及其特征尚不明确。

Method: 以含辅助比特投影的量子虚时间演化（ancilla-projected QITE）制备基态，并通过张量网络优化将精确目标态压缩为浅层量子线路；在硬件上通过序参量、自旋与二聚体关联、结构因子、关联比及快照分布区分各相。

Result: 序参量的交叉定位了转变点，其有限尺寸外推接近大规模数值得到的热力学极限值；基于器件标定的噪声模型定量解释了硬件上测得的幅度衰减。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，竞争有序相之间被 Landau 范式禁止的转变可以在当前量子处理器上被分辨。

Abstract: A deconfined quantum critical point (DQCP) is a continuous transition between ordered phases with unrelated broken symmetries, beyond the Landau-Ginzburg-Wilson paradigm. In one dimension, such a transition between a $z$-polarized ferromagnet (zFM) and a valence-bond solid (VBS) is well established numerically in a frustrated spin-$1/2$ chain. Here we ask whether a superconducting quantum processor can resolve this transition and its signatures despite finite-size effects and device noise. The ground states are prepared by ancilla-projected quantum imaginary-time evolution (QITE), whose exact target states are compressed by tensor-network optimization into shallow depth quantum circuits. The hardware data distinguish the phases through their order parameters, spin and dimer correlations, structure factors, correlation ratios, and snapshot distributions. The crossing of the order parameters locates the transition, and its finite-size extrapolation lies close to the thermodynamic-limit value obtained from large-scale numerics. A noise model based on the device calibration accounts quantitatively for the reduced amplitudes measured on hardware. Our findings demonstrate that a Landau-forbidden transition between competing orders can be resolved on current quantum processors.

</details>


### [103] [Interlocked Vortex Order in the Frustrated $A_3$V$_9$Te$_{13}$ Lattice ($A$=Cs,Rb)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21854)
*Zilong Li,H. -Q. Yuan,Xiaoqun Wang,Chao Cao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于第一性原理计算，$A_3$V$_9$Te$_{13}$（$A$ = Cs, Rb）的阻挫（frustration）V亚晶格自发稳定为涡旋/反涡旋反铁磁基态，并呈现类kagome金属的电子结构及拓扑特征，为阻挫驱动手性磁性（chiral magnetism）与拓扑电子态的耦合提供平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新合成的 $A_3$V$_9$Te$_{13}$ 为探索超越传统kagome体系的几何阻挫（geometric frustration）、磁性与关联电子态之间的相互作用提供了独特平台。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）确定磁基态，并结合海森堡模型（Heisenberg model）分析磁构型约束，进一步考察电子结构、奈尔温度（Néel temperature）的磁场依赖以及自旋轨道耦合（spin–orbit coupling）效应。

Result: 阻挫V亚晶格稳定出总能量显著低于其他构型的涡旋或反涡旋反铁磁基态；该相具有Dirac型能带交叉、范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularities）与费米面附近平带。海森堡分析显示强磁构型约束，固定1个三角形的自旋构型即可传播并确定其余全部构型。奈尔温度对外磁场大小与方向依赖极弱，与实验观测一致。Cs基化合物基态能带为金属性，布里渊区（BZ）内有6个Weyl点；Rb基化合物因C$_3$对称性解除，自旋轨道耦合在常压下打开约27 meV能隙。

Conclusion: $A_3$V$_9$Te$_{13}$ 是研究阻挫驱动手性磁性及其与拓扑电子态相互作用的潜在平台。

Abstract: Recently synthesized $A_3$V$_9$Te$_{13}$ ($A$ = Cs, Rb) provides a unique platform for exploring the interplay between geometric frustration, magnetism, and correlated electronic states beyond conventional kagome systems. Based on first-principles calculations, we demonstrate that the frustrated V sublattice naturally stabilizes a robust vortex or anti-vortex antiferromagnetic ground state, whose total energy is substantially lower than all other configurations. In addition, the Heisenberg model analysis show strong constraints on the ground state magnetic configuration, such that fixing spin configuration of 1 triangle propagates and fixes all others. The vortex or anti-vortex phase hosts electronic structures reminiscent of kagome metals, including Dirac-like band crossings, van Hove singularities, and flat bands near the Fermi level. Owing to the noncollinear spin texture and quasi-two-dimensional magnetic character, the Néel temperature exhibits an extremely weak dependence on both the magnitude and direction of external magnetic fields, consistent with experimental observations. For Cs-based compound, the ground state band structure is metallic with 6 Weyl points in the BZ; while for the Rb-based compound, spin--orbit coupling opens an approximately 27-meV gap at ambient pressure due to the lifted C$_3$ symmetry. Our results establish $A_3$V$_9$Te$_{13}$ as a promising platform for studying frustration-driven chiral magnetism and its interplay with topological electronic states.

</details>


### [104] [Putting a Temperature on Electron Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22134)
*Paul G. Graf,Florian Matz,Markus Penz,Christian Schilling*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将“更好地量化电子关联（electron correlation）可带来更高效关联处理”的常见观点转化为一个面向基态（ground states）的具体、系统性框架，并揭示关联度量（correlation measure）的共轭变量可作为“关联温度（correlation temperature）”，从而在平均场（mean-field）计算成本下讨论关联问题的可解程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普遍认为，对电子关联如何量化的更深入理解应能促成更高效的关联问题处理方法；本文旨在证实该观点，并将其落实为可操作的框架。其动机在于把关联度量与计算成本可控的理论方法相连接，以回应关联问题能否在低计算代价下求解的根本疑问。

Method: 框架将关联能（correlation energy）建模为关联度量的函数，而这些度量（如 nonfreeness）仅依赖单粒子约化密度矩阵（one-particle reduced density matrix），因此自然落在约化密度矩阵泛函理论（reduced density matrix functional theory）之内，同时保持平均场（mean-field）级别的成本。该框架为方法（如 i-DMFT）提供理性解释，并通过 Hubbard 二聚体（Hubbard dimer）的解析说明与小型分子体系的数值计算阐释其核心概念。

Result: 与所采用度量共轭的变量（conjugate variable）表现为“关联温度”。该框架将关联度量、泛函理论（functional theory）与有效温度（effective temperatures）联系起来，并在 Hubbard 二聚体与小型分子体系中展示了其关键概念。

Conclusion: 该框架使一个根本问题变得触手可及：关联问题中有多大比例可以在平均场成本下解决；并将关联度量、泛函理论与有效温度统一在同一体系内。

Abstract: It is common wisdom that a better understanding of how to quantify electron correlation should lead to more efficient treatments of the correlation problem. Here, we not only substantiate this view but turn it into a concrete, systematic framework for ground states. Since prominent correlation measures such as the nonfreeness depend only on the one-particle reduced density matrix, modeling the correlation energy as a function of such a measure naturally places the framework within reduced density matrix functional theory, while keeping the cost at mean-field level. Strikingly, the variable conjugate to the measure emerges as a ``correlation temperature'', thereby connecting correlation measures, functional theory, and effective temperatures. The framework rationalizes methods such as i-DMFT, and we illustrate its key concepts analytically for the Hubbard dimer and numerically for small molecular systems. This brings a fundamental question within reach: how much of the correlation problem can be solved at mean-field cost?

</details>


### [105] [A rigid commensurate host and a defect that switches: vacancies in the frustrated alternating spin-(1, 1/2) Heisenberg chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22140)
*R. L. Silva,R. C. Silva,A. R. Pereira*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文使用 SU(2) 对称密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）确定了阻挫交替自旋-(1,½) 海森堡链（Heisenberg chain）中 Lieb-Mattis 矩消失后所进入的 Singlet 相的临界性质，并追踪单个空位（vacancy）穿过该相时的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替自旋-(1,½) 海森堡链在阻挫次近邻耦合 J2 下经不连续转变失去 Lieb-Mattis 矩，但所进入 Singlet 相的本质此前未被确定。

Method: 采用 SU(2) 对称 DMRG，将总自旋作为量子数标签，识别该相并追踪单个空位（通过移除中心 S=1 格点实现掺杂）穿越该相的过程。

Result: 总自旋直接从 S=L/4 塌缩至 S=0，无部分极化窗口；Singlet 相为临界相：单-三重态能隙（singlet-triplet gap）按 1/L 关闭，纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）对数增长，中心荷（central charge）c≈1.8，调制波矢从 L=80 外推至 200 为公度的 3π/4，且与 J2 无关至 5×10⁻⁵。

Conclusion: 移除中心 S=1 格点使掺杂链基态在 J2*=0.3068(3) 处由三重态切换为 Singlet，而母体在该点无特征；三重态不束缚于空位而是分布于其留下的两个片段之一。关联透过空位的透射在 J2* 以下饱和于有限值，T(∞)=0.102(15) 与 0.036(9)（对应 J2=0.28、0.30），两侧均排除截断链律 T∝L⁻¹。并证明纵向关联函数在三重态扇区中不是可观测量。

Abstract: The alternating spin-$(1,\tfrac12)$ Heisenberg chain with a frustrating next-nearest-neighbor coupling $J_2$ loses its Lieb--Mattis moment through a discontinuous transition to a singlet whose nature was left open. Using an SU(2)-symmetric density-matrix renormalization group, in which the total spin is a quantum-number label, we identify that phase and follow a single vacancy through it. The total spin collapses directly from $S=L/4$ to $S=0$, with no partially polarized window. The singlet phase is critical: its singlet--triplet gap closes as $1/L$, its entanglement entropy grows logarithmically with a central charge $c\simeq1.8$, and its modulation wavevector, extrapolated from $L=80$ to $200$, is commensurate at $3π/4$ and independent of $J_2$ to $5\times10^{-5}$. Removing the central $S=1$ site switches the ground state of the doped chain from a triplet to a singlet at $J_2^{\ast}=0.3068(3)$, where the host shows no feature; the triplet is not bound to the vacancy but spread over one of the two segments it leaves. The transmission of correlations through the vacancy saturates at a finite value below $J_2^{\ast}$, $T(\infty)=0.102(15)$ and $0.036(9)$ at $J_2=0.28$ and $0.30$, and the cut-chain law $T\propto L^{-1}$ is excluded on both sides. We also show that the longitudinal correlator is not an observable in a triplet sector.

</details>


### [106] [Pt-induced semimetallic transport and enhanced thermoelectric response in the half-Heusler alloy YNi_{0.5}Pt_{0.5}Bi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22253)
*Vikas Chahar,Shivam Rathod,Lankun Wang,Sneh,Shubhadeep Bej,Divya Prakash Dubey,Dinesh Dixit,Abdelmoneim Elkhouly,Raúl Cardoso-Gil,Zihang Liu,Xiao Xu,Rie Y. Umetsu,Ratnamala Chatterjee,Kaustuv Manna*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过 Ni/Pt 合金化将半赫斯勒合金（half-Heusler alloy）YNiBi 调控为半金属（semimetal）输运，室温 zT 达 0.13。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半赫斯勒合金可通过化学取代调控电、热输运，但需进一步探索 Ni/Pt 位点取代对 YNiBi 基化合物热电与磁输运的协同调控作用。

Method: 采用放电等离子体烧结（spark plasma sintering）制备 YNi_{0.5}Pt_{0.5}Bi，测量其热电与磁输运性质，并结合单抛物带（single-parabolic-band）分析与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）进行解释。

Result: Pt 取代使材料呈空穴主导的半金属输运，霍尔载流子浓度低至 1.9×10^{19} cm^{-3}，300 K、14 T 下出现 18% 的不饱和磁阻；室温 Seebeck 系数由约 90 增至 130 μV K^{-1}，热导率降至 3.15 W m^{-1} K^{-1}，功率因子为 13.7 μW cm^{-1} K^{-2}，zT=0.13。计算显示 Pt 诱导带隙闭合及费米能级附近低态密度。

Conclusion: Ni/Pt 合金化可同时调控 YNiBi 基半赫斯勒化合物的电子、磁输运与热电性能，是有效的性能优化途径。

Abstract: Half-Heusler alloys provide a versatile platform for tuning electronic and thermal transport through chemical substitution. Here, we investigate the thermoelectric and magnetotransport properties of spark-plasma-sintered YNi_{0.5}Pt_{0.5}Bi. Partial substitution of Ni by Pt drives YNiBi toward semimetallic transport, characterized by hole-dominated conduction, a low Hall carrier concentration of $\mathrm{1.9 \times 10^{19} cm^{-3}}$, and a sizeable nonsaturating magnetoresistance of 18$\%$ at 300 K under 14 T. Compared with YNiBi, Pt alloying increases the room-temperature Seebeck coefficient from approximately 90 to 130 $\mathrm{μV K^{-1}}$, while reducing the thermal conductivity to 3.15 $\mathrm{W m^{-1} K^{-1}}$. Single-parabolic-band analysis indicates an enhanced carrier effective mass, resulting in a room-temperature power factor of 13.7 $\mathrm{μW cm^{-1} K^{-2}}$ and a figure of merit ($zT$= 0.13). First-principles calculations further show a Pt-induced closing of the electronic band gap and a low density of states near the Fermi level, consistent with the experimentally observed semimetal-like transport. These results demonstrate that Ni/Pt alloying provides an effective route for simultaneously tuning the electronic, magnetotransport, and thermoelectric properties of YNiBi-based half-Heusler compounds.

</details>


### [107] [Local wave-packet spectroscopy of the Drude weight in Tomonaga-Luttinger liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22257)
*Takayuki Yokoyama,Yasuhiro Tada*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究具备 U(1) 电荷（U(1) charge）$q$ 的 Tomonaga-Luttinger 液体（Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid, TLL）中由局域脉冲电场（local pulse electric field）激发的波包。玻色化（bosonization）表明局域脉冲产生两个反向传播、累积电荷为 $\pm qK$、速度为 $v$ 的波包，其运动导致线性增长的电荷极化 $P(t)=2qDt$，从而可由实时局域动力学提取 Drude 权重（Drude weight）$D=vK$。基于自旋-1/2 XXZ 链的时间依赖变分原理（time-dependent variational principle）模拟验证了该图像。最终由波包诱导极化得到的 Drude 权重与精确 Bethe-ansatz 结果一致，展示了 TLL 内禀输运的局域波包谱学（local-packet spectroscopy）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在通过局域脉冲电场激发的波包，探测 Tomonaga-Luttinger 液体（TLL）的本征输运性质，并实现从实时局域动力学中提取 Drude 权重（Drude weight）。其动机在于以局域波包图像替代传统整体输运测量，建立一种“局域波包谱学（local-packet spectroscopy）”方法。

Method: 采用玻色化（bosonization）理论分析局域脉冲电场在 TLL 中产生的波包结构，推导其累积电荷与传播速度；并通过时间依赖变分原理（time-dependent variational principle）对自旋-1/2 XXZ 链进行数值模拟，在时空平面观察密度、电流、能量与纠缠（entanglement）分布，并与精确 Bethe-ansatz 结果比较 Drude 权重。

Result: 玻色化表明局域脉冲产生两个反向传播、累积电荷为 $\pm qK$、速度为 $v$ 的波包，其运动在非平衡导电 TLL 中产生线性增长的电荷极化 $P(t)=2qDt$。数值模拟在时空平面可视化了密度、电流、能量与纠缠分布，证实了预测的波包电荷与传播速度；脉冲区域附近残余的过剩能量与纠缠在弱脉冲极限下被抑制。由波包诱导极化获得的 Drude 权重与精确 Bethe-ansatz 结果一致。

Conclusion: 研究证明局域脉冲电场在 TLL 中生成的波包可用于探测其内禀输运，且由波包诱导的极化提取的 Drude 权重与精确 Bethe-ansatz 一致，确立了局域波包谱学（local-packet spectroscopy）作为研究 TLL 本征输运的有效手段。

Abstract: We investigate wave packets generated by a local pulse electric field in a Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid (TLL) with a U(1) charge $q$. Bosonization shows that a local pulse field creates two counterpropagating packets with opposite accumulated charges $\pm qK$ and velocity $v$, where $K$ and $v$ are the TLL parameter and velocity. The packet motion produces a linearly growing charge polarization $P(t)=2 qDt$ in the conducting TLL at non-equilibrium, allowing the Drude weight $D=vK$ to be extracted from real-time local dynamics. We test this local-packet description by time-dependent variational principle simulations of the spin-1/2 XXZ chain. The simulations visualize the density, current, energy, and entanglement profiles in the space-time plane and confirm the predicted packet charge and propagation velocity. We also show that excess energy and entanglement remaining near the pulse region are suppressed in the weak-pulse limit. Finally, the Drude weight obtained from the packet-induced polarization agrees with the exact Bethe-ansatz result, demonstrating local-packet spectroscopy of intrinsic transport in a TLL.

</details>

### [108] [Disentangling geometry & growth in cosmic neutrino mass constraints: a more physical split and diagnostic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20977)
*Fei Ge,Frank J. Qu,Hanyue Wang,Emmanuel Schaan,W. L. Kimmy Wu,Alexander Friedland*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将中微子质量总和 $\sum m_\nu$ 按物理效应拆分为控制几何（geometry）的 $\sum m_\nu^{geo}$ 与控制结构增长（growth）的 $\sum m_\nu^{growth}$，并允许取负值，用以检验当前宇宙学数据对"负中微子质量"的偏好究竟源自几何还是结构增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期宇宙学数据显示中微子质量总和可能低于振荡实验（neutrino mass oscillation experiments）所允许的最小值，偏好"负"中微子质量。由于大质量中微子同时影响宇宙膨胀历史与结构增长，需厘清该偏好来自观测的几何还是结构增长，以及两者是否一致。

Method: 在玻尔兹曼代码（Boltzmann-code）层面拆分膨胀率：$\sum m_\nu^{geo}$ 设定用于距离计算的 $H(z)$，$\sum m_\nu^{growth}$ 设定用于微扰演化（perturbation evolution）的 $H(z)$，并扩展至负值区间。结合 Planck 主 CMB、Planck+ACT+SPT CMB 透镜（CMB lensing）与 DESI BAO 数据，并对 Advanced SO CMB 与 DESI DR3 BAO 进行 Fisher 预报。

Result: 几何与增长通道给出相近的灵敏度与"负"偏好：$\sum m_\nu^{geo} = -0.07\pm0.10$ eV、$\sum m_\nu^{growth} = -0.13^{+0.10}_{-0.12}$ eV（均 68% C.L.）。两者之差作为 $Λ$CDM 模型的零检验（null test），当前数据以 $0.4\sigma$ 通过。预报显示，若 $\sum m_\nu^{geo} = -0.07$ eV 的负均值持续，仅几何通道即可在 $3.0\sigma$ 排除 $0.058$ eV；错误暗能量（dark energy）模型使 $\sum m_\nu^{geo}$ 偏离 $0.058$ eV 达 $3.1$-$4.2\sigma$、$\sum m_\nu^{growth}$ 达 $1.1$-$1.3\sigma$，而低估再电离光学深度（reionization optical depth）则反转该模式，增长通道为 $2.7$-$3.6\sigma$、几何通道至多 $0.23\sigma$。

Conclusion: 几何与增长两通道可作为诊断中微子质量张力（neutrino mass tension）可能来源的有力新工具。当前数据尚无法区分几何与增长两个通道的差异（零检验通过），但未来 CMB 与 BAO 数据有望通过两通道响应的不同模式辨别张力的物理起源。

Abstract: Recent cosmological data hint at a neutrino mass sum below the minimum value allowed by the neutrino mass oscillation experiments, with a preference for the opposite of massive neutrinos ("negative" neutrino masses). Since massive neutrinos impact the expansion history of the Universe, which in turn modifies the growth of structure, we ask whether such a preference originates from the observed geometry or structure growth, and whether the two agree. In this paper, we introduce a more physical split of $\sum m_ν$ into parameters $\sum m_ν^{geo}$ and $\sum m_ν^{growth}$ based on their physical effects, with extension to negative values. We split the expansion rate at the Boltzmann-code level: $\sum m_ν^{geo}$ sets $H(z)$ for distance computation, and $\sum m_ν^{growth}$ sets $H(z)$ for perturbation evolution. Combining Planck primary CMB, Planck+ACT+SPT CMB lensing and DESI BAO data, we find similar sensitivity and "negative" preference from geometry and growth with $\sum m_ν^{geo} = -0.07\pm0.10$ eV (68% C.L.) and $\sum m_ν^{growth} = -0.13^{+0.10}_{-0.12}$ eV (68% C.L.). The difference of the two is a null test of the $Λ$CDM model, passed by current data at $0.4σ$ level. Forecasts on Advanced SO CMB and DESI DR3 BAO show that $0.058$ eV would be rejected at $3.0σ$ from the geometry channel alone, if the negative mean of $\sum m_ν^{geo} = -0.07$ eV persists. The two channels can also provide a powerful new diagnostic between possible origins of the neutrino mass tension. With future CMB and BAO data, our Fisher forecasts show that an incorrect dark energy model displaces $\sum m_ν^{geo}$ from $0.058$ eV by $3.1$-$4.2σ$ and $\sum m_ν^{growth}$ by $1.1$-$1.3σ$, while an underestimated reionization optical depth reverses the pattern, $2.7$-$3.6σ$ in $\sum m_ν^{growth}$ against at most $0.23σ$ in $\sum m_ν^{geo}$. (Abridged)

</details>


### [109] [Nonlocal Gravity, Restricted CPL Dynamics and DESI Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20720)
*Luca Amendola,Ismael Ayuso,Amlan Chakraborty,Javier Rubio,Subinoy Das*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该研究重新审视 Maggiore–Mancarella 非局域 $R\,\Box^{-2}R$ 引力模型的宇宙学预言，重点考察其背景演化及在最新 DESI 重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillation, BAO）数据下的观测可行性。结果显示，现有数据将非局域参数约束至接近 $\\Lambda$CDM 极限，无非局域偏离的统计显著偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局域引力修正（nonlocal modifications of gravity）为宇宙加速（cosmic acceleration）提供了一种理论上有动机的机制，使其无需引入基本宇宙学常数即可动态出现。作者据此重新评估该模型在最新观测数据下的可检验性。

Method: 将 Maggiore–Mancarella 非局域 $R\,\Box^{-2}R$ 模型的宇宙学动力学实现于玻尔兹曼求解器（Boltzmann solver），并利用宇宙微波背景（Cosmic Microwave Background, CMB）、超新星（supernova）与 DESI BAO 数据进行马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov chain Monte Carlo, MCMC）分析。同时显式构造非局域参数到有效 $(w_0,w_a)$ 对的映射。

Result: 在 Chevallier–Polarski–Linder 平面上，模型并非铺满整个唯象参数空间，而是沿一条受限轨迹运动：对固定的标准宇宙学参数，每个非局域参数取值对应唯一的有效 $(w_0,w_a)$ 对。观测约束表明非局域参数被限制在 $\\Lambda$CDM 极限附近，且无不显著偏离。

Conclusion: 该非局域模型的预言性（predictive character）构成其核心特征，但当前 CMB、超新星与 DESI BAO 数据尚不支持对非局域偏离的统计显著偏好，模型与 $\\Lambda$CDM 在现有精度下难以区分。

Abstract: Nonlocal modifications of gravity provide a theoretically motivated framework in which cosmic acceleration can emerge dynamically without introducing a fundamental cosmological constant. We revisit the cosmological predictions of the Maggiore--Mancarella nonlocal $R\,\Box^{-2}R$ model, with particular emphasis on its background dynamics and observational viability in light of recent DESI baryon acoustic oscillation measurements. A key feature of the theory is its predictive character: for fixed standard cosmological parameters, each value of the nonlocal parameter determines a specific effective pair $(w_0,w_a)$, so that the model traces a restricted trajectory in the Chevallier--Polarski--Linder plane rather than spanning the full phenomenological parameter space. We construct this mapping explicitly and confront the model with observations by implementing its cosmological dynamics in a Boltzmann solver and performing a Markov chain Monte Carlo analysis using Cosmic Microwave Background, supernova, and DESI BAO data. We find that current datasets constrain the nonlocal parameter close to the $Λ$CDM limit, with no statistically significant preference for nonlocal deviations.

</details>


### [110] [Efficient numerical evolution of self-gravitating systems with dark-matter-baryon scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20702)
*Marc Kamionkowski,Vera Gluscevic,Kris Sigurdson*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该工作提出了快速数值算法 NSphere-IDM，用于演化包含暗物质与气体的球对称系统，并计入暗物质-重子散射（dark-matter-baryon scattering）。该方法在 NSphere 基础上耦合一维拉格朗日气体处理，运行成本极低，可在笔记本电脑上完成大规模参数空间探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗物质相互作用（dark-matter interactions）对星系演化（galaxy evolution）的影响通常需要完整三维 N 体/流体模拟，计算资源消耗巨大。因此需要一种能以极低成本快速扫描大范围暗物质参数空间的替代工具，同时为全三维模拟提供标定与基准。

Method: 方法基于球对称 N 体程序 NSphere，将球对称无碰撞系统六维相空间（phase space）压缩为三维，并加入一维拉格朗日（Lagrangian）气体处理。暗物质-重子散射以蒙特卡洛（Monte-Carlo）过程在暗物质数值粒子与气体壳层之间实现，另引入适用于弱冷却情形的辐射气体冷却（radiative gas cooling）算法。

Result: 气体、暗物质-重子散射与冷却仅带来运行时间的增量开销，使系统可在笔记本电脑上演化，所需计算资源远小于完整三维 N 体/流体模拟的极小部分。示例给出约 $10^{13}\,M_\odot$ 晕（halo）在散射截面 $\sigma_{\rm tot}/m_\chi=0.1\,{\rm cm}^2\,{\rm g}^{-1}$、无气体冷却时的演化，以及存在辐射冷却且冷却因与暗物质粒子相互作用而改变的算例。

Conclusion: 所得代码 NSphere-IDM 可在大范围暗物质参数空间上快速探索暗物质相互作用对星系演化的影响，且易于纳入其他新物理（如暗物质自相互作用或分布函数各向异性）。该代码亦可为完整三维 N 体或流体动力学模拟提供有用的标定与基准。

Abstract: We present a fast algorithm to numerically evolve a spherically symmetric system consisting of dark matter and gas, including dark-matter-baryon scattering. The method augments the spherical N-body code NSphere, which collapses the six-dimensional phase space of a spherical collisionless system to three dimensions, with a one-dimensional Lagrangian treatment of gas. Dark-matter-baryon scattering is implemented as a Monte-Carlo process between the dark-matter numerical particles and the gas shells. We also implement an algorithm for radiative gas cooling that is valid for weak cooling. The gas, as well as dark-matter-baryon scattering and cooling, adds only incrementally to the run time of the code, allowing systems to be evolved on a laptop computer, a tiny fraction of the computational resources required for a full 3D N-body/hydro simulation. To illustrate, we show the evolution of a $\sim 10^{13}\,M_\odot$ halo with a dark-matter-baryon scattering cross section $σ_{\rm tot}/m_χ=0.1\,{\rm cm}^2\,{\rm g}^{-1}$ but no gas cooling. We then show examples with radiative gas cooling where the cooling is altered through interaction with dark-matter particles. The resulting code, NSphere-IDM, allows the implications of dark-matter interactions for galaxy evolution to be explored quickly over large volumes of the dark-matter parameter space, and other new physics (e.g., dark-matter self-interactions or anisotropy in the distribution function) is easily incorporated. The code can also provide useful calibrations and benchmarks for fully 3D N-body or hydrodynamical simulations.

</details>


### [111] [Stimulated capture of axion dark matter around primordial black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21951)
*Nuno P. Branco,João G. Rosa*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究原初黑洞（primordial black holes, PBH）周围轴子（axion）及类轴子粒子经由四阶自相互作用（quartic self-interactions）的受激捕获，给出玻色增强增长克服宇宙学稀释的解析判据，并探讨其在脉冲星磁层共振转换与有限尺寸微引力透镜两方面的观测效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子云在原初黑洞周围的形成与存活受宇宙学稀释与轴子-光子耦合（axion-photon coupling）实验上限的双重约束，需要明确可形成且长期存续的参数区间。

Method: 采用解析方法，识别玻色增强增长（Bose-enhanced growth）超过宇宙学稀释的参数区域，推导氢原子基态云（hydrogenic ground-state cloud）高效形成与长期存活的解析判据，并将其与当前轴子-光子耦合限制进行对照。

Result: 共振条件使转换壳层（conversion shell）位于中子星表面之外；云的大轴子占据数（occupation number）可补偿较小的转换概率，产生的瞬变谱线流量密度（transient line flux densities）在银河系遭遇率（Galactic encounter rate）观测相关区域可超过 VLA 与 ALMA 的标称灵敏度。此外，当云尺寸与爱因斯坦半径（Einstein radius）相当或更大时，有限尺寸效应会改变标准点透镜微引力透镜光变曲线。

Conclusion: PBH+云系统具有可观测性：脉冲星磁层中的共振轴子-光子转换可产生可探测瞬变谱线信号，云尺寸引起的有限尺寸效应亦可在微引力透镜光变曲线中留下偏离标准点透镜的印记。

Abstract: We study the stimulated capture of axion and axion-like particles around primordial black holes, mediated by quartic self-interactions. We identify the parametric regions where Bose-enhanced growth overcomes cosmological dilution and derive analytic criteria for efficient formation and long-term survival of the hydrogenic ground-state cloud, confronting them with current bounds on axion-photon couplings. We discuss observational implications of these PBH+cloud systems, including resonant axion-to-photon conversion in neutron star magnetospheres and finite-size microlensing distortions. In the former case, the resonance condition selects a conversion shell outside the neutron star surface, and we show that the large axion occupation number of the cloud can compensate for the small conversion probability. The resulting transient line flux densities can exceed nominal VLA and ALMA sensitivities in regions where the Galactic encounter rate is observationally relevant. Independently, when the cloud size becomes comparable to or larger than the Einstein radius, finite-size effects can modify the standard point-lens microlensing light curve.

</details>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Long-lived local coherences at high temperature from frozen domain walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20724)
*Sarang Gopalakrishnan,Romain Vasseur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了具有量子群对称性（quantum group symmetry）的自旋链中存在一种使局域相干（local coherences）异常长时存活的机制，通过 Mazur 界与局域性论证给出横自旋自关联函数的下界 C(t) ≳ 1/t²，并在 XXZ 链大各向异性极限下精确算出 C(t) ~ √3/(18√t)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同电荷扇区（charge sectors）之间的局域相干不与流体力学模式（hydrodynamic modes）重叠，在有限温度下通常快于任意幂律衰减。论文旨在解释为何在某些系统中这类相干能异常持久，并给出其衰减速率的下界。

Method: 研究对象为服从量子群对称性的可积与混沌自旋链（含 XXZ 链与 Fredkin 链）。方法上使用由量子群对称性导出的 Mazur 界（Mazur bound），结合局域性（locality）与"非下穿"（"non-undershoot"）假设推导相干衰减的代数下界；并在 XXZ 链的大各向异性极限（无限温度）下显式计算衰减率。

Result: 横自旋自关联函数 C(t) ≡ ⟨σ⁻ₓ(t) σ⁺ₓ(0)⟩ 至多以代数形式衰减，满足 C(t) ≳ 1/t²，该界并不紧。在 XXZ 链大各向异性、无限温度极限下，显式计算结果为 C(t) ~ √3/(18√t)；对一般易轴各向异性（easy-axis anisotropy）亦计算了衰减率，并在混沌模型中估计其标度为 C(t) ~ t^{-2/z}（z 为输运指数，transport exponent）。

Conclusion: 量子群对称性可保护局域相干免于快于幂律的衰减，使其在可积乃至混沌自旋链中异常长时存活；所导出的代数下界与具体模型的衰减速率表明相干衰减由量子群对称性支配，并与输运指数相关。

Abstract: Local coherences between distinct charge sectors--measured by the single-particle Green's function or the transverse spin autocorrelation function--do not overlap with hydrodynamic modes. At finite temperature, therefore, they decay faster than any power law in generic chaotic systems. We present a mechanism by which these coherences can persist anomalously long, for integrable and chaotic spin chains (including the XXZ and Fredkin spin chains) that obey a quantum group symmetry. We show--using a Mazur bound derived from the quantum-group symmetry, together with locality and a "non-undershoot" assumption--that the transverse spin autocorrelation function $C(t) \equiv \langle σ^-_x(t) σ^+_x(0)\rangle$ can decay at most algebraically: $C(t) \gtrsim 1/t^2$. This bound is not tight: in the large-anisotropy limit of the XXZ spin chain, at infinite temperature, we explicitly compute $C(t) \sim \sqrt{3}/(18 \sqrt{t})$. We also compute this decay rate in the XXZ spin chain for general easy-axis anisotropy, and estimate it to scale as $C(t) \sim t^{-2/z}$ (for transport exponent $z$) in the chaotic models.

</details>


### [113] [Fisher information analysis of a Langevin System with Thermal and Athermal Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20963)
*Eduardo S. Nascimento,Welles A. M. Morgado*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文探讨利用 Fisher 信息（Fisher Information, FI）估计与 Brownian Gyrator 类似的 Langevin 方程系统中的耦合参数 $b$ 和非热噪声强度 $D$，发现 FI 对角分量表现出非平凡行为，而非对角分量随参数单调变化，这一差异有助于对 $b$ 和 $D$ 进行准确估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fisher 信息已被证明是在仅有统计数据的条件下估计系统参数的有效工具。本文的动机在于将这一方法应用于类似 Brownian Gyrator 的 Langevin 方程系统，以解决耦合参数与非热噪声强度未知时的参数估计问题。

Method: 研究对象为与 Brownian Gyrator 类似的 Langevin 方程系统，其中耦合参数 $b$ 与非热噪声强度 $D$ 被视为未知量。采用 Fisher 信息方法，分别讨论自由度之间耦合参数的估计以及与外部噪声效应相关的量 $D$ 的估计。

Result: Fisher 信息的对角分量（diagonal components）表现出非平凡（nontrivial）的行为，而非对角分量（off-diagonal components）则对所关注的参数呈现单调依赖（monotonic dependence）。这一区别可能有助于对 $b$ 和 $D$ 进行准确估计。

Conclusion: FI 对角与非对角分量的不同行为特征为耦合参数 $b$ 及非热噪声强度 $D$ 的准确估计提供了潜在途径。

Abstract: Fisher Information (FI) has proven to be a useful tool for estimating the parameters of a system when only statistical data are available. We apply this method to a system of Langevin equations analogous to the Brownian Gyrator, in which the coupling parameter $b$ and the athermal noise strength $D$ are treated as unknown quantities. Using the FI, we discuss the estimation of the coupling parameter between the degrees of freedom and the quantity $D$ associated with the external noise effects. We find that the diagonal components of the FI exhibit nontrivial behavior, whereas the off-diagonal components display a monotonic dependence on the parameters of interest. This distinction may contribute to an accurate estimation of $b$ and $D$.

</details>


### [114] [The Blume-Capel Model as a $φ^6$ Field Theory: A Monte Carlo and Flow Model Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20982)
*Ejaaz Merali,Ryan Larson,Ehsan Khatami,Richard T. Scalettar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作采用归一化流（normalizing flows）对连续自旋版本的 Blume-Capel 模型进行蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟，在晶场-温度平面计算相图，并验证流模型作为无自相关直接采样器在一阶、二阶相变及三临界点（tricritical point）附近均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蒙特卡洛方法在相变区域，尤其是一阶相变与三临界点附近，常受临界慢化（critical slowing down）与自相关限制。本文旨在考察软自旋连续化后相图如何随自旋软度变化，并检验归一化流能否作为跨越相图各区域的通用采样手段。

Method: 构建连续自旋变体（continuous spin variant）的 Blume-Capel 模型，使用归一化流（normalizing flows）进行无自相关直接采样，并结合有限尺寸标度分析（finite-size scaling）从流样本中提取相变信息；同时与正则及扩展系综蒙特卡洛（canonical and extended-ensemble Monte Carlo）结果对比。

Result: 在晶场-温度平面计算了随自旋软度（softness of the spins）变化的相图；归一化流在整个相图范围内均为有效采样器，包括一阶相变区与三临界点邻域。基于流样本的有限尺寸标度能够定位一阶、二阶相变及三临界点，精度与正则及扩展系综蒙特卡洛相当。

Conclusion: 归一化流可作为相图各区域（含一阶相变与三临界点附近）的可靠采样工具，其配合有限尺寸标度可达到与传统蒙特卡洛相当的相变定位精度。

Abstract: We report Monte Carlo simulations of a continuous spin variant of the Blume-Capel model. We compute the phase diagrams in the crystal-field-temperature plane, as a function of the softness of the spins. We show that normalizing flows, a class of generative models capable of direct sampling without autocorrelation, are effective samplers across the phase diagram, including in the first-order transition region and in the vicinity of the tricritical point. Finite-size scaling analyses of flow samples locate the first- and second-order transitions as well as the tricritical point with accuracy comparable to canonical and extended-ensemble Monte Carlo.

</details>


### [115] [Diffusion-Induced Stabilization of Nonreciprocal Wealth Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21861)
*Pierre Bousseyroux,Gilles Zérah,Michael Benzaquen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在二维方格晶格的空间模型中，结合局部共享（local sharing）、全局再分配（global redistribution）与随机非互惠相互作用（random nonreciprocal interactions），研究等财富份额（equal wealth shares）的稳定性，发现稳定性取决于影响之间的相关性符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察等财富份额在空间扩散、再分配与随机非互惠相互作用共同作用下的稳定条件，明确局部共享作用的正负相关性如何改变所需的再分配强度。

Method: 方法为在无限系统极限（infinite-system limit）下的二维正方晶格（two-dimensional square lattice）上构建空间模型，将局部共享、全局再分配与随机非互惠相互作用相结合，并以一个站点对另一站点影响与其反向影响之间的相关性符号为分类依据进行分析。

Result: 当影响呈非负相关（nonnegatively correlated）时，扩散（diffusion）降低稳定所需的再分配，但仍需正的再分配率；当影响呈负相关（negative correlations）时，无需再分配即可稳定，且稳定性裕度（stability margin）在有限扩散下最大、在强扩散下回归边际稳定（marginal stability）。等份额在财富集中型转移（wealth-concentrating transfers）下也能保持稳定，但仅限中等扩散强度区间。

Conclusion: 结论表明，局部共享对稳定性的作用由相互作用的相关性符号决定，非单调性源于空间平滑（spatial smoothing）与互易反相关（reciprocal anticorrelations）稳定贡献减弱之间的竞争。

Abstract: We study the stability of equal wealth shares in a spatial model combining local sharing, global redistribution, and random nonreciprocal interactions. On a two-dimensional square lattice in the infinite-system limit, the effect of local sharing depends on whether the influence of one site on another tends to have the same or opposite sign as the reverse influence. When these influences are nonnegatively correlated, diffusion reduces the redistribution required for stability, but a positive rate remains necessary. Negative correlations instead allow stability without redistribution, with the largest stability margin at finite diffusion and a return toward marginal stability at strong diffusion. This nonmonotonicity reflects a competition between spatial smoothing and the weakening of the stabilizing contribution of reciprocal anticorrelations. Equal shares can even remain stable under wealth-concentrating transfers, but only within an intermediate range of diffusion strengths.

</details>


### [116] [Dynamical phase transition for a logarithm observable in chaotic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21288)
*Lucianno Defaveri,Naftali R. Smith*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究混沌映射（chaotic map）下动力可观测量 $A=-\sum_{i=1}^N \ln x_i$ 的完整分布 $P_N(A)$，在 $N\gg1$ 极限下发现其右尾呈现反常标度并伴随凝聚相变（condensation transition），尽管矩的累积量（cumulant）仍随 $N$ 线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注动力可观测量（dynamical observable）在全 $N$ 范围内的分布性质，尤其是偏离均值时尾部行为是否遵循标准大偏差（large deviation）标度，并选取倍增映射（doubling map）、帐篷映射（tent map）与逻辑斯蒂映射（logistic map）作为具体研究对象以检验其普适性。

Method: 对由 $x_{n+1}=f(x_n)$ 生成的序列 $x_1,\dots,x_N$ 定义可观测量 $A=-\sum_{i=1}^N \ln x_i$，在 $N\gg1$ 极限下解析求解其分布 $P_N(A)$，分别计算左尾的速率函数（rate function）$I(a)$ 与右尾的大偏差函数（large-deviation function）$F(y)$，并对比三种映射的结果。

Result: 典型涨落（typical fluctuations）为高斯分布，均值与方差均为 $\sim N$ 量级。左尾满足 $P_N(A)\sim e^{-NI(A/N)}$；右尾则呈现反常标度 $P_N(A)\sim e^{-N^{1/3}F((A-\langle A\rangle)/N^{2/3})}$，且 $F(y)$ 对三种映射具有普适形式，仅在标度常数上有差异。在临界值 $y=y_c$ 处出现角点奇异性（corner singularity），具有凝聚相变的特征，将 $0<y<y_c$ 的亚临界相（均匀来源于所有 $x_i$）与 $y>y_c$ 的超临界相（少数 $x_i$ 贡献宏观涨落）分开。

Conclusion: 尽管右尾表现出反常标度，$P_N(A)$ 的累积量（cumulants）在大 $N$ 下仍随 $N$ 线性增长，论文进一步计算了相应系数。结果揭示了动力学涨落中尾部标度与矩行为之间的非平凡分离，且右尾普适性对映射细节不敏感。

Abstract: We consider the full distribution $P_N(A)$ of the dynamical observable $A = - \sum_{i=1}^N \ln x_i$ where $x_1, \dots, x_N$ is a sequence generated by a chaotic map $f(x)$ via $x_{n+1} = f(x_n)$. We study the particular cases where $f(x)$ is the doubling, tent or logistic map, and focus on the limit $N \gg 1$. Typical fluctuations of $A$ are Gaussian with mean $\left\langle A\right\rangle$ and variance which are both of order $\sim N$. We find that the left tail of the distribution ($A < \left\langle A\right\rangle$) scales as $P_{N}(A) \sim e^{-NI(A/N)}$, and calculate the rate function $I(a)$ which depends on the map $f(x)$. Interestingly, we find that the right tail of the distribution ($A > \left\langle A\right\rangle$) follows an anomalous scaling: $P_{N}(A) \sim e^{-N^{1/3} F((A-\left\langle A\right\rangle) / N^{2/3})}$. The large-deviation function $F(y)$ has a universal form for the three maps, differing only in the scaling constants. At a critical value $y=y_c$, it exhibits a corner singularity which has the character of a condensation transition. The transition separates between a subcritical phase $0< y<y_c$ in which the fluctuations are Gaussian and arise homogeneously from all of the $x_i$'s, and a supercritical phase $y>y_c$ in which a small fraction of the $x_i$'s give a macroscopic contribution to the fluctuation. Despite this anomalous behavior of the right tail, we find that the cumulants of the distribution $P_N(A)$ grow linearly with $N$ at large $N$, and calculate the associated coefficients.

</details>


### [117] [Critical Ising Model on the Periodic Cubic Lattice $(\mathbb{Z}/L\mathbb{Z})^{3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21326)
*Shenglan Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究三维周期立方格点（periodic cubic lattice）上最近邻 Ising 模型的临界行为，并配套开展蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟，重点综述严格结果、整理三维临界指数（critical exponents）的高精度估计，并讨论数值测量中的系统误差与相关函数拟合方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维 Ising 模型的临界性质具有长期的理论与数值研究价值：d=3 时自发磁化（spontaneous magnetization）连续性已有严格结果，而 d≥4 时标度极限（scaling limit）具有平凡性（triviality）。在此背景下，需要结合现代高精度估计与数值模拟来检验标度关系（scaling relations）并厘清指数提取的误差来源。

Method: 在三维周期立方格点 (Z/LZ)^3 上研究最近邻 Ising 模型，并给出配套的蒙特卡洛模拟。数值测量包括 Binder 累积量（Binder cumulant）、磁化率（magnetic susceptibility）、比热（specific heat）、两点相关函数（two-point correlation function）以及磁化的有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）；同时整理并核对现代高精度三维 Ising 临界指数估计及其标度关系。

Result: 文中编译了三维 Ising 临界指数的现代高精度估计，并验证了连接这些指数的标度关系；对 Binder 累积量、磁化率、比热、两点相关函数与磁化有限尺寸标度进行了数值测量，并讨论了影响指数比 γ/ν 与 α/ν 提取的系统不确定性（systematic uncertainties）。

Conclusion: 在临界点处相关函数呈代数衰减（algebraic decay），因此对相关函数采用指数拟合（exponential fit）并不合适；指数比 γ/ν 与 α/ν 的提取受系统不确定性影响，需要在分析与拟合策略中加以处理。

Abstract: We investigate the critical behaviour of the nearest-neighbour Ising model on the three-dimensional periodic cubic lattice $(\mathbb{Z}/L\mathbb{Z})^{3}$, and we present a companion set of Monte Carlo simulations. After recalling the rigorous results on the continuity of the spontaneous magnetization in $d=3$ and the triviality of the scaling limit in $d\ge 4$, we compile modern high-precision estimates of the 3D Ising critical exponents and verify the scaling relations that connect them. We then describe numerical measurements of the Binder cumulant, the magnetic susceptibility, the specific heat, the two-point correlation function, and the finite-size scaling of the magnetization. We discuss the systematic uncertainties that affect the extraction of the exponent ratios $γ/ν$ and $α/ν$, and we emphasize that an exponential fit of the correlation function is not appropriate at the critical point, where the decay is algebraic.

</details>


### [118] [Localization induced by Sinai disorder in mass-exchange models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21777)
*Michele Giusfredi,Luca Avena,Paolo Politi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在有限段上的保守连续质量交换模型（conservative continuous-mass-exchange model），键上的分裂规则带有淬火无序（quenched disorder）。尽管动力学一般不可逆，其平稳平均质量剖面可通过关联的生灭链（birth-and-death chain）精确求解，并具备基于简单随机游走势的吉布斯表示（Gibbs representation）。参数 β√N 区分出三种渐近剖面，并刻画了宏观质量涨落、局部化中心的反正弦律（arcsine laws）及无序平均质量测度，还讨论了极值质量的极限随机变量与局部化转变附近的逆参与比（inverse participation ratio）标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象为带淬火无序的保守质量交换动力学，其动力学一般不可逆，平稳态通常难以精确求解。本文旨在揭示此类不可逆系统中平稳平均质量剖面仍可精确求解的机制，并刻画无序强度与系统尺度竞争下的剖面形态与涨落规律。

Method: 在有限段的键上引入淬火无序的分裂规则，构建保守连续质量交换模型；借助关联的生灭链（birth-and-death chain）求解平稳平均质量剖面，并给出基于简单随机游走势的吉布斯表示（Gibbs representation）；通过参数 β√N（N 为键数，β 度量无序强度）分析剖面相区，并利用有限随机游走路径与条件化游走的联系刻画极值质量与逆参与比（inverse participation ratio）的标度。

Result: 平稳平均质量剖面被证明可精确求解并具有吉布斯表示；β√N 分离出渐近平坦剖面、布朗交叉（Brownian crossover）与集中于全局最小值的局部化剖面三种情形。宏观质量涨落得到刻画，并识别出局部化中心、左右谷竞争及无序平均质量测度的反正弦律（arcsine laws）。对极值质量，给出以有限随机游走路径从最小值视角与条件化为正的无界游走相联系的极限随机变量；在局部化转变点 β=0 附近，极值质量的逆参与比（inverse participation ratio）呈现约 k^{-3/2} 的衰减标度，与该条件化游走的标度极限一致，且逆参与比呈现标度形式。

Conclusion: 不可逆的保守无序质量交换动力学在平稳平均层面具有精确可解性与吉布斯结构，其剖面形态由 β√N 统一控制，并伴随反正弦律涨落与局部化转变。极值质量的标度行为与条件化随机游走的标度极限相衔接，逆参与比在转变点附近表现出 k^{-3/2} 衰减及标度形式。

Abstract: We introduce a conservative continuous-mass-exchange model with quenched disorder in the splitting laws on the bonds of a finite segment. Although the dynamics is generically irreversible, its stationary mean mass profile remarkably turns out to be exactly solvable through an associated birth-and-death chain and has a Gibbs representation in terms of a simple random-walk potential. If $N$ is the number of bonds and $β$ measures the strength of disorder, the parameter $β\sqrt N$ separates an asymptotically flat profile, a Brownian crossover, and a profile localized near the global minimum. We characterize the fluctuations of macroscopic masses and identify arcsine laws for the localization centre, the competition between left and right valleys, and the disorder-averaged mass measure. Furthermore, for the extremal masses, we discuss the emergent limiting random variable in terms of the literature linking finite RW paths seen from its minimum to infinite-horizon walks conditioned to stay positive. Finally, in proximity of the localization transition point $β=0$, we show that the inverse participation ratio for the extremal masses give rises to a scaling with decay of order $k^{-3/2}$ consistent with the scaling limit of the latter conditioned walk. In the same limit, the inverse participation ratio shows a scaling form.

</details>


### [119] [Synchronization induced by stochastic subsystem resetting in complex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21842)
*Kevin Paramero-Arias,Alejandro P. Riascos*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了对网络耦合振荡器（network-coupled oscillators）中部分节点施加随机重置（stochastic resetting）如何诱导整体同步（synchronization）。结果表明，重置可通过网络结构、频率异质性与重置子系统组织的相互作用，使未被重置的节点也产生集体相干性（collective coherence）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示仅对子集节点施加重置是否能诱导并控制整个复杂网络（complex networks）的集体同步，并阐明网络拓扑、频率分布与重置配置在其中所起的作用。

Method: 在无限重置速率极限下，将重置节点视为被固定到参考相位，通过同步附近线性化，把剩余子系统的动力学映射为接地拉普拉斯（grounded-Laplacian）问题；在线性区之外，构建平均场（mean-field）描述，将重置子系统视为对剩余振荡器的有效有序场（ordering field），并结合不同网络拓扑的数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 该框架给出同步水平与重置参数之间的解析关系，可估计达到预设相干度所需的耦合强度；数值模拟证实同步随重置比例与网络连通性的增加而增强；在异质网络（heterogeneous networks）中，重置节点位置显著影响同步响应；对于非零均值的频率分布，重置可与内在集体旋转竞争，使系统由旋转同步态转为锁相态（phase-locked state）。

Conclusion: 子系统重置（subsystem resetting）被确立为一种在复杂网络中诱导与控制集体同步的机制，其效果依赖于网络结构、频率异质性与重置子系统的组织方式。

Abstract: We study synchronization in network-coupled oscillators subject to stochastic resetting of a subset of nodes. We show that resetting can induce collective coherence in oscillators that are never directly reset, through the interplay of network structure, frequency heterogeneity, and the organization of the reset subsystem. In the infinite-resetting-rate limit, the reset nodes become effectively pinned to a reference phase, and linearization near synchronization maps the dynamics of the remaining subsystem onto a grounded-Laplacian problem. This formulation yields an analytical relation between the synchronization level and the reset parameters, providing an estimate of the coupling strength required to achieve a prescribed degree of coherence. Beyond the linear regime, we develop a mean-field description in which the reset subsystem acts as an effective ordering field for the remaining oscillators. Numerical simulations across different network topologies confirm these predictions and show that synchronization is enhanced by increasing both the reset fraction and network connectivity. In heterogeneous networks, the placement of reset nodes can strongly influence the synchronization response. For frequency distributions with nonzero mean, resetting competes with the intrinsic collective rotation and can drive the system from a rotating synchronized state to a phase-locked state. These results establish subsystem resetting as a mechanism for inducing and controlling collective synchronization in complex networks.

</details>


### [120] [Twist-controlled thermodynamic anomalies in bilayer q-state clock models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21949)
*Ze-Heng Yu,Liang He*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作通过蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）证明，扭转（twist）可作为铁磁双层 q 态时钟模型（q-state clock models）中热力学异常（thermodynamic anomalies）的可调控参量，使比热峰（specific-heat peaks）发生分裂或合并，并与莫尔尺度（moire scale）的自旋织构与涡旋分布相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转已在多种物理系统中作为调控集体行为的几何手段，但其能否定性地重组离散自旋模型（discrete spin models）的热力学异常、以及此类变化与微观自旋构型的关系仍缺乏研究。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟，研究铁磁双层 q 态时钟模型在层间耦合（interlayer coupling）足够强时，扭转角（twist angle）与允许自旋取向数 q 对比热峰数量与结构的调控作用，并统计伴随的自旋织构（spin textures）与涡旋分布（vortex distributions）。

Result: 强层间耦合下，扭转可改变比热峰的数量与结构：单峰可分裂为双峰，双峰亦可合并为单峰，具体取决于 q 值；这些变化随扭转角呈非单调（nonmonotonic）行为，且在较大 q 值下出现在更窄的参数范围或完全消失。代表性情形中，峰分裂与莫尔尺度上近乎均匀的自旋区域及涡旋数量减少同时出现。

Conclusion: 研究确立扭转作为离散自旋系统集体热响应（collective thermal response）的控制参量，并提出其对相变行为（phase-transition behavior）的可能修正，包括中间 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless 相的出现或抑制。

Abstract: Twist has emerged as a geometric means of controlling collective behavior across a wide range of physical systems. Whether it can qualitatively reorganize the thermodynamic anomalies of discrete spin models, and how such changes relate to microscopic spin configurations, remains less explored. Here we demonstrate twist-controlled thermodynamic anomalies in ferromagnetic bilayer q-state clock models using Monte Carlo simulations. With sufficiently strong interlayer coupling, twisting changes the number and structure of specific-heat peaks: a single peak can split into two, or two peaks can merge into one, depending on the number of allowed spin orientations. These changes are nonmonotonic in twist angle and occur over narrower parameter ranges, or are absent, for the larger q values examined. The accompanying spin textures and vortex distributions suggest a physical picture in which moire modulation of the interlayer coupling reorganizes local spin alignment and topological defects. In a representative case, peak splitting coincides with nearly uniform spin regions arranged on the moire scale and a reduced vortex population. These findings identify twist as a control parameter for the collective thermal response of discrete spin systems and raise the possibility of modified phase-transition behavior, including the emergence or suppression of an intermediate Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless phase.

</details>


### [121] [Spin-1/2 Heisenberg diamond-like decorated honeycomb lattice in a magnetic field from the perspective of localized magnons and exact diagonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21955)
*Katarina Karlova,Malo Rouxel,Jozef Strecka,Andreas Honecker*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 采用解析与数值互补方法研究磁场下钻石修饰蜂窝晶格（diamond-decorated honeycomb lattice）上的自旋-1/2量子海森堡模型（quantum Heisenberg model），给出完整基态相图与低温磁热性质，并揭示场诱导相变中的Ising临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钻石修饰蜂窝晶格中的自旋阻挫（spin frustration）与磁场耦合可产生丰富相态和磁化平台，但其基态相图及有限温度热力学尚需系统刻画。

Method: 结合解析与数值方法；在高阻挫区采用基于有效经典晶格气体模型（classical lattice-gas model）的局域磁振子（localized-magnon）方法，并与数值结果对照。

Result: 基态相图包含Lieb-Mattis铁磁相（ferrimagnetic）、二聚体-四聚体（dimer-tetramer）、单体-二聚体（monomer-dimer）、自旋倾斜（spin-canted）及饱和顺磁（saturated paramagnetic）相，并出现特征磁化平台与连续/不连续场诱导相变；中间阻挫区有限温度计算显示场诱导相变的比热特征。

Conclusion: 局域磁振子方法可准确描述高阻挫区低温磁性与热力学；在单体-二聚体与Lieb-Mattis铁磁相之间的场诱导相变处出现Ising临界行为（Ising critical behavior）。

Abstract: The spin-$1/2$ quantum Heisenberg model on the diamond-decorated honeycomb lattice in a magnetic field is investigated using complementary analytical and numerical methods. The ground-state phase diagram comprises Lieb-Mattis ferrimagnetic, dimer-tetramer, monomer-dimer, spin-canted, and saturated paramagnetic phases, giving rise to characteristic magnetization plateaus, continuous and discontinuous field-induced transitions. In the highly frustrated regime, a localized-magnon approach based on an effective classical lattice-gas model provides an accurate description of the low-temperature magnetism and thermodynamics in agreement with numerical results. At intermediate frustration, finite-temperature calculations reveal characteristic specific-heat signatures of the field-induced transitions including the development of Ising critical behavior at the field-induced transition between the monomer-dimer and Lieb-Mattis ferrimagnetic phase.

</details>


### [122] [Sluggish random walks with resetting: nonequilibrium aspects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21962)
*Theophilus Dwamena Frimpong,Léa Bresque,Edgar Roldán*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对一维迟缓随机游走（sluggish random walk），在两种随机重置（stochastic resetting）机制下，精确求解了固定时刻概率分布与首达时间（first-passage time）分布的拉普拉斯变换，并给出最优重置率、占据时间统计及重置成本的解析刻画，数值模拟验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迟缓随机游走与随机重置是两类重要的非平衡动力学机制，但二者结合下的首达时间分布、占据时间统计及重置成本尚未获得精确解析描述；本文旨在填补这一空白，并比较泊松重置与阈值重置两种机制的差异。

Method: 针对一维迟缓随机游走，分别考虑泊松分布随机时刻的重置（Poissonian resetting）与达到位置阈值时触发的重置（threshold resetting），推导固定时刻概率分布与首达时间分布拉普拉斯变换（Laplace transform）的精确解析表达式，并计算最优重置率、占据时间（occupation time）统计，以及在线性、幂律、指数三类成本函数下的单次重置成本分布与均值、成本率。

Result: 最优重置率被表示为迟缓参数（sluggishness parameter）的函数；阈值重置下到达原点的平均首达时间随阈值单调递增。占据时间统计在自由动力学与泊松重置下短观测时间呈 Lamperti 型分布，长观测时间趋于以二分之一为中心的高斯分布，阈值重置下则呈现独特的确定性极限。不同成本函数下，成本率在重置率趋于零与阈值趋于无穷的极限中表现出相同的临界行为。数值模拟与解析公式高度吻合。

Conclusion: 研究给出了迟缓随机游走在两类随机重置下的完整解析刻画，揭示最优重置率随迟缓参数的依赖关系、阈值重置对首达时间的单调影响、占据时间的跨尺度分布转变，以及重置成本率的普适临界行为，为理解重置机制的非平衡特性提供了定量基准。

Abstract: We derive exact analytical expressions for the fixed-time probability distribution and the Laplace transform of the distribution of the first-passage time to reach a target for a sluggish random walk in one dimension subject to two different types of stochastic resetting: stochastic resetting at random Poisson-distributed times, and resetting implemented whenever a position threshold is reached. The optimal resetting rate that minimises the mean first-passage time is obtained as a function of the sluggishness parameter, while under threshold resetting the mean first-passage time to the origin is found to increase monotonically with the threshold. We also characterise the occupation-time statistics, retrieving a Lamperti-type distribution under free dynamics and under Poissonian resetting at rate for short observation times, a Gaussian distribution centred at half for long observation times, and a distinct deterministic limit under threshold resetting. To further characterise nonequilibrium aspects of resetting, we evaluate the distribution and mean of the cost incurred per reset under Poissonian resetting, and the cost rate under threshold resetting, for different (linear, power-law, exponential) cost functions, and find the same critical behaviour of the cost rate in the limits of a vanishing resetting rate and an infinite threshold, respectively. Numerical simulations validate with excellent accuracy our analytical formulae.

</details>


### [123] [Partial Localization and Multifractality in Heterogeneous Population Growth with Resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22038)
*Maximilien Bernard,Giuseppe Del Vecchio Del Vecchio,Jean-Philippe Bouchaud,Pierre Le Doussal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个带有均值场再分配（mean-field redistribution）与独立局部随机重置（stochastic resetting）的异质乘性增长（heterogeneous multiplicative growth）模型，刻画了种群或财富在站点间的分布动力学，并识别出离域相与部分局域相及其统计特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型旨在描述种群或财富在具有淬火增长率异质性（quenched growth-rate heterogeneity）的站点上的分布，其中重置代表随机局部灭绝或破产事件，从而探究再分配与重置对增长分布的影响。

Method: 采用均值场再分配与独立局部随机重置相结合的异质乘性增长模型，通过鞍点分析（saddle point analysis）推导增长率，并计算站点种群的稳态分布。

Result: 在大再分配或重置速率下出现离域相（delocalized phase），可计算增长率与给定站点种群的稳态分布；无重置增长阶段产生幂律稳态尾部（power-law stationary tails）且指数依赖于站点。弱重置与再分配时出现部分局域相（partially localized phase），总人口的有限比例集中于少数站点，这些站点身份在O(1)时间尺度上变化，位于规模为N^{u^⋆}（0<u^⋆<1）的次广延候选池中；平均稳态比例呈多重分形（multifractal）行为。

Conclusion: 再分配与重置的强度决定了系统的局域化性质：强重置导致离域相与幂律尾部，弱重置导致部分局域相；该部分局域相伴随次广延候选池中的身份更替，并由多重分形统计描述，鞍点分析给出相应增长率。

Abstract: We study a model of heterogeneous multiplicative growth with mean-field redistribution and independent local stochastic resetting. The model describes populations or wealth distributed over sites with quenched growth-rate heterogeneity, while resets represent random local extinction or bankruptcy events. At large redistribution or resetting rate, we find a delocalized phase and we compute the growth rate as well as the stationary distribution of the population of a given site. We show that reset-free growth episodes generate power-law stationary tails with site-dependent exponents. For weak resetting and redistribution, favorable sites can undergo long reset-free episodes and localize. This produces a partially localized phase, in which a finite fraction of the total population is carried by a small set of sites whose identity changes on an $O(1)$ time scale within a subextensive pool of $N^{u^\star}$ favorable candidates, with $0<u^\star<1$. We derive the corresponding growth rate from a saddle point analysis, and show that the averaged stationary fractions display multifractal behavior.

</details>
