# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Modeling Transport and Mechanics in Anion Exchange Membranes to Optimize Continuous CO2 Separations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20812)
*Samaneh Bayati,Jennifer Wade,Sara Sarbaz,Heidi Feigenbaum,Horacio Lopez-Marques,Winston Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究建立了 Fumasep FAA-3-50 阴离子交换膜（anion exchange membrane）中水传输与力学变形的瞬态有限元模型（finite element model），纳入浓度依赖扩散率、吸湿膨胀（hygroscopic swelling）与非线性黏弹性（nonlinear viscoelastic）响应，并对比非耦合、单向耦合与全耦合三种形式，最终以稳态水传输实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 荷电聚合物膜（charged polymer membrane）的水传输受水合依赖传输性质与聚合物网络变形共同影响：水活度梯度（water-activity gradient）下水浓度空间差异引发膨胀与力学应力，而变形又反过来影响水传输。捕捉这种双向相互作用，对预测非均匀水合条件下的膜行为至关重要。

Method: 构建 Fumasep FAA-3-50 阴离子交换膜的瞬态有限元模型，引入浓度依赖的水扩散率、吸湿膨胀与非线性黏弹性聚合物响应；比较非耦合（uncoupled）、单向耦合（one-way coupled）与全耦合（fully coupled）三种表述，以区分水合依赖扩散率与力学耦合的各自作用。全耦合模型中，局部水浓度控制膨胀与力学响应，而变形、演化中的膜几何与力学压力反馈至水传输。

Result: 模拟预测出随水浓度场演化而同步的时空非均匀膨胀与变形。相较非耦合的浓度依赖扩散模型，力学反馈改变了预测的传输响应，但传输仍以扩散驱动为主。全耦合模型经水活度梯度下的稳态水传输测量验证，再现了实验观测的水合依赖关系，在中等水合水平（intermediate hydration level）吻合最佳。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在水合阴离子交换膜模型中，水合、膨胀、变形与传输的耦合过程具有重要意义；所提框架可用于评估水分辅助传输（moisture-facilitated transport）应用中的膜行为。

Abstract: Water transport in charged polymer membranes is strongly influenced by hydration-dependent transport properties and polymer-network deformation. Under water-activity gradients, spatial variations in water concentration induce swelling and mechanical stresses, while the resulting deformation can influence water transport. Capturing this bidirectional interaction is important for predicting membrane behavior under nonuniform hydration conditions. This study develops a transient finite element model of water transport and mechanical deformation in a Fumasep FAA-3-50 anion exchange membrane. The framework incorporates concentration-dependent water diffusivity, hygroscopic swelling, and nonlinear viscoelastic polymer response. Uncoupled, one-way coupled, and fully coupled formulations are compared to distinguish the effects of hydration-dependent diffusivity and mechanical coupling. In the fully coupled model, local water concentration governs swelling and mechanical response, while deformation, evolving membrane geometry, and mechanical pressure feed back into water transport. Simulations predict spatially and temporally nonuniform swelling and deformation that track the evolving water-concentration field. Relative to the uncoupled concentration-dependent diffusion model, mechanical feedback alters the predicted transport response, although transport remains primarily diffusion-driven. The fully coupled model is validated against steady-state water-transport measurements across water-activity gradients and reproduces the experimentally observed hydration dependence, with strongest agreement at intermediate hydration levels. These results highlight the importance of coupled hydration, swelling, deformation, and transport processes in models of hydrated anion exchange membranes and provide a framework for evaluating membrane behavior in moisture-facilitated transport applications.

</details>


### [125] [Denser-than-crystalline disordered polydisperse ellipse packings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20953)
*Kinsley G. Trlak,Dewni S. de Silva,Salvatore Torquato,Robert S. Hoy*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过在慢速类 Lubachevsky-Stillinger 粒子生长过程中引入 SWAP 蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）移动，制备了宽范围长径比与粒径比下的热化无序椭圆堆积，并首次报道了各向异性粒子中密度高于单分散椭圆晶体的无序堆积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序堆积（disordered packings）能否超越对应晶体的密度是堆积问题的核心议题之一，而此前超出单分散晶体密度的无序堆积仅见于各向同性（如圆盘）体系；各向异性粒子中是否存在此类现象尚不清楚。

Method: 在类 Lubachevsky-Stillinger 的慢速粒子生长过程中加入 SWAP 蒙特卡洛移动以增强热化，针对一系列粒子长径比 α 与最大/最小粒径比 λ 生成无序堵塞椭圆堆积，并进行多尺度结构分析。

Result: 当 λ > λ_xtal(α) 时，堆积密度超过完美单分散椭圆晶体（φ_xtal^mono = π/2√3 ≈ 0.9069）且无明显长程有序；λ_xtal(α) 在 1 ≤ α ≤ 5 范围内低于 2.88，并在 α ≈ 1.2 时降至 1.65 以下。多尺度分析表明结构基元强烈依赖 α 与 λ，但所考察的度量在跨越 λ = λ_xtal(α) 边界时均无突变。α ≈ 1.2、λ ≈ λ_xtal(α) 的堆积呈现分形-六角-塑性-多晶（fractionated-hexatic-plastic-polycrystalline）结构，可恰好等静定（exactly isostatic）、三角化（triangulated）且对堆积率涨落有效超均匀（hyperuniform）；而 α 与 λ 更大的致密堆积不具备这些性质。

Conclusion: 各向异性椭圆粒子可形成密度超过单分散椭圆晶体的无序堆积，这是该类体系中的首次报道；此类堆积的结构特征由 α 与 λ 强烈调控，且晶体密度边界并不对应任何结构度量的突变，提示其转变并非尖锐的结构转变。

Abstract: By employing SWAP Monte Carlo moves during slow Lubachevsky-Stillinger-like particle growth, we generate well-thermalized disordered jammed ellipse packings for a wide range of particle aspect ratios $α$ and maximum/minimum particle-diameter ratios $λ$. For $λ> λ_{\rm xtal}(α)$, the packings are denser than the perfect monodisperse ellipse crystal ($φ_{\rm xtal}^{\rm mono} = π/2\sqrt{3} \simeq 0.9069$) despite lacking any obvious long-range structural order; we find that $λ_{\rm xtal}(α)$ remains below $2.88$ over the range $1 \leq α\leq 5$, and drops below $1.65$ for $α\simeq 1.2$. These disordered $φ> φ_{\rm xtal}^{\rm mono}$ packings are the first of their kind reported for anisotropic particles. Multiscale analyses reveal that their characteristic structural motifs are strongly $α$- and $λ$-dependent, but none of the examined metrics change sharply as the $λ= λ_{\rm xtal}(α)$ boundary is crossed. For example, we find that packings with $α\simeq 1.2$ and $λ\simeq λ_{\rm xtal}(α)$ exhibit a fractionated-hexatic-plastic-polycrystalline structure that (like that of the recently-reported ideal disk glasses) can be exactly-isostatic, triangulated, and effectively-hyperuniform with respect to packing-fraction fluctuations. In contrast, even-denser packings with larger $α$ and $λ$ have none of these properties.

</details>


### [126] [Universal Dynamic Scaling for Sub-Yield Aging and Over-Yield Rejuvenation in A Cyclically Driven Metallic Glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21625)
*Nannan Ren,Hongfei Zhang,Rui Wan,Pengfei Guan,Limin Wang,Baoshuang Shang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过金属玻璃（metallic glass）的分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations），提出一个统一的"动态时钟"，用以解释非晶固体在循环形变下亚屈服老化（sub-yield aging）与过屈服再生（over-yield rejuvenation）的能量景观演化，并揭示了两种截然不同的能量注入路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶固体在循环形变中，亚屈服的 aging 与过屈服的 rejuvenation 究竟源自共同物理机制还是不同微观机制，长期未有定论。该研究旨在消解这一二元分歧，弥合非线性流变学与应力涨落动力学之间的关联。

Method: 采用金属玻璃的分子动力学模拟，分析能量景观导航过程，并区分两类能量注入路径：过屈服区基于 Herschel-Bulkley 非线性流变标度的宏观塑性剪切功一阶响应，以及亚屈服区由二阶局域弹性应力涨落主导的机制。

Result: 过屈服区 rejuvenation 由宏观塑性剪切功一阶响应驱动；亚屈服区宏观功标度失效，其早期能量景观抬升与后续循环 aging 分别由局域弹性应力涨落作为"力学催化剂"与"噪声源"支配。高速率扰动提高深势能阱逃逸概率，抑制结构弛豫的保留效率；并建立弛豫衰减指数与应力涨落指数之间的精确标度恒等式。

Conclusion: 局域应力涨落是速率衰减型亚屈服弛豫的主要起源，该工作将非线性流变学与应力涨落动力学直接桥接，为预测与调控非晶固体的结构演化提供统一框架。

Abstract: Whether sub-yield aging and over-yield rejuvenation in cyclically deformed amorphous solids share a common physical origin or arise from distinct microscopic mechanisms remains a fundamental yet unresolved debate. Using molecular dynamics simulations of a metallic glass, we resolve this duality by uncovering a unified dynamic clock that dictates energy landscape navigation across both regimes. We show that the transition between regimes reflects a fundamental shift in energy injection pathways: (i) In the over-yield regime, rejuvenation is driven as a first-order response to macroscopic plastic shear work derived from Herschel-Bulkley non-linear rheological scaling. (ii) In contrast, this macroscopic work scaling fails in the sub-yield regime, where early-stage energy landscape elevation and subsequent cyclic aging are instead governed by second-order localized elastic stress fluctuations acting as a mechanical catalyst and noise source, respectively. Intense high-rate agitation increases the escape probability from deeper potential energy minima, thereby suppressing the retention efficiency of structural relaxation. By identifying these localized fluctuations as the primary origin of rate-attenuated sub-yield relaxation, we establish an exact scaling identity relating the relaxation attenuation exponent to the stress fluctuation exponent. This work directly bridges non-linear rheology with stress fluctuation dynamics, providing a unified framework to predict and manipulate structural evolution in amorphous solids.

</details>


### [127] [Distribution Functions and Moments of Harmonically Trapped Inertial Run-and-Tumble Particles in a Shear-Thickening Medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21650)
*Subhanker Howlader,Prasenjit Das*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 研究一维谐振势中非热惯性活性粒子在剪切增稠介质中的动力学，结合数值求解 Fokker-Planck 方程与运动方程，发现稳态位置与速度分布随翻转率 λ 出现多重转变并在大 λ 下趋于高斯型，且 ⟨xv⟩(t) 的弛豫随 λ 增大而加快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画非热（athermal）惯性活性粒子在剪切增稠（shear-thickening）介质中的受限动力学，弥补活性粒子与非线性黏性介质耦合下稳态统计与时间弛豫认识不足的问题。

Method: 以一维谐振势约束粒子，活性由强度 Σ、翻转率 λ 的对称二分噪声（dichotomous noise）描述以模拟 run-and-tumble 粒子；介质剪切增稠通过速度依赖黏性力 g(v) ~ tan(v) 引入。基于对应 Fokker-Planck 方程，用有限体积迎风格式（finite-volume upwind scheme）数值计算含时联合概率密度 f(x,v,t)，并数值求解运动方程获得稳态分布，二者相互验证。

Result: 在足够大的 t 下，由 f(x,v,t) 得到的边际分布与运动方程给出的稳态位置分布 P_s(x)、速度分布 W_s(v) 高度吻合。P_s(x) 与 W_s(v) 随 λ 变化呈现若干有趣转变，并在 λ 足够大时趋近高斯型（Gaussian-like）分布。位置-速度关联 ⟨xv⟩(t) 显示，在其他参数固定时 λ 越大，弛豫至稳态越快。

Conclusion: Fokker-Planck 数值解与运动方程模拟结果一致，表明该框架可自洽描述剪切增稠介质中受限惯性活性粒子的稳态统计；翻转率 λ 是调控分布形态转变与弛豫速率的关键参数。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of an athermal inertial active particle confined in a harmonic potential and moving through shear-thickening media in one dimension. The activity is modeled using a symmetric dichotomous noise of strength $Σ$ and flipping rate $λ$, which mimics a run-and-tumble particle. The shear-thickening response of the medium is incorporated through a velocity-dependent viscous force of the form $g(v) \sim \tan(v)$. Starting from the Fokker-Planck equations corresponding to the particle's dynamics, we numerically compute the time-dependent joint probability density function $f(x,v,t)$ using a finite-volume upwind scheme. Next, we numerically solve the particle's equations of motion to obtain the steady-state position $P_s(x)$ and velocity $W_s(v)$ distributions and compare them with the marginal distributions obtained from $f(x,v,t)$ at sufficiently large $t$. The two approaches show excellent agreement. Both $P_s(x)$ and $W_s(v)$ exhibit several intriguing transitions as $λ$ is varied, and they eventually approach Gaussian-like profiles at sufficiently large $λ$. Our study of the time-dependent position-velocity correlation, $\langle xv \rangle(t)$, shows that larger values of $λ$ lead to faster relaxation to the steady state when all other parameters are held constant.

</details>


### [128] [Spatiotemporally correlated activity regulation of random-organizing hyperuniform states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21769)
*Yuhan Peng,Yusheng Lei,Ran Ni*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究证明质心守恒（center-of-mass conservation, COM conservation）并非超均匀性（hyperuniformity）的充分条件：在保持质心守恒的前提下，时空关联的活性调控可破坏超均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界长期将质心守恒视为生成非平衡动态超均匀态（nonequilibrium dynamic hyperuniform states）的重要机制，但其是否为超均匀性的充分条件尚不明确。

Method: 采用粒子模拟（particle simulations）与线性涨落理论（linear fluctuating theory），构建二元混合物：独立演化的驱动粒子的局部密度调控被调控粒子的活性，后者遵循保持质心守恒的偏置随机组织（biased random organization）规则。

Result: 该二元混合物构成反例，显示在质心守恒仍成立的情况下，时空关联的活性调控可导致超均匀性被破坏。

Conclusion: 超均匀性同时取决于活性的空间与时间关联以及支配粒子位移的守恒律；这澄清了质心守恒作为通向超均匀性路径的局限，并强调在设计非平衡多重超均匀混合物（multihyperuniform mixtures）时控制活性关联的重要性。

Abstract: Center-of-mass (COM) conservation has been widely accepted as an important mechanism for generating nonequilibrium dynamic hyperuniform states with vanishing long-wavelength density fluctuations, but whether it is sufficient for hyperuniformity remains unclear. Here, using particle simulations and a linear fluctuating theory, we show that spatiotemporally correlated activity regulation can destroy hyperuniformity while preserving COM conservation. We construct a binary mixture in which the local density of independently evolving driver particles regulates the activity of regulated particles following COM-conserving biased random organization. This counterexample demonstrates that hyperuniformity depends on the spatial and temporal correlations of activity as well as the conservation laws governing particle displacements. Our findings clarify the limits of COM conservation as a route to hyperuniformity and highlight the importance of controlling activity correlations in the design of nonequilibrium multihyperuniform mixtures.

</details>


### [129] [Drying-Induced Size Segregation and Stratification in Colloidal Droplets across Binary, Ternary, and Polydisperse Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21816)
*Zhoaxuan Liu,Marjolein Dijkstra*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 通过布朗动力学（Brownian dynamics, BD）与多粒子碰撞动力学（multi-particle collision dynamics, MPCD）模拟，研究干燥液滴内二元、三元及多分散胶体混合物的蒸发诱导尺寸偏析与分层，揭示液滴受限干燥可作为构建径向尺寸梯度的通用策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究干燥液滴内胶体颗粒的蒸发诱导尺寸偏析（size segregation）与分层（stratification）行为，并将已有平面薄膜干燥研究的结论拓展到液滴受限几何与更复杂混合物体系，为分级结构超颗粒的可控制备提供依据。

Method: 采用布朗动力学（BD）模拟研究二元、三元及多分散胶体混合物在干燥液滴中的蒸发诱导分层；并以多粒子碰撞动力学（MPCD）模拟评估流体动力学相互作用（hydrodynamic interactions）的作用。

Result: 三类混合物均出现干燥诱导分层，大颗粒倾向聚集于液滴内部、小颗粒富集于表面。二元体系中，偏析随Peclet数与粒径差增大、随大颗粒相对体积分数与初始浓度降低而更显著；三元体系在高Peclet数且中间粒径适当时形成三层结构，增加中间颗粒比例可增强小颗粒与大颗粒的分离。多分散体系中，分层条件下平均粒径随径向距离增大而减小，Peclet数越高、粒径分布两端颗粒越多，径向梯度越陡。BD预测的分层强于MPCD，但MPCD中分层仍存在。结果与既往平面薄膜干燥研究定性一致。

Conclusion: 液滴受限干燥为调控胶体组装体的径向组分与尺寸梯度提供了简单而通用的策略，并为分层结构超颗粒（supraparticles）的可控构筑带来新机遇；流体动力学相互作用会削弱但不会消除分层。

Abstract: We study evaporation-induced size segregation and stratification in binary, ternary, and polydisperse mixtures of colloidal particles within a drying droplet using Brownian dynamics (BD) simulations. We show that drying-induced stratification can occur in all three types of mixtures, with larger particles preferentially accumulating in the droplet interior and smaller particles near the droplet surface. In binary mixtures, size segregation becomes more pronounced as the Peclet number and the size difference between particles increase, and as the relative volume fraction of large particles and the initial particle concentration decrease. In ternary mixtures, three distinct layers form when the Peclet number is sufficiently high and the intermediate particle size is suitably selected. Under the conditions studied, increasing the fraction of intermediate-sized particles can also enhance separation between the small and large particles. These results are in qualitative agreement with previous studies of drying planar films. Extending the analysis to polydisperse systems, we find that the average particle size decreases with increasing radial distance under stratifying conditions. Furthermore, higher Peclet numbers and particle-size distributions with more particles at both size extremes lead to steeper radial gradients in particle size. Additionally, we use multi-particle collision dynamics (MPCD) simulations to assess the role of hydrodynamic interactions. BD simulations predict stronger stratification than MPCD simulations, although stratification persists in the MPCD simulations. Our results thus demonstrate that droplet-confined drying offers a simple and versatile strategy for engineering radial composition and size gradients in colloidal assemblies, thereby offering new opportunities for the controlled fabrication of hierarchically structured supraparticles.

</details>


### [130] [From Action Principle to Stochastic Thermodynamics of Elastic and Viscous Active Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21835)
*Valentina Barletta,Michele Lupini,Ian D. Estabrook,Laeschkir Würthner,Isabella R. Graf,Caren Norden,Lamberto Rondoni,Anna Erzberger,Roman Belousov*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文从最小作用量原理（principle of least action）与 Rayleighian 形式出发，构建了活性材料（active materials）的随机热弹性动力学（stochastic thermo- and elastodynamics）框架，将质心集体坐标与流动速度作为正则共轭对，统一了多种软物质与活性流体的描述，并模拟了波动下弹性细胞核的边界驱动变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究受生命物质复杂材料性质的启发，旨在为活性材料建立一个统一的随机热弹性动力学理论框架，以整合原本分散的弹性与黏性软物质描述。

Method: 基于经典最小作用量原理（principle of least action）与外力下的 Rayleighian 形式推导；将局部质心集体坐标（barycentric collective coordinate）与流动速度（streaming velocity）取为正则共轭对，并以集体场变量的拉格朗日力学（Lagrangian mechanics）进行表述。

Result: 该框架统一了脂质膜 Helfrich 理论（Helfrich theory）、薄壳与可变形液滴的线性与非线性弹性、以及活性与被动流体的流体动力学（hydrodynamics）等多种描述；并表明集体场变量的拉格朗日力学可将统计力学（statistical mechanics）的结果与定理直接应用于现有复杂软物质与生命物质模型。作为示例，推导并模拟了存在涨落时弹性细胞核的边界驱动变形模型。

Conclusion: 该随机弹性动力学（stochastic elastodynamics）框架在生物学中具有应用潜力，其统一性使统计力学的定理可迁移至既有软物质与活性材料模型，并以弹性细胞核变形模拟作为可行性示例。

Abstract: Motivated by the complex material properties of living matter, we derive a framework of stochastic thermo- and elastodynamics for active materials from the classical principle of least action and the Rayleighian formalism for external forces. Our approach couples the local barycentric collective coordinate and the streaming velocity as the canonical conjugate pair, which unifies various descriptions of elastic and viscous soft matter, including Helfrich theory for lipid membranes, linear and nonlinear elasticity of thin shells and deformable droplets, as well as hydrodynamics of active and passive fluids. The Lagrangian mechanics of collective field variables permits direct applications of the results and theorems from statistical mechanics to existing models of complex soft and living matter. To illustrate the potential applications of stochastic elastodynamics in biology, we derive and simulate a boundary-driven deformation model of an elastic cell nucleus in the presence of fluctuations.

</details>


### [131] [Phase coexistence and nucleation of aging condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21917)
*Yicheng Qiang,Chengjie Luo,David Zwicker*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究将相分离的Flory-Huggins模型与老化动力学的陷阱模型（trap model）结合，用以刻画生物分子凝聚体（biomolecular condensates）从液态向固态老化的基本行为，并给出包含亚稳共存液态区的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物分子凝聚体通过相分离形成，并常随时间从液态液滴老化转变为固态。为捕捉老化凝聚体的基本行为，需要一个统一描述其热力学与动力学的理论框架。

Method: 将相分离的Flory-Huggins模型与老化动力学的陷阱模型（trap model）相连接，构建组合模型，该模型基于一个基本自由能结构。

Result: 组合模型给出丰富的相图（phase diagram），其中包括亚稳共存液态（metastable coexisting liquids）区域；致密的亚稳液态作为玻璃成核路径中的中间态，使玻璃呈现两步成核（two-step nucleation）。

Conclusion: 结果具有普适性，因其源自基本自由能结构，为描述老化生物分子凝聚体的热力学与动力学提供了统一基础。

Abstract: Biomolecular condensates form by phase separation and often age over time by transitioning from liquid-like droplets to solid-like states. To capture the fundamental behavior of aging condensates, we connect the Flory-Huggins model of phase separation with the trap model of aging dynamics. This combined model yields a rich phase diagram, including a regime of metastable coexisting liquids. Interestingly, the dense metastable liquid serves as an intermediate state in the nucleation pathway of the glass, which thus nucleates in two steps. Our results are generic, since they follow from a basic free-energy structure, providing a unified foundation for describing the thermodynamics and kinetics of aging biomolecular condensates.

</details>


### [132] [PANDA-D: Interface-free determination of two dynamic contact angles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21996)
*Georgii K. Vandyshev,Grigorii S. Gorkaev,Nikolay D. Kondratyuk,Kirill M. Gerke,Ilia V. Kopanichuk*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: PANDA-D 通过联合拟合密度分布与层状质心位移，可在不提取液–气界面轮廓的前提下同时测定受驱液滴的前进角与后退角。该方法在合成算例中重构误差为 0.29°（均方根），在力驱动 Lennard–Jones 液卷中与独立圆拟合结果一致至 0.84°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子模拟中的动态接触角（dynamic contact angle）通常依赖二维液–气轮廓（liquid–vapor contour）与近壁切线拟合（near-wall tangent fit）提取，这类方法引入阈值设定与切线外推，易产生人为误差且难以自动化。此外，仅凭密度分布无法判断哪一侧弯月面（meniscus）处于前进状态，存在方向歧义。

Method: 提出 PANDA-D，作为 Predicting Angle from Nanoscale Density Analysis（PANDA）的扩展。方法不提取界面，而是联合拟合密度分布与相对于液卷（roll）的逐层质心位移（layerwise center-of-mass displacement），以消除前进/后退方向的歧义；再通过圆弯月面模型（circular-meniscus model）将剖面映射为后退角、前进角及有效近壁偏移量（effective near-wall offset）。

Result: 在具有预设角度的合成液卷上，重构误差为 0.29°（均方根）。对晶壁间力驱动的 Lennard–Jones 液卷，联合拟合可同时复现两组测量剖面，而仅用密度的拟合虽能匹配密度却会给出错误的非对称性；所得角度与独立圆拟合角度一致至 0.84°（均方根）。在四种壁–流体吸引强度下，后退角随驱动力减小、前进角随驱动力增大，在所研究范围内近似线性。

Conclusion: 由于在主要估计中排除了轮廓阈值与近壁切线外推，双剖面方法（two-profile approach）可实现跨分子轨迹的可重复、自动化接触角分析。

Abstract: Contact angles are central descriptors of wetting and capillary behavior at solid--fluid interfaces. Dynamic contact angles in molecular simulations are commonly extracted from two-dimensional liquid--vapor contours and near-wall tangent fits. We introduce PANDA-D, an extension of Predicting Angle from Nanoscale Density Analysis (PANDA) that determines both contact angles of a driven liquid roll without extracting an interface. The density profile measures occupied layer width but does not identify which meniscus is advancing. The method resolves this ambiguity by jointly fitting density and the layerwise center-of-mass displacement relative to the roll. A circular-meniscus model maps these profiles to receding and advancing angles and an effective near-wall offset. On synthetic rolls with prescribed angles, the reconstruction error is $0.29^\circ$ root-mean-square. We then analyze force-driven Lennard--Jones rolls between crystalline walls. Joint fits reproduce both measured profiles, whereas density-only fits can match density while assigning incorrect asymmetry. The resulting angles agree with independently determined circle-fit angles to $0.84^\circ$ root-mean-square. Across four wall--fluid attractions, the receding angle decreases and the advancing angle increases with force, approximately linearly over the studied ranges. By eliminating contour thresholds and near-wall tangent extrapolation from the primary estimate, the two-profile approach enables reproducible, automated angle analysis across molecular trajectories.

</details>


<div id='physics.hist-ph'></div>

# physics.hist-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [On Algebraic Background-Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22042)
*Luka Vinck,Sebastian De Haro*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 该文以代数进路（algebraic approach）为切入点，讨论半经典引力（semiclassical gravity）中背景独立性（background-independence）的含义。作者通过将观测者世界线（worldline）上的可观测量进行引力修饰（gravitational dressing），构造出不依赖具体时空实现的 *-代数（*-algebra）。在比较文献中三种背景独立性概念后，提出与表示独立性（representation-independence）相关的新型代数背景独立性概念，并引申出半经典与微扰两种背景独立性概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期有主张称代数方法可对半经典引力给出背景无关描述，但其中涉及的背景独立性概念尚未被清晰界定与批判性评估。本文旨在为量子引力（quantum gravity）的代数进路提供入门指引，并厘清该进路所诉诸的背景独立性含义。

Method: 采用代数框架（algebraic framework），沿宇宙学情境中观测者的世界线构造可观测量代数；通过将可观测量"引力修饰"到观测者世界线，使抽象 *-代数层面无需指涉时空背景，不同背景对应代数上的不同态（states）。随后将该框架与文献中三种有影响力的背景独立性概念进行比较分析。

Result: 论证现有三种背景独立性概念均未能完全刻画该 *-代数被视为背景无关的含义。据此提炼出一种与 *-代数表示独立性相绑定的新型"代数背景独立性"（algebraic background-independence）概念，并进一步导出两个适用于半经典理论的变体：半经典背景独立性（semiclassical background-independence）与微扰背景独立性（perturbative background-independence）。

Conclusion: 该代数式背景独立性概念为评估半经典量子引力方法适用范围与局限提供了有用工具，同时构成对半经典引力背景无关描述的批判性澄清。

Abstract: It has recently been claimed that algebraic methods can be used to give a background-independent description of semiclassical gravity. This paper aims to provide an entry point into recent algebraic approaches to quantum gravity and to analyse and critically assess the conception of background-independence here involved. We discuss an algebraic framework for formulating an algebra of observables along the worldline of an observer in a cosmological setting. By 'gravitationally dressing' the observables to the observer's worldline, the reference to the spacetime background can be set aside at the level of the abstract *-algebra. Thus this *-algebra is taken to be independent of any particular spacetime realisation, with different states on the algebra corresponding to different backgrounds. We compare this algebraic framework to three influential notions of background-independence in the literature and argue that none of these fully captures the sense in which the *-algebra is taken to be background-independent. Building on this, we extract a novel notion of algebraic background-independence tied to the representation-independence of the *-algebra. This algebraic conception motivates two further concepts of background-independence tailored to semiclassical theories: semiclassical background-independence and perturbative background-independence. We suggest that these provide useful tools for evaluating the scope and limitations of semiclassical approaches to quantum gravity.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Enabling large language model agents for high-fidelity many-body perturbation theory calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20779)
*Benran Zhang,Byeongchan Lee,Zien Zhu,Chih-En Hsu,Zhenfa Zheng,Hung-Chung Hsueh,Young Woo Choi,Mauro Del Ben,Zhenglu Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作评估了大型语言模型（large language model, LLM）智能体在多体微扰论（many-body perturbation theory, MBPT）工作流构建中的可靠性，发现前沿模型无法一次性正确搭建 GW、GW-BSE 与 GWPT 流程；为此提出结合专家知识与确定性执行的 AI-Donkey 框架，使各类模型均能生成完整可运行的工作流，并实现一维、二维与体相材料的自主计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBPT 方法（含 GW 近似、GW-BSE、GWPT）可对材料激发态性质与多粒子相互作用给出准确的第一性原理描述，但其应用受限于多软件工作流复杂、物理参数相互依赖以及对理论专业知识的高要求。LLM 智能体在自动化计算任务上展现潜力，但 MBPT 仍属未解决的挑战。

Method: 首先以工作流基准测试现有 LLM 智能体（包括 Opus 4.7、GPT-5.5 等前沿模型）在 GW、GW-BSE、GWPT 上的表现，分析其错误来源；随后提出 AI-Donkey 智能体框架（agentic framework），将专家整理的知识与确定性执行（deterministic execution）相结合，以支撑高保真的 GW、GW-BSE 与 GWPT 计算及分析。

Result: 开箱即用智能体在 GW、GW-BSE、GWPT 工作流基准上的平均成绩分别为 54%、36% 与 19%，表现出强烈的模型依赖性与较大的运行间波动，错误通常源于跨阶段不一致性且在引入若干阶段后才显现。采用 AI-Donkey 后，从专有前沿模型到开放权重模型的全部被测智能体均能生成完整、可运行的 MBPT 工作流，且未观察到运行间波动。

Conclusion: AI-Donkey 可在一维、二维与体相材料上实现自主计算与后处理，包括极性半导体中 Fröhlich 电子-声子耦合（Fröhlich electron-phonon coupling）的多体增强。该工作为计算材料科学中可靠智能体工作流的知识工程（knowledge engineering）提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Many-body perturbation theory (MBPT) methods, including the GW approximation, the GW plus Bethe-Salpeter equation (GW-BSE) method, and GW perturbation theory (GWPT), among others, provide accurate first-principles descriptions of excited-state properties and multiparticle interactions of materials. However, their use remains limited due to complex multi-software workflows, interdependent physical parameters, and substantial theoretical expertise. Large language model (LLM) agents, which couple a language model to software tools, show potential in automating computational tasks, but MBPT remains an unaddressed challenge. We show that LLM agents tested in our work, including frontier models such as Opus 4.7 and GPT-5.5, do not reliably construct MBPT workflows in one attempt, suggesting that correct setup requires expert knowledge and consistency that current models lack. Across tested model-harness combinations, out-of-the-box agents average 54%, 36%, and 19% on GW, GW-BSE, and GWPT workflow benchmarks, with strong model dependence and large run-to-run variation. Errors typically originate from cross-stage inconsistencies that surface only several stages after they are introduced. We therefore develop AI-Donkey, an agentic framework that combines expert-curated knowledge with deterministic execution to enable high-fidelity GW, GW-BSE, and GWPT calculations and analyses. With AI-Donkey, all tested agents, from frontier proprietary models to open-weight models, produce complete, runnable MBPT workflows with no observed run-to-run variation. We further demonstrate autonomous calculations and post-processing on 1D, 2D, and bulk materials, including the many-body enhancement of Fröhlich electron-phonon coupling in polar semiconductors. Our approach provides a general framework for knowledge engineering to enable reliable agentic workflows in computational materials science.

</details>


### [135] [Identifying intercalation electrodes for electrochemical ion recovery and desalination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20834)
*Sougat Purohit,Andreas Kuhlmann,Marten Huck,Hans-Georg Steinrück,Gopalakrishnan Sai Gautam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过高通量计算工作流，从 Materials Project 数据库 20,414 种含 Li、Mg、Ca、Na、K 的结构中筛选适用于电化学离子捕获的插层电极（intercalation electrode），最终识别出 11 种有望用于选择性离子回收的电极和 1 种可能的脱盐（desalination）电极。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水资源同时满足饮用、工业和农业需求，并可作为有价值离子的来源；从水溶液中捕获离子可得到选择性离子回收（如 Li$^+$）的产物或可用的脱盐产水。电化学方法配合插层电极具有能效优势，但现有代表性电极（olivine-FePO$_4$、spinel-Mn$_2$O$_4$）存在若干缺陷，因此需要寻找更稳健的替代电极材料。

Method: 构建高通量计算工作流（high-throughput computational workflow），从 Materials Project 数据库中 20,414 种含 Li、Mg、Ca、Na 和/或 K 的结构中筛选潜在插层电极。具体手段包括：构建适用于海水条件下电极的 Pourbaix 图（Pourbaix diagram）、修正海水中亚稳态（metastability）的判定标准、计算平均插层电压（average intercalation voltage）与电极结构亚稳态程度，并利用计算图（computational graph）编码离子选择性。

Result: 识别出共 11 种有望用于选择性离子回收的电极以及 1 种可能的脱盐电极。选择性 Li 回收的候选材料包括 Fe$_2$(MoO$_4$)$_3$、Li$_3$Fe$_2$(PO$_4$)$_3$ 和 LiFeP$_2$O$_7$。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了热力学层面的洞见、定性趋势以及一份可供实验测试的插层电极候选清单，有望推动电化学离子回收（electrochemical ion recovery）领域的发展。

Abstract: Water sources serve as a requirement for human consumption, industrial, and agricultural needs and a resource for valuable ions. Appropriate treatment of water that captures ions from the aqueous solution can give rise to suitable `products', such as critical ions (such as Li$^+$) in selective ion recovery and usable water in the case of desalination (i.e., removal of non-essential ions). Among myriad ion capture techniques, we focus on electrochemical methods using intercalation electrodes, an energy-efficient approach capable of both selectively extracting specific ions or non-selectively extracting multiple ions from diverse water sources. However, practical realization of electrochemical ion capture requires the use of robust cation intercalation electrodes, with the state-of-the-art olivine-FePO$_4$ and spinel-Mn$_2$O$_4$ exhibiting several shortcomings. Thus, we develop a high-throughput computational workflow to identify potential intercalation electrodes for both ion recovery and desalination from a pool of 20,414 Li, Mg, Ca, Na, and/or K-containing structures from the Materials Project database. Based on the construction of Pourbaix diagrams relevant for electrodes operating under seawater conditions, modifying criteria used for defining metastability in seawater, computing average intercalation voltages and the extent of metastability of electrode structures, and computational graphs to encode ionic selectivity, we identify a total of 11 promising electrodes for selective ion recovery and one possible desalination electrode. The promising materials identified in this work include Fe$_2$(MoO$_4$)$_3$, Li$_3$Fe$_2$(PO$_4$)$_3$, LiFeP$_2$O$_7$ for selective Li-recovery. Our study provides thermodynamic insights, qualitative trends, and a promising list of intercalation electrodes for experimental testing that should advance the field of electrochemical ion recovery.

</details>


### [136] [Transition-Metal-Oxygen Covalency Controls Ligand-Hole Stability in Li-Rich Cathode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20837)
*Kuan H. Hsu,Eder G. Lomeli,Jaehoon Kim,Gukhyun Lim,Amelia Zhao,Wen-Shin Lu,Joshua J. Kas,John Vinson,Brian Moritz,Jihyun Hong,Wanli Yang,Thomas P. Devereaux*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过核心能级谱（core-level spectroscopy）建模，比较Li2MO3（M = Mn, Ru, Ir）的电荷补偿机制，指出TM-O共价性（covalency）而非氧空穴（oxygen hole）是否存在，是决定高价氧化还原可逆性的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富锂正极（lithium-rich cathodes）因过渡金属（transition metal, TM）配位过量锂而具备高比容量，但利用这部分锂会将体系推向异常高的形式氧化态，引发以气体释放为信号的副反应，通常归因于氧氧化。不同TM化学组成导致循环性能差异巨大，表明TM与O活性均起关键作用，但其细节尚不明确。

Method: 对Li2MO3（M = Mn, Ru, Ir）正极材料建模核心能级谱（core-level spectroscopy），以解析不同体系间电荷补偿机制（charge compensation mechanism）的差异。

Result: 配体空穴（ligand holes）在整个系列中均重要，但其稳定性强烈依赖过渡金属-氧电子结构。Li2MnO3基态已呈强负电荷转移（negative-charge-transfer）特征，脱锂会迅速增强配体空穴特性并促发不稳定性；由Mn转向Ru和Ir可提高TM-O杂化（hybridization）并降低走向极端负电荷转移态的倾向，使额外空穴在更宽电荷范围内离域于共价TM-O网络。

Conclusion: TM-O共价性（covalency），而非氧空穴特征的缺失，是控制可逆高价氧化还原（reversible high-valent redox）的关键因素；这为在不依赖可逆分子氧（molecular oxygen）形成的前提下稳定高能量密度正极提供了路径。

Abstract: Lithium-rich cathodes boast a large specific capacity due to the presence of excess lithium per transition metal. However, making use of that excess Li drives the cathodes to unusually high formal oxidation states, often triggering unwanted side reactions, typically signaled by gas release and commonly associated with oxygen oxidation. By varying the transition metal (TM) chemistry in Li-rich materials, cyclability can vary drastically, with some materials suffering rapid capacity fade while others are able to maintain large and stable voltage windows. Such observations indicate a crucial role for both TM and O activities, but the details remain elusive. Here, we model core-level spectroscopy to understand differences in the charge compensation mechanism between different Li2MO3 (M = Mn, Ru, Ir) cathode materials. We find that ligand holes are important throughout the series, but their stability depends strongly on the transition-metal-oxygen electronic structure. In Li2MnO3, the ground state is already strongly negative-charge-transfer-like, so further delithiation rapidly increases ligand-hole character and promotes instability. Moving from Mn to Ru and Ir increases TM-O hybridization while reducing the tendency toward an extreme negative-charge-transfer state, allowing additional holes to remain delocalized over the covalent TM-O network over a wider range of charge. These results identify TM-O covalency, rather than the absence of oxygen-hole character, as a key factor controlling reversible high-valent redox and suggest a route for stabilizing high-energy-density cathodes without invoking reversible molecular oxygen formation.

</details>


### [137] [Grain-Size Dependence of Thermal and Electrical Properties in (Cr,Mo,Ta,V,W)C1-δ High-Entropy Carbide Ceramics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20864)
*Ali Sarikhani,Gregory E. Hilmas,David W. Lipke,Douglas E. Wolfe,Stefano Curtarolo,Shen J. Dillon,William G. Fahrenholtz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 高熵碳化物（high-entropy carbides）$(Cr,Mo,Ta,V,W)C_{1-δ}$ 陶瓷经放电等离子烧结（spark plasma sintering）后晶粒从约 9 增至 29 μm，但室温热导率（6.8-7.1 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹）与电阻率（151.9-177.0 μΩ cm）均无系统性晶粒尺寸依赖，表明本征无序与缺陷化学可能主导输运性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵碳化物中热与电输运可能受晶界散射（grain-boundary scattering）影响，但相关微结构区间尚不明确。研究旨在厘清晶粒尺寸变化是否显著改变输运性质。

Method: 以放电等离子烧结在 1750-1950 °C 制备近全致密 $(Cr,Mo,Ta,V,W)C_{1-δ}$ 陶瓷，获得平均晶粒尺寸 9.3 ± 4.9 至 28.8 ± 15.3 μm 并保持岩盐（rock-salt）相。热容由实验测量并用 Neumann-Kopp 定律估算，后者用于计算热导率；同时测量室温电阻率。

Result: 室温热导率为 6.8-7.1 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹，电阻率为 151.9-177.0 μΩ cm，两者在考察晶粒尺寸范围内均无系统性晶粒尺寸依赖。据 Wiedemann-Franz 定律与理论洛伦兹数（Lorenz number）估算，电子对室温热导率贡献约占总热导率的 58-68%。

Conclusion: 在所研究条件下，加工引起的晶粒粗化（grain coarsening）未产生可清晰分辨的输运性质变化，说明本征无序（intrinsic disorder）与缺陷化学（defect chemistry）可能对性能施加更强影响。

Abstract: Thermal and electrical transport in high-entropy carbides can be influenced by grain-boundary scattering, but the relevant microstructural regime remains uncertain. Here, near-fully dense $(Cr,Mo,Ta,V,W)C_{1-δ}$ ceramics were densified by spark plasma sintering at 1750-1950 °C, producing average grain sizes from 9.3 $\pm$ 4.9 to 28.8 $\pm$ 15.3 $μ$m while retaining the rock-salt phase. Heat capacities were measured experimentally and estimated by the Neumann-Kopp law with the latter used to calculate thermal conductivity. Thermal conductivity values were in the range of 6.8-7.1 W $m^{-1}$ $K^{-1}$ at room temperature, but showed no systematic dependence on grain size within the investigated range. Room-temperature electrical resistivity values were in the range of 151.9 to 177.0 $μΩ$ cm and likewise showed no grain-size dependence. The electron contribution to room-temperature thermal conductivity was estimated to be approximately 58-68% of total thermal conductivity as estimated using the Wiedemann-Franz law and the theoretical Lorenz number. The results indicated that processing-induced grain coarsening did not produce clearly resolvable transport-property changes under the conditions investigated leading to the conclusion that intrinsic disorder and defect chemistry may exert stronger influences on properties.

</details>


### [138] [Sb-5$p$-mediated antiparallel Nd-Fe coupling in Nd$_3$Fe$_3$Sb$_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20883)
*S. Palazzese,Sh. Yamamoto,T. Kotte,R. Ray,F. Pabst,S. Chattopadhyay,D. I. Gorbunov,T. Herrmannsdörfer,K. Kummer,A. Kurtanidze,E. Weschke,O. Prokhnenko,K. Yamamoto,Y. Kotani,H. Nojiri,M. Ruck,Manuel Richter,J. Wosnitza*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合高场 X 射线磁圆二色（XMCD）谱、磁化测量与密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，揭示了 Nd$_3$Fe$_3$Sb$_7$ 中反平行 Nd-Fe 耦合的微观机制，即经由 Fe 3$d$-Sb 5$p$-Nd 5$d$ 杂化路径实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在由轻稀土与过渡金属组成的金属间化合物中，反平行的稀土-过渡金属（$R$-$T$）耦合并非常见现象，其微观起源尚不明确，该文旨在阐明 Nd$_3$Fe$_3$Sb$_7$ 中这一反常耦合的机制。

Method: 采用高场 X 射线磁圆二色（XMCD）谱学，辅以磁化测量与密度泛函理论（DFT）计算；其中 Sb $M_{4,5}$ 边 XMCD 用于探测 Sb 5$p$ 态的自旋极化，脉冲强场 XMCD 用于追踪场诱导的自旋重取向。

Result: Sb $M_{4,5}$ 边 XMCD 显示 Sb 5$p$ 态的自旋极化与 Fe 3$d$ 磁矩反平行；DFT 计算揭示 Sb 磁矩具有位点依赖特征，并联合 XMCD 观测到的 Fe-Sb 反平行排列，确认 Fe 3$d$-Sb 5$p$-Nd 5$d$ 杂化路径；脉冲强场 XMCD 观察到 Nd 与 Fe 磁矩 $c$ 轴投影演化所导致的场诱导自旋重取向。

Conclusion: Nd$_3$Fe$_3$Sb$_7$ 为在含 $p$ 元素的轻稀土-过渡金属（light-$R$-$T$）金属间化合物中，探索由过渡金属 3$d$-配体 $p$-稀土 5$d$ 杂化所介导的反平行 $R$-$T$ 耦合提供了平台。

Abstract: We uncover the microscopic mechanism underlying the antiparallel Nd-Fe coupling in the intermetallic 4$f$-3$d$-5$p$ compound Nd$_3$Fe$_3$Sb$_7$ by means of high-field x-ray magnetic circular dichroism (XMCD) spectroscopy, complemented by magnetization measurements and density-functional-theory (DFT) calculations. This coupling is unexpected for intermetallics composed of light rare-earth and transition-metal elements. Sb $M_{4,5}$-edge XMCD measurements indicate a clear spin polarization of Sb 5$p$ states antiparallel to the Fe 3$d$ moments. DFT calculations reveal the site-dependent character of the Sb moments and, together with the Fe-Sb antiparallel alignment seen by XMCD, identify a Fe 3$d$-Sb 5$p$-Nd 5$d$ hybridization pathway for the antiparallel Nd-Fe coupling. Pulsed-field XMCD reveals a field-induced spin reorientation through the evolution of the $c$-axis projections of the Nd and Fe moments. Nd$_3$Fe$_3$Sb$_7$, thus, provides a platform for exploring antiparallel rare-earth-transition-metal ($R$-$T$) coupling mediated by transition-metal 3$d$-ligand $p$-rare-earth 5$d$ hybridization in light-$R$-$T$ intermetallics with $p$-elements.

</details>


### [139] [Material dependence of direct light-induced charge injection from metallic nanoparticles into adsorbates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20886)
*Jordan Alan Edwards,Johannes Lischner*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文建立了一种预测大尺寸球形金属纳米粒子向吸附CO分子注入热电子与热空穴速率的理论方法，结合从头算密度泛函理论与宏观电动力学，对九种金属进行计算，发现热电子注入普遍强于热空穴注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属纳米粒子通过局域表面等离激元的化学界面阻尼（chemical interface damping）直接向吸附分子注入电荷，被视为规避热载流子快速热化（thermalization）的可行途径，但缺乏对注入速率的预测手段。

Method: 方法将吸附物-金属相互作用以从头算密度泛函理论（ab initio density functional theory）细致描述，并与局域表面等离激元（localized surface plasmon）的宏观电动力学（macroscopic electrodynamics）处理相结合，计算大尺寸球形纳米粒子向吸附CO分子的热电子与热空穴注入速率。

Result: 应用于九种金属，包括传统等离激元金属（Ag、Au、Cu）、新兴等离激元金属（Al）及非等离激元金属（Ni、Rh、Pd、Ir、Pt）。所有金属中热电子注入均大于热空穴注入，源于CO前线轨道排列：最低未占据分子轨道（LUMO）比最高占据分子轨道（HOMO）更靠近金属费米能级。光学光子范围内电子注入速率最大者为Al、Ag、Cu、Au，其中Cu与Au表现出显著的等离激元增强；非等离激元金属因LUMO相对金属费米能级较高而注入较弱。

Conclusion: 研究结果为针对特定化学过程的金属纳米尺度光催化剂（photocatalysts）的理性设计提供了途径。

Abstract: Direct injection of charge from metallic nanoparticles into adsorbed molecules via the chemical interface damping of localized surface plasmons has emerged as a promising avenue to avoid the rapid thermalization of hot carriers. In this paper, we develop an approach for predicting hot-electron and hot-hole injection rates from large spherical nanoparticles into adsorbed carbon monoxide (CO) molecules. The approach combines a detailed description of the adsorbate-metal interactions via ab initio density functional theory with a macroscopic electrodynamics treatment of the localized surface plasmon. We apply this approach to nine different metals including both traditional (Ag, Au, Cu) and emerging (Al) plasmonic metals as well as non-plasmonic metals (Ni, Rh, Pd, Ir, Pt). For all metals, hot-electron injection is larger than hot-hole injection. This is a consequence of the alignment of the CO frontier orbitals, with the CO lowest unoccupied molecular orbital (LUMO) being closer to the metal Fermi energy than the highest occupied molecular orbital (HOMO). The largest electron injection rates in the optical photon ranges are obtained for Al, Ag, Cu and Au, with Cu and Au showing significant plasmonic enhancement. The non-plasmonic metals show weak electron injection as a consequence of their high LUMO energies relative to the metal Fermi level. The results of our study pave the way towards a rational design of metallic nanoscale photocatalysts for specific chemical processes.

</details>


### [140] [MASON: Materials Atomistic Structure builder Operated in Natural language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21015)
*Xinyue Zhang,Jixiang Li,Bin Shao,Baishun Yang,Zhiyang Liu,Weichao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: MASON 是一个面向原子尺度结构构建的 AI 原生工具包，可通过模型上下文协议（Model Context Protocol, MCP）被大语言模型智能体调用，从而可靠地完成复杂晶体结构建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理计算中构建可靠的原子尺度模型（atomistic model）既关键又困难；大语言模型智能体（large language model agent）虽能辅助结构生成，却缺乏直接的晶体学操作（crystallographic operation）能力，难以稳定构建复杂结构。

Method: 提出 MASON，一个 AI 原生的原子结构构建工具包，通过模型上下文协议（Model Context Protocol, MCP）供智能体调用，为其配备专用的结构构建工具。

Result: 在一个端到端（end-to-end）示例中，智能体依据自然语言请求成功构建了 CO/Pt(111) 吸附模型（adsorption model）。

Conclusion: 通过为智能体提供专用结构构建工具，MASON 实现了可靠且高效的原子模型构建，并为自主第一性原理工作流（autonomous first-principles workflow）奠定基础。

Abstract: Building reliable atomistic models is a critical and often challenging step in first-principles calculations. Although large language model agents can assist with structure generation, they lack direct capabilities for crystallographic operations and therefore struggle to construct complex structures reliably. Here we present MASON, an AI-native toolkit for atomistic structure construction that can be invoked by agents through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). In an end-to-end example, an agent builds a CO/Pt(111) adsorption model from a natural language request. By equipping agents with dedicated structure-building tools, MASON enables reliable and efficient atomistic model construction and provides a foundation for autonomous first-principles workflows.

</details>


### [141] [Large Language Model-Assisted Systematic Study of Anomalous Adsorption Phenomena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21050)
*Fengxu Yang,Matthew L. Schneider,Weitong Chen,Jack D. Evans*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用大语言模型（large language model）流水线从文献中挖掘软多孔晶体（soft porous crystals）的客体诱导相变事件，构建含547个相变、240种材料的数据库，并发现门控开启与呼吸两类行为可由压力响应函数区分，而非孔结构参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软多孔晶体的客体诱导结构转变可产生非常规吸附等温线，对气体分离与储存具有应用价值；但相关证据以叙述性形式散落于文献，尚未被系统汇总，阻碍了跨材料的比较分析。

Method: 采用大语言模型流水线对文献进行异常吸附挖掘，抽取相变事件及其材料、吸附质、温度和压力元数据，并将每个事件归类到既定的行为类别；提取结果以人工标注为基准进行评估，并进一步与晶体学结构关联。

Result: 提取阶段的F1分数达0.96；规模化应用后，从4252篇筛选文献中的200篇获得240种不同材料的547个相变，构成经整理并链接晶体学结构的语料库。分析显示门控开启（gate-opening）框架的转变压力显著低于呼吸（breathing）框架（p = 3.8E-3），而两类在孔径、空隙率、连接体化学或金属种类上未检测到差异。

Conclusion: 研究支持对响应性多孔材料采取功能性而非纯结构性的描述，并强调需要建立社区数据库，记录该类材料每一次已报道相变所对应的客体、温度与压力条件。

Abstract: Soft porous crystals can undergo guest-induced structural transitions producing unconventional adsorption isotherms, which are valuable for gas separation and storage applications. However, the evidence for these transitions is scattered across the literature in narrative form and has not been aggregated. Here we use a large language model pipeline to mine the literature for anomalous adsorption, extracting phase-transition events together with material, adsorbate, temperature and pressure metadata and classifying each into an established behavioural class. Benchmarked against human annotation, the extraction stage attains an F1 score of 0.96. When applied at scale, the pipeline yields a curated corpus of 547 transitions across 240 distinct materials, drawn from 200 of 4,252 screened publications, which we further link to crystallographic structures. Analysis of this database shows that the principal behavioural classes, gate-opening and breathing, are distinguished by their functional response. Gate-opening frameworks transform at a significantly lower pressure than breathing frameworks (p = 3.8E-3). In contrast, no difference in pore size, void fraction, linker chemistry or metal identity was detected between the classes. Our findings argue for a functional rather than purely structural description of responsive porous materials. They also highlight the need for a community database that records the guest, temperature and pressure of every reported transition of these materials.

</details>


### [142] [Broadband radio-frequency TEM specimen holders for in situ biasing excitation and 100-ps-scale electron beam control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21275)
*Christophe Gatel,Marine Resano,Bérangère Disic,Nasibeh Parsaei,Emmanuel Perrin,Hervé Parvery,Romain Queslier,Bertrand Gatti,Pierre Abeilhou,Alexandre Alibert,Christian Pertel,Cécile Marcelot,Nicolas Biziere*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作报道了自制的双通道与四通道射频（radio-frequency, RF）透射电子显微镜（transmission electron microscope, TEM）样品杆，采用同轴传输线与小型化阻抗匹配印制电路板（printed circuit board, PCB）结合的混合架构，实现GHz级宽带电激励与亚纳秒电子束控制的通用平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在实际工作频率下研究电驱动纳米器件，并发展亚纳秒量级的全电学时间分辨实验，需要在TEM内部实现宽带电激励。

Method: 设计基于同轴传输线与小型化阻抗控制PCB混合架构的双通道/四通道RF样品杆，通过可拆卸机械接触实现与可更换TEM芯片的免引线键合直接电连接；以矢量网络分析仪测量完整电通路的宽带传输，并用该架构驱动小型化静电电子束偏转器。

Result: 以代表性TEM芯片为例，双通道样品杆的-3 dB带宽为7.9 GHz，插入损耗在18 GHz以内保持低于12 dB；偏转器组件的时间响应兼容接近100 ps的电脉冲，最短可达时间受现有波形发生器限制；200 ps探测窗口下的高分辨TEM成像未显示空间分辨率的可检测退化，并在代表operando测量的条件下成功实施探测持续时间低至0.5 ns的离轴电子全息。

Conclusion: 结果建立了一个结合GHz电激励与亚纳秒电子束控制的通用宽带平台；Hitachi HF3300显微镜的双级配置为未来使用独立驱动RF样品杆分别进行样品激励与电子探针控制的全电学泵浦-探测（pump-probe）TEM方案提供了基础。

Abstract: Broadband electrical excitation inside a transmission electron microscope (TEM) is required to investigate electrically driven nanodevices at their actual operating frequencies and to develop fully electrical time-resolved experiments at a sub-nanosecond range. Here, we report the performances of homemade two- and four-channel radio-frequency (RF) TEM specimen holders based on a hybrid architecture combining coaxial transmission lines with miniaturized impedance-controlled printed circuit boards (PCB). Removable mechanical contacts provide direct electrical connection to interchangeable TEM chips without wire bonding. Vector network analyzer measurements demonstrate broadband transmission through the complete electrical path up to the specimen. With a representative TEM chip, the two-channel holder exhibits a -3 dB bandwidth of 7.9 GHz, while insertion losses remain below 12 dB up to 18 GHz. The same RF holder architecture was used to drive a miniaturized electrostatic electron-beam deflector. The measured temporal response indicates that the holder deflector assembly remains compatible with electrical pulse durations approaching 100 ps, with the shortest accessible times presently limited by the available waveform generator. High-resolution TEM imaging with a 200 ps probe window does not show detectable degradation of spatial resolution, while off-axis electron holography is successfully performed for probe durations down to 0.5 ns under conditions representative of operando measurements. These results establish a versatile broadband platform combining GHz electrical excitation and sub-nanosecond electron-beam control. The dual-stage configuration of our Hitachi HF3300 microscope further provides the basis for a future fully electrical pump-probe TEM approach using independently driven RF holders for specimen excitation and electron-probe control.

</details>


### [143] [Synergistic Graphene and PEDOT:PSS Layers on Silicon for Enhanced Efficiency and Stability in Schottky Junction Solar Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21563)
*Kentaro Nakajo,Hojun Im,Keiichiro Nishimura,Masahiro Teraoka,Masaya Kikuchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究在平面三层 PEDOT:PSS/Gr/n-Si 肖特基结（Schottky junction）结构中实现了效率与稳定性的协同提升，PCE 达 8.52%（对照 Gr/n-Si 为 4.56%），并凭借石墨烯的原子级不可渗透性在 75 天环境暴露后仍保持约 90% 初始效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯/n 型硅（Gr/n-Si）与 PEDOT:PSS/n-Si 太阳能电池（solar cells）虽具成本优势，但同时实现高功率转换效率（power conversion efficiency, PCE）与长期环境稳定性仍是严峻挑战。研究旨在通过三层混合结构同时改善这两项指标。

Method: 构建平面三层 PEDOT:PSS/Gr/n-Si 肖特基结（Schottky junction）架构，并在未织构（untextured）衬底上将混合器件与纯 Gr/n-Si、PEDOT:PSS/n-Si 参考电池进行系统对比，以分离碳晶格的光电与势垒功能。结合综合分析揭示 PEDOT:PSS 层与石墨烯在界面电荷传输中的互补作用。

Result: PEDOT:PSS 层同时充当抗反射涂层（anti-reflection coating）、对下层石墨烯的适度 p 型掺杂剂（p-type dopant）及电导增强层，优化界面电荷传输，使三层器件 PCE 达 8.52%，显著高于纯 Gr/n-Si 参考电池（4.56%）。稳定性方面，裸 PEDOT:PSS/n-Si 结在 75 天环境暴露后仅保留约 29% 初始效率，而石墨烯作为环境屏障抑制吸湿性 PEDOT:PSS 上层的湿气诱导降解（moisture-induced degradation），使三层器件同期保留约 90% 初始效率。

Conclusion: 石墨烯兼具高结晶电荷传输界面与不可渗透保护屏障（impermeable protective barrier）的双重互补角色，为设计高效、环境稳定的混合光伏器件（hybrid photovoltaics）提供了有效策略。

Abstract: While graphene/n-type silicon (Gr/n-Si) and PEDOT:PSS/n-Si solar cells offer a cost-effective alternative to conventional photovoltaics, achieving simultaneous high power conversion efficiency (PCE) and long-term environmental stability remains a formidable challenge. Here, we report the synergistic enhancement of both performance and stability in a planar trilayer PEDOT:PSS/Gr/n-Si Schottky junction architecture. By systematically benchmarking this hybrid device against pristine Gr/n-Si and PEDOT:PSS/n-Si reference cells fabricated on untextured substrates, we isolate the fundamental optoelectronic and barrier functions of the carbon lattice. Comprehensive analyses reveal that the PEDOT:PSS layer simultaneously functions as an anti-reflection coating, a moderate p-type dopant for the underlying graphene, and a conductivity enhancer, collectively optimizing interfacial charge transport. Consequently, the trilayer device achieves a PCE of 8.52%, markedly outperforming the pristine Gr/n-Si reference cell (4.56%). Crucially, the atomic impermeability of the graphene sheet serves as a robust environmental barrier. While bare PEDOT:PSS/n-Si junctions retained only ~29% of their initial efficiency after 75 days of ambient exposure, graphene shields the active junction from moisture-induced degradation of the hygroscopic PEDOT:PSS overlayer. This enables the trilayer device to retain approximately 90% of its initial efficiency over the same 75-day period. The complementary roles of graphene as both a highly crystalline charge transport interface and an impermeable protective barrier establish a highly effective strategy for engineering high-efficiency, environmentally stable hybrid photovoltaics.

</details>


### [144] [Wavelength-selective bidirectional plasticity in self-powered Se/ZnMgO visual synapses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21618)
*Zacharie Jehl Li-Kao,Miho Akasaka,Myeongok Kim,Kunal J. Tiwari,Antoine Moore,Mykhailo A. Koltsov,Sergio Giraldo,Saiki Kitagawa,Marcel Placidi,Taizo Kobayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作报道了 Se/ZnMgO 薄膜光电二极管（thin film photodiodes），其零偏压（zero bias）下的光电流可同时写入与读取突触权重，且权重变化方向仅由光子能量（photon energy）决定：365 nm 脉冲增强权重，455 nm 脉冲削弱权重，全程无需消耗电能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工视觉突触（artificial visual synapses）虽可在单一器件中集成感知、存储与处理，但多数需外加电压读取突触权重，且光通常只能增强而不能削弱权重，缺乏双向、无源调控手段。

Method: 采用 Se/ZnMgO 薄膜光电二极管，在零偏压下以光电流作为权重读写量；通过不同波长（365 nm 与 455 nm）等光子通量脉冲及光谱分辨的光浸泡（light soaking）实验，结合光学仿真与漂移-扩散仿真（drift-diffusion simulations）分析界面缺陷（interface defects）的作用，并在柔性衬底上测试弯折调制与再学习行为。

Result: 等光子通量下 365 nm 脉冲使光电流增加 38 %，455 nm 脉冲使其下降，高光子通量下抑制（depression）达 30 %；每条分支最多可分辨 19 个态，且不消耗电能。短波长增强、低能量光擦除，交叉点由界面缺陷密度决定；存储态可被反向偏压（reverse bias，电压依赖）与亚带隙光（sub-gap light）擦除。器件具备再学习特性，响应受柔性衬底弯折调制；基于实测权重更新的网络在手写数字识别中达 89 % 精度（无噪声）与 85 %（含实测噪声），浮点权重为 97.5 %。

Conclusion: 该器件实现了由光子能量单独调控方向的无源光突触权重更新，其机制归因于界面缺陷被电子填充所对应的增强过程，为低功耗、可双向调控的视觉突触硬件及神经网络应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Artificial visual synapses combine sensing, memory and processing in a single device, but most of them need an external voltage to read the synaptic weight and light is generally able to strengthen the weight rather than to weaken it. We report here Se/ZnMgO thin film photodiodes in which the photocurrent at zero bias writes and reads the synaptic weight, with a direction of change set by the photon energy alone. At equal photon flux, 365 nm pulses increase the photocurrent by 38 % while 455 nm pulses decrease it, and the depression reaching 30 % at higher flux, with up to 19 distinguishable levels per branch and no electrical energy spent. Spectrally resolved light soaking shows that short wavelengths potentiate while low-energy light erases. The interesting property is therefore the photon energy-dependent competition between potentiation and depression, the crossover being set by the defect population of the interface. Optical simulations show that both wavelengths are absorbed within the first tens of nanometres of Se, and the stored state is erased by a reverse bias in a voltage-dependent way and by sub-gap light, which indicates that potentiation corresponds to interface defects filled with electrons, in agreement with drift-diffusion simulations. The devices also show relearning, their response is modulated by bending on flexible substrates, and a network built from the measured weight updates reaches 89 % accuracy on handwritten digit recognition without noise and 85 % with the measured noise, against 97.5 % for floating-point weights.

</details>


### [145] [Directed impulsive molecular dynamics reveal energy-routing pathways of bond cleavage in TATB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21637)
*Guanchen Dong,Xiaona Huang,Yuxuan Chen,Linghua Tan,Guangcheng Yang,Rui Su,Lin Wang,Pengfei Guan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以 TATB 为例，构建了仅需 4,630 个构型的小样本振动忠实机器学习势（machine-learning potential），并提出定向脉冲分子动力学（directed impulsive molecular dynamics）协议，追踪非平衡能量流，发现初始键断裂强烈依赖于被激发的振动模式而非仅由断键势垒决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含能材料（energetic materials）对外部刺激的响应不仅是均匀热化（uniform thermalization），理解方向性的能量再分配对结构与点火设计至关重要。非平衡能量流的微观路径难以解析，构成该研究的核心问题。

Method: 以 TATB 为对象，仅用 4,630 个构型构建振动忠实的机器学习势；并开发定向脉冲分子动力学协议，通过沿选定振动模式反复沉积等量动能，在统计控制下放大模式特异性响应并追踪其后续非平衡演化。

Result: 将能量再分配转化为可直接追踪的路径过程后，发现初始键断裂强烈取决于初始被激发的模式，而非仅由断键势垒决定。易触发反应的模式高效地将能量导向断键坐标，而低效模式则将能量转移至类软模式（soft-like modes）或将其困在能量流动不畅的模式中。

Conclusion: 含能材料的定向能量再分配可由振动模式选择主导，机器学习势结合定向脉冲分子动力学可将非平衡能量流解析为可追踪的路径过程，为结构设计与点火设计提供模式层面的机理依据。

Abstract: Responses of energetic materials to external stimuli involve more than uniform thermalization. Understanding directional energy redistribution is essential for structural and ignition design. Here, we use TATB as an example to construct a vibrationally faithful machine-learning potential from only 4,630 configurations. To resolve the elusive nonequilibrium energy flow, we develop directed impulsive molecular dynamics, a statistically controlled protocol that repeatedly deposits equal kinetic energy along selected vibrational modes. By amplifying mode-specific responses while tracking the ensuing nonequilibrium evolution, this approach turns energy redistribution into a directly traceable routing process. We find that initial bond cleavage depends strongly on the initially excited mode, rather than being determined by bond-cleavage barriers alone. Specifically, modes that readily trigger reaction efficiently channel energy towards a bond cleavage coordinate, whereas modes that are less effective at inducing break divert energy into soft-like modes or trap it in modes from which energy flow is inefficient.

</details>


### [146] [Defect Candidates for Single-Photon Emission in Monolayer GaS and GaSe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21670)
*Michael Lorke*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用面向二维体系开发的各向异性屏蔽交换泛函，考察单层GaS与GaSe中本征空位及取代缺陷的自旋分辨电子结构，并将镓空位（尤其GaS中）识别为潜在单光子发射源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解本征空位与取代缺陷如何改变单层GaS和GaSe的带隙内电子结构，并评估这些缺陷用于基于缺陷的单光子发射（single-photon emission）的可行性。

Method: 方法上，采用为二维材料体系开发的各向异性屏蔽交换泛函（anisotropic screened-exchange functional）计算自旋分辨电子结构；并结合基于能量的缺陷关联态分类、空间分辨投影与分电荷密度（partial charge densities）进行分析。

Result: 结果显示，镓空位（gallium vacancies）被识别为候选单光子发射体，其中GaS中的镓空位尤为突出。

Conclusion: 结论是单层GaS与GaSe中的镓空位具有作为缺陷型单光子发射源的潜力，尤其以GaS体系为代表。

Abstract: We investigate the spin-resolved electronic structure of native vacancies and substitutional defects in monolayer GaS and GaSe using an anisotropic screened-exchange functional developed for two-dimensional material systems. By combining an energy-based classification of defect-associated states with spatially resolved projections and partial charge densities, we examine how these defects modify the in-gap electronic structure and assess their relevance for defect-based single-photon emission. The results identify gallium vacancies, particularly in GaS, as candidates single-photon emitters.

</details>


### [147] [Novel high-pressure silicon allotrope with quartz topology from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21683)
*Samir F. Matar,Vladimir L. Solozhenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 引入一种具有石英拓扑结构的新型硅同素异形体 qtz Si，并借助第一性原理密度泛函理论与四种已知同素异形体（dia Si-I、lon Si-IV、bsn Si-II、gsi Si-III）做对比；qtz Si 在原子平均体积、密度、体模量与最高声子频率上最高，剪切模量最低，且为半金属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索硅的新型同素异形体（allotrope），并系统评估其与已知相在力学、动力学稳定性和电子结构上的差异，以拓展对硅多形态（polymorphism）的认识。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论（density functional theory），对 qtz Si 及 dia Si-I、lon Si-IV、bsn Si-II、gsi Si-III 进行计算，包括状态方程（equation of state）、能量-体积关系、Born 稳定性判据、布里渊区（Brillouin zone）声子频率以及电子结构计算。

Result: qtz Si 具有最小的原子平均体积（18.06 Å³）、最高的密度（2.582 g/cm³）、最高的体模量（98 GPa）和最高的最大声子频率（16 THz），但剪切模量（37 GPa）最低；五种同素异形体均满足 Born 稳定性判据且全布里渊区声子频率为正，即力学与动力学稳定；qtz Si 为零带隙半金属（semimetal），dia Si 与 lon Si 为半导体，bsn 与 gsi 呈弱金属性；能量-体积状态方程显示 gsi 与 qtz 较 dia、bsn、lon 高约 0.11–0.15 eV/atom。

Conclusion: gsi 与 qtz 在能量上高于 dia、bsn、lon，符合其被认定为高压相或亚稳相（metastable phase）的判断；qtz Si 作为新提出的石英拓扑硅相，具备力学与动力学稳定性及半金属特性。

Abstract: A novel silicon allotrope, qtz Si, with quartz topology is proposed and investigated alongside four known allotropes (dia Si-I, lon Si-IV, bsn Si-II, and gsi Si-III) using first-principles density functional theory. qtz Si is found to exhibit the smallest atom-averaged volume (18.06 A3), highest density (2.582 g/cm3), highest bulk modulus (98 GPa), and highest maximum phonon frequency (16 THz) among the five, while showing the lowest shear modulus (37 GPa). All five allotropes are confirmed to satisfy the Born stability criteria and to display positive phonon frequencies throughout the Brillouin zone, i.e., they are mechanically and dynamically stable. Electronic structure calculations show that qtz Si is a semimetal with a vanishing gap, whereas dia Si and lon Si are semiconductors, and bsn and gsi allotropes are weakly metallic. Energy-volume equations of state place gsi and qtz 0.11-0.15 eV/atom above dia, bsn, and lon, consistent with their identification as high-pressure or metastable phases.

</details>


### [148] [Bond-informed search for ordered configurations in multi-principal element alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21718)
*Gaurav Arora,Alex Tai,Rodrigo Freitas,David Cereceda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 MECx 框架，用基于键计数空间分层采样训练的替代能量模型引导蒙特卡洛搜索，在仅 100 个 DFT 标注构型下即可在 BCC 多主元合金中识别出比直接 DFT-蒙特卡洛更低能量的有序构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多主元合金（multi-principal element alloys, MPEAs）中的有序构型决定局部结构与众多力学、热力学性质，但其识别极为困难：构型空间随元素数目组合式增长，而密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT）驱动的蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）搜索在每一步试探中都需重新进行电子结构计算，计算代价高昂。

Method: 构建 MECx（Minimum Energy Configuration eXplorer）框架，以在键计数空间（bond-count space）中分层采样的构型所训练的替代能量模型（surrogate energy model）来引导蒙特卡洛搜索，从而替代逐步 DFT 计算。该方法在三元、四元与五元 BCC 合金上验证，训练集仅为 100 个 DFT 标注构型，并测试模型向二元子体系的迁移能力。

Result: 在实际计算限制内，MECx 找到的有序构型能量低于直接基于 DFT 的蒙特卡洛结果。这些构型表现出明确的特定原子对有序特征，包括类 B2 与类 B32 基元（motifs），并随温度升高发生渐进无序化。经 MPEA 训练的模型无需再训练即可迁移至部分二元子体系；所得有序构型与直接 DFT-蒙特卡洛结果一致，并在模拟中子衍射图谱中产生可实验区分的超晶格反射（superlattice reflections）。

Conclusion: MECx 通过替代模型引导搜索，显著降低了 MPEA 有序构型识别的 DFT 计算负担，并在多组元体系及二元子体系中给出可验证、可实验观测的有序结构预测。

Abstract: Ordered configurations in multi-principal element alloys (MPEAs) control their local structure and influence a broad range of mechanical and thermodynamic properties. Yet they are notoriously difficult to identify. Locating low-energy ordered candidates requires exploring a configurational space that grows combinatorially with the number of elements, and Monte Carlo searches driven by density-functional theory (DFT) need a new electronic-structure calculation at every trial step. Here we introduce MECx (Minimum Energy Configuration eXplorer), a framework that guides Monte Carlo searches with a surrogate energy model trained on configurations stratified in bond-count space. Applied to ternary, quaternary, and quinary BCC alloys using compact training sets of 100 DFT-labeled configurations, MECx identifies ordered configurations with lower energies than those found by direct DFT-based Monte Carlo within practical computational limits. These configurations exhibit distinct pair-specific ordering, including B2- and B32-like motifs, and undergo progressive disordering as temperature increases. The MPEA-trained models also transfer to selected binary subsystems without retraining. The resulting ordered configurations agree with those obtained by direct DFT-based Monte Carlo and produce experimentally distinguishable superlattice reflections in simulated neutron diffraction patterns.

</details>


### [149] [Low magnon damping of a EuSe film down to millikelvin temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21733)
*Rostyslav O. Serha,Jan Hofer,Khrystyna O. Levchenko,Andreas Ney,Sergej O. Demokritov,Gunther Springholz,Andrii V. Chumak*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过磁强计与宽频铁磁共振表征了外延生长的变磁体EuSe薄膜（生长于抗磁性BaF₂衬底上），在10 K至30 mK温区研究了其磁性与磁振子损耗。结果显示EuSe在极低温下具有低阻尼、长磁振子寿命，是混合量子系统与量子磁振子学（quantum magnonics）的候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子磁振子学（quantum magnonics）的发展需要能在毫开尔文（millikelvin）温度下保持低磁振子损耗、并具备超越既有铁石榴石（iron garnets）与铁磁金属之功能的磁性材料。铕硫属化合物（europium chalcogenides）为磁性半导体，其每Eu²⁺离子7 μ_B的磁矩来自半充满且轨道角动量为零的4f壳层，预期自旋轨道耦合与内禀阻尼较弱，因而被视为天然候选材料。

Method: 采用磁强计（magnetometry）与宽频铁磁共振（broadband ferromagnetic resonance），对生长在抗磁性BaF₂上的变磁体（metamagnet）EuSe外延薄膜进行表征，温度范围为10 K至30 mK。

Result: EuSe的饱和磁化强度为1100 kA/m，并在低于0.25 T的磁场下可实现反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）、亚铁磁（ferrimagnetic）与铁磁（ferromagnetic）相。与Gilbert阻尼型铁磁体不同，场取向薄膜的线宽随频率与磁场升高而下降，且在750 mK以下不再依赖温度；在30 mK、68 GHz与1.8 T条件下线宽达5.5 mT，接近块体单晶值，对应2 ns的磁振子寿命与Gilbert阻尼参数上界1.1×10⁻³。

Conclusion: EuSe及铕硫属化合物因其极低温低损耗特性，被视为混合量子系统（hybrid quantum systems）与量子磁振子学中有前景的材料。

Abstract: The rapid development of quantum magnonics over the past decade has created a demand for magnetic materials that keep their magnon losses low at millikelvin temperatures and add functionality beyond that of the established iron garnets and ferromagnetic metals. The europium chalcogenides are natural candidates $-$ they are magnetic semiconductors whose moment of $7\,μ_{\mathrm{B}}$ per Eu$^{2+}$ ion stems from a half-filled $4f$ shell with zero orbital momentum, so that spin-orbit coupling and intrinsic damping are expected to be weak. Here we characterize an epitaxial film of the metamagnet EuSe on diamagnetic BaF$_2$ by magnetometry and broadband ferromagnetic resonance from 10 K down to 30 mK. EuSe combines a saturation magnetization of 1100 kA/m with antiferromagnetic, ferrimagnetic and ferromagnetic phases that are reached with fields below 0.25 T. In contrast to a Gilbert-damped ferromagnet, the linewidth of the field-aligned film decreases with increasing frequency and field, and below 750 mK it no longer depends on temperature. At 30 mK, 68 GHz and 1.8 T, it reaches 5.5 mT, close to the values of bulk single crystals, which corresponds to a magnon lifetime of 2 ns and to an upper bound of $1.1\times10^{-3}$ for the Gilbert damping parameter. These properties make EuSe and the europium chalcogenides promising materials for hybrid quantum systems and quantum magnonics.

</details>


### [150] [Coexistence of Altermagnetic and Compensated Ferrimagnetic Motifs in a Noncollinear Multiferroic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21770)
*Vikrant Chaudhary,Fu Li,Hui Liu,Mengli Hu,Rajyavardhan Ray,Chen Shen,Jeroen van den Brink,Hongbin Zhang,Harish K. Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 多铁硼酸盐（multiferroic boracite）Ni$_3$B$_7$O$_{13}$Cl 的共面非共线磁态在同一实空间不同 Ni 亚格子上同时承载交错磁体（altermagnet, ALM）与补偿亚铁磁体（compensated ferrimagnet, CFIM）两种自旋劈裂机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共线补偿磁体中的非相对论自旋劈裂分为两类：交错磁体（ALM）因自旋亚格子存在旋转或镜像关联，其动量选择性劈裂在 $Γ$ 点消失；补偿亚铁磁体（CFIM）因亚格子对称性不等价，可在整个布里渊区（Brillouin zone）包括 $Γ$ 点产生劈裂。本工作旨在探究两类 motif 能否共存于同一材料体系。

Method: 采用第一性原理电子结构计算，对多铁硼酸盐 Ni$_3$B$_7$O$_{13}$Cl 的共面非共线磁态进行能带与自旋织构（spin texture）分析，并对比不同铁电态下的能带分辨自旋极化及相关输运响应。

Result: 电子结构与自旋织构显示，$d$ 波型与 $s$ 波型自旋极化分量共存，分别对应 ALM 与 CFIM motif。相反的铁电态呈现反转的能带分辨自旋极化，表明存在磁电耦合（magnetoelectric coupling），并伴随线性位移电流与圆注入电流的符号反转。

Conclusion: Ni$_3$B$_7$O$_{13}$Cl 在实空间不同 Ni 亚格子上同时实现 ALM 与 CFIM 两类自旋劈裂 motif，且其自旋极化与电流响应受铁电极化调控，展示出磁电耦合与可切换自旋输运特性的潜力。

Abstract: Collinear compensated magnets with nonrelativistic spin splitting comprise altermagnets (ALMs), whose rotation- or mirror-related opposite-spin sublattices produce momentum-selective splitting that vanishes at $Γ$, and compensated ferrimagnets (CFIMs), whose symmetry-inequivalent sublattices allow splitting throughout the Brillouin zone, including at $Γ$. Here, we show that the coplanar noncollinear magnetic state of multiferroic boracite Ni$_3$B$_7$O$_{13}$Cl hosts both motifs on distinct Ni sublattices in real space. The electronic structure and spin texture exhibit coexisting $d$-wave-like and $s$-wave-like spin-polarization components associated with the ALM and CFIM motifs, respectively. Opposite ferroelectric states exhibit reversed band-resolved spin polarizations, indicating magnetoelectric coupling, and sign reversals of linear shift and circular injection currents.

</details>


### [151] [Large in-plane optical anisotropy in epitaxial CrN(113) films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21856)
*Duc V. Dinh,Frank Peiris,Oliver Brandt*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过等离子体辅助分子束外延（plasma-assisted molecular beam epitaxy）在 AlN(11\bar{2}2) 模板上制备了 CrN(113) 外延薄膜，并发现尽管晶体近乎无应变且为立方结构，其光学响应仍表现出强烈的面内偏振依赖，推测源于各向异性表面形貌形成的亚波长光栅型超表面（metasurface）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CrN 兼具力学强度、半导体行为与热电功能，但迄今主要以多晶材料或沿高对称取向（如 (001)、(111)）的外延薄膜形式合成，低对称取向的生长及其对物性的影响尚待探索。

Method: 采用等离子体辅助分子束外延在 AlN(11\bar{2}2) 模板上生长 CrN(113) 外延薄膜，并利用变角光谱椭偏术（variable-angle spectroscopic ellipsometry）在 1.25–5 eV 光子能量范围测量其光学响应。

Result: 椭偏测量显示，尽管薄膜晶体结构近乎无应变且为立方相，其光学响应仍具有出人意料的强偏振依赖性，正交面内偏振下的赝光学常数 n 与 k 在整个测量光谱范围内差异超过 10%。

Conclusion: 作者评估多种可能成因后认为，该偏振依赖最可能源自 CrN(113) 的各向异性表面形貌，其在强吸收区形成作为亚波长光栅（subwavelength grating）的超表面。

Abstract: CrN, a transition-metal nitride crystallizing in the cubic rocksalt structure, exhibits a unique combination of mechanical robustness, semiconducting behavior, and thermoelectric functionality. To date, however, CrN has been synthesized primarily as polycrystalline material or as epitaxial films grown along high-symmetry orientations such as (001) and (111). Here, we prepare epitaxial CrN(113) films on AlN(11\bar{2}2) templates by plasma-assisted molecular beam epitaxy. The low-symmetry interface and highly direction dependent lattice mismatch are expected to modify the physical properties of CrN relative to the bulk material. Indeed, variable-angle spectroscopic ellipsometry in the photon-energy range from 1.25 to 5 eV reveals a surprisingly strong polarization dependence of the optical response despite the nearly strain-free cubic crystal structure of the films. The pseudo-optical constants n and k differ by more than 10% for orthogonal in-plane polarizations throughout the investigated spectral range. We evaluate possible origins of this effect and conclude that it most likely arises from anisotropic surface morphology of CrN(113), which creates a metasurface acting as a subwavelength grating in the strongly absorbing regime.

</details>


### [152] [VoroTypes: automated enumeration of Voronoi cell topologies for structural identification of complex crystals in atomistic simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21911)
*Emanuel A. Lazar,Chris H. Rycroft*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: VoroTypes 是一个软件包，可对任意晶体结构枚举在原子位置小量一般性扰动下可能出现的完整 Voronoi 胞拓扑族，并将其导出为 VoroTop 框架的拓扑过滤器，从而把分子动力学模拟中的逐原子结构识别扩展到复杂晶体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有逐原子结构识别方法受限于少量简单参考结构，难以处理 Frank–Kasper 相、Laves 相等复杂晶体及其亚晶格分辨需求，因此需要一个可系统枚举复杂结构拓扑族并生成过滤器的工具。

Method: 该软件包以标准晶体学描述（CIF 或 POSCAR 文件）为输入，通过精确对称性约减（exact symmetry reduction）与有效性剪枝（validity pruning）加速枚举，并输出可直接载入独立 VoroTop 程序或 OVITO 的过滤器。

Result: VoroTypes 能够枚举任意晶体结构在一般性小扰动下的完整 Voronoi 胞拓扑族，并生成拓扑过滤器，实现含亚晶格分辨的复杂晶体逐原子结构识别；同时提供覆盖 Materials Project 中一千余种结构的预计算过滤器库。

Conclusion: VoroTypes 将 VoroTop 的拓扑分析能力从简单参考结构推广至复杂晶体体系，并通过预计算过滤器库提升了方法的可用性与适用范围。

Abstract: VoroTypes enumerates, for an arbitrary crystal structure, the complete family of Voronoi cell topologies that can arise under small generic perturbations of atomic positions, and exports it as a topological filter for the VoroTop framework. This extends per-atom structural identification in molecular dynamics simulations, previously limited to a handful of simple reference structures, to complex crystals such as Frank--Kasper and Laves phases, with per-sublattice resolution. The package takes a standard crystallographic description (a CIF or POSCAR file) as input, applies exact symmetry reduction and validity pruning to accelerate the enumeration, and produces filters that load directly into the standalone VoroTop program or into OVITO. A library of pre-computed filters covers over a thousand structures from the Materials Project.

</details>


### [153] [Coherent and Incoherent Time-Resolved Diffuse Scattering Intensity from First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22006)
*Matteo Furci,Giovanni Marini,Stefano Mocatti,Matteo Calandra*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出时间分辨漫散射（time-resolved diffuse scattering）强度的一阶含时第一性原理表述，使相干与非相干贡献在同一理论框架下并立出现，并以光激发锗为例验证了实验观测的频率与非相干背景上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有的对X射线自由电子激光（X-ray free-electron laser）与超快电子衍射（ultrafast electron diffraction）漫散射的解释多依赖简化模型，缺乏系统性的第一性原理框架。

Method: 在探针脉冲相干时间长于电子特征时间、短于离子运动时间的假设下，采用密度矩阵（density-matrix）形式，在瞬时非平衡玻恩-奥本海默简正模（Born-Oppenheimer normal modes）基下推导位移-位移关联函数（displacement-displacement correlators）的运动方程；并用Wannier插值在EPIq代码中实现，应用于光激发锗。

Result: 理论上区分出由声子占据数（phonon occupations）控制的非相干贡献与由反常声子关联函数（anomalous phonon correlators）控制的相干贡献；相干动力学由光致声子频率移动（phonon-frequency shift）与非平衡载流子分布上的电子-声子散射（electron-phonon scattering）共同诱导。计算所得漫散射强度再现了实验观测的振荡频率与上升的非相干背景。

Conclusion: 该表述为超快实验中时间分辨漫散射的系统性解释开辟了路径。

Abstract: Time-resolved diffuse scattering from X-ray free-electron lasers and ultrafast electron diffraction gives access to the nonequilibrium lattice dynamics of photoexcited solids in reciprocal space. Existing interpretations have often relied on simplified models. Here we present a first-principles formulation of the time-dependent first-order diffuse intensity in which coherent and incoherent contributions emerge on equal footing. By assuming the probe-pulse coherence time to be longer than the characteristic electronic timescales, but shorter than those of the ionic motion, we work in the density-matrix formalism and derive equations of motion for the displacement-displacement correlators in a basis of instantaneous, out-of-equilibrium Born-Oppenheimer normal modes. We show how the incoherent contribution, governed by the phonon occupations, and a coherent contribution, governed by the anomalous phonon correlators, emerge. The non-trivial coherent dynamics is shown to be induced both by the photoinduced phonon-frequency shift and by electron-phonon scattering off the nonequilibrium carrier distribution. We implement the resulting equations using Wannier interpolation in the \textsc{EPI}q code and apply them to photoexcited germanium. The calculated diffuse intensity reproduces both the oscillation frequencies and the rising incoherent background observed experimentally, opening the way to a systematic interpretation of time-resolved diffuse scattering in ultrafast experiments.

</details>


### [154] [From picometre displacements to a three-dimensional lattice gas of polar skyrmions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22309)
*Joohee Bang,Nives Strkalj,Hugo Aramberri,Stephan Rosenkranz,Arkadiy Simonov,Morgan Trassin,Thomas Weber*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究运用三维差分对分布函数（3D-ΔPDF）分析 X 射线漫散射，揭示 PbTiO3/SrTiO3 超晶格中的极性斯格明子（polar skyrmions）构成三维晶格气体，其有序性仅延伸至数个近邻，且层间耦合强于层内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极性斯格明子是磁性斯格明子的电学类比物；已知其在 PbTiO3/SrTiO3 超晶格中形成阵列但仅为短程有序。然而该无序集合体在三维中如何组织、其微观结构如何支撑这一组织，此前基本无法获取。

Method: 采用 X 射线漫散射（X-ray diffuse scattering）的三维差分对分布函数（three-dimensional difference pair distribution function, 3D-ΔPDF）分析，对 PbTiO3/SrTiO3 超晶格中极性斯格明子阵列的短程有序结构进行三维表征。

Result: 极性斯格明子泡呈现三维晶格气体（three-dimensional lattice gas）行为：占据离散格点，有序性仅延伸至数个近邻，且跨 SrTiO3 隔离层的耦合强度为层内的两到三倍，与极化延伸入隔离层的图像一致。泡内极化以 Néel 型（Néel-type）方式为主旋转，Pb 与 Ti 以可比的皮米级位移共同旋转，表明 A 位与 B 位阳离子均参与极性畸变。

Conclusion: 极性斯格明子阵列可被描述为三维晶格气体，其层间耦合由极化向 SrTiO3 隔离层的延伸所支持；A、B 位阳离子共同贡献极性畸变，为理解此类无序极性织构的三维组织及其微观起源提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Polar skyrmions, the electric analogues of magnetic skyrmions, form arrays in PbTiO3/SrTiO3 superlattices that are known to be only short-range ordered, but how this disordered ensemble is organized in three dimensions, and how its microscopic structure underlies this organization, have remained largely inaccessible. Here, using three-dimensional difference pair distribution function (3D-ΔPDF) analysis of X-ray diffuse scattering, we show that the polar skyrmion bubbles form a three-dimensional lattice gas: they occupy discrete sites, but their order extends over only a few neighbours and is two to three times stronger across the SrTiO3 spacers than within a layer. This interlayer coupling is consistent with the polarization of the bubbles extending into the spacers. Within each bubble, the polarization rotates in a predominantly Néel-type manner, and Pb and Ti rotate together with comparable picometre displacements, demonstrating that both A- and B-site cations take part in the polar distortion.

</details>

### [155] [Vision-Guided Closed-Loop Autonomous Navigation of Light-Driven Janus Microbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21222)
*Kanhu Charan Andia,Harsh Prashant Katole,Parth Nitinkumar Bhosale,Hemant Kumar,Dhruv Pratap Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一套视觉引导的闭环导航框架，用于约3 μm的光驱动立方体状赤铁矿/TiO2 Janus微机器人，实现了高精度轨迹跟踪、自由手绘路径、迷宫自主导航及动态避障与货物递送。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微机器人可在复杂环境中承担微尺度运输、操作与任务执行，但在微米尺度下，尤其当周围环境动态变化时，可靠导航面临显著挑战。

Method: 采用自定义训练的YOLOv11目标检测模型进行实时视觉定位，结合离散化路点（waypoint）磁转向实现闭环导航；在动态环境中引入A*路径规划进行实时重规划，并整合货物捕获与递送功能。

Result: 在测试的路径几何中，超过90%的实测轨迹偏差小于6 μm；框架支持任意用户自定义自由手绘轨迹与物理受限迷宫的自主导航；可实时重规划并避开多个5 μm球形Janus微马达，同时实现多货物粒子的选择性捕获、自主运输与递送至预定位置。

Conclusion: 研究建立了一个集成平台，将可编程轨迹控制、自主路径规划、实时环境适应与任务执行统一于单一光驱动Janus微机器人系统中。

Abstract: Artificial microbots capable of precise and autonomous navigation could provide versatile platforms for microscale transport, manipulation, and task execution in complex environments. However, reliable navigation becomes increasingly challenging at the micrometre scale, particularly when the surrounding environment changes dynamically. Here, we develop a vision-guided closed-loop navigation framework for light-driven cube-shaped hematite/TiO2 Janus microbots with a characteristic size of approximately 3 μm. Real-time visual localization using a custom-trained YOLOv11 object-detection model is integrated with discretized waypoint-based magnetic steering to enable precise navigation along complex prescribed trajectories, with more than 90% of the measured trajectory deviations remaining below 6 μm across the tested path geometries. The framework further enables arbitrary user-defined free-hand trajectories and autonomous navigation through a physically confined maze. In dynamically changing environments, A* path planning enables real-time path replanning while avoiding multiple independently swimming 5 μm spherical Janus micromotors. Finally, the microbot selectively captures multiple cargo particles and autonomously transports and delivers them to a predefined location while simultaneously avoiding dynamic obstacles. These results establish an integrated platform combining programmable trajectory control, autonomous path planning, real-time environmental adaptation, and task execution within a single light-driven Janus microbot system.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>
