### [156] [Transport in multilayer graphene: resistance from stacking faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20713)
*C. M. Wilson,R. Ganesh,K. V. Samokhin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立理论框架，计算任意堆垛石墨烯（graphene）沿 $c$ 轴（$c$-axis）的电阻（resistance），将层间输运约化为可用 Landauer 范式处理的一维问题。研究表明，Bernal 与菱形（rhombohedral）石墨界面因费米面失配（Fermi surface mismatch）而出现绝缘行为，且堆垛无序（stacking disorder）导致非自平均（non-self-averaging）的中位电阻随堆垛尺寸指数增长，体现局域化（localization）驱动的绝缘特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernal 石墨（graphite）沿堆垛轴方向呈现高电阻的各向异性输运（anisotropic transport），柱状（pillar）实验将其归因于层错（stacking fault），但观测到的电阻存在异常大的非高斯（non-Gaussian）样本间涨落。该现象缺乏统一的理论描述，需要建立可处理任意堆垛排布的理论框架。

Method: 基于无论堆垛顺序如何均存在面内平移对称性（in-plane translational symmetry）的思想，以面内动量（in-plane momentum）为好量子数，将 $c$ 轴输运约化为有效一维问题，并用 Landauer 范式（Landauer paradigm）求解。计算对象包括 Bernal 与菱形体系中的多种简单层错、嵌入 Bernal 电极间的菱形“板层”（slab），以及具有堆垛无序的多层样品。

Result: Bernal 与菱形石墨虽均为金属性，但菱形板层嵌入 Bernal 电极的体系呈绝缘行为，归因于费米面失配。堆垛无序多层样品的电阻表现为非自平均，类似一维导线中无序的强局域化效应；其中位电阻（median resistance）随堆垛尺寸呈指数增长，表明由局域化驱动的绝缘特性。

Conclusion: 介观石墨柱（mesoscopic graphitic pillar）的输运测量可作为层错的标志，尤其能够探测局域化驱动的物理（localization-driven physics）。

Abstract: Multilayer graphene exhibits rich possibilities for stacking. While natural bulk graphite predominantly occurs in the Bernal structure, where layers are stacked in an $-ABAB-$ pattern, rhombohedral stacking has sprung to prominence recently. It is well known that Bernal graphite exhibits anisotropic transport, with high resistance to currents along the stacking axis. Careful experiments using pillar geometries have measured out-of-plane resistance, attributing it to stacking faults where planes slip out of the nominal $ABAB$ sequence. The observed resistance shows anomalously large, non-Gaussian, sample-to-sample variations. In this article, we build a theoretical framework to calculate $c$-axis resistance of graphene stacks with an arbitrary stacking arrangement. Our approach builds on the idea that, regardless of stacking sequence, we have in-plane translational symmetry. With in-plane momentum as a good quantum number, $c$-axis transport reduces to an effectively one-dimensional problem that can be addressed using the Landauer paradigm. We calculate the resistances of a variety of simple stacking faults in both Bernal and rhombohedral systems. We also calculate the resistance of a rhombohedral 'slab' embedded between Bernal leads. Remarkably, despite both Bernal and rhombohedral graphite being metallic, the system exhibits insulating behaviour. We attribute this to Fermi surface mismatch. We next calculate the resistance of a multilayer sample with stacking disorder. Resistance is non-self-averaging, akin to a one-dimensional wire where disorder has a strong localizing effect. The median resistance grows exponentially with stack size, indicating insulating character driven by localization. Our results demonstrate that transport measurements on mesoscopic graphitic pillars serve as signatures of stacking faults, and in particular, probe localization-driven physics.

</details>


### [157] [Resonant enhancement of Raman scattering in a MoS$_2$ nanotube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20755)
*D. R. Kazanov,A. V. Poshakinskiy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在多壁 MoS₂ 纳米管管壁中受限的回音壁模式（whispering-gallery modes）可共振增强其自身光学声子的自发拉曼散射（spontaneous Raman scattering）；当声子能量匹配模式阶梯的自由光谱范围（free spectral range）时出现双共振（doubly resonant）增强，且在 A 激子（A exciton）附近无需几何调谐即可满足匹配条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作关注如何利用纳米管几何结构中的受限光学模式来增强与自身晶格声子的拉曼相互作用，并探索激子色散是否可替代几何调谐来实现共振匹配。

Method: 基于多壁 MoS₂ 纳米管，分析其管壁回音壁模式对自发拉曼散射的共振增强机制，考察泵浦波与斯托克斯波（Stokes wave）同时命中相邻模式的能量条件，并借助 A 激子附近的激子色散（excitonic dispersion）压缩模式阶梯以扫描其间距。

Result: 信号强度按 $(Q_{\rm pump}Q_{\rm sc})^2$ 标度；在 $E_{\rm pump}=1551$~meV 时对 $A_{1g}$ 声子达成匹配而无需几何调谐；未匹配时激发峰分裂为泵浦共振与散射共振双峰（doublet），其劈裂量可度量残余失谐（residual detuning）；选择定则（selection rules）将双共振限制于 TM→TM 通道并排除 $E_{1g}$。

Conclusion: 回音壁模式与激子色散共同作用，可在多壁 MoS₂ 纳米管中实现自发的双共振拉曼增强，且匹配条件由激子色散自动扫过各类层内声子能量，选择定则进一步限定了允许的偏振通道。

Abstract: The whispering-gallery modes confined in the wall of a multiwall MoS$_2$ nanotube resonantly enhance the spontaneous Raman scattering by its own optical phonons. The enhancement is doubly resonant --- pump and Stokes waves hit two adjacent modes at one pump energy, and the signal scales as $(Q_{\rm pump}Q_{\rm sc})^2$ - whenever a phonon energy matches the free spectral range of the mode ladder. Near the A exciton the excitonic dispersion compresses the ladder and sweeps its spacing through all intralayer phonon energies of MoS$_2$, so the match, reached for $A_{1g}$ at $E_{\rm pump}=1551$~meV, requires no geometric tuning. A missed match splits the excitation peak into a pump- and a scattered-resonant doublet whose splitting measures the residual detuning. Selection rules confine the double resonance to the TM$\to$TM channel and exclude $E_{1g}$.

</details>


### [158] [One-way electron mirrors from single-layer stacking shifts in rhombohedral graphite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20905)
*Konrád Kandrai,András Balogh,Dóra Varga,Márton Szendrő,András Pálinkás,József Cserti,János Koltai,Krisztián Márity,Péter Vancsó,Géza I. Márk,Alexandre Mayer,Zoltán Tajkov,Levente Tapasztó,Péter Nemes-Incze*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在六层菱面体石墨（rhombohedral graphite）中，单层堆垛位移（single-layer stacking shift）构成的壁可作为电子（electron）的单向镜（one-way mirror），在不破坏线性（linearity）、时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）或能量守恒的前提下实现不对称反射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单向镜或非对称散射体（asymmetric scatterer）是光子系统的关键元件，声学系统亦可实现非对称反射；但凝聚态（condensed matter）平台中，尚缺乏不破坏线性、时间反演对称性与能量守恒的单向镜。模式选择（mode selection）被视为可能路径，即探针仅耦合部分模式时，界面从其两侧分别表现为镜面与透明。

Method: 采用扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）观测六层菱面体石墨中单层堆垛位移所形成壁两侧的干涉条纹（interference fringe）行为；并结合层分辨紧束缚（layer-resolved tight-binding）计算与波包动力学（wave-packet dynamics）计算进行复现与机制分析。

Result: STM中约 10 nm 尺度的干涉条纹仅出现在壁的堆垛层错（stacking fault）一侧，并沿其狄拉克能带（Dirac band）分布，而菱面体一侧保持透明。理论计算复现该现象，并将不对称性归因于跨壁的层极化（layer polarization）失配，其中针尖提供选择性耦合。

Conclusion: 该机制构成电子光学（electron optics）中的可移动元件，其单向镜效应仅由晶体结构（crystal structure）定义；由于堆垛孤子（stacking soliton）可在外部场下滑移，该元件具有可调控性。

Abstract: One-way mirrors or asymmetric scatterers are key components of photonic systems, protecting sources from back-reflections and routing light along a chosen path. Acoustic systems can also be engineered to show asymmetric reflection. However, a condensed matter platform acting as a one-way mirror without breaking linearity, time-reversal symmetry or energy conservation has been lacking. One route to achieving this is mode selection, where a probe coupled to only some modes sees a boundary as a mirror from one side and as transparent from the other. Here we show that single-layer stacking shifts in six-layer rhombohedral graphite provide this platform, acting as one-way mirrors for electrons. In scanning tunneling microscopy, 10 nm scale interference fringes appear only on the stacking fault side of a wall, tracking its Dirac band, the rhombohedral side remaining transparent. Layer-resolved tight-binding and wave-packet dynamics calculations reproduce this and trace the asymmetry to the mismatched layer polarization across the wall, with the tip supplying the selective coupling. Because stacking solitons glide under external fields, this mirror forms a movable element of electron optics defined by the crystal structure alone.

</details>


### [159] [Transdimensional Chern insulators in rhombohedral multilayer graphene superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21310)
*Min Li,Xin Lu,Lei Wang,Jianpeng Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在扭角多层石墨烯/六方氮化硼（twisted RMG/hBN）莫尔超晶格（moiré superlattice）中，与莫尔势耦合可使"跨维度轨道铁磁金属"演化为量子化陈绝缘体（Chern insulator）相，形成兼具面内与面外轨道磁化的"跨维度陈绝缘体"（transdimensional Chern insulator, TDCI）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在轻度电子掺杂的菱面体多层石墨烯（rhombohedral multilayer graphene, RMG）"跨维度"（transdimensional）区间的实验，观测到垂直与面内磁场下的反常霍尔滞后（anomalous Hall hysteresis），暗示在相互作用驱动的"费米月牙"金属（Fermi-lune metal）中出现了跨维度轨道磁性。本文旨在探索该轨道铁磁金属与莫尔势耦合后的拓扑相演化。

Method: 采用无限制哈特里-福克计算（unrestricted Hartree-Fock calculations）研究相互作用驱动的重构，并建立最小两带模型（minimal two-band model）描述 TDCI 特性；进一步分析其集体激发（collective excitations），考察面内磁场对软模的影响。

Result: 相互作用驱动的重构隔离出一个自旋-谷极化（spin-valley-polarized）的 |C| = 1 陈带，导致兼具面内与面外轨道磁化的 TDCI 态。最小两带模型揭示非互易速度（non-reciprocal velocity）与面内轨道磁化之间的紧密联系。集体激发分析识别出可能引发密度波失稳（density wave instabilities）的软电荷与自旋模；面内磁场可硬化软密度波模，并可能驱动从密度波态到 TDCI 的转变。当 |C| = 1 带被分数填充时，多体基态可能是破坏旋转与镜像对称性的分数量子陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator）。

Conclusion: 与莫尔势耦合使跨维度轨道铁磁金属可演化为量子化陈绝缘体相，TDCI 具有面内与面外轨道磁性、非互易速度等独特性质。其软模与相变行为、以及分数填充下的分数量子陈绝缘体可能性，构成了该体系的潜在研究方向。

Abstract: Recent experiments on lightly electron-doped rhombohedral multilayer graphene (RMG) in the "transdimensional" regime revealed anomalous Hall hysteresis under both perpendicular and in-plane magnetic fields, pointing to the emergence of transdimensional orbital magnetism in an interaction-driven "Fermi-lune" metal. Here we demonstrate that, by coupling to the moiré potential, this transdimensional orbital ferromagnetic metal can evolve into quantized Chern insulator phases in twisted RMG/hBN moiré superlattices. Using unrestricted Hartree-Fock calculations, we find that interaction-driven reconstruction isolates a spin-valley-polarized $|C| = 1$ Chern band, leading to a "transdimensional Chern insulator" (TDCI) state exhibiting both in-plane and out-of-plane orbital magnetizations. A minimal two-band model is introduced to describe the unique properties of TDCI, which unveils the intimate connection between non-reciprocal velocity and in-plane orbital magnetization. We further analyze the collective excitations of TDCIs, and identify soft charge and spin modes that may lead to density wave instabilities in certain cases. An in-plane magnetic field can harden the soft density-wave mode and may drive a transition from density-wave state to TDCI. Finally, when the $|C| = 1$ band is fractionally filled, the many-body ground state may be a fractional Chern insulator with broken rotation and mirror symmetries.

</details>


### [160] [Edge state reconstruction in proximitized graphene and its probe using RKKY interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21416)
*Seerat Khan,SK Firoz Islam*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在锯齿型石墨烯纳米带中，边缘局域在位势（on-site potential）可驱动边缘态重构，使原本的谷间（inter-valley）边缘态转变为纯谷内（intra-valley）态；电荷电导因透射通道数不变而无法探测该重构，而边缘磁杂质间的 RKKY 交换相互作用可有效探测量子带色散移动并区分均匀型与交错型自旋轨道耦合（SOC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯置于衬底上会产生近邻诱导自旋轨道耦合（proximity induced spin-orbit coupling），破坏亚晶格对称性并产生交错内禀 SOC 与 Rashba SOC；在锯齿型石墨烯纳米带中该交错 SOC 形成亚晶格锁定的赝螺旋（pseudohelical）边缘态，需明确边缘势对这类边缘态的影响及其可探测手段。

Method: 在锯齿型石墨烯纳米带模型中引入仅作用于纳米带边缘的有限在位势（on-site potential），分析边缘态的谷结构变化；计算电荷电导；并计算位于纳米带边缘的两个磁杂质之间的 Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida（RKKY）磁交换相互作用，分别在均匀与交错 SOC 情形下考察其各项。

Result: 边缘在位势使谷间边缘态发生重构并变为纯谷内态，同时具备自旋与谷极化；电荷电导因透射通道数不变而无法反映该重构；RKKY 相互作用可探测移动后的能带色散。强内禀与 Rashba SOC 下 RKKY 相互作用完全各向异性（fully anisotropic）；亚晶格交错与边缘势诱导的重构在 RKKY 中产生不同特征，重构使 RKKY 各项随空间间距出现更快振荡；均匀与交错 SOC 对应的 DM 项（Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya term）振幅存在显著失配。

Conclusion: RKKY 相互作用可作为边缘态重构的有效探针，其振荡频率的变化反映边缘态重构；DM 项振幅对均匀型与交错型 SOC 的显著差异亦可作为判定 SOC 类型的探针，弥补电荷电导在探测该类重构上的不足。

Abstract: Proximity induced spin-orbit coupling (SOC) in graphene emerges when it is placed on a substrate, breaking sublattice symmetry and giving rise to staggered intrinsic SOC together with Rashba spin-orbit coupling. In zigzag graphene nanoribbons, this staggered SOC produces pseudohelical edge states exhibiting sublattice-locked rather than spin-locked helicity. We investigate the effect of a finite on-site potential, restricted to the edges of the ribbon, on these edge states. We find the inter-valley edge states undergo reconstruction and become purely intra-valley, exhibiting both spin and valley polarization. The charge conductance calculations fail to capture this reconstruction, since the number of transmitted channels remains unchanged. We show that the Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) magnetic exchange interaction, between two magnetic impurities situated on the ribbon edge can be used to probe the shifted band dispersion, for both the uniform and staggered spin-orbit coupling. In the presence of strong intrinsic and Rashba SOC, the RKKY interaction becomes fully anisotropic. Both sublattice staggering and edge potential-induced reconstruction lead to distinct features in the RKKY interaction. We show that the RKKY interaction terms acquire faster oscillations as a function of the spatial separation between two magnetic impurities, in response to the edge state reconstruction, establishing it as an effective probe of the edge states. Additionally, the significant mismatch in the amplitudes of the DM term corresponding to the uniform and staggered SOC, can also act as a probe of the type of SOC.

</details>


### [161] [Imaging defects in two-dimensional crystals by convergent beam electron diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21542)
*Tatiana Latychevskaia,Pengru Huang,Kostya S. Novoselov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究展示会聚束电子衍射（CBED）可对二维材料中的单个缺陷实现单次拍摄的原子级精度成像，并结合密度泛函理论计算给出各类石墨烯缺陷的独特指纹图谱，进而通过迭代相位恢复（iterative phase retrieval）从单一 CBED 图样重建原子位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBED 近期已在二维材料上得到验证，可用于成像原子面内与面外位移、层间距及单个吸附物。本研究旨在进一步将 CBED 拓展至二维材料中单个缺陷的原子级精度成像，并探索其从单次测量获取结构信息的潜力。

Method: 采用单次强度测量（single-shot intensity measurement）的 CBED 方法，并结合基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）的结构计算，对石墨烯中多种缺陷进行 CBED 图样模拟；同时使用迭代相位恢复（iterative phase retrieval）从单一 CBED 图样中重建原子位置。

Result: 模拟结果显示，石墨烯中各类缺陷各自呈现独特的 CBED 指纹分布（fingerprint distribution）；并且可由单一 CBED 图样经迭代相位恢复重建出原子位置，包括石墨烯中的单个原子缺陷。

Conclusion: CBED 结合 DFT 结构计算与迭代相位恢复，为二维材料中单个缺陷的原子级精度成像与原子位置重建提供了一种单次测量的可行途径。

Abstract: Convergent beam electron diffraction (CBED), recently demonstrated on two-dimensional (2D) materials, offers a number of interesting applications such as imaging atomic in- and out-of plane shifts, interlayer distances, and individual adsorbates. In this study, we show how CBED allows for atomic-precision imaging of individual defects in 2D materials using one single-shot intensity measurement. In combination with structural calculations using density functional theory, we present simulated CBED patterns for various defects in graphene, each of which exhibits a unique fingerprint distribution. We also show how atomic positions, including the individual atomic defects in graphene, can be reconstructed by iterative phase retrieval from a single CBED pattern.

</details>


### [162] [Theory of Dispersion and Exchange Interactions Subjected to Surface Roughness in Application to Signal Transmission through a Nanoswitch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21652)
*Galina L. Klimchitskaya,Alexander S. Korotkov,Vera V. Loboda,Vladimir M. Mostepanenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文对一种电阻式纳米开关（resistive nanoswitch）进行理论描述，计算其电致弹性力、色散力（范德华力与卡西米尔力）及交换排斥力，并纳入表面粗糙度影响，揭示其可在无吸合（pull-in）情况下循环工作，并分析隧穿电流与信号失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在为可用于两种工作模式切换的电阻式纳米开关提供理论描述，需在考虑表面粗糙度的构型下统一处理电致弹性、色散与交换排斥等相互作用力，以理解其平衡间距与工作稳定性。

Method: 色散力采用Lifshitz理论描述；在相互作用体间距极短、该理论不适用时，采用经非可加性效应修正的原子间势加和法（additive summation）。交换排斥力同样通过原子间势加和并结合邻近力近似（proximity force approximation）求得。

Result: 结果显示，纳米开关在悬臂尖端与接地板之间存在可随驱动电压变化的平衡最近间距，并在电压开启与关闭时以循环方式工作而不发生吸合（pull-in）。同时得到尖端与板之间的隧穿电流，并考察了通过纳米开关传输信号中出现的失真。

Conclusion: 低频信息信号中的幅度失真可通过增大载波信号与信息信号幅度之差而变得足够小，并讨论了所得结果的潜在应用。

Abstract: We present the theoretical description of a resistive nanoswitch designated for a change-over between the two operation modes by calculating the electri elastic, dispersion (van der Waals and Casimir), and also exchange repulsive forces in its configuration with an account of surface roughness. The dispersion forces are described by means of the Lifshitz theory and, at the shortest separations between the interacting bodies, where this theory is inapplicable, by the method of additive summation of the interatomic potentials corrected for the role of the effects of nonadditivity. The exchange repulsion is also found by a summation of the interatomic potentials and using the proximity force approximation. It is shown that the nanoswitch possesses the equilibrium closest separation between a cantilever tip and a ground plate calculated as a function of the actuation voltage and operates in a cyclic manner with no pull-in when this voltage is switched on and off. We have found the tunnel current between a cantilever tip and a plate and investigated distortions appearing in the signal transmitted through a nanoswitch. According to our results, the amplitude distortions arising in the low-frequency information signal can be made sufficiently small by increasing a difference between the amplitudes of the carrier and information signals. Possible applications of the obtained results are discussed.

</details>


### [163] [Indirect Control of Buried CoB Spin Textures via C$_{60}$-Induced Modification of Ir Spin-Orbit Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21704)
*Colin Kirkbride,Christopher E A Barker,Timothy Moorsom,Kayla Fallon,Daniel Roe,Trevor Almeida,Sara Villa,Christopher H Marrows,Stephen McVitie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: C$_{60}$分子薄膜可显著改变Pt/CoB/Ir多层体系中埋入磁自旋织构，使磁畴周期收缩，机制源于C$_{60}$/Ir界面的自旋轨道耦合增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机材料如何调控磁结构是有机自旋电子学的核心问题，本文旨在探究C$_{60}$分子覆盖层对含界面Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（iDMI）的埋入磁性多层体系中自旋织构的影响。

Method: 采用洛伦兹透射电子显微镜（Lorentz TEM）在零场与原位场环境下测量磁畴行为，结合Fresnel模式与像素化4D-STEM差分相位衬度模式，并辅以体磁测量与微磁模拟。

Result: 沉积C$_{60}$后磁畴周期减小；4D-STEM分析表明其源于有效磁各向异性与界面DMI强度的显著增强，二者共同导致整个多层堆栈中自旋织构密度收缩。

Conclusion: 提出基于C$_{60}$/Ir界面自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的机制，说明有机分子覆盖层可作为调控埋入磁性多层体系自旋织构的有效途径，对新一代自旋电子与神经形态器件设计具有意义。

Abstract: Understanding how organic materials modulate magnetic structure is a central challenge in organic spintronics. Here, we demonstrate that C$_{60}$ molecular films exert a pronounced and quantifiable influence on the buried magnetic spin texture in Pt/CoB/Ir based multilayer systems hosting interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (iDMI). Using Lorentz transmission electron microscopy, the magnetic domain behaviour in field-free and in-situ field environments is measured. In Fresnel mode, we reveal a reduction in magnetic domain periodicity upon C$_{60}$ deposition. Then, using pixelated 4D-STEM differential phase contrast mode, we establish that this modification arises from a substantial enhancement of effective magnetic anisotropy and interfacial DMI strength. Coupled with bulk magnetometry and micromagnetic simulations, we propose a mechanism based on spin-orbit coupling at the C$_{60}$/Ir interface which modifies the iDMI and drives a contraction of spin texture density throughout the multilayer stack. These findings extend the impact of organic molecular capping films as effective and accessible routes for engineering spin textures to buried magnetic multilayer systems, with direct implications for the design of next-generation spintronic and neuromorphic devices.

</details>


### [164] [Microwave spectroscopic evidence of a moiré-commensurate electron crystal in monolayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21875)
*A. Solodovnyk,P. Colgan,K. Watanabe,T. Taniguchi,L. W. Engel,S. Dietrich*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过约50 mK、15.4--18 T下的宽带微波透射测量，探测莫尔对齐（moiré-aligned）单层石墨烯最低朗道能级（lowest Landau level）的电导率，并观测到随磁场沿固定载流子密度漂移的一系列极小值，判定其为莫尔公度电子晶体（moiré-commensurate electron crystals）的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于区分随固定填充因子（Landau filling）演化的传统量子霍尔态与随固定载流子密度演化的莫尔公度电子晶体，从而检验莫尔体系中电子晶体（广义维格纳晶体，generalized Wigner crystal）是否可在最低朗道能级中稳定存在。

Method: 在约50 mK低温与15.4--18 T磁场下，通过共面波导（coplanar waveguide）进行宽带微波透射测量，并以低频减高频的频带差分D(ν)隔离纵向通道信号。

Result: 在D(ν)中分辨出一系列极小值，其随磁场沿固定载流子密度线漂移而非沿固定朗道填充因子漂移。最强极小值在所有磁场下均存在，且不对应任何分数量子霍尔（fractional quantum Hall）或整数Hofstadter态，位于能带填充s=1/3处，即每三个莫尔元胞一个电子；较弱极小值位于邻近公度填充附近。

Conclusion: 最强极小值与DaSilva、Jung和MacDonald所预言的最有利固定密度（t=0）公度——广义维格纳晶体——相符，表明该信号源于莫尔公度电子晶体而非量子霍尔或Hofstadter态。

Abstract: We probe the conductivity of the lowest Landau level of a moiré-aligned monolayer graphene device by broadband microwave transmission through a coplanar waveguide ($\sim$50 mK, 15.4--18 T), isolating the longitudinal channel through a low-minus-high frequency band difference $D(ν)$. We resolve a series of minima in $D(ν)$ that drift with field along lines of fixed carrier density rather than fixed Landau filling, the hallmark of moiré-commensurate electron crystals. The strongest minimum is present at every field and matches no fractional quantum Hall or integer Hofstadter state. It lies at band filling $s=1/3$, one electron for every three moiré cells---the generalized Wigner crystal that DaSilva, Jung, and MacDonald predict as the most favorable fixed-density ($t=0$) commensuration. The weaker minima fall near neighboring commensurate fillings.

</details>


### [165] [Exciton superfluidity in two-dimensional bilayers at zero and high magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22037)
*Sankar Das Sarma,Yang-Zhi Chou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作系统比较了零磁场下二维电子-空穴双层与总填充因子 ν_T=1 的量子霍尔双层中，层间激子超流（interlayer exciton superfluidity）随层间距（layer separation）的演化，并区分层间相干性（interlayer coherence）的存在性、能量尺度与空间稳定性三个层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无屏蔽 Hartree-Fock 理论（unscreened Hartree-Fock theory）预言任意有限层间距下均存在相干性，且能量尺度随间距增大而反比衰减，但该预言未计入屏蔽、关联与空间不稳定性，因此需要检验这些因素如何修正上述图像。

Method: 零磁场情形下，在随机相近似（random-phase approximation, RPA）屏蔽下求解具有完整动量与频率依赖的线性化能隙方程（linearized gap equation），且不引入截断；并考察 ladder 近似与二阶 crossed-rung 修正的影响。高磁场情形下，在最低朗道能级（lowest Landau level）内获得解析的均匀 Hartree-Fock 鞍点解，并分析其有限波矢集体模不稳定性。

Result: 零磁场下，ladder 近似中等离激元（plasmon）对配对的增强大部分被二阶 crossed-rung 修正抵消；静态屏蔽在高密度极限下决定主导配对指数。大间距时配对尺度随间距平方呈指数衰减，但失稳仍然存在。高磁场下，均匀 Hartree-Fock 鞍点在任意有限间距均相干，其有限波矢集体模不稳定性倾向于激子超固态（excitonic supersolid），从而将空间均匀性的丧失与相干性的丧失区分开来。

Conclusion: 研究推测量子霍尔双层连续演化至复合费米子（composite fermions）的弱配对，其尺度在大间距下远低于平均场预言；该抑制可在有限温度下产生表观临界层间距，而无需基态量子相变。该比较将层间相干性的存在性、能量尺度与空间稳定性加以分离。

Abstract: We study interlayer exciton superfluidity versus layer separation in two-dimensional electron-hole bilayers at zero magnetic field and quantum Hall bilayers at total filling $ν_T=1$. Unscreened Hartree-Fock theory predicts coherence at every finite separation, with an energy scale decaying inversely with separation at large distances. We examine how screening, correlations, and spatial instabilities modify this prediction. At zero field, we solve the linearized gap equation with the full momentum and frequency dependence of the interaction screened within the random-phase approximation, without an imposed cutoff. The apparent plasmon enhancement of pairing in the ladder approximation is largely cancelled by the second-order crossed-rung correction. Static screening determines the leading pairing exponent in the controlled high-density limit at fixed separation. At large separation, the pairing scale falls exponentially with squared separation while the instability persists. Extending this result to fixed density assumes Fermi-liquid layers and higher-order dynamical corrections subleading to the static pairing interaction. At high field, we obtain an analytic uniform Hartree-Fock saddle point in the lowest Landau level, coherent at every finite separation. Within Hartree-Fock theory, its finite-wave-vector collective-mode instability favors an excitonic supersolid, distinguishing loss of spatial uniformity from loss of coherence. We conjecture that the quantum Hall bilayer evolves continuously toward weak pairing of composite fermions, with a scale far below the mean-field prediction at large separation. This suppression could produce an apparent critical separation at finite temperature without a ground-state quantum phase transition. The comparison separates the existence, energy scale, and spatial stability of interlayer coherence.

</details>


### [166] [Extended strange metal and superconductivity in partially filled moiré Hubbard bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22307)
*Yiyu Xia,Yichi Zhang,Zhongdong Han,Hongyuan Li,Brandon Bauer,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Wenjin Zhao,Jie Shan,Kin Fai Mak*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在扭转双层 WSe2 的半填充 Hubbard 带（Hubbard band）中，实验观测到一种在掺杂浓度与扭转角上均呈扩展分布的奇异金属（extended strange metal, ESM），其处于洁净极限（clean limit）、持续至 100 mK，并跨越多个对称破缺基态；由此还衍生出远离 Mott 绝缘态与范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularity）的新超导穹顶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔半导体（Moiré semiconductor）可实现 Hubbard 模型，但既有研究多集中于半带填充附近弱 Mott 绝缘态附近的带宽调控 Mott 转变与相图；远离 Mott 绝缘体的部分填充 Hubbard 带区域仍基本未被探索。本文旨在进入该未探索区间，考察其是否承载新的物质状态。

Method: 以扭转双层 WSe2（twisted bilayer WSe2）为平台，在部分填充的 Hubbard 带中进行输运测量，系统扫描掺杂密度与扭转角，并测量低至 100 mK 的温度依赖，以界定所观测状态的洁净程度、温度尺度与相图范围。

Result: 观测到扩展奇异金属（ESM）：其在掺杂密度与扭转角上均有延展，处于洁净极限，可维持至 100 mK，并跨越多个对称破缺基态；仅在小扭转角（电子关联强）下出现。此外，观测到由 ESM 发展而来的新超导穹顶（superconducting dome），其位置远离 Mott 绝缘态与该材料的范霍夫奇点。

Conclusion: 该 ESM 的特征不同于已报道的、与量子临界性（quantum criticality）相关的奇异金属，支持其为一种本征物质状态的观点。该工作为在洁净极限下探索部分填充 Hubbard 带的新区间提供了途径。

Abstract: Moiré semiconductors provide a physical realization of the Hubbard model. Bandwidth-tuned Mott transitions and complex phase diagrams mimicking those of high-temperature superconductors have been demonstrated near a weak Mott insulating state at half-band-filling. The platform also provides an opportunity to explore the physics of a partially filled Hubbard band at doping densities far away from the Mott insulator, a regime that has remained largely unexplored. Here we report the emergence of an extended strange metal (ESM), in both doping density and twist angle, in a partially filled Hubbard band of twisted bilayer WSe2. The ESM is in the clean limit, persists down to 100mK, and spans over multiple symmetry-breaking ground states; it also appears only at small twist angles, where electronic correlations are strong. These observations distinguish the state from the reported strange metals associated with quantum criticality and favor the viewpoint that it is an intrinsic state of matter. We also report the observation of a new superconducting dome being developed from the ESM but far away from both the Mott insulating state and the van Hove singularity of the material. Our work opens the door to exploring the new regime of partially filled Hubbard bands in the clean limit.

</details>


### [167] [Parafermions in polarized $ν=2/3$ Fractional Quantum (Anomalous) Hall Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22348)
*Steffen Bollmann,Andreas Haller,Thomas L. Schmidt,Jukka I. Väyrynen,Elio J. König*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在自旋极化 ν=2/3 分数量子霍尔态边缘的邻近超导体结构中，通过边缘偶极相互作用使中性扇区成隙，从而在电荷扇区的双正弦-戈尔登模型中实现 Z₃ 仲费米子零模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受二维量子材料中分数量子陈绝缘体实验迹象的启发，探索在邻近超导体的自旋极化 ν=2/3 分数量子霍尔态边缘实现 Z₃ 仲费米子零模（parafermion zero modes, PZMs）。与自旋非极化的 Halperin 态不同，极化 ν=2/3 边缘包含反向传播的电子模与分数准空穴模，产生多种竞争的隧穿与配对过程。

Method: 聚焦于固定点附近，此时边缘模式重组为解耦的电荷扇区与中性扇区，中性扇区对应涌现的偶极自由度。采用玻色化（bosonization）与重整化群（renormalization group）分析，给出边缘间偶极相互作用使中性扇区成隙的参数空间。

Result: 该机制稳定出一个主导隧穿通道与一个主导配对通道，在电荷扇区产生双正弦-戈尔登模型（double sine-Gordon model），并在畴壁处支持 Z₃ PZMs。清洁体系中该机制需对相互作用强度与作用范围有一定控制；无序则可提供更普适的路径：短程、边缘不关联的无序会抑制稳定仲费米子模所需的同步钉扎，而当两边缘的无序充分关联（如平滑无序势所预期）时，支持 PZMs 的参数区间增大。

Conclusion: 自旋极化 ν=2/3 分数量子霍尔态边缘可承载 Z₃ 仲费米子零模，其稳定依赖于边缘间偶极相互作用对中性扇区的成隙；关联无序可扩大该参数区间，提供比清洁体系更通用的实现途径。

Abstract: Motivated by recent experimental signatures of fractional Chern insulators in 2D quantum materials, we investigate the realization of $\mathbb Z_3$ parafermion zero modes (PZMs) on edges of spin-polarized $ν=2/3$ fractional quantum Hall states which are proximitized by superconductors. Unlike the spin-unpolarized Halperin state, the polarized $ν=2/3$ edge contains counterpropagating electron and fractional quasihole modes, giving rise to a plethora of competing tunneling and pairing processes. We focus on the vicinity of the fixed point where these modes reorganize into decoupled charge and neutral sectors, with the neutral sector describing an emergent dipole degree of freedom. Using bosonization and renormalization group analysis, we lay out the parameter space in which the interedge dipole interactions gap the neutral sector to stabilize one dominant tunneling and one dominant pairing channel resulting in a double sine-Gordon model in the charge sector which supports $\mathbb{Z}_3$ PZMs at domain walls. We discuss both clean and disordered systems. In clean systems this mechanism requires a certain degree of control of the interaction strength and range. In contrast, disorder can provide a more generic route: While short-ranged, edge-uncorrelated disorder % on the two edges suppresses the simultaneous pinning required for stable parafermion modes, when the disorder on the two edges is sufficiently correlated, as expected for smooth disorder potentials, the parameter regime hosting PZMs increases.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Worldsheet string theory on $\mathrm{AdS}_3 \times \mathbf{S}^3 \times \mathrm{K3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20696)
*Lorenz Eberhardt,Matthias R. Gaberdiel,Sameer Murthy,Mukund Rangamani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 AdS₃×S³×K3 上纯 NS-NS 流（pure NS-NS flux）支撑的弦理论在 RNS 形式（RNS formalism）下的世界面配分函数构造问题，指出标准自旋结构求和（spin structure sum）所得结果与时空调制（spacetime supersymmetry）矛盾，并据此提出修正方案，使其唯一地由时空调制确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然 RNS 形式原则上可写下该背景下的弦理论，且可通过 BRST 上同调（BRST cohomology）验证手征谱（chiral spectrum）与超引力（supergravity）一致，但按标准规则对自旋结构求和以保证世界面模不变性（worldsheet modular invariance）时，所得配分函数与时空调制相冲突，作者意在解释此矛盾并给出解决途径。

Method: 作者通过要求时空调制，唯一地固定配分函数的物理态投影（physical state projection）；并将该投影后的世界面配分函数与超引力谱进行比对作为一致性检验，同时从世界面出发计算对偶共形场论（CFT）的椭圆亏格（elliptic genus）。

Result: 所提出的配分函数方案同时重现了 Type IIB 与 Type IIA 理论的超引力谱；投影后的世界面配分函数不分解为玻色部分与费米部分（bosonic and fermionic part），并涉及非模协变（not modular covariant）的函数。计算得到的椭圆亏格包含全纯短弦（holomorphic short string）与非全纯长弦（non-holomorphic long string）两部分贡献；后者呈现赝模函数（mock modular function）特征但存在关键差异，前者具有对称轨道流形（symmetric orbifold）的诱人结构。此外还指出了一种无快子（tachyons）的非超对称理论的世界面构造。

Conclusion: 在 AdS₃×S³×K3 的纯 NS-NS 流背景下，标准 RNS 世界面配分函数构造需以时空调制作为物理态投影的判据加以修正；修正后的配分函数可再现超引力谱并经椭圆亏格计算揭示短弦的对称轨道流形结构及长弦的赝模函数特征，同时为无快子的非超对称理论提供了世界面构造思路。

Abstract: The RNS formalism describing string theory on $\mathrm{AdS}_3 \times \mathbf{S}^3 \times \mathrm{K3}$ supported by pure NS-NS flux is in principle straightforward to write down. One can directly check that the worldsheet theory reproduces the correct chiral spectrum expected from supergravity by analyzing the BRST cohomology. However, when one attempts to construct the worldsheet partition function using the standard rules of the RNS formalism by appropriately summing over spin structures to ensure worldsheet modular invariance, one finds a result in tension with spacetime supersymmetry. We explain why this is the case and, more importantly, propose a resolution. Specifically, we fix the physical state projection for the partition function uniquely by requiring spacetime supersymmetry. Perhaps surprisingly, the projected worldsheet partition function does not factorize into a bosonic and a fermionic part and involves functions that are not modular covariant. As a consistency check, we show that our prescription for the worldsheet partition function reproduces the supergravity spectrum for both Type IIB and IIA theories. Furthermore, we compute the elliptic genus of the dual CFT from the worldsheet, which has both a holomorphic short string and a non-holomorphic long string contribution. The latter displays characteristics of a mock modular function, albeit with some key differences, while the former has the tantalizing structure of a symmetric orbifold. We also point out the worldsheet construction of a non-supersymmetric theory, whose spectrum is free of tachyons.

</details>


### [169] [An SYK wormhole under the microscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20699)
*Valérie Bettaque,Martin Sasieta,Brian Swingle,Alejandro Vilar López*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在 Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）模型中导出重算符热单点函数在微观耦合实现间涨落的大 N 虫洞鞍点，提出其对应双喇叭几何；在双标度极限下虫洞被表述为弦连接两边界的新兴体 Hilbert 空间中的跃迁振幅，并在 Schwarzian 极限下与 Jackiw-Teitelboim（JT）引力开宇宙 Hilbert 空间中的量子跃迁振幅对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于检验 SYK 中的重算符随机性假说（heavy-operator randomness hypothesis），即重算符的热单点函数在微观耦合实现间的涨落是否可由虫洞几何描述。通过将 SYK 虫洞振幅与 JT 引力耦合物质的结果对比，可为该假说提供直接检验。

Method: 方法为在 SYK 模型中推导描述重算符热单点函数方差的大 N 虫洞鞍点（wormhole saddle），并将其解释为双喇叭几何（double trumpet geometry）。在双标度极限（double-scaling limit）下，虫洞被表示为弦（chords）构成的新兴体 Hilbert 空间中的跃迁振幅；在 Schwarzian 极限下弦数量增殖，虫洞对应 JT 引力开宇宙 Hilbert 空间中的量子跃迁振幅。此外，微观 SYK 耦合指定虫洞的 α 参数，并在固定耦合实现下计算对应 α 态中的可观测量。

Result: 结果表明虫洞振幅可与 JT 引力耦合物质的结果进行对比，从而直接检验 SYK 的重算符随机性假说。文中给出了相关半虫洞（half-wormhole）的体波函数，该波函数在长度基（length basis）中表现出不自行平均（do not self-average）的无序关联；并将体描述扩展至闭宇宙的 Hilbert 空间。此外指出虫洞几何所源自的关联弦在结构上类似局域观测者世界线（local observer worldlines）。

Conclusion: 结论是 SYK 中的大 N 虫洞鞍点可由双喇叭几何描述，并在双标度与 Schwarzian 极限下分别对应弦 Hilbert 空间与 JT 引力开宇宙 Hilbert 空间中的跃迁振幅，为检验重算符随机性假说提供了途径；同时半虫洞波函数的不自行平均关联、闭宇宙 Hilbert 空间的扩展，以及关联弦与局域观测者世界线的结构相似性，提示了体描述的新方向。

Abstract: We derive a large-N wormhole saddle in the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model for the variance of the thermal one-point function of a heavy operator across realizations of the microscopic couplings. We propose that this saddle describes a double trumpet geometry. In the double-scaling limit, we express the wormhole as a transition amplitude in an emergent bulk Hilbert space of chords connecting the two boundaries. In the Schwarzian limit, the chords proliferate and the wormhole defines a quantum transition amplitude in the open-universe Hilbert space of Jackiw-Teitelboim (JT) gravity. Comparing the SYK wormhole amplitude with its counterpart in JT gravity coupled to matter provides a direct test of the heavy-operator randomness hypothesis in SYK.
  The microscopic SYK couplings specify the α-parameters of the wormhole. For a fixed realization of the couplings, we compute the observable in the corresponding α-state. We provide the bulk wavefunction of the associated half-wormhole, which exhibits erratic correlations in the length basis that do not self-average. We extend the bulk description to the Hilbert space of a closed universe. We point out that the correlation chords from which the wormhole geometry emerges structurally resemble local observer worldlines.

</details>


### [170] [Region bounds, error margins and certified positivity in the numerical conformal bootstrap; Or the first mathematically rigorous bounds on the 3d Ising CFT critical exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20701)
*Marten Reehorst*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作将数值共形自举（numerical conformal bootstrap）的边界从“采样点上的数值结果”提升为覆盖参数空间区域、精确算术可机器校验的严格定理，并在(Δ_σ, Δ_ε)平面上给出包含 3d Ising 岛的排除区域与临界指数 η、ν 的双侧严格界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已发表的共形自举界限常被称为严格，但实际仅在采样点、截断有理近似、有限自旋列表及浮点舍入条件下成立，并非真正的定理。作者旨在弥合这四处缺口，使界限成为在参数空间区域上成立的严格结果。

Method: 通过在区域（含外部维度空间）上高效搜索正泛函（positive functionals）、将大自旋交叉矢量（large-spin crossing vectors）的正性表述为区域约束、由共形块递推关系（conformal-block recursion relations）的新解析界得到对真实未截断交叉矢量为正的泛函，并用精确算术验证其正性；验证依赖至多三变量的 Bernstein 基正性证书、Q(√2) 上的精确算术、无限自旋尾部的紧化处理，以及真块与其截断之差的显式界。

Result: 作为旗舰应用，研究在(Δ_σ, Δ_ε)平面上认证了一个排除区域：当 Δ_σ∈[0.505, 0.5405] 时，除包含 3d Ising 岛的小矩形外，全部 Δ_ε∈(1/2, 3] 均被排除，从而在明确陈述的谱假设下给出临界指数 η 与 ν 的双侧严格界限。此外还证明了混合 σ-ε 系统的解析输入，可去除自旋-2 间隙（spin-2 gap）假设。

Conclusion: 该方法可避免常见的实际失效模式并加速自举搜索：区域界限将岛的映射转化为少量一维二分。搜索与认证的核心代码及全部可机器校验证书均已公开。

Abstract: Numerical conformal bootstrap bounds are commonly called rigorous, yet as published they are not theorems: they hold at sampled points rather than on regions of parameter space, for truncated rational approximations of the conformal blocks, for a finite list of spins, and up to floating-point rounding. We close all four gaps. We show how to search efficiently for functionals positive on regions, including external-dimension space; how positivity on the large-spin crossing vectors can be imposed as such a region constraint; how new analytic bounds on the conformal-block recursion relations give functionals positive on the true, untruncated crossing vectors; and how to certify the positivity of these functionals in exact arithmetic. The certification rests on Bernstein-basis positivity certificates in up to three variables, exact arithmetic in $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt2)$, a compactified treatment of the infinite spin tail, and an explicit bound on the difference between the true blocks and their truncations, so that infinitely many positivity conditions reduce to finitely many machine-checked sign conditions. As a flagship application we certify an exclusion region in the $(Δ_σ,Δ_ε)$ plane: for $Δ_σ\in[0.505,0.5405]$ all of $Δ_ε\in(1/2,3]$ is excluded except a small rectangle containing the 3d Ising island, giving rigorous two-sided bounds on the critical exponents $η$ and $ν$ under explicitly stated spectrum assumptions. The analytic inputs for the mixed $σ$-$ε$ system, which would remove the spin-2 gap assumption, are also proved. The same methods prevent common practical failure modes and can make bootstrap searches faster: region bounds turn the mapping of an island into a handful of one-dimensional bisections. The essential code to reproduce the search and the certification, and all machine-checkable certificates, are released.

</details>


### [171] [Finite-cutoff geometry from double-scaled SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20703)
*Sergio E. Aguilar-Gutierrez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了双标度 SYK（double-scaled SYK, DSSYK）模型的有限截断全息描述，通过对 Kourkoulou-Maldacena 态与热场双态（thermofield-double）的微观形变，得到分别对应 $T^2$ 与 $T^2+\Lambda_1$ 的低维形变，并在集合平均下对应有限截断正弦膨胀引力（sine dilaton gravity）中的虫洞态及低/高能区的有限截断 AdS 与 dS 几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为 DSSYK 的全息字典中有限截断边界、常平均曲率（constant mean curvature, CMC）切片与膜（brane）提供微观框架，从而在有限截断情形下统一描述边界理论形变、虫洞与视界内外几何。

Method: 方法上构造 Kourkoulou-Maldacena 与热场双 SYK 态的微观形变，使其双标度极限复现 $T^2$ 与 $T^2+\Lambda_1$ 形变；对能谱流方程求解微扰与非微扰分支，并研究世界尽头（end-of-the-world, ETW）膜在全息字典中的引入方式及 CMC 切片的有限截断长度评估。

Result: 结果表明集合平均理论描述有限截断正弦膨胀引力中的虫洞态，以及低能区的有限截断反德西特（anti-de Sitter）与高能区的德西特（de Sitter）几何；能谱流方程中仅微扰分支允许物理量子态；ETW 膜引入额外边界形变，依据可归一化束缚态的存在性给出黑洞或永恒可穿越虫洞几何；CMC 切片的有限截断长度可解释为边界理论中的复杂度（complexity）。

Conclusion: 论文为 DSSYK 全息字典中的有限截断边界、CMC 切片与膜提供了微观研究框架，并讨论了引入物质（matter）的可能推广。

Abstract: We develop a finite-cutoff holographic description of the double-scaled SYK (DSSYK) model. We construct microscopic deformations of Kourkoulou-Maldacena and thermofield-double SYK states whose double-scaling limits reproduce a lower-dimensional analogue of $\text{T}\overline{\text{T}}$ and $\text{T}\overline{\text{T}}+Λ_2$ deformations, which we denote $T^2$ and $T^2+Λ_1$, respectively. The ensemble-averaged theory describes wormhole states in finite-cutoff sine dilaton gravity, and finite-cutoff anti-de Sitter and de Sitter geometries in the low- and high-energy regimes, respectively. The analysis includes both perturbative and non-perturbative solutions to the energy spectrum flow equation, and the regions behind and beyond the respective horizons. Only the perturbative branch admits physical quantum states. We study how to incorporate end-of-the-world (ETW) branes in the finite cutoff holographic dictionary. The ETW brane leads to an additional boundary theory deformation, which leads to black hole or eternal traversable wormhole geometries depending on the existence of normalizable bound states. We extend the finite cutoff length evaluations for constant mean curvature (CMC) slices, and interpret them as complexity in the boundary theory. We discuss possible extensions with matter. Our results provide a microscopic framework for studying the finite-cutoff boundaries, CMC slices and branes in the holographic dictionary of DSSYK.

</details>


### [172] [Gluon Scattering in Expanding de Sitter Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20707)
*Audrey Lindsay,Tomasz R. Taylor*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在四维德西特空间（de Sitter space）膨胀庞加莱图（Poincaré patch）中，以共动永恒观察者（comoving/eternal observer）的世界线为基准构造了观察者中心的 S-矩阵（observer-centric S-matrix），并应用于库仑规范（Coulomb gauge）下的纯杨-米尔斯理论（pure Yang-Mills theory），计算了树图（tree level）三胶子和四胶子振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德西特空间中缺乏类时渐近观察者与标准 S-矩阵定义的困难，促使作者从观察者视角重新定义散射振幅。选择共动永恒观察者，是因为它是唯一在全部固有时（proper time）内留在膨胀庞加莱图内的测地观察者。

Method: 渐近态定义在共动永恒观察者的世界线上，自由哈密顿量（free Hamiltonian）取为该观察者固有时平移对应的守恒荷。由此构造 S-矩阵，并论证其构造不依赖于所选的具体永恒观察者。随后将框架应用于库仑规范下的纯杨-米尔斯理论。

Result: 在树图阶计算得到三胶子与四胶子振幅。这些振幅的结构与规范流（gauge currents）的宇宙学关联函数（cosmological correlators）相似，并受德西特等距群（de Sitter isometries）的沃德恒等式（Ward identities）约束。

Conclusion: 所构造的观察者中心 S-矩阵为德西特空间中散射振幅的定义提供了自洽框架，且其结果与德西特等距对称性一致，为研究膨胀宇宙中的规范理论振幅提供了新途径。

Abstract: We construct an observer-centric S-matrix in the expanding Poincaré patch of four-dimensional de Sitter space. The asymptotic states are defined along the worldline of a comoving, or eternal, observer, the only kind of geodesic observer that remains in the patch for all proper time, and the free Hamiltonian is the conserved charge generating translations in her proper time. The resulting S-matrix is independent of which eternal observer is chosen. We apply the construction to pure Yang--Mills theory in Coulomb gauge and compute the three- and four-gluon amplitudes at tree level. The amplitudes have a structure similar to the cosmological correlators of gauge currents and are constrained by the Ward identities of de Sitter isometries.

</details>


### [173] [6d SCFTs and terminal singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20721)
*Andrea Sangiovanni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文指出现有6d \(\mathcal{N}=(1,0)\) SCFT分类存在一个可被利用的开口，可在此基础上为E-string 6d SCFT添加额外物质扇区，并通过圆上紧致化得到相应的5d \(E_n\)理论版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6d \(\mathcal{N}=(1,0)\) SCFT的分类通过F-theory中椭圆纤维化三folds的枚举取得了很大成功，但该分类仍留有未被利用的可能性。作者旨在探索这一开口，从而扩展E-string SCFT的结构。

Method: 利用椭圆纤维化底流形曲线上嵌套的终端奇点（terminal singularities）实现对E-string 6d SCFT的修饰，并通过在一个圆上做维度约化（dimensional reduction）构造被额外物质扇区修饰的5d \(E_n\)理论。

Result: 成功构造了带额外物质扇区的6d E-string SCFT及对应的5d \(E_n\)理论，且所得RG流级联（RG flow cascade）的末端复现了文献中新近构造的一个5d理论，从而解释了其6d起源。

Conclusion: 该装饰机制揭示了分类中一个此前未被利用的空间，并将某已知5d理论与6d E-string结构联系起来。作者对装饰后的6d与5d理论是否构成新颖SCFT保持不可知态度。

Abstract: The classification of 6d $\mathcal{N}=(1,0)$ SCFTs has been carried out with great success, enumerating consistent elliptically fibered threefolds in F-theory. In this note, we observe that the E-string 6d SCFT can be decorated with extra matter sectors. This decoration exploits an opening allowed by the current classification, implementing terminal singularities nested on the curve of the base of the elliptic fibration that supplies the tensor branch. Additionally, via dimensional reduction on a circle, we identify versions of the 5d $E_n$ theories decorated by extra matter sectors. As a bonus, the end of the resulting RG flow cascade reproduces a 5d theory recently constructed in the literature, explaining its 6d origin. We remain agnostic as to whether the decorated 6d and 5d theories constitute novel SCFTs.

</details>


### [174] [Orientation-Dependent Casimir Energy and Generalized Torque in Lorentz-Violating Rectangular Compactifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20745)
*Zohreh Ashkipoor Gofreh,Reza Moazzemi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文推导了在恒定、CPT偶（CPT-even）类以太（aether-like）洛伦兹破缺背景下，自由大质量实标量场于矩形紧致化（compactification）\(\mathbb{R}^{1,r}\times T^p\) 上的重整化卡西米尔能（renormalized Casimir energy），并刻画其对取向的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究洛伦兹破缺背景与各向异性紧致化格点（lattice）之间相对取向如何影响重整化真空能及其方向性响应，此前尚缺乏系统性刻画。

Method: 允许背景矢量空间部分相对紧致化格点取任意取向；通过对非紧致动量积分（integrating out）将有效空间动能矩阵化为其 Schur 补（Schur complement），并用泊松重求和（Poisson resummation）得到在稳定域内闭合且指数收敛的贝塞尔函数格点和（Bessel-function lattice sum）。

Result: 所得表达式将取向无关的动能归一化因子 \([1+\lambda u_\mu u^\mu]^{-1/2}\) 与取向相关的镜像格点度规（image-lattice metric）形变分离；坐标对齐构型作为特例被恢复，并给出严格的无质量极限与紧致方向压强。对二维紧致子空间，显式分析了真空能的角依赖并导出广义卡西米尔力矩（generalized Casimir torque）。

Conclusion: 结果确立了洛伦兹破缺背景与各向异性紧致化格点之间的相对取向如何进入重整化真空能及其方向性响应。

Abstract: We derive the renormalized Casimir energy of a free massive real scalar field on the rectangular compactification \(\mathbb{R}^{1,r}\times T^p\) in the presence of a constant CPT-even aether-like Lorentz-violating background. Allowing the spatial part of the background vector to have an arbitrary orientation relative to the compactification lattice, we characterize the resulting orientation-dependent geometric response of the vacuum. Integrating out the noncompact momenta reduces the effective spatial kinetic matrix to its Schur complement, and Poisson resummation yields a closed, exponentially convergent Bessel-function lattice sum within the stability domain. The resulting expression separates an orientation-independent kinetic normalization, \([1+λu_μu^μ]^{-1/2}\), from an orientation-dependent deformation of the image-lattice metric. Coordinate-aligned configurations are recovered as special cases, and the exact massless limit and the compact-direction pressures are obtained. For a two-dimensional compact subspace, we explicitly analyze the angular dependence of the vacuum energy and derive the associated generalized Casimir torque. These results establish how the relative orientation between a Lorentz-violating background and an anisotropic compactification lattice enters the renormalized vacuum energy and its directional response.

</details>


### [175] [The Magic Barrier: A Universal Feature of Quantum Thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20768)
*Jakob J. Minar,Andreas Schäfer,Leonhard Schmotzer,Clemens Seidl*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文证明"魔法势垒"（magic barrier）——表现为快速纠缠增长过程中非稳定子性（nonstabilizerness）的瞬态极大值——是量子遍历理论（quantum ergodic theories）中热化（thermalization）的普适特征，且与随机矩阵（random matrix）谱性质密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示非稳定子性在热化过程中的动力学行为及其是否具有普适性，并探究其与随机矩阵谱性质之间的内在联系。此前对纠缠增长与量子混沌的理解尚缺乏对"魔法势垒"这一现象的普适性刻画。

Method: 从纯积态（pure product state）出发，针对取自三个高斯系综（three Gaussian ensembles）的随机矩阵哈密顿量所生成的幺正动力学，解析求解纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）、反平坦度（antiflatness）、纠缠容量（capacity of entanglement）及非局域迹距离魔法（nonlocal trace distance magic）的时间演化。

Result: 反平坦度、纠缠容量与非局域魔法的瞬态极大值出现在与纠缠增长最大速率相同的特征时间尺度上。该现象在三个高斯系综的随机矩阵哈密顿量下均成立。

Conclusion: 魔法势垒是随机矩阵理论（RMT）热化的普适特征，暗示其在量子混沌系统中具有普适性；对遍历量子理论中热化的定量模拟本质上需要量子计算（quantum computation）。

Abstract: We show that the emergence of a ``magic barrier'', characterized by a transient maximum of nonstabilizerness during rapid entanglement growth, is a universal feature of thermalization within quantum ergodic theories, which is intimately connected to random matrix spectral properties. Starting from a pure product state, we analytically determine the time evolution of entanglement entropy, antiflatness, capacity of entanglement, and nonlocal trace distance magic under unitary dynamics generated by random matrix Hamiltonians drawn from all three Gaussian ensembles. We find that the transient maxima of antiflatness, capacity of entanglement, and nonlocal magic occur on the same characteristic timescale as the maximum rate of entanglement growth. These results establish that the magic barrier is a universal feature of RMT thermalization, suggesting universality across quantum chaotic systems. We conclude that quantitative simulations of thermalization in ergodic quantum theories inherently require quantum computation.

</details>


### [176] [All Order Gravitational Wave Tails for Subleading Post-Newtonian Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20770)
*Debanjan Karan,Babli Khatun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将牛顿极限下低频引力波形尾部项（tail terms）的普适性结论推广到后牛顿展开的首个次领头阶，推导任意数量非相对论物体纯引力散射时的完整尾部项级数，并验证了既有相对论猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低频引力波形包含形如 $u^{-r}(\ln|u|)^{r-1}$ 的无穷尾部项级数，其在牛顿极限下的系数已被证明具有普适性；本文旨在考察该普适性在后牛顿展开次领头阶是否依然成立。

Method: 考虑任意数量入射与出射非相对论物体、仅通过长程引力相互作用，在后牛顿展开中保留首个次领头阶，由推迟展开（retardation expansion）的首个次领头修正导出完整尾部项级数。

Result: 该修正完全由大质量粒子的渐近牛顿轨迹决定，与相互作用细节无关；对 $2\to2$ 散射，领头对数级数可求和为闭合形式，结果与 arXiv:2610.07134 提出的相对论猜测一致。

Conclusion: 尾部项系数的普适性在后牛顿展开的次领头阶仍然成立，其仅依赖于渐近数据，支持先前相对论猜测的有效性。

Abstract: The low-frequency gravitational waveform emitted in a classical scattering process contains an infinite tower of late- and early-time tail terms of the form $u^{-r}(\ln|u|)^{r-1}$, $r \geq 1$. In the Newtonian limit, the coefficients of this entire tower have recently been shown to be universal, i.e. fixed by the asymptotic data of the scattering objects alone. At the retained order in the velocity expansion, these leading-logarithmic tails are determined to all orders in Newton's constant $G$. We extend this result to the first subleading order in the post-Newtonian expansion of the asymptotic states. We consider scattering of an arbitrary number of incoming and outgoing nonrelativistic objects interacting only through gravity at long range, and we derive the complete tower of tail terms at this order. The correction arises from the first subleading term in the retardation expansion and is determined by the asymptotic Newtonian trajectories of the massive particles. Its coefficients are again determined entirely by the asymptotic trajectories and are independent of the details of the interaction. For $2\to2$ scattering, the leading-logarithmic series can be summed in closed form. Our results agree with a relativistic conjecture proposed in earlier work arXiv:2610.07134.

</details>


### [177] [Exact AdS and pp-waves in multi-gravity beyond pairwise interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20839)
*Joakim Flinckman*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在无鬼（ghost-free）多重引力（multi-gravity）理论中，超越成对相互作用（pairwise interactions），对任意数量 $\mathcal{N}$ 的标架（vielbein）和任意维度 $D\geq3$ 构造了精确的 AdS 波（anti-de Sitter waves）与 pp-波（pp-waves）解。沿公共零方向对成比例的 AdS 与 Minkowski 解进行形变，将非线性场方程化为波剖面的耦合线性微分方程；系统在微扰 Fierz-Pauli 质量矩阵的同一本征基下对角化，分解为一个无质量与 $\mathcal{N}-1$ 个有质量本征剖面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索超越成对相互作用的多场引力理论是否支持成对理论（pairwise theories）无法给出的新型精确引力波解，从而检验多重引力中真正多场相互作用的物理效应。

Method: 沿公共零方向形变成比例的 AdS 与 Minkowski 解，将完整非线性场方程约化为波剖面的耦合线性微分方程，且不限制振幅；随后在与微扰 Fierz-Pauli 质量矩阵相同的本征基下对角化该系统。

Result: 系统被分离为一个无质量与 $\mathcal{N}-1$ 个有质量本征剖面，并给出显式的径向与横向解。施加 AdS 边界衰减条件后，每个非负质量平方模式保留合适的径向分支，而负质量平方剖面（含 Breitenlohner-Freedman 阈值处的对数解）被排除；同时通过局域平坦极限（local flat limit）将 AdS 波与 pp-波相联系。

Conclusion: 显式例子展示了无法由无鬼成对理论复现的波组态，表明真正的多场相互作用支持新的精确引力波解。

Abstract: We construct exact anti-de Sitter (AdS) waves and pp-waves in ghost-free multi-gravity beyond pairwise interactions for an arbitrary number $\mathcal{N}$ of vielbeins, in any spacetime dimension $D\geq3$. Deforming proportional AdS and Minkowski solutions along a common null direction reduces the full nonlinear field equations to coupled linear differential equations for the wave profiles, without restricting their amplitudes. The system is diagonalised in the same eigenbasis as the perturbative Fierz-Pauli mass matrix, separating the solutions into one massless and $\mathcal{N}-1$ massive eigenprofiles. We obtain explicit radial and transverse solutions and impose an AdS boundary falloff. This falloff admits the appropriate radial branch of every mode with non-negative squared mass and excludes profiles with negative squared masses, including logarithmic solutions at the Breitenlohner-Freedman threshold. We also relate the AdS waves to pp-waves through a local flat limit. Explicit examples exhibit wave configurations that cannot be reproduced by ghost-free pairwise theories, demonstrating that genuine multi-field interactions support new exact gravitational-wave solutions.

</details>


### [178] [Beyond the polynomial ansatz: regular $AdS_3\times M_8$ solutions of M-theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20853)
*Carolina Matté Gregory*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 Gauntlett 等提出的 cohomogeneity-one $AdS_3\times M_8$ 系统的正则紧致解（regular compact solutions），其中 $M_8$ 是三个常曲率黎曼面上的 $S^2$ 丛。作者证明非平坦且无正曲率因子的曲率符号扇区均被端点论证排除，并在其余扇区构造含四个径向形状参数的解析形变族。结论指出非多项式性是参数空间中的普遍现象，且曲率符号判据在该拟设内是充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在厘清 $AdS_3\times M_8$ 系统正则紧致解的完整分类与参数结构，特别是区分已知多项式解与更一般的解。核心问题是判定曲率符号条件何时足以保证解的存在性，并刻画通解偏离多项式形式的程度。

Method: 方法上采用端点论证（endpoint argument）排除无正曲率因子的非平坦扇区，并通过解析形变（analytic deformation）在保留基底几何固定的前提下构造四参数正则解族。此外引入四个磁通比（magnetic flux ratios）作为参考解附近的局部坐标，并利用有理逆映射（rational inverse）从有限磁通比重构候选端点数据。

Result: 结果表明已知多项式解构成上述解族中的真解析轨迹（proper analytic locus），故非多项式性在参数空间中具有普遍性，尽管与端点匹配多项式的偏差可能极小。在完全正曲率扇区中，证明存在显式的四维有限磁通比邻域位于物理磁通像内；磁周期与领头全息中心荷（holographic central charge）可归约为端点数据，且等变局域化公式（equivariant localization formula）的非平凡磁通依赖在 Brown–Henneaux 归一固定后与端点结果作为精确代数恒等式一致。

Conclusion: 结论认为，除完全平坦的例外情形外，曲率符号判据在该拟设内既是必要条件也是充分条件；非多项式修正可被量化，并可在显式收敛假设下分析有限退化（finite degenerations）。研究为 AdS$_3$ 超引力解的分类与全息对偶提供了更精细的参数刻画。

Abstract: We study regular compact solutions of the cohomogeneity-one $AdS_3\times M_8$ system of Gauntlett, Mac Conamhna, Mateos and Waldram, where $M_8$ is an $S^2$ bundle over three constant-curvature Riemann surfaces. We find that an endpoint argument rules out every non-flat curvature-sign sector without a positive-curvature factor, while an analytic deformation gives regular families with four radial shape parameters in all remaining sectors, with the base geometry held fixed. The known polynomial solutions form a proper analytic locus in these families. Non-polynomiality is therefore generic in parameter space, although the deviation from an endpoint-matching polynomial can be very small. Apart from the completely flat exception, the curvature-sign criterion is necessary and sufficient within this ansatz. Four magnetic flux ratios give local coordinates near the reference solution and its small-curvature deformations, and a rational inverse reconstructs candidate endpoint data directly from finite flux ratios. In the fully positive sector we prove that one explicit four-dimensional finite-ratio neighbourhood lies inside the physical flux image. The magnetic periods and leading holographic central charge reduce to endpoint data, and, once the common Brown--Henneaux normalization is fixed, the nontrivial flux dependence of the equivariant localization formula agrees with the endpoint result as an exact algebraic identity. We also quantify the non-polynomial corrections and analyse finite degenerations under explicit convergence hypotheses.

</details>


### [179] [Local supersymmetry implies momentum map/Gauntlett-Pakis equations and vanishing generalized Komar forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20931)
*Patrick Meessen,Tomás Ortín*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文证明 Killing 旋量双线性（Killing spinor bilinears）所满足的一阶微分方程，可直接由局域超对称代数（local supersymmetry algebra）对玻色场的作用导出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Killing 旋量双线性所满足的一阶微分方程（动量映射方程、Killing 矢量方程及超对称 Killing 矢量广义 Komar 荷（generalized Komar charge）的消失）此前有其独立的导出途径，本文旨在给出统一且更直接的推导来源，即以局域超对称代数对玻色场的作用为出发点。摘要未给出更具体的动机表述。

Method: 方法为从局域超对称代数在玻色场上的作用出发，直接推导 Killing 旋量双线性所满足的一阶微分方程，包括动量映射方程（momentum map equations，亦称 Gauntlett–Pakis 方程）、Killing 矢量方程（Killing vector equations）以及广义 Komar 荷的消失。作者通过若干复杂度递增的示例展示该推导过程，其中一个示例为 11 维超引力（11-dimensional supergravity）。

Result: 摘要说明该推导在多个复杂度递增的示例中成立，且其中一个示例为 11 维超引力，表明该方法可适用于高维、复杂情形。摘要未给出具体计算细节或定量结果。

Conclusion: Killing 旋量双线性的一阶微分方程可由局域超对称代数对玻色场的作用直接导出，该结论在包括 11 维超引力在内的多个示例中得到展示。

Abstract: The first-order differential equations satisfied by Killing spinor bilinears (momentum map equations or Gauntlett--Pakis equations, Killing vector equations and the vanishing of the generalized Komar charge of the supersymmetric Killing vector) can be derived directly from the action of the local supersymmetry algebra on bosonic fields. We show how this comes about in several examples of increasing complexity, one of which is 11-dimensional supergravity.

</details>


### [180] [Regge Dilaton Gravity from BF Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21058)
*Florian Girelli,Christopher Pollack*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在离散路径积分（discrete path integral）层面研究二维伸缩子引力（two-dimensional dilaton gravity）的规范描述与几何描述之间的关系，通过构造均匀弯曲单纯形上的 Regge 离散化与带状加细 BF 理论离散化，并借助规范固定（gauge fixing）证明二者对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在厘清引力理论的规范表述（gauge description）与几何表述（geometric description）之间的联系，此前该关系多在连续层面讨论，而在离散路径积分层面的对应尚不明确。

Method: 采用均匀弯曲单纯形（homogeneously curved simplices）构建欧几里得二维伸缩子引力的 Regge 离散化（Regge discretization），并构建适用于均匀弯曲时空的二维 BF 理论的带状加细离散化（ribbon refined discretization）；随后对带状 BF 离散化施加适当的规范固定（gauge fixing）。

Result: 对于欧几里得号差（Euclidean signature）以及宇宙学常数为零或负的情形，带状 BF 离散化经规范固定后可导出 Regge 伸缩子引力表述，从而建立两种表述之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 该对应关系明确展示了带有相应测度（measure）的几何 Regge 路径积分如何从其规范理论表述中涌现，为两类描述在离散层面的等价性提供了具体机制。

Abstract: In this work we investigate the relation between gauge and geometric descriptions of two-dimensional dilaton gravity at the level of discrete path integrals. We develop a Regge discretization of Euclidean two-dimensional dilaton gravity using homogeneously curved simplices, as well as a ribbon refined discretization of two-dimensional BF theory adapted to homogeneously curved spacetimes. For Euclidean signature and vanishing or negative cosmological constant we show how the ribbon BF discretization, through appropriate gauge fixings, leads to the Regge dilaton gravity formulation. The resulting correspondence makes explicit how a geometric Regge path integral with its associated measure emerges from its gauge theoretic formulation.

</details>


### [181] [Stability and dynamics of bumblebee models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21066)
*Quentin G. Bailey,Robertus Potting,Dario T. Walter-Cardona*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究在 Minkowski 时空中具有 Maxwell 动能项、由非导数势（nonderivative potential）$V(X)$（$X=B_\mu B^\mu$）诱发自发洛伦兹破缺（spontaneous Lorentz violation）的矢量场论，即 bumblebee 模型。结果确认一类光滑势（含纯三次势）的哈密顿量密度有下界并在 $X=b^2>0$ 处取全局最小值，而标准二次势的哈密顿量无下界因而不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自发洛伦兹破缺（spontaneous Lorentz violation）是探讨洛伦兹对称性可能失效的重要途径，bumblebee 模型是其典型实现。本文动机在于检验何种势形式能提供稳定真空，并考察该模型的动力学与初值问题性质。

Method: 在 Minkowski 时空下取 Maxwell 动能项与非导数势 $V(X)$，分析哈密顿量密度是否有下界；求解类时（timelike）情形的哈密顿运动方程并检验其在全局最小值处的正则性；计算最小值附近小涨落的线性化谱（linearized spectrum）；并解析与数值研究球对称特解。

Result: 确认平滑势（包括纯三次势）使哈密顿密度有下界并在满足 $X=b^2>0$ 的非零类时期望值处取全局最小，诱发自发洛伦兹破缺；标准二次势的哈密顿量无下界，因而并不稳定；类时情形的哈密顿方程在全局最小处正则；还发现光滑初始组态的时间演化可产生类激波奇点（shock-like singularities），并识别出使场方程失去双曲性（hyperbolicity）从而破坏 Cauchy 初值问题适定性的组态子集。

Conclusion: 该模型在特定势下可具有稳定真空与正则的类时运动方程，但其时间演化可能出现类激波奇点，且部分场组态导致方程丧失双曲性与 Cauchy 问题适定性失效，因此并非全局良好定义。

Abstract: In this work, we study vector field theories with spontaneous Lorentz violation induced by a nonderivative potential $V(X)$ (with $X = B_μB^μ$), known as bumblebee models, with a Maxwell kinetic term in Minkowski spacetime. We confirm a recent result showing that a class of smooth potentials, including a purely cubic potential, leads to a Hamiltonian density that is bounded from below and assumes a global minimum for nonzero timelike expectation values of the vector field satisfying $X = b^2 > 0$, inducing spontaneous Lorentz-symmetry breaking. We show explicitly that the standard quadratic potential has a Hamiltonian that is unbounded from below and thus unstable. We analyze the Hamiltonian equations of motion for the timelike case and demonstrate that they are regular at the global minimum. Furthermore, we examine the linearized spectrum of small fluctuations around the minimum. We also study particular solutions with spherical symmetry analytically and numerically, without assuming linearization. We find that the time evolution of smooth initial configurations can develop shock-like singularities, and we explain why this behavior is expected in this model. We also identify a subset of the space of field configurations in which the field equations cease to be hyperbolic, leading to a breakdown of well-posedness of the Cauchy initial-value problem.

</details>


### [182] [Difference between pair creation and anomaly matching: Nontrivial aspects of invertible symmetries and defects in $SU(N)$ models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21478)
*Yoshiki Fukusumi,Shuma Nakashiba*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究在 $\mathbb{Z}_{N}$ 对称性保持条件下，$SU(N)_{K}$ 与 $SU(N)_{K'}$ Wess-Zumino-Witten（WZW）模型之间的关联，揭示其结构基础为拓扑量子场论中的（半）整数自旋简单流与共形场论中的反常匹配条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机在于刻画 $SU(N)_{K}$ WZW 模型与另一 $SU(N)_{K'}$ WZW 模型在保持未破缺 $\mathbb{Z}_{N}$ 对称性时的关联，并厘清配比条件与反常匹配条件在其中的作用与差异。

Method: 方法上，通过考察折叠模型 $SU(N)_{K}\boxtimes_{\mathbb{Z}_{N}} \overline{SU(N)_{K'}}$ 或 $SU(N)_{K}\boxtimes_{\mathbb{Z}_{N}} SU(N)_{K'}$ 的（半）整数自旋简单流条件与反常匹配条件，分别推导相关的模约束关系。

Result: 结果显示，简单流条件给出广义配比条件 $K= \pm R^{2}K' (\text{mod}\,N)$，其中 $\text{gcd}(R,N)=1$；反常匹配条件则给出较弱的 $K= \pm RUK' (\text{mod}\,N)$，其中 $\text{gcd}(R,N)=\text{gcd}(U,N)=1$。此外，不施加配比条件的反常匹配会导致 $\mathbb{Z}_{N}$ 缺陷与对称性守恒律的内在失配。

Conclusion: 结论指出，配比条件在范畴论意义上典范地将对称性与缺陷联系起来，但其能否作为禁止无质量重整化群流的指导原则尚不明确。

Abstract: We study the connection between the $SU(N)_{K}$ Wess-Zumino-Witten (WZW) model and $SU(N)_{K'}$ WZW models, enforcing the unbroken $\mathbb{Z}_{N}$ symmetries. The fundamental underlying structures are the (half-)integer spin simple currents in the topological quantum field theories and anomaly matching conditions in conformal field theories. For the (half-)integer spin simple current condition of the folded models $SU(N)_{K}\boxtimes_{\mathbb{Z}_{N}} \overline{SU(N)_{K'}}$ or $SU(N)_{K}\boxtimes_{\mathbb{Z}_{N}} SU(N)_{K'}$, we obtain the generalized pairing condition $K= \pm R^{2}K' (\text{mod}.N)$ with $\text{gcd}(R,N)=1$. On the other hand, the anomaly matching condition results in the weaker condition $K= \pm RUK' (\text{mod}.N)$ with $\text{gcd}(R,N)=\text{gcd}(U,N)=1$. Surprisingly, the anomaly matching without the pairing condition results in the intrinsic mismatch of the preservation law of the $\mathbb{Z}_{N}$ defects and symmetries. The pairing condition canonically relates symmetries and defects, as in category theory, whereas it is unclear whether it can be a guiding principle in prohibiting the massless renormalization group flows.

</details>


### [183] [Krylov complexity and oscillatory quantum dynamics in two-field inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21574)
*Tao Li,Lei-Hua Liu,Hai-Bing Fu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究双场暴胀（two-field inflation）中原初扰动（primordial perturbations）的Krylov空间动力学，通过求解曲率扰动与熵扰动的模式方程，构建二次哈密顿量并计算Krylov复杂度等量，揭示两个压缩通道在视界穿越前后具有不同的动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初扰动在双场暴胀中的量子态演化涉及曲率与熵扰动的耦合，理解其量子复杂性随视界穿越的演化有助于刻画暴胀期间的量子动力学特征。已有研究多关注单场情形，双场下多通道压缩的Krylov动力学尚待探讨。

Method: 从曲率扰动与熵扰动的二次作用量出发，采用Bunch-Davies初始条件求解模式方程，构建二次哈密顿量；将高斯量子态描述为具有两个压缩通道的四模压缩态（four-mode squeezed state），推导压缩强度与压缩角的演化，并据此计算Krylov复杂度（Krylov complexity）、Krylov熵（Krylov entropy）及通道分辨的Lanczos系数。

Result: 数值结果显示，两通道在视界内均处于弱压缩状态，视界穿越后呈现不同的压缩历史，具有不同的起始时间与振荡型Krylov动力学：第二通道较早发展出强压缩并主导中间演化，第一通道则延迟增强。对应的Krylov复杂度在视界穿越处呈振荡结构，在强压缩的超视界（superhorizon）区域出现显著涨落。

Conclusion: 这些振荡源于进入压缩方程的相对相位，并通过不同通道Krylov复杂度之间的非线性关系被放大，表明多通道压缩动力学对双场暴胀原初扰动的量子复杂度演化具有关键影响。

Abstract: We investigate Krylov-space dynamics of primordial perturbations in two-field inflation. Starting from the quadratic action for coupled curvature and entropy perturbations, we solve the mode equations with Bunch-Davies initial conditions and construct the corresponding quadratic Hamiltonian. The Gaussian quantum state is described by a four-mode squeezed state with two squeezing channels. We derive the evolution of the squeezing strength and squeezing angle and use them to evaluate the Krylov complexity, Krylov entropy, and channel-resolved Lanczos coefficients. Our numerical results show that the two channels remain weakly squeezed inside the horizon but develop different squeezing histories after horizon exit. The two channels exhibit different onset times and distinct oscillatory Krylov dynamics. The second channel develops strong squeezing earlier and dominates the intermediate evolution, whereas the first channel shows a delayed enhancement. The corresponding Krylov complexities exhibit oscillatory structures across horizon crossing and pronounced fluctuations in the strongly squeezed superhorizon regime. These oscillations originate from the relative phases entering the squeezing equations and are amplified through the nonlinear relation between Krylov complexity of different channels.

</details>


### [184] [Fortuitous BMN Cohomology at Every $N$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21592)
*Robert de Mello Koch,Minkyoo Kim,Sinong Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作将 $SU(3)$ 的 brane-fused 构造推广至任意 $SU(N)$（$N\\geq3$），通过选取单标量本征值对构造修正双色字母并代入 $SU(2)$ 阈值算符 $O_{24}$，得到规范不变的闭多项式算符，证明其非精确且 fortuitous，从而给出所有 $N\\geq3$ 的 fortuitous BMN 上同调存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限 $N$ 迹关系（finite-$N$ trace relations）可产生不存在稳定大 $N$ 祖先的 $Q$-上同调类；$SU(3)$ 的 brane-fused 构造提示这类态可保留 $SU(2)$ 非阿贝尔核心（nonabelian core）并伴随标量毛（scalar hair）。论文旨在将这一图像系统化为对每个 $SU(N)$ 的普遍构造。

Method: 从 $SU(2)$ 阈值算符（threshold operator）$O_{24}$ 出发，选取一个标量的一对本征值，构造 Q-变换严格遵循 $SU(2)$ 规则的修正双色字母（corrected two-colour letters）；代入 $O_{24}$ 得到每对对应的闭有理算符，乘以本征值间隔因子（eigenvalue-separation factors）消去分母，并对所有本征值对求和得到规范不变多项式；再借助精确块限制恒等式（exact block-restriction identity）恢复 $O_{24}$ 的非零倍数。

Result: 限制映射将每个稳定类映为稳定的 $SU(2)$ 类，由此证明所得算符非精确（non-exact）且为 fortuitous，即使纳入非库仑稳定类（non-Coulombic stable classes）亦成立；进而证明 fortuitous BMN 上同调（BMN cohomology）在每个 $N\\geq3$ 均存在。

Conclusion: 该构造为非阿贝尔核心加标量修饰（nonabelian core with scalar dressing）提供了一种精确的代数实现，并给出 fortuitous BMN 上同调在所有 $N\\geq3$ 的普遍存在性证明。

Abstract: Finite-$N$ trace relations can produce $Q$-cohomology classes with no stable large-$N$ ancestor. The brane-fused construction at $SU(3)$ suggests that such states can retain an $SU(2)$ nonabelian core accompanied by scalar hair. We develop this idea into a general construction for every $SU(N)$, $N\geq3$. Starting from the $SU(2)$ threshold operator $O_{24}$, we select pairs of eigenvalues of one scalar and construct corrected two-colour letters whose $Q$-transformations obey the $SU(2)$ rules exactly. Substitution into $O_{24}$ gives a closed rational operator for each pair. Multiplying by eigenvalue-separation factors removes the denominators, and summing over all pairs produces a gauge-invariant polynomial. An exact block-restriction identity recovers a nonzero multiple of $O_{24}$. The restriction maps every stable class to a stable $SU(2)$ class, proving that the resulting operator is non-exact and fortuitous, even when non-Coulombic stable classes are included. This proves the existence of fortuitous BMN cohomology at every $N\geq3$. The construction is an exact algebraic realization of a nonabelian core with scalar dressing.

</details>


### [185] [Breaking de Sitter Symmetry with Cosmic Topology: Cosmological Role of Infrared Vacuum Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28637)
*Anna Negro,Kurt Hinterbichler,Glenn D. Starkman,Yashar Akrami,Stefano Anselmi,Javier Carrón Duque,Mikel Martin Barandiaran,Andrius Tamosiunas*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在环面空间截面的德西特时空中，局域的 Hadamard 条件不受紧致化影响，但红外零模自由度不再被整体德西特不变性排除；该零模可表现为等效拓扑诱导源，贡献状态方程 P=-ρ 的宇宙学常数项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当空间截面拓扑非平凡时，通常用于选取 Bunch-Davies 真空的对称性论证不再成立，因此需要重新考察红外真空选取问题。作者以环面空间截面的德西特时空中标量场为对象，研究该情形下量子态自由度的影响。

Method: 采用半经典（semiclassical）框架，考察满足局域 Hadamard 条件的量子态；将环面等同（toroidal identifications）破缺整体德西特不变性所遗留的零模自由度视为有效拓扑诱导源，并分析其对暴胀德西特膨胀的影响。

Result: 红外区受环面等同影响，导致整体德西特不变性破缺，从而消除丢弃零模贡献的常规理由。对无质量标量场，零模部分可对应力能量张量（stress-energy tensor）贡献满足 P=-ρ 的状态方程，表现为宇宙学常数。

Conclusion: 驱动早期宇宙德西特阶段的源不必仅由作用量的局域项决定，也可依赖量子态的红外结构；非平凡拓扑作为德西特对称性破缺的具体实现，将原本未定的红外自由度转化为与半经典膨胀直接相关的贡献，并可能适用于当前宇宙的加速膨胀。

Abstract: When spatial sections of spacetime are topologically nontrivial, the symmetry arguments usually used to choose the Bunch-Davies vacuum are no longer guaranteed to apply. We study this problem for a scalar field on de Sitter spacetime with toroidal spatial sections. The Hadamard conditions, which ensure the same short-distance structure as in flat space, are local and naturally insensitive to the compactification. By contrast, we find that the infrared sector is affected by the toroidal identifications that break global de Sitter invariance, thereby removing the usual argument for discarding zero-mode contributions. We treat the remaining zero-mode freedom as an effective topology-induced source and analyze its effects on an inflationary de Sitter expansion within a semiclassical setting. We show that, for a massless scalar, the zero-mode sector can contain a contribution to the stress-energy tensor with equation of state $P=-ρ$, behaving as a cosmological constant. Thus, the source driving an early-Universe de Sitter phase need not be determined solely by local terms in the action, but can also depend on the infrared structure of the quantum state. Nontrivial topology, as a concrete realization of de Sitter symmetry violation, turns the otherwise-unfixed infrared freedom in vacuum selection to a contribution directly relevant to the semiclassical expansion. This contribution could potentially be applicable to the current accelerated expansion of the Universe.

</details>


### [186] [Renormalization of Quantum Gravity with Lagrange Multiplier Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21706)
*F. T. Brandt,J. Frenkel,S. Martins-Filho,D. G. C. McKeon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 Lagrange 乘子（Lagrange multiplier, LM）表述下研究 Einstein 引力与标量场耦合的重整化（renormalization），证明量子 LM 场的一圈重整化可在离壳（off-shell）条件下吸收六个独立发散结构中的五个，剩余一个正比于标量动能项平方的抵消项无法被吸收。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LM 表述将量子 LM 场耦合到规范固定后的引力与标量场运动方程，从而在离壳层面区分运动方程发散与不可约发散结构。纯引力情形下 LM 重整化可吸收全部发散，本文旨在考察引入标量场耦合后该性质是否保持。

Method: 采用 Lagrange 乘子表述，将一对量子 LM 场 $(\lambda'_{\mu\nu}, \sigma')$ 耦合至规范固定的引力与标量场运动方程；利用该框架不存在更高圈修正的性质，在一圈阶对两个量子 LM 场进行重整化，并分析六个独立发散结构及 LM 场重定义（field redefinition）的可吸收性。

Result: 量子 LM 场的一圈重整化在离壳条件下吸收五个独立发散结构；剩余抵消项正比于 $(\partial_\mu\bar\phi\,\partial^\mu\bar\phi)^2$，在壳（on shell）不为零，且不能被任何 LM 场重定义吸收，因为此类重定义仅生成在壳消失的抵消项。对比之下，纯引力（无物质）情形下 LM 重整化吸收全部一圈发散，无需额外抵消项。

Conclusion: LM 表述提供了离壳层面的清晰分离：运动方程发散被吸收进 LM 场重整化，而单一不可约结构被单独分离出来；标量场耦合破坏了纯引力中全部发散可被吸收的性质。

Abstract: We study the renormalization of Einstein gravity coupled to a scalar field in the Lagrange multiplier (LM) formulation, where a pair of quantum LM fields, $(λ'_{μν},σ')$, is coupled to the gauge-fixed gravitational and scalar equations of motion. Using the fact that in this approach there are no higher loop corrections, we show that renormalizing the two quantum LM fields at one-loop order absorbs, off-shell, five out of the six independent divergent structures. The remaining off-shell counterterm, proportional to $ (\partial_μ\barφ\,\partial^μ\barφ)^2, $ is nonzero on shell and cannot be absorbed by any LM field redefinition, since such redefinitions generate only counterterms that vanish on shell. In contrast, in pure gravity (no matter), the LM renormalization absorbs all one-loop divergences and no additional counterterms are required. The LM formulation therefore provides a sharp off-shell separation between equation-of-motion divergences, absorbed into LM field renormalizations, and the single irreducible structure.

</details>


### [187] [One generation of the Standard Model as a quantum error-correcting code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21879)
*Shiroman Prakash,K. L. Ramya*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 将标准模型单代费米子（含右手中微子）编码为五个比特的组合结构，并证明反常消除（anomaly cancellation）等价于三正交码（triorthogonal code）中三次型消失这一定义性质；去除右手中微子后得到 $[[15,1,3]]_p$ 码，$p=2$ 时为 Bravyi-Kitaev 码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 右手中微子与单代费米子的五比特标记方式同反常消除之间存在已知联系，但此前缺乏精确表述。本文旨在把这一联系形式化为量子纠错码（quantum error-correcting code）语言下的严格对应。

Method: 将单代费米子的五比特标记视为组合结构，把反常消除条件刻画为某个三次型（cubic form）的消失，该条件正是三正交码的定义性质——三正交码是魔态蒸馏（magic state distillation）所依赖的量子纠错码。通过去除右手中微子构造出对应的量子码。

Result: 去除右手中微子后，对每个素数 $p$ 均得到一个 $[[15,1,3]]_p$ 码；其中 $p=2$ 时即为 Bravyi-Kitaev 码。在 qutrit（三能级量子系统）情形下，该码是唯一的最短距离为 3 的三正交码。

Conclusion: 反常消除与三正交码的定义性质之间存在精确对应，标准模型单代费米子结构可自然地映射到具体量子纠错码，并给出 $p=2$ 与 qutrit 情形下的唯一性刻画。

Abstract: Together with a right-handed neutrino, the fermions of one generation of the Standard Model are labelled by five bits, a combinatorial structure tied to anomaly cancellation. We make this precise. Anomaly cancellation is the vanishing of a cubic form, the defining property of triorthogonal codes, the quantum error-correcting codes behind magic state distillation. Removing the right-handed neutrino gives a $[[15,1,3]]_p$ code for every prime $p$, including the Bravyi-Kitaev code for $p = 2$. For qutrits it is the unique shortest distance-three triorthogonal code.

</details>


### [188] [Vortex clusters and vortex-filled bubbles in a modified Abelian-Higgs model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21930)
*D. Miguélez-Caballero,S. Navarro-Obregón,Muneto Nitta,Keisuke Ohashi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究真空流形为两个不连通圆的单分量阿贝尔-希格斯（Abelian-Higgs）模型，发现其具有类 type-1.5 行为，并给出涡旋（vortex）—畴壁（domain wall）束缚态的解析与稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有多连通真空流形（vacuum manifold）的标量场模型可同时支持拓扑缺陷，但单分量模型中涡旋与畴壁共存时的相互作用与束缚机制尚待刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用含八阶标量势（eighth-order scalar potential）的单分量阿贝尔-希格斯模型；解析构造畴壁（domain wall），推导涡旋—壁（vortex-wall）相互作用的渐近形式，并对重合多涡旋（coincident multivortices）的涨落谱（fluctuation spectra）进行分析以识别其分裂不稳定性（splitting instabilities）。

Result: 模型支持两类涡旋及连接两个真空分量的畴壁；涡间相互作用呈非单调性，出现空间分离涡旋的束缚态。内真空（inner-vacuum）涡旋通过短程排斥与长程吸引形成团簇（clusters），外真空（outer-vacuum）涡旋则形成被封闭畴壁限制的“涡旋填充气泡（vortex-filled bubbles）”。

Conclusion: 该单分量阿贝尔-希格斯模型展现出类 type-1.5 行为，其涡旋—畴壁束缚结构由非单调涡间相互作用与渐近涡旋—壁相互作用共同决定，多涡旋涨落谱中的分裂不稳定性可用于判别这些组态的稳定性。

Abstract: We investigate a single-component Abelian-Higgs model with an eighth-order scalar potential whose vacuum manifold consists of two disconnected circles. The model supports two species of vortices and domain walls interpolating between the two vacuum components. We find type-1.5-like behavior characterized by nonmonotonic intervortex interactions and bound states of spatially separated vortices. Inner-vacuum vortices form clusters through short-range repulsion and long-range attraction, whereas outer-vacuum vortices form vortex-filled bubbles in which a closed domain wall confines multiple inner-vacuum-vortex cores. We construct the domain wall analytically, derive the asymptotic vortex-wall interactions, and analyze the fluctuation spectra of coincident multivortices to identify their splitting instabilities.

</details>


### [189] [Monopole-deformed Kim-Park dualities from elementary dualities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22054)
*Sungjoon Kim,Jehyun Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ 伴随（adjoint）SQCD（超对称量子色动力学）在酉规范群下、单极子形变（monopole-deformed）Kim-Park 对偶性的椭球配分函数（ellipsoid partition function）恒等式，推导仅以 Aharony 与 Benini-Benvenuti-Pasquetti 对偶性为基本构件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是验证并刻画单极子形变的 Kim-Park 对偶性，通过椭球配分函数恒等式给出该对偶关系在配分函数层面的一致性证据，并借助已知对偶性作为基本构件来完成推导。

Method: 将伴随场（adjoint）去禁闭（deconfining）为 $C_p[U(N)]$ 箭图尾（quiver tail），显式构造对偶序列；再通过禁闭（confinement）与降秩对偶链（rank-reducing duality chain）得到预期的磁对偶配分函数，仅使用 Aharony 与 Benini-Benvenuti-Pasquetti 对偶性作为构件。

Result: 得到预期的磁对偶配分函数，包含单态（singlet）贡献；同时给出 $C_p[U(N)]$ s-禁闭（s-confinement）的另一种配分函数推导，并提供 Quiver Lab——一个用于在箭图上实现与可视化对偶移动的独立 HTML 应用。

Conclusion: 该工作以配分函数恒等式形式支持了单极子形变的 Kim-Park 对偶性，说明其可由 Aharony 与 Benini-Benvenuti-Pasquetti 对偶性仅作为构件导出，并附带可用于箭图对偶操作的 Quiver Lab 工具。

Abstract: We derive the ellipsoid partition function identities for the monopole-deformed Kim-Park dualities of 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ adjoint SQCD with unitary gauge groups. The derivation uses only Aharony and Benini-Benvenuti-Pasquetti dualities as building blocks. By deconfining the adjoint into a $C_p[U(N)]$ quiver tail, we construct explicit duality sequences. Confinement and a rank-reducing duality chain then yield the expected magnetic partition functions, including the singlet contributions. We also present an alternative partition-function derivation of the $C_p[U(N)]$ s-confinement and provide Quiver Lab, a standalone HTML application for implementing and visualizing duality moves on quiver diagrams.

</details>


### [190] [Aspects of Codimension two Holography in de Sitter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22101)
*Kanhu Kishore Nanda,Somnath Porey,Masataka Watanabe,Gopal Yadav*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 Yadav (2024) 工作基础上，进一步研究 de Sitter（dS）空间中的余维二全息（codimension two holography），通过计算全息纠缠熵、配分函数与 Renyi 熵为该对偶提供进一步证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者延续 Yadav (2024) 的研究，旨在深化对 de Sitter 空间中余维二全息对偶的理解，并检验其在不同切片方式下的自洽性。

Method: 将 d 维体时空（bulk）dS 空间用两块世界末端（end of the world）(d-1) 维膜切片，膜分别赋予 Lorentzian dS 或 Euclidean AdS 度规，并在共同的 (d-2) 维空间相接，对偶 CFT 即生活于此。针对两种切片分别显式计算全息纠缠熵、配分函数与 Renyi 熵，并比较其异同。

Result: 两种切片下的全息纠缠熵、配分函数与 Renyi 熵计算结果显示出相似性与差异性，从而为该对偶关系提供了进一步证据。

Conclusion: 计算结果支持 de Sitter 空间中的余维二全息对偶，并对该全息框架的进一步推广作出讨论。

Abstract: Following \cite{Yadav:2024ray} we embark on a further study of the codimension two holography in de Sitter (dS) space. The $d$ dimensional bulk dS space is sliced by two end of the world $(d-1)$ dimensional branes which are bestowed with either Lorentzian dS or Euclidean AdS metric joining at a common $(d-2)$ dimensional space where the dual CFT lives. Through explicit computations of the holographic entanglement entropy, partition function, and Renyi entropy in both slicings for the bulk dS space emphasizing their similarities as well as differences we provide further evidence for this duality. We also make comments on further generalization of the codimension two holography.

</details>


### [191] [Non-Coulomb $Q$-Cohomology and Quantum Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22332)
*Tanay Kibe,Sam van Leuven,Kayleigh Mathieson*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维 $\mathcal N=4$ 超杨-米尔斯理论中经典 BPS 上同调类的量子提升（quantum lifting），证明单圈修正 $Q_1$ 湮灭所有单调类（monotone class），且其非零像均为非库仑型，从而库仑单调类在有限秩下该阶存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 BPS 上同调（BPS cohomology）中某些类在 Cartan 限制下消失，其非库仑特征与量子提升机制尚不清晰；需要理解这些单调类（monotone）在量子修正下是否存活，以及量子提升的结构性限制。

Method: 以规范微子双线性 $\mathcal S=\text{Tr}(\lambda_+\lambda_-)$ 的幂为对象；对 $SO(7)$ 识别 $\mathcal S^4$ 为已知提升单调类的超共形后代，并显式构造其偶然提升伙伴（fortuitous lifting partner）；进而对一般规范群分析单圈超荷修正 $Q_1$ 的作用，并借助标量判别式的高次幂湮灭性质进行论证。

Result: 单圈修正 $Q_1$ 湮灭经典上同调中的每个单调类，且每个非零 $Q_1$ 像均为非库仑型；因此每个非零单圈配对均具有偶然源与非库仑靶。$SO(7)$ 中 $\mathcal S^4$ 被识别为已知提升单调类的超共形后代，并构造了其偶然提升伙伴。BMN 区域内非库仑单调类在单圈阶同样存活。

Conclusion: 经典 BPS 上同调的量子提升受一般规范群下的结构性限制：库仑单调类在有限秩的单圈阶存活；每个非库仑类（偶然或单调）在经典上同调中均被标量判别式的足够高次幂湮灭，此性质被解释为部分无毛性质（partial no-hair property）。

Abstract: We study quantum lifting of classical BPS cohomology classes in four-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory. Certain powers of the gaugino bilinear $\mathcal S=\text{Tr}(λ_+λ_-)$ in orthogonal theories provide non-Coulomb monotones: multi-graviton classes that vanish upon Cartan restriction. For $SO(7)$, we identify $\mathcal S^4$ as a superconformal descendant of a known lifted monotone class, explaining the latter's non-Coulomb character, and explicitly construct a fortuitous lifting partner for $\mathcal S^4$. This construction provides a strategy for finding analogous partners for higher powers at higher ranks. Our main result establishes restrictions on the quantum lifting of classical BPS cohomologies for general gauge groups. We show that the one-loop correction $Q_1$ to the supercharge annihilates every monotone class in classical cohomology, while every non-zero $Q_1$ image is of non-Coulomb type. Thus every non-zero one-loop pairing has a fortuitous source and a non-Coulomb target, so that Coulomb monotones survive at this order at finite rank. In the BMN sector, our results imply that non-Coulomb monotones survive at one loop order as well. A crucial step in the argument shows that every non-Coulomb class, fortuitous or monotone, is annihilated in classical cohomology by a sufficiently high power of a scalar discriminant, which we interpret as a partial no-hair property.

</details>
